/* =====================================================================
   CLOUD + AUTH  (Google sign-in; staff and sanitized board roles)
   ===================================================================== */
let db = null;
let cloudOn = false;
let MODE = null;                 // "staff" | "board"
let USER = { email: "", name: "" };
let SESSION = null;

const DEPT_ORDER = ["opsmtl", "opsto", "marketing", "fundraising", "hr", "board"];
const DEPT_LABEL = { opsmtl: "Ops Montreal", opsto: "Ops Toronto", marketing: "Marketing", fundraising: "Fundraising", hr: "Human Resources", board: "Board", finance: "Finance" };
const DEPT_COLOR = { opsmtl: "#103F39", opsto: "#3a6ea5", marketing: "#6F64AB", fundraising: "#c87a2f", hr: "#b3402f", board: "#4a4f57", finance: "#b9851b" };
const CATEGORIES = ["Budget or purchase", "Hiring or people", "Legal, risk, or governance", "Strategy or direction", "Partnership or external", "Ethics or policy"];
const CAT_COLORS = { "Budget or purchase": "#c87a2f", "Hiring or people": "#b3402f", "Legal, risk, or governance": "#4a4f57", "Strategy or direction": "#103F39", "Partnership or external": "#3a6ea5", "Ethics or policy": "#6F64AB" };

function isStaff() { return MODE === "staff"; }
function esc(s) { const d = document.createElement("div"); d.textContent = s == null ? "" : String(s); return d.innerHTML.replace(/"/g, "&quot;").replace(/'/g, "&#39;"); } /* audit 26/09/26: quotes too, esc() is used inside attribute values */
function catTag(cat) { if (!cat) return ""; return '<span class="cat-tag" style="background:' + (CAT_COLORS[cat] || "#797773") + '">' + esc(cat) + "</span>"; }

function initCloud() {
  const dot = document.getElementById("cloudDot");
  const txt = document.getElementById("cloudStatus");
  try {
    db = window.supabase.createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, { auth: { flowType: "pkce" } });
    cloudOn = true;
    dot.className = "dot ok";
    txt.textContent = "Cloud connected";
  } catch (e) {
    cloudOn = false;
    dot.className = "dot local";
    txt.textContent = "Cloud error, reload to retry";
  }
}

async function signInGoogle() {
  const err = document.getElementById("gateErr");
  if (location.protocol === "file:") {
    document.getElementById("gateFileNote").style.display = "block";
    return;
  }
  const { error } = await db.auth.signInWithOAuth({
    provider: "google",
    options: {
      redirectTo: location.origin + location.pathname,
      scopes: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/calendar.readonly",
      queryParams: { access_type: "offline", prompt: "select_account" }
    }
  });
  if (error) err.textContent = "Sign-in failed: " + error.message;
}

async function signOutAll() {
  try { await db.auth.signOut(); } catch (e) { /* proceed */ }
  location.hash = "";
  location.reload();
}

function showGate(kind, email) {
  const g = document.getElementById("gate");
  const msg = document.getElementById("gateMsg");
  const alt = document.getElementById("gateAlt");
  g.style.display = "flex";
  if (kind === "denied") {
    /* wave175b: an account that is not on the team is signed out at once (never left signed in on the Hub); the
       message shows on the sign-in screen after the reload */
    try { sessionStorage.setItem("wa_gate_denied", String(email || "")); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    msg.textContent = "Signing out…";
    try { Object.keys(localStorage).filter(function (k) { return /^sb-.*-auth-token/.test(k); }).forEach(function (k) { localStorage.removeItem(k); }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    signOutAll();
    return;
  }
  /* wave175b: the sign-in screen shows at once rather than after the loading screen's 9 second failsafe */
  try { document.documentElement.classList.add("wa-ready"); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  let denied = null;
  try { denied = sessionStorage.getItem("wa_gate_denied"); sessionStorage.removeItem("wa_gate_denied"); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  if (kind === "signin" && denied !== null) {
    msg.innerHTML = "The account <b>" + esc(denied) + "</b> is not on the Welfare Avenue team, so it was signed out. The Hub is for staff (a welfareavenue.com account) and board members Steel or Suhaib have added. Volunteers and donors sign in on welfareavenue.com.";
  } else if (kind === "error") {
    msg.textContent = "Could not reach the cloud. Check the connection and reload.";
  }
  if (location.protocol === "file:") document.getElementById("gateFileNote").style.display = "block";
  document.getElementById("gateGoogleBtn").addEventListener("click", signInGoogle);
  const so = document.getElementById("gateSignOutBtn");
  if (so) so.addEventListener("click", signOutAll);
}
function hideGate() { document.getElementById("gate").style.display = "none"; }

function displayName(session) {
  const m = (session.user && session.user.user_metadata) || {};
  return m.full_name || m.name || (session.user.email || "").split("@")[0];
}
function firstName() { return (USER.name || "").split(" ")[0]; }

/* =====================================================================
   LIVE DATA LAYER
   ===================================================================== */
let DB_DECISIONS = [], DB_META = {}, DB_UPDATES = [], DB_TASKS = [], DB_MEMBERS = [], DB_ASSIGN = [], DB_CHANGES = [];
let DB_SENTIMENT = [], DB_HOURS = [], DB_CLICKUP = [];
let MEMBER_BY_ID = {};
let LAST_SEEN = null;
let MEETINGS = {};              // dept -> [{title, start, link}]

/* One-time seed if the cloud tables are ever empty (disaster recovery). */
async function seedIfEmpty() {
  if (!cloudOn || !isStaff()) return;
  try {
    const d = await db.from("decisions").select("id").limit(1);
    if (d.data && d.data.length === 0) {
      await db.from("decisions").insert(
        DECISIONS.map((x, i) => ({ pos: i + 1, title: x.t, area: x.area, suggested_move: x.move }))
      );
    }
    const m = await db.from("dept_meta").select("department").limit(1);
    if (m.data && m.data.length === 0) {
      await db.from("dept_meta").insert(
        DEPT_ORDER.map(id => ({ department: id, read: SECTIONS[id].read, progress: SECTIONS[id].progress || "", priorities: SECTIONS[id].priorities || [] }))
      );
    }
  } catch (e) { /* seeding is best effort */ }
}

async function loadAll() {
  const base = [
    db.from("decisions").select("*").order("pos", { ascending: true }),
    db.from("dept_meta").select("*"),
    db.from("dept_updates").select("*"),
    db.from("dept_tasks").select("*"),
    db.from("team_members").select("*").order("name", { ascending: true }),
    db.from("task_assignees").select("*"),
    db.from("dept_sentiment").select("*").gte("captured_on", new Date(Date.now() - 40 * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10)),
    db.from("slack_hours").select("*").gte("week_start", new Date(Date.now() - 42 * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10)),
    Promise.resolve({ data: [] }) /* audit 26/09/26: clickup_weekly is no longer read anywhere (wave116g), so it is not loaded */
  ];
  if (isStaff()) base.push(db.from("change_log").select("*").order("changed_at", { ascending: false }).limit(300));
  const res = await Promise.all(base);
  DB_DECISIONS = res[0].data || [];
  DB_META = {}; (res[1].data || []).forEach(r => DB_META[r.department] = r);
  DB_UPDATES = res[2].data || [];
  DB_TASKS = res[3].data || [];
  DB_MEMBERS = (res[4].data || []).filter(r => r.active !== false);
  DB_ASSIGN = res[5].data || [];
  DB_SENTIMENT = res[6].data || [];
  DB_HOURS = res[7].data || [];
  DB_CLICKUP = res[8].data || [];
  DB_CHANGES = isStaff() ? (res[9].data || []) : [];
  MEMBER_BY_ID = {}; DB_MEMBERS.forEach(m => MEMBER_BY_ID[m.id] = m);
}

async function loadLastSeen() {
  try {
    const r = await db.from("user_state").select("last_seen_at").eq("email", USER.email).maybeSingle();
    LAST_SEEN = r.data ? r.data.last_seen_at : null;
  } catch (e) { LAST_SEEN = null; }
}
async function markSeen() {
  try { await db.from("user_state").upsert({ email: USER.email, last_seen_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }); } catch (e) { /* best effort */ }
}

/* keep Slack deep links pointing at wherever the site actually lives */
async function maybeUpdateSiteUrl() {
  if (!isStaff() || !location.protocol.startsWith("http")) return;
  try {
    const here = location.origin + location.pathname;
    const r = await db.from("app_config").select("value").eq("key", "site_url").maybeSingle();
    if (r.data && r.data.value !== here) {
      await db.from("app_config").update({ value: here, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq("key", "site_url");
    }
  } catch (e) { /* best effort */ }
}

function taskAssignees(taskId) {
  return DB_ASSIGN.filter(a => a.task_id === taskId).map(a => MEMBER_BY_ID[a.member_id]).filter(Boolean);
}

/* accessors */
function getDecisions() {
  return DB_DECISIONS.map(r => ({
    ref: r.id, pos: r.pos, title: r.title, area: r.area, move: r.suggested_move,
    done: !!r.done, by: r.updated_by, at: r.updated_at,
    cat: r.reason_category, note: r.reason_note, decidedNote: r.decided_note, decidedAt: r.decided_at,
    confidential: !!r.confidential, opened: r.opened_at, raisedBy: r.raised_by, owner: r.owner
  }));
}
function getMeta(id) {
  if (DB_META[id]) return DB_META[id];
  return { read: SECTIONS[id].read, progress: SECTIONS[id].progress || "", priorities: SECTIONS[id].priorities || [] };
}
function getUpdates(id) {
  return DB_UPDATES.filter(r => r.department === id)
    .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.created_at) - new Date(a.created_at))
    .map(r => ({ id: r.id, label: r.entry_label, text: r.text, at: r.created_at, confidential: !!r.confidential }));
}
function getTasks(id) {
  return DB_TASKS.filter(r => r.department === id)
    .sort((a, b) => (a.sort || 0) - (b.sort || 0))
    .map(r => ({
      ref: r.id, subsection: r.subsection, title: r.title, owner: r.owner, status: r.status,
      flag: !!r.flag, priority: !!r.priority, done: !!r.done, by: r.updated_by, at: r.updated_at,
      opened: r.opened_at, closed: r.closed_at, due: r.due_date,
      cat: r.reason_category, note: r.reason_note, confidential: !!r.confidential,
      assignees: taskAssignees(r.id)
    }));
}

/* =====================================================================
   WRITE-BACKS  (all attributed to the signed-in user, all change-logged)
   ===================================================================== */
function who() { return USER.name || USER.email || "Unknown"; }

async function logChange(entityType, entityId, field, oldV, newV) {
  try {
    await db.from("change_log").insert({
      entity_type: entityType, entity_id: String(entityId), field: field,
      old_value: oldV == null ? null : String(oldV), new_value: newV == null ? null : String(newV),
      changed_by: who()
    });
  } catch (e) { /* logging is best effort */ }
}

async function toggleTask(ref, done) {
  const patch = { done: done, updated_by: who(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
  const r = await db.from("dept_tasks").update(patch).eq("id", ref);
  if (r.error) { toast("Save failed, check connection"); return false; }
  const row = DB_TASKS.find(x => x.id === ref);
  if (row) { Object.assign(row, patch); row.closed_at = done ? new Date().toISOString() : null; }
  logChange("dept_task", ref, "done", done ? "open" : "closed", done ? "closed" : "open");
  toast(done ? "Marked done, saved" : "Reopened, saved");
  return true;
}

function reassignTask(ref, deptId) {
  const li = document.querySelector('li[data-task="' + ref + '"]');
  const cur = (DB_TASKS.find(x => x.id == ref) || {}).owner || "";
  const holder = li ? li.querySelector(".ci-sub") : null;
  if (!holder || holder.querySelector("select")) return;
  const sel = document.createElement("select");
  sel.style.cssText = "margin-left:8px;font-size:12px;padding:4px 8px;max-width:220px;";
  let opts = '<option value="">Reassign to...</option><option value="__unassigned">Unassigned</option><option value="__custom">Type a name...</option>';
  DEPT_ORDER.forEach(d => {
    const ms = DB_MEMBERS.filter(m => m.department === d);
    if (!ms.length) return;
    opts += '<optgroup label="' + esc(DEPT_LABEL[d] || d) + '">';
    ms.forEach(m => opts += '<option value="' + esc(m.name) + '"' + (m.name === cur ? " selected" : "") + ">" + esc(m.name) + "</option>");
    opts += "</optgroup>";
  });
  sel.innerHTML = opts;
  holder.appendChild(sel);
  sel.focus();
  let saving = false;
  sel.addEventListener("change", async () => {
    let w = sel.value;
    if (!w) { sel.remove(); return; }
    if (w === "__unassigned") w = "";
    if (w === "__custom") { w = prompt("Owner name:", cur); if (w === null) { sel.remove(); return; } }
    saving = true;
    const r = await db.from("dept_tasks").update({ owner: w, updated_by: who(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq("id", ref);
    if (r.error) { toast("Reassign failed, check connection"); sel.remove(); return; }
    const row = DB_TASKS.find(x => x.id == ref); if (row) row.owner = w;
    logChange("dept_task", ref, "owner", cur, w);
    renderSection(deptId); renderNeedsMe();
    toast("Reassigned to " + (w || "unassigned"));
  });
  sel.addEventListener("blur", () => { setTimeout(() => { if (!saving && sel.parentElement) sel.remove(); }, 250); });
}

async function addUpdate(id) {
  const label = prompt("Update label (e.g. Jul 2 or Jul 2 (Otter)):", "");
  if (label === null) return;
  const text = prompt("Update text:", "");
  if (!text) return;
  const confidential = confirm("Hide this update from the board view?\nOK = confidential, Cancel = visible to board viewers");
  const r = await db.from("dept_updates").insert({ department: id, entry_label: label || "", text: text, added_by: who(), confidential: confidential, created_at: new Date().toISOString() });
  if (r.error) { toast("Save failed"); return; }
  logChange("dept_update", id, "added", null, (label || "") + ": " + text);
  await loadAll(); renderSection(id); renderWhatsNew(); toast("Update added");
}

/* ---------- modal framework ---------- */
function openModal(html) {
  const back = document.getElementById("modalBack");
  document.getElementById("modalBox").innerHTML = html;
  back.style.display = "flex";
}
function closeModal() { document.getElementById("modalBack").style.display = "none"; }
document.getElementById("modalBack").addEventListener("click", e => { if (e.target.id === "modalBack") closeModal(); });

function categoryOptions(sel) {
  let o = '<option value="">No category</option>';
  CATEGORIES.forEach(c => o += '<option value="' + esc(c) + '"' + (sel === c ? " selected" : "") + ">" + esc(c) + "</option>");
  const isCustom = sel && CATEGORIES.indexOf(sel) < 0;
  o += '<option value="__other"' + (isCustom ? " selected" : "") + ">Other (type below)</option>";
  return o;
}

async function suggestReason(kind, title, area, move) {
  try {
    const { data, error } = await db.functions.invoke("suggest-reason", { body: { kind: kind, title: title, area: area || "", move: move || "" } });
    if (error || !data || !data.reason_category) return null;
    return data;
  } catch (e) { return null; }
}

/* ---------- task add / edit modal ---------- */
let PICKER = [];   // selected member ids

function pickerChipHtml() {
  return PICKER.map(id => {
    const m = MEMBER_BY_ID[id];
    return m ? '<span class="as-chip" data-remove="' + id + '" title="Remove">' + esc(m.name) + " &times;</span>" : "";
  }).join("") || '<span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted);">No assignees yet</span>';
}
function refreshPickerChips() {
  const el = document.getElementById("pkChips");
  if (!el) return;
  el.innerHTML = pickerChipHtml();
  el.querySelectorAll("[data-remove]").forEach(ch => ch.addEventListener("click", () => {
    PICKER = PICKER.filter(x => x !== Number(ch.dataset.remove));
    refreshPickerChips();
  }));
}

function taskModal(deptId, task) {
  const sec = SECTIONS[deptId];
  const isEdit = !!task;
  PICKER = isEdit ? task.assignees.map(a => a.id) : [];
  const subs = (sec.subsections || []).map(s => s.title);
  const deptMembers = DB_MEMBERS.filter(m => m.department === deptId);
  const otherMembers = DB_MEMBERS.filter(m => m.department !== deptId);
  let memberOpts = '<option value="">+ Add assignee from roster...</option>';
  if (deptMembers.length) {
    memberOpts += '<optgroup label="' + esc(DEPT_LABEL[deptId]) + ' team">';
    deptMembers.forEach(m => memberOpts += '<option value="' + m.id + '">' + esc(m.name) + (m.role ? " (" + esc(m.role) + ")" : "") + "</option>");
    memberOpts += "</optgroup>";
  }
  memberOpts += '<optgroup label="Everyone else">';
  otherMembers.forEach(m => memberOpts += '<option value="' + m.id + '">' + esc(m.name) + " (" + esc(DEPT_LABEL[m.department] || m.department || "") + ")</option>");
  memberOpts += "</optgroup>";

  const subOpts = subs.length
    ? '<label>Workstream</label><select id="tmSub"><option value="">General</option>' +
      subs.map(s => '<option value="' + esc(s) + '"' + (isEdit && task.subsection === s ? " selected" : "") + ">" + esc(s) + "</option>").join("") + "</select>"
    : "";

  openModal(
    "<h3>" + (isEdit ? "Edit task" : "Add task") + '</h3>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">' + esc(DEPT_LABEL[deptId]) + ". Date added is automatic; set a due date so aging is visible.</p>" +
    '<label>Task</label><input type="text" id="tmTitle" value="' + (isEdit ? esc(task.title) : "") + '" placeholder="What needs to happen" />' +
    subOpts +
    '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Due date</label><input type="date" id="tmDue" value="' + (isEdit && task.due ? task.due : "") + '" /></div>' +
    '<div><label>Owner (accountable)</label><input type="text" id="tmOwner" value="' + (isEdit ? esc(task.owner || "") : "") + '" placeholder="e.g. Amanda" /></div></div>' +
    "<label>Assignees</label>" +
    '<div class="picker-chips" id="pkChips"></div>' +
    '<select id="pkAdd">' + memberOpts + "</select>" +
    '<div class="m-row" style="margin-top:12px;"><div><label style="margin-top:0;"><input type="checkbox" id="tmFlag"' + (isEdit && task.flag ? " checked" : "") + " /> Needs an executive decision</label></div>" +
    '<div><label style="margin-top:0;"><input type="checkbox" id="tmConf"' + (isEdit && task.confidential ? " checked" : "") + " /> Confidential (hidden from board view)</label></div></div>" +
    '<div id="tmReasonWrap" style="display:' + (isEdit && task.flag ? "block" : "none") + ';">' +
    "<label>Reason category</label><select id=\"tmCat\">" + categoryOptions(isEdit ? task.cat : "") + "</select>" +
    '<input type="text" id="tmCatOther" placeholder="Custom category" style="margin-top:6px;display:none;" />' +
    '<label>Why this needs an executive (one line)</label><input type="text" id="tmNote" placeholder="Leave blank and the Hub writes it on save" value="' + (isEdit ? esc(task.note || "") : "") + '" /></div>' +
    '<div class="m-foot"><button class="btn ghost" id="tmCancel">Cancel</button><button class="btn" id="tmSave">' + (isEdit ? "Save changes" : "Add task") + "</button></div>"
  );

  refreshPickerChips();
  document.getElementById("pkAdd").addEventListener("change", e => {
    const v = Number(e.target.value);
    if (v && PICKER.indexOf(v) < 0) PICKER.push(v);
    e.target.value = "";
    refreshPickerChips();
  });
  document.getElementById("tmFlag").addEventListener("change", e => {
    document.getElementById("tmReasonWrap").style.display = e.target.checked ? "block" : "none";
  });
  const catSel = document.getElementById("tmCat");
  const catOther = document.getElementById("tmCatOther");
  function syncCatOther() { catOther.style.display = catSel.value === "__other" ? "block" : "none"; }
  catSel.addEventListener("change", syncCatOther); syncCatOther();
  if (isEdit && task.cat && CATEGORIES.indexOf(task.cat) < 0) { catOther.value = task.cat; catOther.style.display = "block"; }
  document.getElementById("tmCancel").addEventListener("click", closeModal);
  document.getElementById("tmSave").addEventListener("click", () => saveTaskModal(deptId, task));
}

async function saveTaskModal(deptId, task) {
  const title = document.getElementById("tmTitle").value.trim();
  if (!title) { toast("Give the task a title"); return; }
  const subEl = document.getElementById("tmSub");
  const flag = document.getElementById("tmFlag").checked;
  let cat = null, note = null;
  if (flag) {
    const catSel = document.getElementById("tmCat").value;
    cat = catSel === "__other" ? (document.getElementById("tmCatOther").value.trim() || null) : (catSel || null);
    note = document.getElementById("tmNote").value.trim() || null;
    if (!cat || !note) {
      toast("The Hub is writing the reason...");
      const s = await suggestReason("task", title, DEPT_LABEL[deptId] || "", document.getElementById("tmOwner").value.trim());
      if (s) { cat = cat || s.reason_category; note = note || s.reason_note; }
      if (!cat) cat = "Strategy or direction";
      if (!note) note = "Needs an executive call";
    }
  }
  const patch = {
    title: title,
    subsection: subEl ? (subEl.value || null) : (task ? task.subsection : null),
    owner: document.getElementById("tmOwner").value.trim(),
    due_date: document.getElementById("tmDue").value || null,
    flag: flag,
    reason_category: cat,
    reason_note: note,
    confidential: document.getElementById("tmConf").checked,
    updated_by: who(),
    updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
  };
  let taskId;
  if (task) {
    const r = await db.from("dept_tasks").update(patch).eq("id", task.ref);
    if (r.error) { toast("Save failed"); return; }
    taskId = task.ref;
    if (task.title !== title) logChange("dept_task", taskId, "title", task.title, title);
    if ((task.due || null) !== patch.due_date) logChange("dept_task", taskId, "due_date", task.due, patch.due_date);
    if ((task.owner || "") !== patch.owner) logChange("dept_task", taskId, "owner", task.owner, patch.owner);
    if (task.flag !== flag) logChange("dept_task", taskId, "flag", String(task.flag), String(flag));
    if (task.confidential !== patch.confidential) logChange("dept_task", taskId, "confidential", String(task.confidential), String(patch.confidential));
  } else {
    patch.department = deptId;
    patch.status = "Open";
    patch.sort = 999;
    const r = await db.from("dept_tasks").insert(patch).select("id").single();
    if (r.error || !r.data) { toast("Save failed"); return; }
    taskId = r.data.id;
    logChange("dept_task", taskId, "added", null, title);
  }
  /* sync assignees */
  const before = task ? task.assignees.map(a => a.id) : [];
  const toAdd = PICKER.filter(id => before.indexOf(id) < 0);
  const toDel = before.filter(id => PICKER.indexOf(id) < 0);
  if (toAdd.length) await db.from("task_assignees").insert(toAdd.map(id => ({ task_id: taskId, member_id: id })));
  if (toDel.length) await db.from("task_assignees").delete().eq("task_id", taskId).in("member_id", toDel);
  if (toAdd.length || toDel.length) {
    const names = ids => ids.map(i => (MEMBER_BY_ID[i] || {}).name).filter(Boolean).join(", ");
    logChange("dept_task", taskId, "assignees", names(before) || "none", names(PICKER) || "none");
  }
  closeModal();
  await loadAll();
  renderSection(deptId); renderDashboard(); renderNeedsMe(); renderWhatsNew();
  toast(task ? "Task updated" : "Task added");
}

/* ---------- decision add / edit modal ---------- */
function decisionModal(dec) {
  const isEdit = !!dec;
  const areas = Object.keys(AREA_COLORS);
  openModal(
    "<h3>" + (isEdit ? "Edit decision" : "Add an executive decision") + "</h3>" +
    '<p class="m-sub">Every decision carries a reason category and a one line rationale so the list stays triageable. Leave the reason blank and the Hub writes it on save.</p>' +
    '<label>Decision</label><input type="text" id="dmTitle" value="' + (isEdit ? esc(dec.title) : "") + '" placeholder="The call that needs to be made" />' +
    '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Area</label><select id="dmArea">' +
    areas.map(a => '<option value="' + esc(a) + '"' + (isEdit && dec.area === a ? " selected" : "") + ">" + esc(a) + "</option>").join("") +
    '</select></div><div><label>Who decides</label><select id="dmOwner">' +
    ["Both", "Steel", "Suhaib"].map(o => '<option value="' + o + '"' + (isEdit && dec.owner === o ? " selected" : "") + ">" + o + "</option>").join("") +
    "</select></div></div>" +
    "<label>Suggested move</label><input type=\"text\" id=\"dmMove\" value=\"" + (isEdit ? esc(dec.move || "") : "") + '" />' +
    "<label>Reason category</label><select id=\"dmCat\">" + categoryOptions(isEdit ? dec.cat : "") + "</select>" +
    '<input type="text" id="dmCatOther" placeholder="Custom category" style="margin-top:6px;display:none;" />' +
    "<label>Why this needs an executive (one line)</label><input type=\"text\" id=\"dmNote\" value=\"" + (isEdit ? esc(dec.note || "") : "") + '" />' +
    '<label style="margin-top:14px;"><input type="checkbox" id="dmConf"' + (isEdit && dec.confidential ? " checked" : "") + " /> Confidential (hidden from board view)</label>" +
    '<div class="m-foot"><button class="btn ghost" id="dmCancel">Cancel</button><button class="btn" id="dmSave">' + (isEdit ? "Save changes" : "Add decision") + "</button></div>"
  );
  const catSel = document.getElementById("dmCat");
  const catOther = document.getElementById("dmCatOther");
  function syncCatOther() { catOther.style.display = catSel.value === "__other" ? "block" : "none"; }
  catSel.addEventListener("change", syncCatOther); syncCatOther();
  if (isEdit && dec.cat && CATEGORIES.indexOf(dec.cat) < 0) { catOther.value = dec.cat; catOther.style.display = "block"; }
  document.getElementById("dmCancel").addEventListener("click", closeModal);
  document.getElementById("dmSave").addEventListener("click", () => saveDecisionModal(dec));
}

async function saveDecisionModal(dec) {
  const title = document.getElementById("dmTitle").value.trim();
  if (!title) { toast("Give the decision a title"); return; }
  const catSel = document.getElementById("dmCat").value;
  let cat = catSel === "__other" ? (document.getElementById("dmCatOther").value.trim() || null) : (catSel || null);
  let note = document.getElementById("dmNote").value.trim() || null;
  if (!cat || !note) {
    toast("The Hub is writing the reason...");
    const s = await suggestReason("decision", title, document.getElementById("dmArea").value, document.getElementById("dmMove").value.trim());
    if (s) { cat = cat || s.reason_category; note = note || s.reason_note; }
    if (!cat) cat = "Strategy or direction";
    if (!note) note = "Needs an executive call";
  }
  const patch = {
    title: title,
    area: document.getElementById("dmArea").value,
    suggested_move: document.getElementById("dmMove").value.trim(),
    owner: document.getElementById("dmOwner").value,
    reason_category: cat,
    reason_note: note,
    confidential: document.getElementById("dmConf").checked
  };
  if (dec) {
    const r = await db.from("decisions").update(patch).eq("id", dec.ref);
    if (r.error) { toast("Save failed"); return; }
    logChange("decision", dec.ref, "edited", dec.title, title);
  } else {
    patch.pos = DB_DECISIONS.reduce((m, r) => Math.max(m, r.pos || 0), 0) + 1;
    patch.done = false;
    patch.raised_by = who();
    patch.opened_at = new Date().toISOString();
    const r = await db.from("decisions").insert(patch).select("id").single();
    if (r.error) { toast("Save failed"); return; }
    logChange("decision", r.data ? r.data.id : "", "added", null, title);
    /* Slack: new executive decision (Budget and Legal get extra emphasis server-side) */
    try {
      db.functions.invoke("notify", { body: { type: "decision_raised", decision: {
        title: title, area: patch.area, reason_category: cat, reason_note: note, owner: patch.owner, raised_by: who()
      } } });
    } catch (e) { /* notification is best effort */ }
  }
  closeModal();
  await loadAll();
  renderDecisions(); renderNeedsMe(); renderWhatsNew();
  toast(dec ? "Decision updated" : "Decision added");
}

/* ---------- decided-note capture on check-off ---------- */
function decideModal(dec, checkbox) {
  openModal(
    "<h3>Record the decision</h3>" +
    '<p class="m-sub">' + esc(dec.title) + "</p>" +
    "<label>What was decided (kept as the record)</label>" +
    '<textarea id="ddNote" placeholder="e.g. Approved the $200 booth fee for McGill tabling"></textarea>' +
    '<div class="m-foot"><button class="btn ghost" id="ddCancel">Cancel</button><button class="btn" id="ddSave">Mark decided</button></div>'
  );
  document.getElementById("ddCancel").addEventListener("click", () => { checkbox.checked = false; closeModal(); });
  document.getElementById("ddSave").addEventListener("click", async () => {
    const note = document.getElementById("ddNote").value.trim();
    const r = await db.from("decisions").update({
      done: true, decided_note: note || null, decided_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      updated_by: who(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
    }).eq("id", dec.ref);
    if (r.error) { toast("Save failed"); checkbox.checked = false; closeModal(); return; }
    logChange("decision", dec.ref, "decided", "open", note || "decided");
    closeModal();
    await loadAll();
    renderDecisions(); renderNeedsMe(); renderDashboard();
    toast("Decision recorded");
  });
}

async function reopenDecision(dec) {
  if (!confirm("Reopen this decision?")) return false;
  const r = await db.from("decisions").update({
    done: false, decided_note: null, decided_at: null,
    updated_by: who(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
  }).eq("id", dec.ref);
  if (r.error) { toast("Save failed"); return false; }
  logChange("decision", dec.ref, "reopened", "decided", "open");
  await loadAll();
  renderDecisions(); renderNeedsMe(); renderDashboard();
  toast("Reopened");
  return true;
}

/* =====================================================================
   RENDER HELPERS
   ===================================================================== */
function fmtDay(s) { if (!s) return ""; const d = new Date(s); if (isNaN(d)) return ""; return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: "short", day: "numeric" }); }
function daysBetween(a, b) { if (!a || !b) return null; const n = Math.round((new Date(b) - new Date(a)) / 86400000); return isNaN(n) ? null : Math.max(0, n); }
function metaLine(by, at) {
  if (!by) return "";
  const d = at ? new Date(at).toLocaleDateString() : "";
  return "Done by " + by + (d ? " on " + d : "");
}
function escapeHtml(s) { const d = document.createElement("div"); d.textContent = s == null ? "" : String(s); return d.innerHTML.replace(/"/g, "&quot;").replace(/'/g, "&#39;"); }

let toastTimer = null;
function toast(msg) {
  const t = document.getElementById("toast");
  t.textContent = msg; t.classList.add("show");
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove("show"), 2600);
}

function highlightEl(selector) {
  const el = document.querySelector(selector);
  if (!el) return;
  el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "center" });
  el.classList.add("pulse");
  setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove("pulse"), 2800);
}

function dueBadge(it) {
  if (it.done || !it.due) return "";
  const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  const due = new Date(it.due + "T00:00:00");
  const over = Math.round((today - due) / 86400000);
  const label = due.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: "short", day: "numeric" });
  if (over < 0) return '<span class="due-badge">Due ' + label + "</span>";
  if (over === 0) return '<span class="due-badge amber">Due today</span>';
  return '<span class="due-badge ' + (over > 7 ? "red" : "amber") + '">' + over + "d overdue</span>";
}

function itemLi(it) {
  let dateLine;
  if (it.done) {
    const dd = daysBetween(it.opened, it.closed);
    dateLine = (it.opened ? "Opened " + fmtDay(it.opened) + " &middot; " : "") + "Closed " + (fmtDay(it.closed) || "?") + (dd != null ? " &middot; " + dd + "d to close" : "");
  } else {
    const age = daysBetween(it.opened, new Date().toISOString());
    dateLine = it.opened ? "Opened " + fmtDay(it.opened) + (age != null && age > 0 ? " &middot; " + age + "d open" : "") : "";
  }
  const chips = (it.assignees || []).map(a => '<span class="as-chip">' + esc(a.name) + "</span>").join("");
  const btns = isStaff() && !it.done
    ? ' <button class="edit-btn" data-edit="' + it.ref + '">edit</button><button class="reassign-btn" data-ref="' + it.ref + '" title="Reassign owner">reassign</button>'
    : "";
  return '<li data-task="' + it.ref + '" class="' + (it.done ? "done" : "") + '">' +
    '<input type="checkbox" data-ref="' + it.ref + '" ' + (it.done ? "checked" : "") + (isStaff() ? "" : " disabled") + " />" +
    '<div class="ci-body">' +
      '<div class="ci-title">' + (it.flag ? '<span class="flag">&#9873; </span>' : "") + it.title + catTag(it.cat) + (it.confidential && isStaff() ? '<span class="lock-tag">board hidden</span>' : "") + dueBadge(it) + "</div>" +
      '<div class="ci-sub">Owner: ' + (it.owner || "unassigned") + (it.status ? " &middot; " + it.status : "") + btns + "</div>" +
      (chips ? '<div class="ci-sub">' + chips + "</div>" : "") +
      (it.flag && it.note ? '<div class="ci-sub" style="font-style:italic;">Why: ' + esc(it.note) + "</div>" : "") +
      '<div class="ci-meta">' + dateLine + (it.done && it.by ? " &middot; by " + it.by : "") + "</div>" +
    "</div></li>";
}

function wireTaskEvents(scope) {
  scope.querySelectorAll("li[data-task]").forEach(li => {
    const ref = Number(li.dataset.task);
    const row = DB_TASKS.find(x => x.id === ref);
    const deptId = row ? row.department : null;
    const cb = li.querySelector("input[type=checkbox]");
    if (cb && isStaff()) cb.addEventListener("change", async () => {
      const ok = await toggleTask(ref, cb.checked);
      if (ok) { if (deptId) renderSection(deptId); renderDashboard(); renderNeedsMe(); }
    });
    const eb = li.querySelector("[data-edit]");
    if (eb) eb.addEventListener("click", () => { const t = getTasks(deptId).find(x => x.ref === ref); if (t) taskModal(deptId, t); });
    const rb = li.querySelector(".reassign-btn");
    if (rb) rb.addEventListener("click", e => { e.preventDefault(); reassignTask(ref, deptId); });
  });
}

/* =====================================================================
   OPEN DECISIONS
   ===================================================================== */
let DEC_FILTER = { cat: "", owner: "" };

function decisionMatches(d) {
  if (DEC_FILTER.cat && (d.cat || "") !== DEC_FILTER.cat) return false;
  if (DEC_FILTER.owner) {
    const o = d.owner || "Both";
    if (DEC_FILTER.owner === "Steel" && !(o === "Steel" || o === "Both")) return false;
    if (DEC_FILTER.owner === "Suhaib" && !(o === "Suhaib" || o === "Both")) return false;
    if (DEC_FILTER.owner === "Both" && o !== "Both") return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function decLi(d) {
  const age = d.done ? null : daysBetween(d.opened, new Date().toISOString());
  const ageStr = age == null ? "" : (age === 0 ? "opened today" : age + "d open");
  const metaBits = d.done
    ? [d.decidedAt ? "Decided " + fmtDay(d.decidedAt) : "", metaLine(d.by, d.at)].filter(Boolean).join(" &middot; ")
    : [ageStr, d.raisedBy ? "raised by " + esc(window.waWho ? waWho(d.raisedBy) : d.raisedBy) : "", "decides: " + esc(d.owner || "Both")].filter(Boolean).join(" &middot; ");
  return '<li data-decision="' + d.ref + '" class="' + (d.done ? "done" : "") + '">' +
    '<input type="checkbox" ' + (d.done ? "checked" : "") + (isStaff() ? "" : " disabled") + " />" +
    '<div class="ci-body">' +
      '<div class="ci-title">' + d.pos + ". " + d.title + '<span class="area-tag">' + (d.area || "") + "</span>" + catTag(d.cat) + (d.confidential && isStaff() ? '<span class="lock-tag">board hidden</span>' : "") + "</div>" +
      (d.move ? '<div class="ci-sub">Suggested move: ' + d.move + "</div>" : "") +
      (d.note ? '<div class="ci-sub" style="font-style:italic;">Why: ' + esc(d.note) + "</div>" : "") +
      (d.done && d.decidedNote ? '<div class="ci-sub" style="color:var(--wa-green);font-weight:600;">Decided: ' + esc(d.decidedNote) + "</div>" : "") +
      (isStaff() && !d.done ? '<div class="ci-sub"><button class="edit-btn" data-editdec="' + d.ref + '">edit</button></div>' : "") +
      '<div class="ci-meta">' + metaBits + "</div>" +
    "</div></li>";
}

function wireDecisionEvents(scope) {
  scope.querySelectorAll("li[data-decision]").forEach(li => {
    const ref = Number(li.dataset.decision);
    const d = getDecisions().find(x => x.ref === ref);
    if (!d) return;
    const cb = li.querySelector("input[type=checkbox]");
    if (cb && isStaff()) cb.addEventListener("change", async () => {
      if (cb.checked && !d.done) decideModal(d, cb);
      else if (!cb.checked && d.done) { const ok = await reopenDecision(d); if (!ok) cb.checked = true; }
    });
    const eb = li.querySelector("[data-editdec]");
    if (eb) eb.addEventListener("click", () => decisionModal(d));
  });
}

function renderDecFilters(openDecs) {
  const wrap = document.getElementById("decFilters");
  if (!wrap) return;
  const cats = []; openDecs.forEach(d => { if (d.cat && cats.indexOf(d.cat) < 0) cats.push(d.cat); });
  let html = '<button class="fchip' + (!DEC_FILTER.cat ? " on" : "") + '" data-fcat="">All categories</button>';
  cats.forEach(c => html += '<button class="fchip' + (DEC_FILTER.cat === c ? " on" : "") + '" data-fcat="' + esc(c) + '">' + esc(c) + "</button>");
  html += '<span style="flex:1"></span>';
  [["", "Anyone"], ["Steel", "Steel"], ["Suhaib", "Suhaib"], ["Both", "Both only"]].forEach(o => {
    html += '<button class="fchip' + (DEC_FILTER.owner === o[0] ? " on" : "") + '" data-fown="' + o[0] + '">' + o[1] + "</button>";
  });
  wrap.innerHTML = html;
  wrap.querySelectorAll("[data-fcat]").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", () => { DEC_FILTER.cat = b.dataset.fcat; renderDecisions(); }));
  wrap.querySelectorAll("[data-fown]").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", () => { DEC_FILTER.owner = b.dataset.fown; renderDecisions(); }));
}

function renderDecisions() {
  const all = getDecisions();
  const open = all.filter(d => !d.done && decisionMatches(d));
  const done = all.filter(d => d.done).sort((a, b) => new Date(b.decidedAt || b.at || 0) - new Date(a.decidedAt || a.at || 0));
  const ul = document.getElementById("decisionList");
  ul.innerHTML = open.length ? open.map(decLi).join("") : '<li style="color:var(--wa-muted);list-style:none;padding:10px 4px;">No open decisions match this filter.</li>';
  const dul = document.getElementById("decisionDoneList");
  dul.innerHTML = done.length ? done.map(decLi).join("") : '<li style="color:var(--wa-muted);">Nothing decided yet.</li>';
  document.getElementById("decDoneSummary").textContent = "Decided (" + done.length + ")";
  renderDecFilters(all.filter(d => !d.done));
  wireDecisionEvents(document.getElementById("overview"));
}

/* =====================================================================
   WHAT'S NEW STRIP
   ===================================================================== */
function renderWhatsNew() { /* superseded: wave2 redeclares this at top level (hoisting makes wave2 the live binding from load); body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }

/* =====================================================================
   MEETING TIMES (Google Calendar, with cache and seeded fallback)
   ===================================================================== */
function fmtMeet(start) {
  if (!start) return "";
  const d = new Date(start);
  if (isNaN(d)) return "";
  const hasTime = String(start).length > 10;
  const opts = hasTime
    ? { weekday: "short", hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit", timeZone: "America/Toronto" }
    : { weekday: "short", month: "short", day: "numeric", timeZone: "America/Toronto" };
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", opts).format(d).replace(",", "");
}

function meetChips(id) {
  const live = MEETINGS[id];
  const meta = DB_META[id] || {};
  if (live && live.length) {
    return '<div class="meet-band">' + live.map(m =>
      '<span class="info-chip"><span class="ic">&#9200;</span><b>' + esc(m.title) + "</b> " + fmtMeet(m.start) +
      (m.link ? ' &middot; <a href="' + esc(m.link) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Join</a>' : "") + "</span>"
    ).join("") + "</div>";
  }
  if (meta.meeting_schedule) {
    return '<div class="meet-band"><span class="info-chip"><span class="ic">&#9200;</span><b>Meetings</b> ' + esc(meta.meeting_schedule) + "</span></div>";
  }
  return "";
}

async function loadMeetings() {
  MEETINGS = {};
  let liveOk = false;
  const token = SESSION && SESSION.provider_token;
  if (isStaff() && token) {
    try {
      const tMin = new Date().toISOString();
      const tMax = new Date(Date.now() + 14 * 86400000).toISOString();
      const url = "https://www.googleapis.com/calendar/v3/calendars/primary/events?singleEvents=true&orderBy=startTime&maxResults=150&timeMin=" + encodeURIComponent(tMin) + "&timeMax=" + encodeURIComponent(tMax);
      const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { Authorization: "Bearer " + token } });
      if (res.ok) {
        const events = (await res.json()).items || [];
        DEPT_ORDER.forEach(id => {
          const meta = DB_META[id] || {};
          const pats = (meta.calendar_match || []).map(p => String(p).toLowerCase());
          if (!pats.length) return;
          const hits = []; const seen = {};
          for (const ev of events) {
            const title = ev.summary || "";
            if (!pats.some(p => title.toLowerCase().indexOf(p) >= 0)) continue;
            if (seen[title]) continue;
            seen[title] = true;
            hits.push({ title: title, start: (ev.start && (ev.start.dateTime || ev.start.date)) || null, link: ev.hangoutLink || "" });
            if (hits.length >= 2) break;
          }
          if (hits.length) MEETINGS[id] = hits;
        });
        liveOk = true;
        const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
        DEPT_ORDER.forEach(id => {
          if (!MEETINGS[id]) return;
          const meta = DB_META[id] || {};
          const stale = !meta.meeting_cache_at || (Date.now() - new Date(meta.meeting_cache_at).getTime()) > 20 * 3600000;
          if (stale) db.from("dept_meta").update({ meeting_cache: MEETINGS[id], meeting_cache_at: nowIso }).eq("department", id).then(() => {});
        });
      } else if (res.status === 403 || res.status === 401) {
        console.warn("Calendar fetch not available (" + res.status + "). If this persists, enable the Google Calendar API for the WA Dashboard Google Cloud project. Falling back to cached or seeded times.");
      }
    } catch (e) { console.warn("Calendar fetch skipped:", e); }
  }
  if (!liveOk) {
    DEPT_ORDER.forEach(id => {
      const meta = DB_META[id] || {};
      if (meta.meeting_cache && meta.meeting_cache_at && (Date.now() - new Date(meta.meeting_cache_at).getTime()) < 8 * 86400000) {
        MEETINGS[id] = meta.meeting_cache;
      }
    });
  }
}

/* =====================================================================
   PULSE, HOURS, CLICKUP HELPERS
   ===================================================================== */
function weekStartOf(d) {
  const day = (d.getDay() + 6) % 7;
  const m = new Date(d);
  m.setDate(d.getDate() - day);
  return m.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

function pulseChip(id) {
  const rows = DB_SENTIMENT.filter(s => s.department === id).sort((a, b) => String(b.captured_on).localeCompare(String(a.captured_on)));
  if (!rows.length) return "";
  const cur = rows[0], prev = rows[1];
  const arrow = prev ? (Number(cur.score) > Number(prev.score) ? " &#8599;" : (Number(cur.score) < Number(prev.score) ? " &#8600;" : " &#8594;")) : "";
  const conf = cur.confidence === "low" ? " (low signal)" : "";
  return '<div class="meet-band"><span class="info-chip" title="Team pulse from Slack channel tone, scored 1 to 10 by the Hub against a fixed rubric. The arrow compares the previous reading. Channels only, DMs are never scored."><span class="ic">&#9825;</span><b>Team pulse</b> ' + esc(String(cur.score)) + "/10" + arrow + (cur.note ? " &middot; " + esc(cur.note) : "") + conf + "</span></div>";
}

function clickupChip(id) {
  const rows = DB_CLICKUP.filter(c => c.department === id).sort((a, b) => String(b.week_start).localeCompare(String(a.week_start)));
  if (!rows.length) return "";
  const cur = rows[0];
  const counts = cur.status_counts || {};
  let inprog = 0;
  Object.keys(counts).forEach(k => { if (/progress|doing|active|review/.test(k)) inprog += Number(counts[k] || 0); });
  return '<div class="meet-band"><span class="info-chip" title="From the ClickUp weekly snapshot"><span class="ic">&#10003;</span><b>ClickUp this week</b> ' + Number(cur.closed_n || 0) + " closed &middot; " + Number(cur.created_n || 0) + " created" + (inprog ? " &middot; " + inprog + " in progress" : "") + "</span></div>";
}

function hoursByName(id) {
  const rows = DB_HOURS.filter(h => h.department === id);
  if (!rows.length) return null;
  const thisWeek = weekStartOf(new Date());
  const byName = {};
  rows.forEach(h => {
    const n = h.member_name || h.slack_name;
    if (!byName[n]) byName[n] = { cur: 0, hist: [] };
    if (h.week_start === thisWeek) byName[n].cur += Number(h.hours || 0);
    else byName[n].hist.push(Number(h.hours || 0));
  });
  return byName;
}

function hoursBlock(id) {
  const byName = hoursByName(id);
  if (!byName) return "";
  const tags = Object.keys(byName).sort((a, b) => byName[b].cur - byName[a].cur).map(n => {
    const v = byName[n];
    const avg = v.hist.length ? v.hist.reduce((s, x) => s + x, 0) / v.hist.length : null;
    return '<span class="team-tag"><b>' + esc(n) + "</b> &middot; " + v.cur.toFixed(1) + "h this week" + (avg != null ? " &middot; avg " + avg.toFixed(1) + "h/wk" : "") + "</span>";
  }).join("");
  return '<h3 class="block">Hours in (from the check-in channels)</h3><div class="team-tags">' + tags + "</div>";
}

function hoursCardHtml() {
  if (!DB_HOURS.length) return "";
  const thisWeek = weekStartOf(new Date());
  const rows = [];
  DB_HOURS.forEach(h => {
    const key = (h.member_name || h.slack_name) + "|" + (h.department || "");
    let r = rows.find(x => x.key === key);
    if (!r) { r = { key: key, name: h.member_name || h.slack_name, dept: h.department, cur: 0, hist: [] }; rows.push(r); }
    if (h.week_start === thisWeek) r.cur += Number(h.hours || 0);
    else r.hist.push(Number(h.hours || 0));
  });
  rows.sort((a, b) => b.cur - a.cur);
  const tr = rows.map(r => {
    const avg = r.hist.length ? r.hist.reduce((s, x) => s + x, 0) / r.hist.length : null;
    return "<tr><td>" + esc(r.name) + "</td><td>" + (r.dept ? (DEPT_LABEL[r.dept] || r.dept) : '<span style="color:var(--wa-muted);">unmatched</span>') + "</td><td>" + r.cur.toFixed(1) + "h</td><td>" + (avg != null ? avg.toFixed(1) + "h" : "-") + "</td></tr>";
  }).join("");
  return '<div class="card dept-card" style="border-top-color:#3a6ea5">' +
    '<h2 style="color:#3a6ea5">Team hours</h2>' +
    '<p class="read">Weekly hours parsed from the check-in-out channels. A check in or out counts at its message time unless the message states an explicit time like "in @ 5:15pm", which wins. Sessions missing a check out are not counted.</p>' +
    '<table class="wa-tbl"><thead><tr><th>Member</th><th>Department</th><th>This week</th><th>Avg per week</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + tr + "</tbody></table></div>";
}

function clickupCardHtml() {
  if (!DB_CLICKUP.length) return "";
  const weeks = [];
  DB_CLICKUP.forEach(c => { if (weeks.indexOf(c.week_start) < 0) weeks.push(c.week_start); });
  weeks.sort().reverse();
  const cur = weeks[0], prev = weeks[1];
  const tr = DEPT_ORDER.map(id => {
    const c = DB_CLICKUP.find(x => x.department === id && x.week_start === cur);
    if (!c) return "";
    const p = prev ? DB_CLICKUP.find(x => x.department === id && x.week_start === prev) : null;
    const openN = Object.keys(c.status_counts || {}).reduce((s, k) => s + Number((c.status_counts || {})[k] || 0), 0);
    const pOpen = p ? Object.keys(p.status_counts || {}).reduce((s, k) => s + Number((p.status_counts || {})[k] || 0), 0) : null;
    const delta = pOpen != null ? openN - pOpen : null;
    return "<tr><td>" + (DEPT_LABEL[id] || id) + "</td><td>" + Number(c.closed_n || 0) + "</td><td>" + Number(c.created_n || 0) + "</td><td>" + openN + (delta != null ? " (" + (delta >= 0 ? "+" : "") + delta + " vs last wk)" : "") + "</td></tr>";
  }).join("");
  if (!tr) return "";
  return '<div class="card dept-card" style="border-top-color:#6F64AB">' +
    '<h2 style="color:#6F64AB">ClickUp movement</h2>' +
    '<p class="read">Task status movement per department from the weekly ClickUp snapshot: closed and created this week, plus how the open pile is trending.</p>' +
    '<table class="wa-tbl"><thead><tr><th>Department</th><th>Closed this wk</th><th>Created</th><th>Open tasks</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + tr + "</tbody></table></div>";
}

/* =====================================================================
   DEPARTMENT SECTIONS
   ===================================================================== */
function renderSection(id) {
  const sec = SECTIONS[id];
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!sec || !el) return;
  const col = sec.color;
  const meta = getMeta(id);
  const updates = getUpdates(id);
  const tasks = getTasks(id);
  const members = DB_MEMBERS.filter(m => m.department === id);

  const chipTitle = sec.title + '<span class="deptchip" style="background:' + col + '">Department</span>';
  const banner = meta.header_image_url
    ? '<div class="dept-banner"><img src="' + esc(meta.header_image_url) + '" alt="' + esc(DEPT_LABEL[id] || id) + ' in action" loading="lazy" /><div class="shade"></div><h2>' + chipTitle + "</h2></div>"
    : '<h2 style="color:' + col + '">' + chipTitle + "</h2>";

  const teamHtml = members.length
    ? members.map(m => '<span class="team-tag"><b>' + esc(m.name) + "</b>" + (m.role ? " &middot; " + esc(m.role) : "") + "</span>").join("")
    : (sec.team || []).map(t => '<span class="team-tag"><b>' + t[0] + "</b> &middot; " + t[1] + "</span>").join("");

  const updHtml = updates.length
    ? updates.map(u => "<li>" + (u.confidential && isStaff() ? '<span class="lock-tag">board hidden</span> ' : "") + '<span class="u-date">' + (u.label || "") + "</span> - " + u.text + "</li>").join("")
    : '<li style="color:var(--wa-muted);list-style:none;">No updates yet.</li>';
  const prioHtml = (meta.priorities || []).map(p => "<li>" + p + "</li>").join("");

  const openTasks = tasks.filter(t => !t.done);
  const doneTasks = tasks.filter(t => t.done);

  let itemsBlock;
  if (sec.subsections) {
    const known = sec.subsections.map(s => s.title);
    itemsBlock = '<h3 class="block" style="color:' + col + '">Workstreams</h3>' + sec.subsections.map(sub => {
      const rows = openTasks.filter(t => t.subsection === sub.title);
      return '<div class="subsection" style="--sc:' + sub.color + '">' +
        '<h3 class="subhead">' + sub.title + "</h3>" +
        '<p class="sub-sum">' + sub.summary + "</p>" +
        '<ul class="checklist">' + (rows.length ? rows.map(itemLi).join("") : '<li style="color:var(--wa-muted);">No open tasks.</li>') + "</ul>" +
      "</div>";
    }).join("");
    const leftovers = openTasks.filter(t => known.indexOf(t.subsection) < 0);
    if (leftovers.length) {
      itemsBlock += '<div class="subsection" style="--sc:' + col + '"><h3 class="subhead">General and new items</h3><p class="sub-sum">Added by the Hub from a meeting, or by hand, not yet filed under a workstream.</p><ul class="checklist">' + leftovers.map(itemLi).join("") + "</ul></div>";
    }
  } else {
    itemsBlock = '<h3 class="block" style="color:' + col + '">Open items (check to mark actioned)</h3>' +
      '<ul class="checklist">' + (openTasks.length ? openTasks.map(itemLi).join("") : '<li style="color:var(--wa-muted);">No open items.</li>') + "</ul>";
  }

  const completedBlock =
    '<details class="completed"><summary>Completed (' + doneTasks.length + ")</summary>" +
    '<ul class="checklist">' + (doneTasks.length ? doneTasks.map(itemLi).join("") : '<li style="color:var(--wa-muted);">Nothing completed yet.</li>') + "</ul></details>";

  const addRow = isStaff()
    ? '<div class="add-row"><button class="btn ghost mini" data-add="update">+ Add update</button> <button class="btn ghost mini" data-add="task">+ Add task</button> <button class="btn ghost mini" data-add="rerun" title="Process any unprocessed transcripts for this department">&#8635; Re-run from transcripts</button></div>'
    : "";

  el.innerHTML =
    '<div class="card dept-card" style="border-top-color:' + col + '">' +
      banner +
      meetChips(id) +
      pulseChip(id) +
      clickupChip(id) +
      '<p class="read">' + (meta.read || "") + "</p>" +
      '<div class="progress-box"><h3 class="block" style="color:' + col + ';margin-top:0">Progress this period</h3><p>' + (meta.progress || "") + "</p></div>" +
      (prioHtml ? '<h3 class="block" style="color:' + col + '">High priority</h3><ul class="prio">' + prioHtml + "</ul>" : "") +
      '<h3 class="block">Team</h3><div class="team-tags">' + teamHtml + "</div>" +
      hoursBlock(id) +
      '<h3 class="block">Most recent updates</h3><ul class="updates">' + updHtml + "</ul>" +
      itemsBlock + addRow + completedBlock +
      '<h3 class="block">Transcripts that contributed</h3>' +
      '<p style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);margin:0;">' + sec.contributed + "</p>" +
    "</div>";

  wireTaskEvents(el);
  const addU = el.querySelector('[data-add="update"]');
  const addT = el.querySelector('[data-add="task"]');
  const addR = el.querySelector('[data-add="rerun"]');
  if (addU) addU.addEventListener("click", () => addUpdate(id));
  if (addT) addT.addEventListener("click", () => taskModal(id, null));
  if (addR) addR.addEventListener("click", () => rerunDept(id));
}

function renderAllSections() { DEPT_ORDER.forEach(renderSection); }

/* =====================================================================
   DASHBOARD (cycle time + aging + overdue)
   ===================================================================== */
function renderDashboard() {
  const el = document.getElementById("dashboard");
  if (!el) return;
  if (!DB_TASKS.length) {
    el.innerHTML = '<div class="card"><h2>Task completion dashboard</h2><p class="read">No task data loaded yet.</p></div>';
    return;
  }
  const days = (a, b) => (new Date(b) - new Date(a)) / 86400000;
  const rows = DEPT_ORDER.map(id => {
    const t = DB_TASKS.filter(r => r.department === id);
    const open = t.filter(r => !r.done);
    const closed = t.filter(r => r.done && r.opened_at && r.closed_at);
    const avg = closed.length ? closed.reduce((s, r) => s + days(r.opened_at, r.closed_at), 0) / closed.length : null;
    const avgAge = open.length ? open.reduce((s, r) => s + days(r.opened_at || new Date().toISOString(), new Date().toISOString()), 0) / open.length : null;
    return { id, label: DEPT_LABEL[id], color: DEPT_COLOR[id], open: open.length, doneN: t.filter(r => r.done).length, avg, avgAge };
  });
  const maxAvg = Math.max(1, ...rows.map(r => r.avg || 0));
  const allClosed = DB_TASKS.filter(r => r.done && r.opened_at && r.closed_at);
  const overall = allClosed.length ? allClosed.reduce((s, r) => s + days(r.opened_at, r.closed_at), 0) / allClosed.length : null;
  const totalOpen = DB_TASKS.filter(r => !r.done).length;
  const totalDone = DB_TASKS.filter(r => r.done).length;

  const bars = rows.map(r => {
    const w = r.avg != null ? Math.max(6, Math.round((r.avg / maxAvg) * 100)) : 0;
    return '<div class="dash-row">' +
      '<div class="dash-label">' + r.label + "</div>" +
      '<div class="dash-bar-wrap">' + (r.avg != null
        ? '<div class="dash-bar" style="width:' + w + "%;background:" + r.color + '">' + r.avg.toFixed(1) + "d</div>"
        : '<span class="dash-none">no completed tasks yet</span>') + "</div>" +
      '<div class="dash-nums">' + r.open + " open &middot; " + r.doneN + " done</div>" +
    "</div>";
  }).join("");

  /* decision aging */
  const openDecs = getDecisions().filter(d => !d.done)
    .map(d => ({ d: d, age: daysBetween(d.opened, new Date().toISOString()) || 0 }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.age - a.age);
  const agingRows = openDecs.map(x =>
    '<tr><td><a href="#decision/' + x.d.ref + '">' + x.d.title + "</a>" + catTag(x.d.cat) + "</td><td>" + esc(x.d.owner || "Both") + "</td><td>" + x.age + "d</td></tr>"
  ).join("");

  /* overdue tasks */
  const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const overdue = DB_TASKS.filter(r => !r.done && r.due_date && r.due_date < todayStr)
    .map(r => ({ r: r, over: Math.round((new Date(todayStr) - new Date(r.due_date)) / 86400000) }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.over - a.over);
  const overdueRows = overdue.map(x =>
    '<tr><td><a href="#task/' + x.r.id + '">' + x.r.title + "</a></td><td>" + (DEPT_LABEL[x.r.department] || "") + "</td><td>" + (x.r.owner || "") + "</td><td>" + x.r.due_date + '</td><td><span class="due-badge ' + (x.over > 7 ? "red" : "amber") + '">' + x.over + "d</span></td></tr>"
  ).join("");

  el.innerHTML =
    '<div class="card dept-card" style="border-top-color:var(--wa-green)">' +
      '<h2 style="color:var(--wa-green)">Task completion dashboard</h2>' +
      '<p class="read">Average time from when a task was first raised to when it was closed, by department. New tasks track exact open and close dates; items seeded before tracking began use an approximate open date, so early figures are rough and sharpen over time.</p>' +
      '<div class="progress-box"><b>' + totalOpen + "</b> open &middot; <b>" + totalDone + "</b> completed" + (overall != null ? " &middot; overall avg <b>" + overall.toFixed(1) + " days</b> to close" : "") + "</div>" +
      '<h3 class="block" style="color:var(--wa-green)">Average days to close, by department</h3>' +
      '<div class="dash">' + bars + "</div>" +
      '<h3 class="block">Open workload and aging</h3>' +
      '<table class="wa-tbl"><thead><tr><th>Department</th><th>Open</th><th>Avg age of open</th><th>Completed</th><th>Avg days to close</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
      rows.map(r => "<tr><td>" + r.label + "</td><td>" + r.open + "</td><td>" + (r.avgAge != null ? r.avgAge.toFixed(1) + "d" : "-") + "</td><td>" + r.doneN + "</td><td>" + (r.avg != null ? r.avg.toFixed(1) + "d" : "-") + "</td></tr>").join("") +
      "</tbody></table>" +
    "</div>" +
    '<div class="card dept-card" style="border-top-color:var(--wa-gold)">' +
      '<h2 style="color:#a87f2c">Decision aging</h2>' +
      '<p class="read">Every open executive decision and how long it has waited. Aging decisions also surface in Slack automatically at 14 and 30 days.</p>' +
      (agingRows ? '<table class="wa-tbl"><thead><tr><th>Decision</th><th>Decides</th><th>Open</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + agingRows + "</tbody></table>" : '<p style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:13px;">No open decisions. Clean slate.</p>') +
    "</div>" +
    '<div class="card dept-card" style="border-top-color:var(--wa-flag)">' +
      '<h2 style="color:var(--wa-flag)">Overdue tasks</h2>' +
      '<p class="read">Open tasks past their expected due date. Amber up to a week over, red beyond.</p>' +
      (overdueRows ? '<table class="wa-tbl"><thead><tr><th>Task</th><th>Department</th><th>Owner</th><th>Due</th><th>Over</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + overdueRows + "</tbody></table>" : '<p style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:13px;">Nothing overdue.</p>') +
    "</div>" + hoursCardHtml() + clickupCardHtml();
}

/* =====================================================================
   NEEDS ME + HANDOFF
   ===================================================================== */
let NM = { person: null, handoff: false };

function renderNeedsMe() {
  if (!isStaff()) return;
  const el = document.getElementById("needsme");
  if (!el) return;
  if (NM.person === null) {
    const fn = firstName().toLowerCase();
    NM.person = fn === "steel" ? "Steel" : (fn === "suhaib" ? "Suhaib" : "");
  }
  const p = NM.person.toLowerCase();
  const decs = getDecisions().filter(d => !d.done).filter(d => {
    if (!NM.person) return true;
    const o = d.owner || "Both";
    return o === NM.person || o === "Both";
  });
  const matchTask = t => {
    const mine = !NM.person
      || (t.owner || "").toLowerCase().indexOf(p) >= 0
      || t.assignees.some(a => a.name.toLowerCase().indexOf(p) === 0);
    if (NM.handoff) return mine;
    return t.flag && (mine || !(t.owner || "").trim());
  };
  const groups = DEPT_ORDER.map(id => ({ id: id, rows: getTasks(id).filter(t => !t.done).filter(matchTask) })).filter(g => g.rows.length);

  let html = '<div class="card dept-card" style="border-top-color:var(--wa-gold)">' +
    "<h2>&#9873; " + (NM.handoff ? "Handoff checklist" + (NM.person ? ": " + NM.person : "") : "Needs me") + "</h2>" +
    '<p class="read">' + (NM.handoff
      ? "Every open item this person owns or is assigned to, grouped by department. The working list for handing each workstream to one accountable owner."
      : "The whole board collapsed to what needs an executive: open decisions plus flagged tasks.") + "</p>" +
    '<div class="filters">' +
    [["", "Anyone"], ["Steel", "Steel"], ["Suhaib", "Suhaib"]].map(o => '<button class="fchip' + (NM.person === o[0] ? " on" : "") + '" data-nmp="' + o[0] + '">' + o[1] + "</button>").join("") +
    '<span style="flex:1"></span>' +
    '<button class="fchip' + (!NM.handoff ? " on" : "") + '" data-nmh="0">Needs a decision</button>' +
    '<button class="fchip' + (NM.handoff ? " on" : "") + '" data-nmh="1">Handoff view</button>' +
    "</div>";

  if (!NM.handoff) {
    html += '<h3 class="block" style="color:#a87f2c">Open executive decisions (' + decs.length + ')</h3><ul class="checklist">' +
      (decs.length ? decs.map(decLi).join("") : '<li style="color:var(--wa-muted);">None for this filter.</li>') + "</ul>";
  }
  groups.forEach(g => {
    html += '<div class="nm-group"><h3 class="block" style="color:' + DEPT_COLOR[g.id] + '">' + DEPT_LABEL[g.id] + " (" + g.rows.length + ')</h3><ul class="checklist">' + g.rows.map(itemLi).join("") + "</ul></div>";
  });
  if (!groups.length && (NM.handoff || !decs.length)) html += '<p style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:13px;">Nothing matches this filter.</p>';
  html += "</div>";
  el.innerHTML = html;
  el.querySelectorAll("[data-nmp]").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", () => { NM.person = b.dataset.nmp; renderNeedsMe(); }));
  el.querySelectorAll("[data-nmh]").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", () => { NM.handoff = b.dataset.nmh === "1"; renderNeedsMe(); }));
  wireTaskEvents(el);
  wireDecisionEvents(el);
}

/* =====================================================================
   ACTIVITY (change log)
   ===================================================================== */
let AF = { type: "", person: "", days: 30 };

function renderActivity() {
  if (!isStaff()) return;
  const el = document.getElementById("activity");
  if (!el) return;
  const people = []; DB_CHANGES.forEach(c => { if (c.changed_by && people.indexOf(c.changed_by) < 0) people.push(c.changed_by); });
  const cutoff = AF.days ? Date.now() - AF.days * 86400000 : 0;
  const rows = DB_CHANGES.filter(c =>
    (!AF.type || c.entity_type === AF.type) &&
    (!AF.person || c.changed_by === AF.person) &&
    (!cutoff || new Date(c.changed_at).getTime() > cutoff)
  );
  const label = c => {
    if (c.entity_type === "dept_task") { const t = DB_TASKS.find(x => String(x.id) === String(c.entity_id)); return t ? t.title : "Task " + c.entity_id; }
    if (c.entity_type === "decision") { const d = DB_DECISIONS.find(x => String(x.id) === String(c.entity_id)); return d ? d.title : "Decision " + c.entity_id; }
    if (c.entity_type === "dept_meta" || c.entity_type === "dept_update") return DEPT_LABEL[c.entity_id] || c.entity_id;
    if (c.entity_type === "transcript") { const t = txCache.find(x => String(x.id) === String(c.entity_id)); return t ? "Transcript: " + (t.title || c.entity_id) : "Transcript " + c.entity_id; }
    return c.entity_id;
  };
  const typeOpts = [["", "All types"], ["dept_task", "Tasks"], ["decision", "Decisions"], ["dept_update", "Updates"], ["dept_meta", "Overviews"], ["transcript", "Transcripts"]];
  el.innerHTML =
    '<div class="card dept-card" style="border-top-color:#797773">' +
    "<h2>Activity</h2>" +
    '<p class="read">Every change to the board, human or AI, newest first: the audit trail and the undo reference. Prior text is preserved in the old value column.</p>' +
    '<div class="filters">' +
    '<select id="afType">' + typeOpts.map(t => '<option value="' + t[0] + '"' + (AF.type === t[0] ? " selected" : "") + ">" + t[1] + "</option>").join("") + "</select>" +
    '<select id="afPerson"><option value="">Everyone</option>' + people.map(pp => "<option" + (AF.person === pp ? " selected" : "") + ">" + esc(pp) + "</option>").join("") + "</select>" +
    '<select id="afDays">' + [[7, "Last 7 days"], [30, "Last 30 days"], [0, "All loaded"]].map(dd => '<option value="' + dd[0] + '"' + (AF.days === dd[0] ? " selected" : "") + ">" + dd[1] + "</option>").join("") + "</select>" +
    "</div>" +
    '<table class="wa-tbl"><thead><tr><th>When</th><th>Who</th><th>What</th><th>Change</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
    (rows.length ? rows.map(c =>
      "<tr><td style=\"white-space:nowrap;\">" + new Date(c.changed_at).toLocaleString(undefined, { month: "short", day: "numeric", hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit" }) + "</td>" +
      "<td>" + esc(c.changed_by || "") + "</td>" +
      '<td class="act-cell-what">' + esc(label(c)) + ' <span style="color:var(--wa-muted);">(' + esc(c.field || "") + ")</span></td>" +
      "<td>" +
        (c.old_value ? '<div class="act-old" title="' + esc(c.old_value) + '">was: ' + esc(String(c.old_value).slice(0, 140)) + (String(c.old_value).length > 140 ? "..." : "") + "</div>" : "") +
        (c.new_value ? '<div class="act-new" title="' + esc(c.new_value) + '">now: ' + esc(String(c.new_value).slice(0, 140)) + (String(c.new_value).length > 140 ? "..." : "") + "</div>" : "") +
      "</td></tr>"
    ).join("") : '<tr><td colspan="4" style="color:var(--wa-muted);">No activity for this filter.</td></tr>') +
    "</tbody></table></div>";
  document.getElementById("afType").addEventListener("change", e => { AF.type = e.target.value; renderActivity(); });
  document.getElementById("afPerson").addEventListener("change", e => { AF.person = e.target.value; renderActivity(); });
  document.getElementById("afDays").addEventListener("change", e => { AF.days = Number(e.target.value); renderActivity(); });
}

/* =====================================================================
   GLOBAL SEARCH
   ===================================================================== */
let searchTimer = null, txSearchResults = [];

function setupSearch() {
  const wrap = document.getElementById("searchWrap");
  wrap.style.display = "inline-block";
  const inp = document.getElementById("globalSearch");
  const pop = document.getElementById("searchResults");
  inp.addEventListener("input", () => {
    const q = inp.value.trim();
    if (q.length < 2) { pop.style.display = "none"; txSearchResults = []; return; }
    renderSearch(q);
    clearTimeout(searchTimer);
    searchTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        const { data } = await db.rpc("search_transcripts", { q: q });
        txSearchResults = data || [];
      } catch (e) { txSearchResults = []; }
      if (inp.value.trim().length >= 2) renderSearch(inp.value.trim());
    }, 350);
  });
  document.addEventListener("click", e => { if (!wrap.contains(e.target)) pop.style.display = "none"; });
}

function renderSearch(q) {
  const ql = q.toLowerCase();
  const out = [];
  DB_DECISIONS.filter(d => (d.title || "").toLowerCase().indexOf(ql) >= 0).slice(0, 4)
    .forEach(d => out.push({ t: "Decision", label: d.title, hash: "#decision/" + d.id }));
  DB_TASKS.filter(t => ((t.title || "") + " " + (t.owner || "")).toLowerCase().indexOf(ql) >= 0).slice(0, 5)
    .forEach(t => out.push({ t: (DEPT_LABEL[t.department] || "Task") + (t.done ? ", done" : ""), label: t.title, hash: "#task/" + t.id }));
  DB_UPDATES.filter(u => (u.text || "").toLowerCase().indexOf(ql) >= 0).slice(0, 3)
    .forEach(u => out.push({ t: (DEPT_LABEL[u.department] || "") + " update", label: (u.entry_label ? u.entry_label + ": " : "") + (u.text || "").slice(0, 90), hash: "#dept/" + u.department }));
  txSearchResults.slice(0, 4)
    .forEach(r => out.push({ t: "Transcript", label: (r.title || "Untitled") + (r.snippet ? ": " + r.snippet.slice(0, 70) : ""), hash: "#tx/" + r.id }));
  const pop = document.getElementById("searchResults");
  pop.innerHTML = out.length
    ? out.map(o => '<div class="sr" data-hash="' + o.hash + '"><span class="sr-type">' + esc(o.t) + "</span>" + esc(o.label) + "</div>").join("")
    : '<div class="sr" style="color:var(--wa-muted);">No matches</div>';
  pop.style.display = "block";
  pop.querySelectorAll(".sr[data-hash]").forEach(s => s.addEventListener("click", () => {
    pop.style.display = "none";
    document.getElementById("globalSearch").value = "";
    location.hash = s.dataset.hash;
  }));
}

/* =====================================================================
   TRANSCRIPTS (auto-processing archive)
   ===================================================================== */
let txCache = [];
const PENDING = new Set();
let pollTimer = null;

function txDeptKey(label) {
  const s = (label || "").toLowerCase();
  if (s === "opsmtl" || s.indexOf("montreal") >= 0) return "opsmtl";
  if (s === "opsto" || s.indexOf("toronto") >= 0) return "opsto";
  if (s.indexOf("marketing") >= 0) return "marketing";
  if (s === "finance" || s.indexOf("financ") === 0) return "finance"; /* wave164: Finance is not part of Fundraising */
  if (s.indexOf("fundrais") >= 0 || s.indexOf("grant") >= 0) return "fundraising";
  if (s === "hr" || s.indexOf("human") >= 0) return "hr";
  if (s.indexOf("board") >= 0) return "board";
  return null;
}
function txFailed(r) { return !r.processed && r.ai_summary && r.ai_summary.indexOf("Processing failed") === 0 && !PENDING.has(r.id); }
function txRecent(r) { return (Date.now() - new Date(r.created_at).getTime()) < 15 * 60000; }
function txProcessing(r) { return !r.processed && !txFailed(r) && (PENDING.has(r.id) || txRecent(r)); }

async function loadTranscriptsData() {
  const { data, error } = await db.from("transcripts").select("*").order("created_at", { ascending: false });
  txCache = (!error && data) ? data : [];
}
async function loadTranscripts() { await loadTranscriptsData(); renderTranscripts(); maybeStartPolling(); }

async function saveTranscript() { /* superseded: wave8 reset without capture; superseded; body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }

async function reprocessTx(id) {
  PENDING.add(id);
  renderTranscripts();
  try {
    const { error } = await db.functions.invoke("process-transcript", { body: { transcript_id: id } });
    if (error) throw error;
    toast("Reprocessing started");
  } catch (e) { PENDING.delete(id); renderTranscripts(); toast("Could not start reprocessing"); }
  maybeStartPolling();
}

async function rerunDept(deptId) {
  await loadTranscriptsData();
  const rows = txCache.filter(r => txDeptKey(r.department) === deptId && !r.processed);
  if (!rows.length) { toast("All " + (DEPT_LABEL[deptId] || "") + " transcripts are processed. Use Reprocess on a specific one in the archive."); return; }
  if (!confirm("Process " + rows.length + " unprocessed transcript(s) for " + (DEPT_LABEL[deptId] || deptId) + "?")) return;
  for (const r of rows) { await reprocessTx(r.id); }
}

function maybeStartPolling() {
  if (txCache.some(txProcessing) && !pollTimer) pollTimer = setInterval(pollTick, 6000);
}
async function pollTick() {
  const beforeOpen = txCache.filter(r => !r.processed).map(r => r.id);
  await loadTranscriptsData();
  const flipped = txCache.filter(r => r.processed && beforeOpen.indexOf(r.id) >= 0);
  if (flipped.length) {
    flipped.forEach(r => PENDING.delete(r.id));
    await loadAll();
    renderAllSections(); renderDecisions(); renderDashboard(); renderWhatsNew(); renderNeedsMe(); renderActivity();
    flipped.forEach(r => {
      const k = txDeptKey(r.department);
      toast((DEPT_LABEL[k] || r.department) + ' updated from "' + (r.title || "transcript") + '"');
    });
  }
  txCache.forEach(r => { if (txFailed(r)) PENDING.delete(r.id); });
  renderTranscripts();
  if (!txCache.some(txProcessing)) { clearInterval(pollTimer); pollTimer = null; }
}

function txStatusChip(r) {
  if (r.processed) {
    const k = txDeptKey(r.department);
    const when = r.processed_at ? " " + fmtDay(r.processed_at) : "";
    return '<span class="tx-status done" data-goto="#dept/' + k + '" title="Open the updated page">Updated ' + (DEPT_LABEL[k] || "") + when + "</span>";
  }
  if (txProcessing(r)) return '<span class="tx-status proc">Processing...</span>';
  if (txFailed(r)) return '<span class="tx-status fail" title="' + esc(r.ai_summary || "") + '">Failed, retry</span>';
  return '<span class="tx-status none">Not processed</span>';
}

function renderTranscripts() { /* superseded: wave2 redeclares this at top level (hoisting makes wave2 the live binding from load); body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }

/* =====================================================================
   TABS, ROUTING, CHROME
   ===================================================================== */
function setupTabs() {
  document.getElementById("tabBar").addEventListener("click", e => {
    const b = e.target.closest(".tab");
    if (!b) return;
    const id = b.dataset.tab;
    location.hash = id === "overview" ? "#decisions" : (DEPT_ORDER.indexOf(id) >= 0 ? "#dept/" + id : "#" + id);
  });
}

function showPanel(id) {
  document.querySelectorAll(".tab").forEach(t => t.classList.toggle("active", t.dataset.tab === id || (id === "overview" && t.dataset.tab === "overview")));
  document.querySelectorAll(".panel").forEach(p => p.classList.remove("active"));
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (el) el.classList.add("active");
  if (id === "dashboard") renderDashboard();
  if (id === "activity") renderActivity();
  if (id === "needsme") renderNeedsMe();
  window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
}

function applyHash() {
  const h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || "").replace(/^#/, "");
  if (!h || h.indexOf("access_token=") >= 0 || h.indexOf("error=") >= 0) { showPanel("overview"); return; }
  const parts = h.split("/");
  const a = parts[0], b = parts[1];
  if (a === "dept" && DEPT_ORDER.indexOf(b) >= 0) showPanel(b);
  else if (a === "decisions" || a === "overview") showPanel("overview");
  else if (a === "needs-me" || a === "needsme") { if (isStaff()) showPanel("needsme"); else showPanel("overview"); }
  else if (a === "task" && b) {
    const t = DB_TASKS.find(x => String(x.id) === b);
    if (t) { showPanel(t.department); setTimeout(() => highlightEl('[data-task="' + b + '"]'), 250); }
    else showPanel("overview");
  }
  else if (a === "decision" && b) {
    showPanel("overview");
    setTimeout(() => {
      const wrap = document.getElementById("decDoneWrap");
      if (wrap && document.querySelector('#decisionDoneList [data-decision="' + b + '"]')) wrap.open = true;
      highlightEl('[data-decision="' + b + '"]');
    }, 250);
  }
  else if (a === "tx" && b) {
    if (!isStaff()) { showPanel("overview"); return; }
    showPanel("transcripts");
    setTimeout(() => {
      const el = document.querySelector('[data-tx="' + b + '"]');
      if (el) el.classList.add("open");
      highlightEl('[data-tx="' + b + '"]');
    }, 250);
  }
  else if (a === "dashboard") showPanel("dashboard");
  else if (a === "transcripts" || a === "activity") { if (isStaff()) showPanel(a); else showPanel("overview"); }
  else showPanel("overview");
}

function renderIdentity() {
  document.getElementById("whoName").textContent = USER.name + (MODE === "board" ? " (board viewer)" : "");
  document.getElementById("signOutBtn").addEventListener("click", signOutAll);
}

function applyBoardChrome() {
  document.getElementById("boardNote").style.display = "block";
  const tx = document.getElementById("txTabBtn"); if (tx) tx.style.display = "none";
  const ac = document.getElementById("actTabBtn"); if (ac) ac.style.display = "none";
  const ad = document.getElementById("addDecisionBtn"); if (ad) ad.style.display = "none";
}

function bindChrome() {
  setupTabs();
  if (isStaff()) {
    const nb = document.getElementById("needsMeBtn");
    nb.style.display = "inline-flex";
    nb.addEventListener("click", () => { location.hash = "#mypage"; });
    /* Phase 1 (U5): header Pull Slack moved to the HR Commitment tab (wave 76); ClickUp pull removed for good (ClickUp is retired). */
    document.getElementById("txSave").addEventListener("click", saveTranscript);
    document.getElementById("txFilter").addEventListener("change", renderTranscripts);
    document.getElementById("txSearch").addEventListener("input", renderTranscripts);
    document.getElementById("addDecisionBtn").addEventListener("click", () => decisionModal(null));
  }
}

/* =====================================================================
   BOOT
   ===================================================================== */
async function startApp() {
  renderIdentity();
  if (MODE === "board") applyBoardChrome();
  bindChrome();
  await seedIfEmpty();
  await Promise.all([loadAll(), loadLastSeen()]);
  renderWhatsNew();
  renderDecisions();
  renderAllSections();
  renderDashboard();
  if (isStaff()) {
    await loadTranscripts();
    renderActivity();
    renderNeedsMe();
    setupSearch();
    maybeUpdateSiteUrl();
  }
  loadMeetings().then(() => renderAllSections()).catch(() => {});
  applyHash();
  window.addEventListener("hashchange", applyHash);
  markSeen();
}

(async function boot() {
  initCloud();
  if (!cloudOn) { showGate("error"); return; }
  db.auth.onAuthStateChange(evt => { if (evt === "SIGNED_OUT") location.reload(); });
  const { data } = await db.auth.getSession();
  const session = data ? data.session : null;
  if (!session) { showGate("signin"); return; }
  if (location.search.indexOf("code=") >= 0) history.replaceState({}, "", location.pathname + location.hash);
  SESSION = session;
  USER = { email: (session.user.email || "").toLowerCase(), name: displayName(session) };
  const roleRes = await db.rpc("get_my_role");
  const role = roleRes.data;
  if (roleRes.error || !role || role === "none") { showGate("denied", USER.email); return; }
  MODE = role;
  hideGate();
  document.getElementById("cloudStatus").textContent = "Cloud connected (saved for everyone)";
  await startApp();
})();


/* =====================================================================
   WA v116a teams registry (24/09/26): runs FIRST after the base
   =====================================================================
   The base defines DEPT_ORDER, DEPT_LABEL, DEPT_COLOR and SECTIONS as
   the six historical departments. This segment sits right after the
   base in MANIFEST so that every later wave (nav groups, workstream
   tabs, dashboards, filters) already sees the new shape:
   - Program Development ('progdev') splits out of Operations Montreal,
     with its own panel, tab button, colour and workstreams.
   - Plain names (Steel's call): Operations Montreal, Program
     Development, Operations Toronto, Fundraising.
   - Fundraising carries three sub-teams as workstreams: Grants, Event
     fundraising, Donor relations (each one a task board with its projects).
   - wave164 (29/09/26), Steel: "still shows fundraising and finance being
