     togther. these should be seperate". Finance is not a Fundraising
     sub-team any more: it has its own key ('finance', label Finance) with
     no team page (like exec). Its tasks, requests and emails live in the
     Finance app; T.inbox lists the teams that receive requests and emails
     (the seven teams plus Finance).
   - The transcript upload selects gain the new team; txDeptKey learns
     the new labels. Data rows move in the wa116 migration; the pipeline
     learns the key in process-transcript v7. Build-time teams_remap.py
     patches the label maps and workstream lists later waves hard code.
   - WA_TEAMS is the one model wave116 (pages, nav, boards) reads.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa116a) return; window.__wa116a = true;
  var PD = 'progdev', PD_LABEL = 'Program Development', PD_COLOR = '#2A8C7A';
  window.WA_TEAMS = {
    order: ['opsmtl', PD, 'opsto', 'marketing', 'fundraising', 'hr', 'board'],
    /* pages each team's menu offers, in order */
    pages: {
      opsmtl: ['overview', 'agenda', 'tasks', 'volunteering'],
      progdev: ['overview', 'agenda', 'tasks'],
      opsto: ['overview', 'agenda', 'tasks'],
      marketing: ['overview', 'agenda', 'tasks'],
      fundraising: ['overview', 'agenda', 'tasks'],   /* 09/10/26 Steel: Grants, Event fundraising and Donor relations live in the Fundraising app, so the team menu no longer lists them (the boards stay for grouping, projects and the overview cards) */
      hr: ['overview', 'agenda', 'tasks'],
      board: ['overview', 'agenda', 'tasks']
    },
    /* sub-teams: a task board per workstream (plus its projects) */
    boards: {
      fundraising: [
        { key: 'grants', label: 'Grants', ws: 'Grants', color: '#b8902f', blurb: 'Applications, reports and funder relationships.' },
        { key: 'events', label: 'Event fundraising', ws: 'Fundraising events', color: '#c87a2f', blurb: 'Flag Football, Rally Paper, the Gift Basket Drive and friends.' },
        { key: 'donors', label: 'Donor relations', ws: 'Donor program', color: '#2c8a6f', blurb: 'Donor program, corporate donors and sponsorships.' }
      ]
    },
    /* project ids that belong to a sub-team even when their tasks carry no workstream */
    boardProjects: { grants: [23], events: [2, 11, 12, 13], donors: [1, 14] },
    /* wave164: the teams that receive requests and emails (Finance has no team page; its inbox is in the Finance app) */
    inbox: ['opsmtl', PD, 'opsto', 'marketing', 'fundraising', 'finance', 'hr', 'board'],
    labels: { opsmtl: 'Operations Montreal', progdev: PD_LABEL, opsto: 'Operations Toronto', marketing: 'Marketing, Branding & Web', fundraising: 'Fundraising', hr: 'Human Resources', board: 'Board & Governance', finance: 'Finance' },
    short: { opsmtl: 'Ops Montreal', progdev: 'Program Dev', opsto: 'Ops Toronto', marketing: 'Marketing', fundraising: 'Fundraising', hr: 'HR', board: 'Board', finance: 'Finance' }
  };
  /* wave137: the executive team (the co-executive directors) has a name but no team page: it is not in order or pages */
  window.WA_TEAMS.labels.exec = 'Executive team'; window.WA_TEAMS.short.exec = 'Executive';
  try {
    if (DEPT_ORDER.indexOf(PD) < 0) DEPT_ORDER.splice(DEPT_ORDER.indexOf('opsmtl') + 1, 0, PD);
    DEPT_LABEL.opsmtl = 'Operations Montreal';
    DEPT_LABEL.opsto = 'Operations Toronto';
    DEPT_LABEL.fundraising = 'Fundraising';
    DEPT_LABEL.finance = 'Finance'; DEPT_COLOR.finance = '#B9851B'; /* wave164 */
    DEPT_LABEL[PD] = PD_LABEL;
    DEPT_COLOR[PD] = PD_COLOR;
    DEPT_LABEL.exec = 'Executive team'; DEPT_COLOR.exec = '#103F39'; /* wave137 */
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--c-progdev', PD_COLOR);
  } catch (e) { /* base constants missing: nothing to extend */ }
  try {
    if (typeof SECTIONS !== 'undefined') {
      if (SECTIONS.opsmtl) {
        SECTIONS.opsmtl.title = 'Operations Montreal';
        SECTIONS.opsmtl.subsections = (SECTIONS.opsmtl.subsections || []).filter(function (s) { return String(s.title || '').toLowerCase() !== 'program development'; });
      }
      if (SECTIONS.opsto) SECTIONS.opsto.title = 'Operations Toronto';
      if (SECTIONS.fundraising) {
        SECTIONS.fundraising.title = 'Fundraising';
        /* wave164: Finance is not a Fundraising workstream (it lives in the Finance app) */
        SECTIONS.fundraising.subsections = (SECTIONS.fundraising.subsections || []).filter(function (s) { return s.title !== 'Finance'; });
      }
      if (!SECTIONS[PD]) {
        SECTIONS[PD] = {
          title: PD_LABEL,
          color: 'var(--c-progdev)',
          read: '',
          progress: '',
          priorities: [],
          team: [],
          updates: [],
          contributed: 'Program Development meetings (Otter) and the #program-development Slack channel.',
          subsections: [
            { title: 'General', color: PD_COLOR, summary: 'Work that is not tied to one programme.', items: [] },
            { title: 'Beneficiary programs', color: '#6F64AB', summary: 'Programmes for the people we serve: employment, feedback, referrals.', items: [] },
            { title: 'Research and data', color: '#3a6ea5', summary: 'Surveys, evaluation and the numbers behind the programmes.', items: [] },
            { title: 'Community team', color: '#c87a2f', summary: 'Community leads, partnerships and outreach.', items: [] }
          ]
        };
      }
    }
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  /* the transcript label to key mapping learns the new team (old labels keep working) */
  try {
    if (typeof txDeptKey === 'function') {
      var __tdk116 = txDeptKey;
      txDeptKey = function (label) {
        var s = String(label || '').toLowerCase();
        if (s === PD || (s.indexOf('program dev') >= 0 && s.indexOf('montreal') < 0 && s.indexOf('toronto') < 0 && s.indexOf('ops') < 0)) return PD;
        return __tdk116(label);
      };
    }
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  function ensure() {
    try {
      var main = document.querySelector('main');
      if (main && !document.getElementById(PD)) {
        var s = document.createElement('section');
        s.id = PD; s.className = 'panel';
        var after = document.getElementById('opsmtl');
        if (after && after.parentNode === main) after.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', s); else main.appendChild(s);
      }
      var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
      if (bar) {
        var col = function (k, fb) { try { return (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[k]) || fb; } catch (e) { return fb; } };
        var om = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="opsmtl"]');
        if (om) om.innerHTML = '<span class="tabdot" style="background:' + col('opsmtl', '#103F39') + '"></span>Operations Montreal';
        var ot = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="opsto"]');
        if (ot) ot.innerHTML = '<span class="tabdot" style="background:' + col('opsto', '#3a6ea5') + '"></span>Operations Toronto';
        var fu = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="fundraising"]');
        if (fu) fu.innerHTML = '<span class="tabdot" style="background:' + col('fundraising', '#c87a2f') + '"></span>Fundraising';
        if (!bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + PD + '"]')) {
          var b = document.createElement('button');
          b.className = 'tab'; b.setAttribute('data-tab', PD);
          b.innerHTML = '<span class="tabdot" style="background:' + PD_COLOR + '"></span>' + PD_LABEL;
          if (om && om.parentNode) om.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', b); else bar.appendChild(b);
        }
      }
      document.querySelectorAll('select').forEach(function (sel) {
        var o = sel.querySelector('option[value="opsmtl"]');
        if (o && !sel.querySelector('option[value="' + PD + '"]')) {
          o.textContent = 'Operations Montreal';
          var n = document.createElement('option'); n.value = PD; n.textContent = PD_LABEL;
          o.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', n);
        }
        var ot2 = sel.querySelector('option[value="opsto"]');
        if (ot2 && /Program Dev/.test(ot2.textContent)) ot2.textContent = 'Operations Toronto';
        var fu2 = sel.querySelector('option[value="fundraising"]');
        if (fu2 && /Grants|Financ/.test(fu2.textContent)) fu2.textContent = 'Fundraising';
        var lm = sel.querySelector('option[value="Ops & Program Dev - Montreal"], option[value="Operations Montreal"]');
        if (lm && !sel.querySelector('option[value="' + PD_LABEL + '"]')) {
          var n2 = document.createElement('option'); n2.value = PD_LABEL; n2.textContent = PD_LABEL;
          lm.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', n2);
        }
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  ensure();
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ensure);
  /* wave116g: executive decision areas the split teams need (the base map is a const object, so keys are added, never reassigned) */
  try {
    if (typeof AREA_COLORS === 'object' && AREA_COLORS) {
      if (!AREA_COLORS['Program Development']) AREA_COLORS['Program Development'] = PD_COLOR;
      if (!AREA_COLORS['Events']) AREA_COLORS['Events'] = '#c87a2f';
      if (!AREA_COLORS['Finance']) AREA_COLORS['Finance'] = '#7a5a0f';
    }
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v2 UI enhancements (injected 22/07/26)
   1. What's new: grouped per-department card grid with new-counts
   2. Transcript archive: department colour chips (menu colours)
   3. Transcript "Move..." department control (staff)
   Overrides the earlier renderWhatsNew / renderTranscripts via hoisting.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  var css = ''
    + '.wn-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(250px,1fr));gap:10px;}'
    + '.wn-card{background:#F4F2EA;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-top:3px solid var(--wa-muted);border-radius:10px;padding:9px 12px;font-size:12.5px;}'
    + '.wn-card-h{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:4px;}'
    + '.wn-card-h .t{font-weight:700;font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;}'
    + '.wn-badge{background:var(--wa-gold);color:var(--wa-ink);border-radius:10px;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;padding:1px 7px;margin-left:8px;}'
    + '.wn-row{padding:5px 0;border-top:1px dashed var(--wa-line);cursor:pointer;}'
    + '.wn-row:first-of-type{border-top:none;}'
    + '.wn-row:hover .wn-text{color:var(--wa-green);}'
    + '.wn-row .wn-text{max-height:38px;overflow:hidden;}'
    + '.tx-item .deptchip{color:#FFFAF2;}'
    + '.tx-deptedit{margin-left:auto;}'
    + '.tx-deptedit select{font-size:11.5px;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:6px;padding:2px 4px;background:#FFFAF2;color:var(--wa-text);}';
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.textContent = css;
  document.head.appendChild(st);
})();

function renderWhatsNew() {
  var el = document.getElementById("whatsNew");
  if (!el) return;
  var items = [];
  DB_UPDATES.forEach(function (u) {
    items.push({ when: u.created_at, dept: u.department, text: (u.entry_label ? u.entry_label + ": " : "") + (u.text || ""), hash: "#dept/" + u.department });
  });
  getDecisions().filter(function (d) { return !d.done && d.opened; }).forEach(function (d) {
    items.push({ when: d.opened, dept: "__exec", text: d.title, hash: "#decision/" + d.ref });
  });
  items.sort(function (a, b) { return new Date(b.when) - new Date(a.when); });
  if (!items.length) { el.style.display = "none"; return; }
  var groups = {};
  items.forEach(function (i) { var k = i.dept || "__exec"; (groups[k] = groups[k] || []).push(i); });
  var order = ["__exec"].concat(DEPT_ORDER).filter(function (k) { return groups[k] && groups[k].length; });
  Object.keys(groups).forEach(function (k) { if (order.indexOf(k) < 0) order.push(k); });
  var totalNew = 0;
  var html = order.map(function (k) {
    var rows = groups[k].slice(0, 4);
    var col = k === "__exec" ? "#a87f2c" : (DEPT_COLOR[k] || "#797773");
    var lab = k === "__exec" ? "Executive decisions" : (DEPT_LABEL[k] || k);
    var newN = groups[k].filter(function (i) { return LAST_SEEN ? new Date(i.when) > new Date(LAST_SEEN) : false; }).length;
    totalNew += newN;
    return '<div class="wn-card" style="border-top-color:' + col + '">'
      + '<div class="wn-card-h"><span class="t" style="color:' + col + '">' + lab + '</span>'
      + (newN ? '<span class="wn-badge">' + newN + ' new</span>' : '') + '</div>'
      + rows.map(function (i) {
          var isNew = LAST_SEEN ? new Date(i.when) > new Date(LAST_SEEN) : false;
          return '<div class="wn-row" data-hash="' + i.hash + '"><div class="wn-text">'
            + (isNew ? '<span class="wn-new"></span>' : '') + esc(i.text)
            + '</div><div class="wn-when">' + fmtDay(i.when) + '</div></div>';
        }).join('')
      + '</div>';
  }).join('');
  el.style.display = "block";
  el.innerHTML = "<h3>What&#39;s new since you last looked"
    + (totalNew ? ' <span class="wn-badge">' + totalNew + ' new</span>' : '')
    + '</h3><div class="wn-grid">' + html + '</div>';
  el.querySelectorAll(".wn-row").forEach(function (w) {
    w.addEventListener("click", function () { location.hash = w.dataset.hash; });
  });
}

function renderTranscripts() {
  var list = document.getElementById("txList");
  if (!list || !isStaff()) return;
  var filt = document.getElementById("txFilter").value;
  var q = (document.getElementById("txSearch").value || "").toLowerCase();
  var rows = txCache.filter(function (r) {
    return (!filt || r.department === filt || txDeptKey(r.department) === txDeptKey(filt))
      && (!q || ((r.title || "") + " " + (r.ai_summary || "")).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) >= 0);
  });
  if (!rows.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="tx-empty">No transcripts yet for this view. Use the form above to add one.</div>';
    return;
  }
  list.innerHTML = rows.map(function (r) {
    var k = txDeptKey(r.department);
    var col = DEPT_COLOR[k] || "#797773";
    var lab = DEPT_LABEL[k] || (r.department || "");
    var dateStr = r.meeting_date ? new Date(r.meeting_date + "T12:00:00").toLocaleDateString() : "";
    var deptSel = '<span class="tx-deptedit" title="Change department"><select data-txdept="' + r.id + '">'
      + '<option value="">Move...</option>'
      + DEPT_ORDER.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '"' + (d === k ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (DEPT_LABEL[d] || d) + '</option>'; }).join('')
      + '</select></span>';
    return '<div class="tx-item" data-tx="' + r.id + '">'
      + '<div class="tx-head">'
      + '<span class="deptchip" style="background:' + col + ';">' + esc(lab) + '</span>' + (r.board_only ? '<span class="lock-tag">board only</span>' : '')
      + '<span class="tx-title">' + esc(r.title || "Untitled") + '</span>'
      + txStatusChip(r)
      + '<span class="tx-date">' + dateStr + '</span>'
      + '</div>'
      + (r.processed && r.ai_summary ? '<div class="tx-sum">' + esc(r.ai_summary) + '</div>' : '')
      + '<div class="tx-actions">'
      + (r.source_url ? '<a href="' + esc(r.source_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="font-size:12px;font-weight:600;">Source &#8599;</a>' : '')
      + (!txProcessing(r) && !r.archive_only ? '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-reproc="' + r.id + '">&#8635; Reprocess</button>' : '')
      + deptSel
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="tx-body">' + escapeHtml(r.body || "") + '</div>'
      + '</div>';
  }).join("");
  list.querySelectorAll(".tx-item .tx-head").forEach(function (h) {
    h.addEventListener("click", function (e) { if (e.target.closest("[data-goto]")) return; h.parentElement.classList.toggle("open"); });
  });
  list.querySelectorAll("[data-goto]").forEach(function (c) { c.addEventListener("click", function () { location.hash = c.dataset.goto; }); });
  list.querySelectorAll("[data-reproc]").forEach(function (b) {
    b.addEventListener("click", function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); reprocessTx(Number(b.dataset.reproc)); });
  });
  list.querySelectorAll("[data-txdept]").forEach(function (s) {
    s.addEventListener("click", function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });
    s.addEventListener("change", function (e) {
      e.stopPropagation();
      var id = Number(s.dataset.txdept), to = s.value;
      if (!to) return;
      moveTranscriptDept(id, to);
    });
  });
}

async function moveTranscriptDept(id, to) {
  var row = txCache.find(function (r) { return r.id === id; });
  var from = row ? row.department : null;
  var r = await db.from("transcripts").update({ department: to }).eq("id", id);
  if (r.error) { toast("Could not move transcript"); return; }
  try {
    await db.from("change_log").insert({ entity_type: "transcript", entity_id: String(id), field: "department", old_value: from, new_value: to, changed_by: who() });
  } catch (e) { /* log only */ }
  toast("Moved to " + (DEPT_LABEL[to] || to));
  await loadTranscripts();
}

   WA v3 UI enhancements (injected 22/07/26)
   Per-department executive decision cards with AI recommendations.
   Decisions whose area maps to a department surface inside that
   department's page (board excluded); the full queue stays in
   Open Executive Decisions.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  var css = ''
    + '.exec-card{border-left:4px solid #a87f2c;}'
    + '.exec-item{padding:8px 0;border-top:1px dashed var(--wa-line);}'
    + '.exec-item:first-of-type{border-top:none;}'
    + '.exec-line b{font-size:13.5px;}'
    + '.exec-owner{margin-left:8px;font-size:11px;color:var(--wa-muted);font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;}'
    + '.exec-move{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);margin-top:2px;}'
    + '.rec-box{background:#f6efe0;border:1px solid #e7d7b0;border-radius:8px;padding:8px 10px;margin-top:6px;font-size:12.5px;white-space:pre-wrap;}'
    + '.exec-actions{margin-top:6px;display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;}';
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.textContent = css;
  document.head.appendChild(st);
})();

function waAreaKey(a) {
  var s = (a || "").toLowerCase();
  if (s.indexOf("mtl") >= 0 || s.indexOf("montreal") >= 0) return "opsmtl";
  if (s === "ops to" || s.indexOf("toronto") >= 0) return "opsto";
  if (s.indexOf("fund") >= 0 || s.indexOf("grant") >= 0) return "fundraising";
  if (s.indexOf("market") >= 0) return "marketing";
  if (s === "hr" || s.indexOf("human") >= 0) return "hr";
  if (s.indexOf("board") >= 0) return "board";
  return null;
}

function renderDeptExecCards() {
  DEPT_ORDER.filter(function (d) { return d !== "board"; }).forEach(renderDeptExecCard);
}

function renderDeptExecCard(deptId) {
  var panel = document.getElementById(deptId);
  if (!panel) return;
  var existing = panel.querySelector(".exec-card");
  if (existing) existing.remove();
  var rows = (typeof DB_DECISIONS !== "undefined" ? DB_DECISIONS : []).filter(function (d) {
    return !d.done && waAreaKey(d.area) === deptId;
  });
  if (!rows.length) return;
  var card = document.createElement("div");
  card.className = "card exec-card";
  card.innerHTML = '<h2 style="color:#a87f2c;">&#9873; Executive decisions: ' + (DEPT_LABEL[deptId] || deptId) + '</h2>'
    + '<p class="read" style="margin-top:2px;">Calls that sit with Steel and Suhaib, not the department team. The full queue stays in Open Executive Decisions.</p>'
    + rows.map(function (d) {
        return '<div class="exec-item" data-dec="' + d.id + '">'
          + '<div class="exec-line"><b>' + esc(d.title) + '</b>' + catTag(d.reason_category) + (d.board_only ? '<span class="lock-tag">board only</span>' : '')
          + (d.owner ? '<span class="exec-owner">' + esc(d.owner) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>'
          + (d.suggested_move ? '<div class="exec-move">Suggested move: ' + esc(d.suggested_move) + '</div>' : '')
          + '<div class="exec-rec" id="rec-' + deptId + '-' + d.id + '">'
          + (d.recommendation ? '<div class="rec-box">' + esc(d.recommendation) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>'
          + '<div class="exec-actions">'
          + (isStaff() ? '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-rec="' + d.id + '">' + (d.recommendation ? '&#8635; Refresh recommendation' : '&#10024; Get recommendation') + '</button>' : '')
          + '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-opendec="' + d.id + '">Open in decisions</button>' + (typeof WA_IS_CIRCLE !== 'undefined' && WA_IS_CIRCLE ? '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-boardonly="' + d.id + '">' + (d.board_only ? 'Make staff visible' : 'Board only') + '</button>' : '')
          + '</div></div>';
      }).join('');
  panel.insertBefore(card, panel.firstChild);
  card.querySelectorAll("[data-rec]").forEach(function (b) {
    b.addEventListener("click", function () { waGetRecommendation(Number(b.dataset.rec), deptId, b); });
  });
  card.querySelectorAll("[data-opendec]").forEach(function (b) {
    b.addEventListener("click", function () { location.hash = "#decision/" + b.dataset.opendec; });
  });
}

async function waGetRecommendation(id, deptId, btn) {
  btn.disabled = true;
  var prior = btn.innerHTML;
  btn.textContent = "Thinking...";
  try {
    const { data, error } = await db.functions.invoke("recommend-decision", { body: { decision_id: id } });
    if (error) throw error;
    var rec = data && data.recommendation;
    if (!rec) throw new Error(data && data.error || "empty");
    var row = (typeof DB_DECISIONS !== "undefined" ? DB_DECISIONS : []).find(function (d) { return d.id === id; });
    if (row) row.recommendation = rec;
    /* wave130: every copy of the card (the classic department card and the team page), and the one next to the button */
    var slots = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[id="rec-' + deptId + '-' + id + '"], [id="w116rec-' + deptId + '-' + id + '"]'));
    var near = btn.closest && btn.closest("[data-dec]"), nearSlot = near && near.querySelector(".exec-rec");
    if (nearSlot && slots.indexOf(nearSlot) < 0) slots.push(nearSlot);
    slots.forEach(function (slot) { slot.innerHTML = '<div class="rec-box">' + esc(rec) + '</div>'; });
    btn.innerHTML = /the recommendation|a recommendation/.test(prior) ? "&#8635; Refresh the recommendation" : "&#8635; Refresh recommendation";
    toast("Recommendation ready");
  } catch (e) {
    btn.innerHTML = prior;
    toast("Recommendation failed, try again");
  }
  btn.disabled = false;
}

if (typeof renderAllSections === "function") {
  var __waRAS = renderAllSections;
  renderAllSections = function () {
    __waRAS();
    try { renderDeptExecCards(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
}

/* ---- menu dropdowns ---- */
(function () {
  var nav = document.querySelector('nav.wa-nav') || document.getElementById('tabBar');
  if (!nav) return;
  var dd = document.createElement('div');
  dd.className = 'wa-dd';
  document.body.appendChild(dd);
  var hideT = null, curDept = null;
  function hide() { dd.style.display = 'none'; curDept = null; }
  function showFor(tab, dept) {
    curDept = dept;
    var r = tab.getBoundingClientRect();
    dd.innerHTML = '<a data-act="overview">Overview</a>'
      + '<a data-act="exec">Executive decisions</a>'
      + '<a data-act="agenda">Agendas</a>'
      + '<a data-act="tx">Transcripts</a>';
    dd.style.left = Math.round(r.left) + 'px';
    dd.style.top = Math.round(r.bottom) + 'px';
    dd.style.position = 'fixed';
    dd.style.display = 'block';
    dd.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(function (a) {
      a.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var act = a.dataset.act, d = curDept;
        hide();
        if (act === 'overview') { location.hash = '#dept/' + d; }
        if (act === 'exec') {
          location.hash = '#dept/' + d;
          setTimeout(function () {
            var c = document.querySelector('#' + d + ' .exec-card');
            if (c) c.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
            else toast('No executive decisions for ' + (DEPT_LABEL[d] || d) + ' right now');
          }, 380);
        }
        if (act === 'agenda') {
          location.hash = '#agendas';
          setTimeout(function () {
            try { var f = document.getElementById('agFilterDept'); if (f) { f.value = d; waRenderAgendasPage(); } } catch (e) { /* boot */ }
          }, 300);
        }
        if (act === 'tx') {
          location.hash = '#transcripts';
          setTimeout(function () {
            var f = document.getElementById('txFilter');
            if (f) {
              var opt = Array.prototype.slice.call(f.options).find(function (o) {
                return txDeptKey(o.value) === d || txDeptKey(o.text) === d;
              });
              if (opt) { f.value = opt.value; renderTranscripts(); }
            }
          }, 380);
        }
      });
    });
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .tab').forEach(function (t) {
    var d = t.dataset.tab;
    if (DEPT_ORDER.indexOf(d) < 0) return;
    t.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () { clearTimeout(hideT); showFor(t, d); });
    t.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () { hideT = setTimeout(hide, 350); });
  });
  dd.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () { clearTimeout(hideT); });
  dd.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () { hideT = setTimeout(hide, 250); });
  window.addEventListener('scroll', hide);
})();

/* ---- task move between departments ---- */
function itemLi(it) {
  let dateLine;
  if (it.done) {
    const dd = daysBetween(it.opened, it.closed);
    dateLine = (it.opened ? "Opened " + fmtDay(it.opened) + " &middot; " : "") + "Closed " + (fmtDay(it.closed) || "?") + (dd != null ? " &middot; " + dd + "d to close" : "");
  } else {
    const age = daysBetween(it.opened, new Date().toISOString());
    dateLine = it.opened ? "Opened " + fmtDay(it.opened) + (age != null && age > 0 ? " &middot; " + age + "d open" : "") : "";
  }
  const chips = (it.assignees || []).map(a => '<span class="as-chip">' + esc(a.name) + "</span>").join("");
  const btns = isStaff() && !it.done
    ? ' <button class="edit-btn" data-edit="' + it.ref + '">edit</button><button class="reassign-btn" data-ref="' + it.ref + '" title="Reassign owner">reassign</button><button class="move-btn" data-move="' + it.ref + '" title="Move to another department">move</button>'
    : "";
  return '<li data-task="' + it.ref + '" class="' + (it.done ? "done" : "") + '">' +
    '<input type="checkbox" data-ref="' + it.ref + '" ' + (it.done ? "checked" : "") + (isStaff() ? "" : " disabled") + " />" +
    '<div class="ci-body">' +
      '<div class="ci-title">' + (it.flag ? '<span class="flag">&#9873; </span>' : "") + it.title + catTag(it.cat) + (it.confidential && isStaff() ? '<span class="lock-tag">board hidden</span>' : "") + dueBadge(it) + "</div>" +
      '<div class="ci-sub">Owner: ' + (it.owner || "unassigned") + (it.status ? " &middot; " + it.status : "") + btns + "</div>" +
      (chips ? '<div class="ci-sub">' + chips + "</div>" : "") +
      (it.flag && it.note ? '<div class="ci-sub" style="font-style:italic;">Why: ' + esc(it.note) + "</div>" : "") +
      '<div class="ci-meta">' + dateLine + (it.done && it.by ? " &middot; by " + it.by : "") + "</div>" +
    "</div></li>";
}

async function waMoveTask(ref, to) {
  var row = (typeof DB_TASKS !== "undefined" ? DB_TASKS : []).find(function (t) { return t.id === ref; });
  var from = row ? row.department : null;
  if (!to || to === from) return;
  var r = await db.from("dept_tasks").update({ department: to, subsection: null, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: who() }).eq("id", ref);
  if (r.error) { toast("Could not move task"); return; }
  try {
    await db.from("change_log").insert({ entity_type: "task", entity_id: String(ref), field: "department", old_value: from, new_value: to, changed_by: who() });
  } catch (e) { /* log only */ }
  toast("Task moved to " + (DEPT_LABEL[to] || to));
  await loadAll();
  renderAllSections(); renderDashboard(); renderNeedsMe();
}

(function () {
  if (typeof wireTaskEvents !== "function") return;
  var __wte = wireTaskEvents;
  wireTaskEvents = function (scope) {
    __wte(scope);
    scope.querySelectorAll("[data-move]").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        var ref = Number(b.dataset.move);
        var row = (typeof DB_TASKS !== "undefined" ? DB_TASKS : []).find(function (t) { return t.id === ref; });
        var cur = row ? row.department : null;
        var sel = document.createElement("select");
        sel.className = "move-sel";
        sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Move to...</option>' + DEPT_ORDER.map(function (d) {
          return '<option value="' + d + '"' + (d === cur ? " disabled" : "") + '>' + (DEPT_LABEL[d] || d) + "</option>";
        }).join("");
        b.replaceWith(sel);
        sel.focus();
        sel.addEventListener("change", function () { if (sel.value) waMoveTask(ref, sel.value); });
      });
    });
  };
})();

(function () {
  var main = document.querySelector("main");
  if (main && !document.getElementById("importantinfo")) {
    var sec = document.createElement("section");
    sec.id = "importantinfo";
    sec.className = "panel";
    sec.innerHTML = '<div class="card info-sec" id="infoCard"><h2>Important info</h2><div id="infoBody">Loading...</div></div>';
    main.appendChild(sec);
  }
})();

var INFO_CACHE = [];
async function renderInfoPage() {
  var body = document.getElementById("infoBody");
  if (!body) return;
  if (!isStaff()) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">This page is available to Welfare Avenue staff accounts only.</p>'; return; }
  var r = await db.from("info_links").select("*").order("kind").order("sort").order("label");
  if (r.error) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Could not load. Refresh to retry.</p>'; return; }
  INFO_CACHE = r.data || [];
  var kinds = [
    { k: "password", title: "Logins and passwords", note: '<div class="pw-note">Visible to every signed-in WA staff account, never to board viewers. Keep it to shared team logins (Canva, social accounts, tools). Do not store banking, government, or personal credentials here.</div>' },
    { k: "video", title: "Training videos (Loom)", note: "" },
    { k: "folder", title: "Shared Google Drive folders", note: "" },
    { k: "link", title: "Other key links", note: "" }
  ];
  body.innerHTML = kinds.map(function (K) {
    var rows = INFO_CACHE.filter(function (i) { return i.kind === K.k; });
    return '<h3>' + K.title + '</h3>' + K.note + (rows.length ? rows.map(function (i) {
      var dep = i.department && DEPT_LABEL[i.department] ? '<span class="dep" style="background:' + (DEPT_COLOR[i.department] || "#797773") + '">' + DEPT_LABEL[i.department] + '</span>' : '';
      var main2 = '<span class="lbl">' + esc(i.label) + '</span>' + dep;
      if (K.k === "password") {
        main2 += (i.username ? ' <span style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:12px;">' + esc(i.username) + '</span>' : '')
          + ' <span class="sec" data-sec="' + i.id + '">&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;</span>'
          + ' <button class="btn ghost mini" data-reveal="' + i.id + '">Show</button>'
          + ' <button class="btn ghost mini" data-copy="' + i.id + '">Copy</button>'
          + (i.url ? ' <a href="' + esc(i.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="font-size:12px;">Open &#8599;</a>' : '');
      } else {
        main2 += (i.url ? ' <a href="' + esc(i.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="font-size:12px;font-weight:600;">Open &#8599;</a>' : '');
      }
      return '<div class="info-row">' + main2
        + '<button class="mini-x" data-del="' + i.id + '" title="Remove">&#10005;</button>'
        + (i.notes ? '<div class="nts">' + esc(i.notes) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
    }).join("") : '<div class="info-row" style="color:var(--wa-muted);">Nothing here yet. Add the first one below.</div>');
  }).join("")
  + '<div class="info-add"><select id="ii-kind"><option value="password">Password / login</option><option value="video">Training video</option><option value="folder">Drive folder</option><option value="link">Other link</option></select>'
  + '<input id="ii-label" placeholder="Label, e.g. Canva team login" />'
  + '<input id="ii-url" placeholder="URL (optional for passwords)" />'
  + '<input id="ii-user" placeholder="Username / email (passwords)" />'
  + '<input id="ii-secret" placeholder="Password (passwords only)" type="password" />'
  + '<select id="ii-dept"><option value="">No department</option>' + DEPT_ORDER.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '">' + DEPT_LABEL[d] + '</option>'; }).join("") + '</select>'
  + '<input id="ii-notes" placeholder="Notes (optional)" />'
  + '<button class="btn mini" id="ii-add" style="background:var(--wa-green);color:#FFFAF2;border:none;border-radius:7px;cursor:pointer;">Add</button></div>';

  body.querySelectorAll("[data-reveal]").forEach(function (b) {
    b.addEventListener("click", function () {
      var i = INFO_CACHE.find(function (x) { return x.id === Number(b.dataset.reveal); });
      var el = body.querySelector('[data-sec="' + b.dataset.reveal + '"]');
      if (!i || !el) return;
      if (b.textContent === "Show") { el.textContent = i.secret || "(empty)"; b.textContent = "Hide"; }
      else { el.innerHTML = "&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;"; b.textContent = "Show"; }
    });
  });
  body.querySelectorAll("[data-copy]").forEach(function (b) {
    b.addEventListener("click", function () {
      var i = INFO_CACHE.find(function (x) { return x.id === Number(b.dataset.copy); });
      if (i && i.secret) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(i.secret).then(function () { toast("Copied"); }); }
    });
  });
  body.querySelectorAll("[data-del]").forEach(function (b) {
    b.addEventListener("click", async function () {
      if (!confirm("Remove this entry for everyone?")) return;
      await db.from("info_links").delete().eq("id", Number(b.dataset.del));
      renderInfoPage();
    });
  });
  var add = document.getElementById("ii-add");
  if (add) add.addEventListener("click", async function () {
    var v = function (id) { return (document.getElementById(id).value || "").trim(); };
    if (!v("ii-label")) { toast("Add a label"); return; }
    var r2 = await db.from("info_links").insert({
      kind: v("ii-kind") || "link", label: v("ii-label"), url: v("ii-url") || null,
      username: v("ii-user") || null, secret: v("ii-secret") || null,
      department: v("ii-dept") || null, notes: v("ii-notes") || null, added_by: who()
    });
    if (r2.error) { toast("Could not save"); return; }
    toast("Added");
    renderInfoPage();
  });
}

(function () {
  if (typeof applyHash === "function") {
    var __ah = applyHash;
    applyHash = function () {
      var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || "").replace(/^#/, "");
      if (h === "importantinfo") { showPanel("importantinfo"); renderInfoPage(); return; }
      __ah();
    };
  }
})();

/* ---- chat assistant widget ---- */
var WA_CHAT_HIST = [];
function waInitChat() {
  if (document.getElementById("waChatBtn")) return;
  var btn = document.createElement("button");
  btn.id = "waChatBtn";
  btn.innerHTML = "&#10024;";
  btn.title = "Ask the WA assistant";
  var panel = document.createElement("div");
  panel.id = "waChatPanel";
  panel.innerHTML = '<div class="wac-h">WA Assistant<small>Ask anything, or tell me to close tasks and complete requests. Staff only.</small></div>'
    + '<div class="wac-msgs" id="wacMsgs"><div class="wac-m bot"><span>Hi, ask me anything about what is happening across Welfare Avenue. I answer only from the dashboard data.</span></div></div>'
    + '<div class="wac-in"><input id="wacInput" placeholder="e.g. What is blocking the kitchen pilot?" /><button id="wacMic" title="Voice input">&#127908;</button><button id="wacSend">&#10148;</button></div>';
  document.body.appendChild(btn);
  document.body.appendChild(panel);
  btn.addEventListener("click", function () {
    panel.style.display = panel.style.display === "flex" ? "none" : "flex";
    if (panel.style.display === "flex") document.getElementById("wacInput").focus();
  });
  function addMsg(cls, text, sources) {
    var m = document.getElementById("wacMsgs");
    var d = document.createElement("div");
    d.className = "wac-m " + cls;
    d.innerHTML = "<span>" + esc(text) + "</span>" + (sources && sources.length ? '<div class="wac-src">Sources: ' + esc(sources.join("; ")) + "</div>" : "");
    m.appendChild(d);
    m.scrollTop = m.scrollHeight;
    return d;
  }
  async function send() {
    var inp = document.getElementById("wacInput");
    var q = (inp.value || "").trim();
    if (!q) return;
    inp.value = "";
    addMsg("user", q);
    WA_CHAT_HIST.push({ role: "user", content: q });
    var thinking = addMsg("bot", "Thinking...");
    try {
      const { data, error } = await db.functions.invoke("ask", { body: { question: q, history: WA_CHAT_HIST.slice(-6, -1) } });
      if (error) throw error;
      thinking.remove();
      var ans = (data && data.answer) || "I could not find that in the dashboard data.";
      addMsg("bot", ans, data && data.sources);
      if (data && data.actions_done) {
        try {
          await loadAll();
          renderAllSections(); renderDashboard(); renderNeedsMe();
          await waLoadRequests(); renderDeptRequestCards();
        } catch (e2) { /* refresh best effort */ }
        toast(data.actions_done + (data.actions_done === 1 ? " change applied" : " changes applied"));
      }
      WA_CHAT_HIST.push({ role: "assistant", content: ans });
    } catch (e) {
      thinking.remove();
      addMsg("bot", "Something went wrong, try again.");
    }
  }
  document.getElementById("wacSend").addEventListener("click", send);
  document.getElementById("wacInput").addEventListener("keydown", function (e) { if (e.key === "Enter") send(); });
}

/* ---- staff-gated boot additions ---- */
(function () {
  if (typeof startApp !== "function") return;
  var __sa = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa();
    try {
      if (isStaff()) {
        var bar = document.getElementById("tabBar");
        if (bar && !bar.querySelector('[data-tab="importantinfo"]')) {
          var b = document.createElement("button");
          b.className = "tab";
          b.dataset.tab = "importantinfo";
          b.innerHTML = '<span class="tabdot" style="background:#6F64AB"></span>Important info';
          var act = document.getElementById("actTabBtn");
          if (act) bar.insertBefore(b, act); else bar.appendChild(b);
        }
        waInitChat();
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();

  if ((location.hash || "") === "#importantinfo") {
    try { showPanel("importantinfo"); renderInfoPage(); } catch (e) { /* boot */ }
  }
});


/* =====================================================================
   WA v5 UI enhancements (injected 22/07/26)
   Department requests module: searchable request catalogue (tab
   "Requests"), typed submission forms from request_types, incoming
   request cards on each department panel with per-type status sets.
   Tables: request_types, dept_requests (RLS staff only).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  var css = ''
    + '.req-search{width:100%;max-width:420px;padding:9px 13px;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:10px;font-size:13px;margin:6px 0 14px;}'
    + '.req-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(240px,1fr));gap:10px;}'
    + '.req-type{background:#F4F2EA;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:10px;padding:11px 13px;cursor:pointer;}'

    + '.req-form{max-width:560px;}'
    + '.req-form label{display:block;font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted);margin:10px 0 3px;}'
    + '.req-form input,.req-form textarea,.req-form select{width:100%;padding:8px 10px;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;box-sizing:border-box;}'
    + '.req-form textarea{min-height:90px;resize:vertical;}'
    + '.req-card{border-left:4px solid var(--wa-green);}'
    + '.req-row{padding:9px 0;border-top:1px dashed var(--wa-line);font-size:13px;}'
    + '.req-row:first-of-type{border-top:none;}'
    + '.req-row .rt{font-weight:700;}'
    + '.req-row .rm{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);margin-top:2px;}'
    + '.req-row .rd{font-size:12px;background:var(--wa-paper);border-radius:8px;padding:7px 10px;margin-top:6px;display:none;white-space:pre-wrap;}'
    + '.req-row.open .rd{display:block;}'
    + '.req-row select{font-size:12px;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:6px;padding:3px 6px;margin-right:8px;}'
    + '.req-actions{margin-top:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;}';
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.textContent = css;
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  var main = document.querySelector('main');
  if (main && !document.getElementById('requests')) {
    var sec = document.createElement('section');
    sec.id = 'requests';
    sec.className = 'panel';
    sec.innerHTML = '<div class="card"><h2>Department requests</h2><p class="read">Find the request you need, fill it in, and it lands on the receiving department&#39;s page with the right status track. Modelled on the ClickUp SOP templates.</p><div id="reqBody">Loading...</div></div>';
    main.appendChild(sec);
  }
})();

var REQ_TYPES = [], REQ_CACHE = [];
async function waLoadRequests() {
  try {
    var a = await db.from('request_types').select('*').eq('enabled', true).order('sort').order('label');
    if (!a.error) REQ_TYPES = a.data || [];
    var b = await db.from('dept_requests').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    if (!b.error) REQ_CACHE = b.data || [];
  } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
}
function waReqType(key) { return REQ_TYPES.find(function (t) { return t.key === key; }); }

function renderRequestsModule(q) {
  var body = document.getElementById('reqBody');
  if (!body) return;
  if (!isStaff()) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Requests are available to staff accounts only.</p>'; return; }
  if (!REQ_TYPES.length) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">No request types are set up yet. They are being imported from the ClickUp SOP templates; check back shortly.</p>'; return; }
  var ql = (q || '').toLowerCase();
  var types = REQ_TYPES.filter(function (t) {
    return !ql || (t.label + ' ' + (t.description || '') + ' ' + (DEPT_LABEL[t.department] || '')).toLowerCase().indexOf(ql) >= 0;
  });
  var mine = REQ_CACHE.slice(0, 6);
  body.innerHTML = '<input class="req-search" id="reqSearch" placeholder="Search request types, e.g. post, email, reimbursement" value="' + esc(q || '') + '" />'
    + '<div class="req-grid">' + (types.length ? types.map(function (t) {
        return '<div class="req-type" data-rt="' + esc(t.key) + '">'
          + '<span class="req-dep" style="background:' + (DEPT_COLOR[t.department] || '#797773') + '">' + (DEPT_LABEL[t.department] || t.department) + '</span>'
          + '<div class="t">' + esc(t.label) + '</div>'
          + '<div class="d">' + esc(t.description || '') + '</div></div>';
      }).join('') : '<p class="read">No request type matches that search.</p>') + '</div>'
    + (mine.length ? '<h3 style="margin:20px 0 4px;font-size:13px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;color:var(--wa-green);">Recent requests</h3>'
      + mine.map(function (r) {
          var t = waReqType(r.type_key);
          return '<div class="req-row"><span class="rt">' + esc(r.title) + '</span> <span class="rm">' + esc(t ? t.label : (r.type_key || '')) + ' &middot; for ' + (DEPT_LABEL[r.department] || r.department) + ' &middot; ' + esc(r.status || '') + (r.done ? ' &middot; completed' : '') + ' &middot; by ' + esc(r.requested_by || '') + '</span></div>';
        }).join('') : '');
  var si = document.getElementById('reqSearch');
  si.addEventListener('input', function () { renderRequestsModule(si.value); });
  if (ql) { si.focus(); si.setSelectionRange(si.value.length, si.value.length); }
  body.querySelectorAll('[data-rt]').forEach(function (c) {
    c.addEventListener('click', function () { renderRequestForm(c.dataset.rt); });
  });
}

function renderRequestForm(key) {
  var body = document.getElementById('reqBody');
  var t = waReqType(key);
  if (!body || !t) return;
  var fields = Array.isArray(t.fields) && t.fields.length ? t.fields : [{ name: 'details', label: 'Details', type: 'textarea', required: true }];
  body.innerHTML = '<a style="cursor:pointer;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;" id="reqBack">&#8592; All request types</a>'
    + '<div class="req-form"><h3 style="margin:12px 0 2px;">' + esc(t.label) + '</h3>'
    + '<p class="read" style="margin-top:2px;">' + esc(t.description || '') + ' Goes to ' + (DEPT_LABEL[t.department] || t.department) + '.</p>'
    + '<label>Request title *</label><input id="rq-title" placeholder="Short summary of what you need" />'
    + fields.map(function (f, i) {
        var inp = f.type === 'textarea' ? '<textarea id="rq-f' + i + '"></textarea>'
          : '<input id="rq-f' + i + '" type="' + (f.type === 'number' ? 'number' : f.type === 'date' ? 'date' : f.type === 'url' ? 'url' : 'text') + '" />';
        return '<label>' + esc(f.label) + (f.required ? ' *' : '') + '</label>' + inp;
      }).join('')
    + '<div style="margin-top:14px;"><button class="btn mini" id="rq-send" style="background:var(--wa-green);color:#FFFAF2;border:none;border-radius:8px;padding:9px 16px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;">Submit request</button></div></div>';
  document.getElementById('reqBack').addEventListener('click', function () { renderRequestsModule(''); });
  document.getElementById('rq-send').addEventListener('click', async function () {
    var title = (document.getElementById('rq-title').value || '').trim();
    if (!title) { toast('Add a request title'); return; }
    var details = {};
    for (var i = 0; i < fields.length; i++) {
      var v = (document.getElementById('rq-f' + i).value || '').trim();
      if (fields[i].required && !v) { toast('Fill in: ' + fields[i].label); return; }
      if (v) details[fields[i].label] = v;
    }
    var statuses = Array.isArray(t.statuses) ? t.statuses : [];
    var r = await db.from('dept_requests').insert({
      type_key: t.key, department: t.department, title: title, details: details,
      status: t.default_status || statuses[0] || 'Requested', requested_by: who()
    });
    if (r.error) { toast('Could not submit, try again'); return; }
    try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'request', entity_id: t.key, field: 'created', old_value: null, new_value: title, changed_by: who() }); } catch (e) {}
    toast('Request sent to ' + (DEPT_LABEL[t.department] || t.department));
    await waLoadRequests();
    renderRequestsModule('');
    renderDeptRequestCards();
  });
}

function renderDeptRequestCards() {
  DEPT_ORDER.forEach(function (d) {
    var panel = document.getElementById(d);
    if (!panel) return;
    var old = panel.querySelector('.req-card');
    if (old) old.remove();
    var rows = REQ_CACHE.filter(function (r) { return r.department === d && !r.done; });
    if (!rows.length) return;
    var card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'card req-card';
    card.innerHTML = '<h2 style="color:var(--wa-green);">&#9993; Incoming requests (' + rows.length + ')</h2>'
      + rows.map(function (r) {
          var t = waReqType(r.type_key);
          var statuses = (t && Array.isArray(t.statuses) && t.statuses.length) ? t.statuses : ['Requested', 'In progress', 'Done'];
          return '<div class="req-row" data-req="' + r.id + '">'
            + '<span class="rt">' + esc(r.title) + '</span>'
            + '<div class="rm">' + esc(t ? t.label : (r.type_key || 'Request')) + ' &middot; from ' + esc((window.waWho ? waWho(r.requested_by) : r.requested_by) || 'unknown') + ' &middot; ' + fmtDay(r.created_at) + ' &middot; <a style="cursor:pointer;" data-tog="' + r.id + '">details</a></div>'
            + '<div class="rd">' + esc(Object.entries(r.details || {}).map(function (kv) { return kv[0] + ': ' + kv[1]; }).join(String.fromCharCode(10)) || 'No details given') + '</div>'
            + '<div class="req-actions">'
            + (isStaff() ? '<select data-rst="' + r.id + '">' + statuses.map(function (s) { return '<option' + (s === r.status ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(s) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
              + '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-rdone="' + r.id + '">Complete</button>' : '<span class="rm">' + esc(r.status || '') + '</span>')
            + '</div></div>';
        }).join('');
    var exec = panel.querySelector('.exec-card');
    if (exec && exec.nextSibling) panel.insertBefore(card, exec.nextSibling);
    else panel.insertBefore(card, panel.firstChild ? panel.firstChild.nextSibling : null);
    card.querySelectorAll('[data-tog]').forEach(function (a) {
      a.addEventListener('click', function () { a.closest('.req-row').classList.toggle('open'); });
    });
    card.querySelectorAll('[data-rst]').forEach(function (s) {
      s.addEventListener('change', async function () {
        var id = Number(s.dataset.rst);
        var row = REQ_CACHE.find(function (x) { return x.id === id; });
        var from = row ? row.status : null;
        var r2 = await db.from('dept_requests').update({ status: s.value, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: who() }).eq('id', id);
        if (r2.error) { toast('Could not update status'); return; }
        if (row) row.status = s.value;
        try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'request', entity_id: String(id), field: 'status', old_value: from, new_value: s.value, changed_by: who() }); } catch (e) {}
        toast('Status: ' + s.value);
      });
    });
    card.querySelectorAll('[data-rdone]').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', async function () {
        var id = Number(b.dataset.rdone);
        var r2 = await db.from('dept_requests').update({ done: true, done_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: who() }).eq('id', id);
        if (r2.error) { toast('Could not complete'); return; }
        var row = REQ_CACHE.find(function (x) { return x.id === id; });
        if (row) row.done = true;
        try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'request', entity_id: String(id), field: 'done', old_value: 'false', new_value: 'true', changed_by: who() }); } catch (e) {}
        toast('Request completed');
        renderDeptRequestCards();
      });
    });
  });
}

var AGENDAS_CACHE = [], AG_ITEMS_CACHE = [], WA_AG_OPEN = {};
var AG_KIND_COLOR = { project: '#103F39', decision: '#a87f2c', update: '#3a6ea5', topic: '#797773' };

async function waLoadAgendas() { /* superseded: wave7 reset without capture; superseded; body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }

function waTaskById(id) { return (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).find(function (t) { return t.id === id; }); }

function renderDeptAgendaCards() { /* superseded: wave7 reset without capture; superseded; body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }

function waWireAgendaCard(card, d) {
  card.querySelectorAll('[data-agtog]').forEach(function (h) {
    h.addEventListener('click', function () {
      var id = Number(h.dataset.agtog);
      WA_AG_OPEN[id] = !WA_AG_OPEN[id];
      h.parentElement.classList.toggle('open');
    });
  });
  card.querySelectorAll('[data-aggen]').forEach(function (b) {
    b.addEventListener('click', async function () {
      b.disabled = true; b.textContent = 'Drafting from transcripts...';
      try {
        const { data, error } = await db.functions.invoke('build-agenda', { body: { department: d } });
        if (error || !(data && data.ok)) throw (error || new Error((data && data.error) || 'failed'));
        toast('Agenda drafted: ' + (data.items || 0) + ' items');
        await waLoadAgendas();
        if (data.agenda_id) WA_AG_OPEN[data.agenda_id] = true;
        renderDeptAgendaCards();
      } catch (e) {
        toast('Could not draft the agenda, try again');
        b.disabled = false; b.innerHTML = '&#10024; Generate agenda from recent activity';
      }
    });
  });
  card.querySelectorAll('[data-agtask]').forEach(function (cb) {
    cb.addEventListener('change', async function () {
      var tid = Number(cb.dataset.agtask);
      var ok = await toggleTask(tid, cb.checked);
      if (!ok) { cb.checked = !cb.checked; toast('Could not update the task'); return; }
      var row = waTaskById(tid);
      if (row) row.done = cb.checked;
      renderSection(cb.dataset.agdept);
      renderDashboard(); renderNeedsMe();
    });
  });
  card.querySelectorAll('[data-agadd]').forEach(function (b) {
    b.addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var aid = Number(b.dataset.agadd);
      var t = card.querySelector('[data-agnt="' + aid + '"]').value.trim();
      var n = card.querySelector('[data-agnn="' + aid + '"]').value.trim();
      if (!t) { toast('Add an item title'); return; }
      var maxSort = AG_ITEMS_CACHE.filter(function (i) { return i.agenda_id === aid; }).reduce(function (m, i) { return Math.max(m, i.sort); }, -1);
      var r = await db.from('agenda_items').insert({ agenda_id: aid, kind: 'topic', title: t, notes: n || null, sort: maxSort + 1 });
      if (r.error) { toast('Could not add'); return; }
      await waLoadAgendas(); renderDeptAgendaCards();
    });
  });
  function move(iid, dir) {
    var it = AG_ITEMS_CACHE.find(function (x) { return x.id === iid; });
    if (!it) return;
    var sibs = AG_ITEMS_CACHE.filter(function (x) { return x.agenda_id === it.agenda_id; }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.sort - b.sort; });
    var idx = sibs.indexOf(it), swap = sibs[idx + dir];
    if (!swap) return;
    Promise.all([
      db.from('agenda_items').update({ sort: swap.sort }).eq('id', it.id),
      db.from('agenda_items').update({ sort: it.sort }).eq('id', swap.id)
    ]).then(async function () { await waLoadAgendas(); renderDeptAgendaCards(); });
  }
  card.querySelectorAll('[data-agup]').forEach(function (b) { b.addEventListener('click', function () { move(Number(b.dataset.agup), -1); }); });
  card.querySelectorAll('[data-agdn]').forEach(function (b) { b.addEventListener('click', function () { move(Number(b.dataset.agdn), 1); }); });
  card.querySelectorAll('[data-agdel]').forEach(function (b) {
    b.addEventListener('click', async function () {
      await db.from('agenda_items').delete().eq('id', Number(b.dataset.agdel));
      await waLoadAgendas(); renderDeptAgendaCards();
    });
  });
  function setStatus(aid, status) {
    db.from('agendas').update({ status: status, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', aid)
      .then(async function (r) {
        if (r.error) { toast('Could not update'); return; }
        try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'agenda', entity_id: String(aid), field: 'status', old_value: null, new_value: status, changed_by: who() }); } catch (e) { /* audit only */ }
        await waLoadAgendas(); renderDeptAgendaCards();
      });
  }
  card.querySelectorAll('[data-agfin]').forEach(function (b) { b.addEventListener('click', function () { setStatus(Number(b.dataset.agfin), 'final'); }); });
  card.querySelectorAll('[data-agdraft]').forEach(function (b) { b.addEventListener('click', function () { setStatus(Number(b.dataset.agdraft), 'draft'); }); });
  card.querySelectorAll('[data-agdone]').forEach(function (b) { b.addEventListener('click', function () { setStatus(Number(b.dataset.agdone), 'done'); }); });
  card.querySelectorAll('[data-agkill]').forEach(function (b) {
    b.addEventListener('click', async function () {
      if (!confirm('Delete this agenda for everyone?')) return;
      await db.from('agendas').delete().eq('id', Number(b.dataset.agkill));
      await waLoadAgendas(); renderDeptAgendaCards();
    });
  });
}

  var __ras6 = renderAllSections;
  renderAllSections = function () {
    __ras6();
    try { renderDeptAgendaCards(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
  if (typeof renderSection === 'function') {
    var __rs6 = renderSection;
    renderSection = function (deptId) {
      __rs6(deptId);
      try {
        renderDeptExecCards();
        renderDeptRequestCards();
        renderDeptAgendaCards();
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }

  }
  var __sa6 = startApp;

    await __sa6();
    try {
      if (isStaff()) { await waLoadAgendas(); renderDeptAgendaCards(); }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();

async function waLoadLeaders() {
  if (WA_LEADERS.length) return;
  if (__waLeadersP) return __waLeadersP;
  __waLeadersP = (async function () {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('team_members').select('name, department, role').eq('active', true).order('name');
      if (!r.error) WA_LEADERS = r.data || [];
    } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
    __waLeadersP = null;
  })();
  return __waLeadersP;
}

      + '<span class="ag-count" id="agCount"></span>'

var __wla7 = null;
waLoadAgendas = async function () {
  try {
    var a = await db.from('agendas').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    if (!a.error) AGENDAS_CACHE = a.data || [];
    var ids = AGENDAS_CACHE.map(function (x) { return x.id; });
    if (ids.length) {
      var b = await db.from('agenda_items').select('*').in('agenda_id', ids).order('sort');
      if (!b.error) AG_ITEMS_CACHE = b.data || [];
    } else AG_ITEMS_CACHE = [];
  } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
};

renderExecAgendaCard = function () { /* moved to the Agendas page */ };
renderDeptAgendaCards = function () {
  DEPT_ORDER.forEach(function (d) {
    var p = document.getElementById(d);
    if (p) { var c = p.querySelector('.agenda-card'); if (c) c.remove(); }
  });
  var ov = document.getElementById('overview');
  if (ov) { var e = ov.querySelector('.exec-ag-card'); if (e) e.remove(); }
  try { waRenderAgendasPage(); } catch (e) { /* boot */ }
};

var WA_AGP_INIT = false;
function waAgendaRowHtml(a) { /* superseded: wave19 reset without capture; superseded; body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }

function waInitAgendasPage() {
  if (WA_AGP_INIT) return;
  var dsel = document.getElementById('agNewDept');
  var fsel = document.getElementById('agFilterDept');
  if (!dsel || !fsel) return;
  WA_AGP_INIT = true;
  dsel.innerHTML = DEPT_ORDER.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '">' + DEPT_LABEL[d] + '</option>'; }).join('');
  DEPT_ORDER.forEach(function (d) {
    var o = document.createElement('option'); o.value = d; o.textContent = DEPT_LABEL[d]; fsel.appendChild(o);
  });
  var tsel = document.getElementById('agNewType');
  var wsel = document.getElementById('agNewWho');
  tsel.addEventListener('change', function () {
    dsel.style.display = tsel.value === 'department' ? '' : 'none';
    wsel.style.display = tsel.value === 'people' ? '' : 'none';
  });
  fsel.addEventListener('change', function () { waRenderAgendasPage(); });
  document.getElementById('agFilterStatus').addEventListener('change', function () { waRenderAgendasPage(); });
  document.getElementById('agNewGen').addEventListener('click', async function () {
    var b = this;
    var payload = null;
    if (tsel.value === 'department') payload = { department: dsel.value };
    if (tsel.value === 'leaders') payload = { scope: 'leaders' };
    if (tsel.value === 'people') {
      var picked = Array.prototype.slice.call(wsel.selectedOptions || []).map(function (o) { return o.value; });
      if (picked.length < 2) { toast('Pick at least 2 attendees (Ctrl or Cmd click to select several)'); return; }
      payload = { scope: picked.length === 2 ? 'oneonone' : 'group', attendees: picked };
    }
    if (!payload) return;
    var dt = document.getElementById('agNewDate').value;
    if (dt) payload.meeting_date = dt;
    b.disabled = true; b.textContent = 'Drafting from transcripts...';
    try {
      const { data, error } = await db.functions.invoke('build-agenda', { body: payload });
      if (error || !(data && data.ok)) throw (error || new Error((data && data.error) || 'failed'));
      toast('Agenda drafted: ' + (data.items || 0) + ' items');
      await waLoadAgendas();
      if (data.agenda_id) WA_AG_OPEN[data.agenda_id] = true;
      waRenderAgendasPage();
    } catch (e) { toast('Could not draft the agenda, try again'); }
    b.disabled = false; b.innerHTML = '&#10024; Generate agenda';
  });
}

var waRenderAgendasPage = async function () {
  var body = document.getElementById('agPageBody');
  if (!body) return;
  if (!isStaff()) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Agendas are available to staff accounts only.</p>'; return; }
  waInitAgendasPage();
  await waLoadLeaders();
  var wsel = document.getElementById('agNewWho');
  if (wsel && !wsel.options.length) {
    wsel.innerHTML = WA_LEADERS.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + esc(m.name) + '">' + esc(m.name) + ' (' + (DEPT_LABEL[m.department] || m.department || '') + ')</option>'; }).join('');
  }
  var fd = (document.getElementById('agFilterDept') || {}).value || 'all';
  var fs = (document.getElementById('agFilterStatus') || {}).value || 'active';
  var rows = AGENDAS_CACHE.filter(function (a) {
    var scopeOk = fd === 'all' ? true : fd === 'exec' ? (a.scope || 'department') !== 'department' : (a.department === fd && (a.scope || 'department') === 'department');
    var stOk = fs === 'all' ? true : fs === 'active' ? (a.status === 'draft' || a.status === 'final') : a.status === fs;
    return scopeOk && stOk;
  });
  var cnt = document.getElementById('agCount');
  if (cnt) cnt.textContent = rows.length + ' agenda' + (rows.length === 1 ? '' : 's');
  body.innerHTML = rows.length ? rows.map(waAgendaRowHtml).join('') : '<p class="read">No agendas match this view. Generate one above.</p>';
  waWireAgendaCard(body, null);
};

    __ah7();
  };
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () {
    if ((location.hash || '') === '#agendas') {
      try { showPanel('agendas'); waRenderAgendasPage(); } catch (e) { /* boot */ }
    }
  });
  var __sa7 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa7();
    try {
      if (isStaff()) {
        var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
        if (bar && !bar.querySelector('[data-tab="agendas"]')) {
          var b = document.createElement('button');
          b.className = 'tab';
          b.dataset.tab = 'agendas';
          b.innerHTML = '<span class="tabdot" style="background:#6F64AB"></span>Agendas';
          var req = bar.querySelector('[data-tab="requests"]');
          var info = bar.querySelector('[data-tab="importantinfo"]');
          bar.insertBefore(b, req || info || document.getElementById('actTabBtn') || null);
        }
        renderDeptAgendaCards();
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();


/* =====================================================================
   WA v8 UI enhancements (25/07/26)
   Board-only confidentiality tier. Rows flagged board_only are blocked
   at the database level for everyone outside the board circle; this
   layer adds the tags, the decision toggle (circle only), and a Board
   only checkbox on transcript upload. The transcript processor also
   auto-flags sensitive content (departures, personnel, compensation,
   legal) and errs toward flagging; circle members can unflag.
   ===================================================================== */
var WA_IS_CIRCLE = false;

document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
  var b = e.target.closest('[data-boardonly]');
  if (!b || !WA_IS_CIRCLE) return;
  e.stopPropagation();
  var id = Number(b.dataset.boardonly);
  var row = (typeof DB_DECISIONS !== 'undefined' ? DB_DECISIONS : []).find(function (d) { return d.id === id; });
  var to = !(row && row.board_only);
  var r = await db.from('decisions').update({ board_only: to, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: who() }).eq('id', id);
  if (r.error) { toast('Could not change visibility'); return; }
  if (row) row.board_only = to;
  try {
    await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'decision', entity_id: String(id), field: 'board_only', old_value: String(!to), new_value: String(to), changed_by: who(), board_only: true });
  } catch (err) { /* audit only */ }
  toast(to ? 'Now board only: hidden from staff' : 'Now visible to staff');
  renderDeptExecCards();
});

saveTranscript = async function () {
  const dept = document.getElementById("txDept").value;
  const date = document.getElementById("txDate").value;
  const title = document.getElementById("txTitle").value.trim();
  const src = document.getElementById("txSource").value.trim();
  const body = document.getElementById("txBody").value.trim();
  if (!title || !body) { toast("Add a title and transcript text"); return; }
  const bo = document.getElementById("txBoardOnly");
  const boardOnly = !!(bo && bo.checked);
  const row = { department: dept, meeting_date: date || null, title: title, body: body, source_url: src || null, created_at: new Date().toISOString(), added_by: who(), board_only: boardOnly };
  const r = await db.from("transcripts").insert(row).select("id").single();
  if (r.error) { toast("Save failed, check connection"); return; }
  if (r.data) PENDING.add(r.data.id);
  ["txTitle", "txBody", "txDate", "txSource"].forEach(x => document.getElementById(x).value = "");
  if (bo) bo.checked = false;
  toast(boardOnly ? "Saved as board only. Everything derived from it stays hidden from staff" : "Saved. The Hub is reading it now; the department page updates in a few minutes");
  await loadTranscripts();
};

(function () {
  var __sa8 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa8();
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('is_board_circle');
      WA_IS_CIRCLE = r && r.data === true;
      if (WA_IS_CIRCLE) {
        var deptSel = document.getElementById('txDept');
        if (deptSel && !document.getElementById('txBoardOnly')) {
          var holder = deptSel.parentElement;
          var d = document.createElement('div');
          d.innerHTML = '<label style="font-size:12.5px;color:#a87f2c;font-weight:700;"><input type="checkbox" id="txBoardOnly" style="margin-right:6px;vertical-align:middle;" />Board only (hidden from all staff, visible to the board circle)</label>';
          d.style.marginTop = '8px';
          holder.appendChild(d);
        }
        renderDeptExecCards();
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();

var WA_ROSTER = [], WA_ME = null, WA_MY_TEAM = [];
function waFirstTok(s) { return (s || '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0].toLowerCase(); }
function waRolePrefix(role) {
  var r = (role || '').toLowerCase();
  if (r.indexOf('program development') === 0) return 'pd';
  if (r.indexOf('operations') === 0) return 'ops';
  return null;
}
async function waResolveMe() {
  if (WA_ME) return;
  try {
    var r = await db.from('team_members').select('name, email, department, role').eq('active', true);
    WA_ROSTER = (r.data || []);
  } catch (e) { WA_ROSTER = []; }
  var em = (USER.email || '').toLowerCase();
  WA_ME = WA_ROSTER.find(function (m) { return (m.email || '').toLowerCase() === em && em; }) || null;
  if (!WA_ME) {
    var ft = waFirstTok(USER.name || em.split('@')[0].split('.')[0]);
    WA_ME = WA_ROSTER.find(function (m) { return waFirstTok(m.name) === ft; }) || null;
  }
  WA_MY_TEAM = [];
  if (!WA_ME) return;
  var leadRe = /\b(lead|leader|director|manager|chair|co-)/i;
  var isExec = WA_ME.department === 'board';
  var isLead = isExec || leadRe.test(WA_ME.role || '');
  if (isExec) {
    WA_MY_TEAM = WA_ROSTER.filter(function (m) { return m !== WA_ME && leadRe.test(m.role || ''); });
  } else if (isLead) {
    var mine = WA_ROSTER.filter(function (m) { return m !== WA_ME && m.department === WA_ME.department; });
    var myPfx = WA_ME.department === 'opsmtl' ? waRolePrefix(WA_ME.role) : null;
    WA_MY_TEAM = myPfx ? mine.filter(function (m) { return waRolePrefix(m.role) === myPfx; }) : mine;
  }
}
function waTasksOf(name) {
  var ft = waFirstTok(name);
  return (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (t) {
    return !t.done && (t.owner || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(ft) >= 0;
  });
}
function waDueBadge(t) {
  if (!t.due_date) return '<span class="mp-due">no date</span>';
  var today = new Date(); today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  var due = new Date(t.due_date + 'T00:00:00');
  var diff = Math.round((due - today) / 86400000);
  if (diff < 0) return '<span class="mp-due over">' + (-diff) + 'd overdue</span>';
  if (diff === 0) return '<span class="mp-due soon">due today</span>';
  if (diff <= 7) return '<span class="mp-due soon">due in ' + diff + 'd</span>';
  return '<span class="mp-due">due ' + esc(t.due_date) + '</span>';
}
function waTaskLine(t) {
  return '<div class="mp-task">' + waDueBadge(t) + '<span>' + esc(t.title) + '</span>'
    + '<span style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:11px;margin-left:auto;">' + (DEPT_LABEL[t.department] || '') + '</span></div>';
}

async function renderMyPage() { /* superseded: wave74 rebuilt My page and replaced this binding outright; original body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }

(function () {
  var __ah9 = applyHash;
  applyHash = function () {
    var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
    if (h === 'mypage') { showPanel('mypage'); renderMyPage(); return; }
    if ((!h || h.indexOf('access_token=') >= 0) && isStaff()) { showPanel('mypage'); renderMyPage(); return; }
    __ah9();
  };
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () {
    if ((location.hash || '') === '#mypage') {
      try { showPanel('mypage'); renderMyPage(); } catch (e) { /* boot */ }
    }
  });
  var __sa9 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa9();
    try {
      if (isStaff()) {
        var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
        if (bar && !bar.querySelector('[data-tab="mypage"]')) {
          var b = document.createElement('button');
          b.className = 'tab';
          b.dataset.tab = 'mypage';
          b.innerHTML = '<span class="tabdot" style="background:#103F39"></span>My page';
          bar.insertBefore(b, bar.firstChild);
        }
        if (!location.hash || location.hash === '#' ) { showPanel('mypage'); renderMyPage(); }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();

   WA v10 UI enhancements (25/07/26)
   Fundraising and Grants page: view toggle (Everything / Fundraising /
   Grants). Classification: subsection keywords first (grant vs event,
   donor, sponsor, fundrais), then people: Grants = Suhaib, Melvina,
   Shima; Fundraising = Alexis, Danielle, Rami, Amanda, Jemeela;
   Daniela and anything ambiguous shows in both views.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  var css = ''
    + '.fr-toggle{display:flex;gap:6px;margin:0 0 12px;}'
    + '.fr-toggle button{padding:7px 15px;border:1px solid var(--wa-line);background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:18px;cursor:pointer;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);}'
    + '.fr-toggle button.on{background:#c87a2f;color:#FFFAF2;border-color:#c87a2f;}';

  st.textContent = css;
  document.head.appendChild(st);

var WA_FR_VIEW = 'all';
function waFrGroup(t) {
  var s = (t.subsection || '').toLowerCase(), o = (t.owner || '').toLowerCase();
  if (s.indexOf('grant') >= 0) return 'grants';
  if (s.indexOf('event') >= 0 || s.indexOf('donor') >= 0 || s.indexOf('sponsor') >= 0 || s.indexOf('fundrais') >= 0) return 'fund';
  var G = ['suhaib', 'melvina', 'shima'];
  var F = ['alexis', 'danielle', 'rami', 'amanda', 'jemeela'];
  var g = G.some(function (n) { return o.indexOf(n) >= 0; });
  var f = F.some(function (n) { return o.indexOf(n) >= 0; });
  if (g && !f) return 'grants';
  if (f && !g) return 'fund';
  return 'both';
}
function waApplyFrFilter() {
  var panel = document.getElementById('fundraising');
  if (!panel) return;
  var bar = panel.querySelector('.fr-toggle');
  if (!bar) {
    bar = document.createElement('div');
    bar.className = 'fr-toggle';
    bar.innerHTML = '<button data-frv="all">Everything</button><button data-frv="fund">Fundraising</button><button data-frv="grants">Grants</button>';
    panel.insertBefore(bar, panel.firstChild);
    bar.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', function () { WA_FR_VIEW = b.dataset.frv; waApplyFrFilter(); });
    });
  }
  bar.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.frv === WA_FR_VIEW); });
  panel.querySelectorAll('li[data-task]').forEach(function (li) {
    var t = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).find(function (x) { return x.id === Number(li.dataset.task); });
    if (!t || t.department !== 'fundraising') return;
    var g = waFrGroup(t);
    li.style.display = (WA_FR_VIEW === 'all' || g === 'both' || g === WA_FR_VIEW) ? '' : 'none';
  });
}
(function () {
  var __ras10 = renderAllSections;
  renderAllSections = function () {
    __ras10();
    try { waApplyFrFilter(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
  var __rs10 = renderSection;
  renderSection = function (deptId) {
    __rs10(deptId);
    try { if (deptId === 'fundraising') waApplyFrFilter(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();

    main.appendChild(sec);

  modal.id = 'ocModal';
  modal.innerHTML = '<div class="box"><button class="x" id="ocClose">&#10005;</button><div id="ocProfile"></div></div>';

var WA_BOARD_LABELS = null;
function ocPerson(m) {
  return '<span class="oc-p" data-ocp="' + esc(m.name) + '">' + esc(m.name) + '<small>' + esc(m.role || 'Team member') + '</small></span>';
}
async function renderOrgChart() {
  var body = document.getElementById('ocBody');
  if (!body) return;
  if (!isStaff()) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">The org chart is available to staff accounts.</p>'; return; }
  await waResolveMe();
  if (WA_BOARD_LABELS === null) {
    try { var b = await db.from('board_circle').select('email, label'); WA_BOARD_LABELS = b.data || []; } catch (e) { WA_BOARD_LABELS = []; }
  }
  var leadRe = /\b(lead|leader|director|manager|chair|co-)/i;
  var execs = WA_ROSTER.filter(function (m) { return m.department === 'board' && /steel|suhaib/i.test(m.name); });
  var boardOthers = WA_ROSTER.filter(function (m) { return m.department === 'board' && !/steel|suhaib/i.test(m.name); });
  var html = '<div class="oc-level">Board of directors</div><div class="oc-row"><div class="oc-card" style="border-top-color:#44403c;max-width:none;">'
    + '<div class="oc-row">' + boardOthers.map(ocPerson).join('') + '</div></div></div>';
  html += '<div class="oc-level">Co executive directors</div><div class="oc-row"><div class="oc-card" style="border-top-color:#6F64AB;max-width:none;"><div class="oc-row">'
    + execs.map(ocPerson).join('') + '</div></div></div>';
  html += '<div class="oc-level">Teams</div><div class="oc-row">';
  DEPT_ORDER.filter(function (d) { return d !== 'board'; }).forEach(function (d) {
    var members = WA_ROSTER.filter(function (m) { return m.department === d; });
    var groups = [];
    if (d === 'opsmtl') {
      groups.push({ label: 'Program development', list: members.filter(function (m) { return waRolePrefix(m.role) === 'pd'; }) });
      groups.push({ label: 'Operations', list: members.filter(function (m) { return waRolePrefix(m.role) !== 'pd'; }) });
    } else {
      groups.push({ label: null, list: members });
    }
    groups.forEach(function (g) {
      if (!g.list.length) return;
      var leads = g.list.filter(function (m) { return leadRe.test(m.role || ''); });
      var rest = g.list.filter(function (m) { return leads.indexOf(m) < 0; });
      html += '<div class="oc-card" style="border-top-color:' + (DEPT_COLOR[d] || '#797773') + '">'
        + '<h4 style="color:' + (DEPT_COLOR[d] || '#797773') + '">' + (DEPT_LABEL[d] || d) + (g.label ? ': ' + g.label : '') + '</h4>'
        + (leads.length ? leads.map(ocPerson).join('') : '')
        + (rest.length ? '<button class="oc-more" data-ocmore="1">Show team (' + rest.length + ')</button><div class="oc-team">' + rest.map(ocPerson).join('') + '</div>' : '')
        + '</div>';
    });
  });
  html += '</div>';
  body.innerHTML = html;
  body.querySelectorAll('[data-ocmore]').forEach(function (btn) {
    btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
      var card = btn.closest('.oc-card');
      card.classList.toggle('open');
      btn.textContent = card.classList.contains('open') ? 'Hide team' : btn.textContent.replace('Hide team', 'Show team');
    });
  });
  body.querySelectorAll('[data-ocp]').forEach(function (p) {
    p.addEventListener('click', function () { waOpenProfile(p.dataset.ocp); });
  });
}
async function waOpenProfile(name) {
  var m = WA_ROSTER.find(function (x) { return x.name === name; });
  if (!m) return;
  var modal = document.getElementById('ocModal');
  var box = document.getElementById('ocProfile');
  var tasks = waTasksOf(m.name);
  var boardRole = (WA_BOARD_LABELS || []).find(function (b) { return (b.label || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(waFirstTok(m.name)) >= 0; });
  var ft = waFirstTok(m.name);
  var mentions = (typeof DB_UPDATES !== 'undefined' ? DB_UPDATES : []).filter(function (u) {
    return (u.text || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(ft) >= 0;
  }).sort(function (a, b) { return new Date(b.created_at) - new Date(a.created_at); }).slice(0, 3);
  box.innerHTML = '<h3>' + esc(m.name) + '</h3>'
    + '<div class="sub">' + esc(m.role || 'Team member') + ' &middot; <span class="ag-dep" style="background:' + (DEPT_COLOR[m.department] || '#797773') + '">' + (DEPT_LABEL[m.department] || m.department || '') + '</span>'
    + (boardRole ? '<br/>Board: ' + esc(boardRole.label) : '')
    + (m.email ? '<br/><a href="mailto:' + esc(m.email) + '">' + esc(m.email) + '</a>' : '') + '</div>'
    + '<h5>Open tasks (' + tasks.length + ')</h5>'
    + (tasks.length ? tasks.slice(0, 6).map(waTaskLine).join('') : '<p class="mp-empty">Nothing open right now.</p>')
    + '<h5>Recent mentions</h5>'
    + (mentions.length ? mentions.map(function (u) { return '<div class="mp-upd">' + esc((u.text || '').slice(0, 140)) + ' <span class="when">' + fmtDay(u.created_at) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') : '<p class="mp-empty">No recent update mentions.</p>')
    + '<h5>Tracked hours (last 3 weeks)</h5><div id="ocHours" class="mp-empty">Loading...</div>';
  modal.style.display = 'flex';
  try {
    var since = new Date(Date.now() - 21 * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    var hr = await db.from('slack_hours').select('week_start, hours, sessions').eq('member_name', m.name).gte('week_start', since).order('week_start');
    var slot = document.getElementById('ocHours');
    if (!slot) return;
    var rows = hr.data || [];
    slot.innerHTML = rows.length
      ? rows.map(function (x) { return x.week_start + ': <b>' + Number(x.hours || 0).toFixed(1) + 'h</b> (' + x.sessions + ' sessions)'; }).join('<br/>')
      : 'No tracked check-ins in the last 3 weeks.';
  } catch (e) { /* optional */ }
}
(function () {
  var __ah11 = applyHash;
  applyHash = function () {
    var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
    if (h === 'orgchart') { showPanel('orgchart'); renderOrgChart(); return; }
    __ah11();
  };
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () {
    if ((location.hash || '') === '#orgchart') {
      try { showPanel('orgchart'); renderOrgChart(); } catch (e) { /* boot */ }
    }
