var WA_PRESENCE = [];
async function waHeartbeat() {
  try {
    if (USER.email) {
      await db.from('user_state').upsert({ email: USER.email.toLowerCase(), last_seen_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() });
    }
  } catch (e) { /* presence is best effort */ }
}
async function waLoadPresence() {
  try {
    var r = await db.from('user_state').select('email, last_seen_at');
    WA_PRESENCE = r.data || [];
  } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
}
function waNameForEmail(email) {
  var m = (WA_ROSTER || []).find(function (x) { return (x.email || '').toLowerCase() === (email || '').toLowerCase(); });
  if (m) return m.name;
  var p = (email || '').split('@')[0].split('.')[0];
  return p ? p.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + p.slice(1) : email;
}
function waOnlineEmails() {
  var cut = Date.now() - 10 * 60000;
  return WA_PRESENCE.filter(function (p) { return p.last_seen_at && new Date(p.last_seen_at).getTime() > cut; });
}

(function () {
  /* dead wrap removed in Phase 0 compaction: wave74 replaced renderMyPage outright, so the presence line is re-implemented there */
  var __roc12 = renderOrgChart;
  renderOrgChart = async function () {
    await __roc12();
    try {
      await waLoadPresence();
      var onNames = waOnlineEmails().map(function (p) { return waNameForEmail(p.email).toLowerCase(); });
      document.querySelectorAll('#ocBody [data-ocp]').forEach(function (el) {
        var n = (el.dataset.ocp || '').toLowerCase();
        if (onNames.some(function (o) { return n.indexOf(o.split(' ')[0]) === 0 || o.indexOf(n.split(' ')[0]) === 0; })) {
          if (!el.querySelector('.wa-on')) el.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<span class="wa-on" title="Online now"></span>');
        }
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
  var __sa12 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa12();
    try {
      if (isStaff()) {
        waHeartbeat();
        setInterval(waHeartbeat, 180000);
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();

function waOrganizeNav() {
  var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
  if (!bar || bar.dataset.waOrg) return;
  bar.dataset.waOrg = '1';
  function grab(id) { return bar.querySelector('[data-tab="' + id + '"]'); }
  function mkGroup(label, ids) {
    var g = document.createElement('span');
    g.className = 'tab-group';
    var h = document.createElement('button');
    h.className = 'tab-group-h';
    h.innerHTML = label + ' <span class="caret">&#9660;</span>';
    var p = document.createElement('div');
    p.className = 'tab-group-p';
    ids.forEach(function (id) { var b = grab(id); if (b) p.appendChild(b); });
    g.appendChild(h); g.appendChild(p);
    h.addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
      ev.stopPropagation();
      var was = g.classList.contains('open');
      bar.querySelectorAll('.tab-group.open').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('open'); });
      if (!was) g.classList.add('open');
    });
    return p.children.length ? g : null;
  }
  var order = [];
  ['mypage', 'overview', 'dashboard'].forEach(function (id) { var b = grab(id); if (b) order.push(b); });
  var gD = mkGroup('Teams', ['opsmtl', 'opsto', 'marketing', 'fundraising', 'hr', 'board']);
  var gW = mkGroup('Workspace', ['agendas', 'requests', 'importantinfo', 'orgchart']);
  var gR = mkGroup('Records', ['transcripts', 'activity']);
  [gD, gW, gR].forEach(function (g) { if (g) order.push(g); });
  order.forEach(function (el) { bar.appendChild(el); });
  bar.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    if (e.target.closest('.tab')) {
      bar.querySelectorAll('.tab-group.open').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('open'); });
      document.body.classList.remove('wa-mob-open');
      var t = document.getElementById('waNavToggle');
      if (t) t.innerHTML = '&#9776; Menu';
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    if (!bar.contains(e.target)) bar.querySelectorAll('.tab-group.open').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('open'); });
  });
  var wrap = bar.parentElement || bar;
  if (!document.getElementById('waNavToggle')) {
    var t = document.createElement('button');
    t.id = 'waNavToggle';
    t.innerHTML = '&#9776; Menu';
    t.addEventListener('click', function () {
      var open = document.body.classList.toggle('wa-mob-open');
      t.innerHTML = open ? '&#10005; Close' : '&#9776; Menu';
    });
    wrap.insertBefore(t, bar);
  }
}

(function () {
  var __sp13 = showPanel;
  showPanel = function (id) {
    __sp13(id);
    try {
      document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .tab-group').forEach(function (g) {
        var h = g.querySelector('.tab-group-h');
        if (h) h.classList.toggle('active', !!g.querySelector('.tab.active'));
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
  var __sa13 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa13();
    try { if (isStaff()) waOrganizeNav(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();


/* =====================================================================
   WA v14 UI enhancements (25/07/26)
   Voice to text in the chat assistant via the browser's built in
   SpeechRecognition (Chrome, Edge, Safari incl. iOS; not Firefox).
   Tap the mic, speak, words stream into the input, tap again or pause
   to stop. Language follows the device locale, so French dictation
   works for francophone team members.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.textContent = ''
    + '#wacMic{background:var(--wa-paper);color:var(--wa-text);border:1px solid var(--wa-line);border-radius:9px;padding:8px 11px;cursor:pointer;font-size:14px;}'
    + '#wacMic.rec{background:#b3402f;color:#FFFAF2;border-color:#b3402f;animation:waPulse 1.2s infinite;}'
    + '@keyframes waPulse{0%{box-shadow:0 0 0 0 rgba(179,64,47,.5);}70%{box-shadow:0 0 0 9px rgba(179,64,47,0);}100%{box-shadow:0 0 0 0 rgba(179,64,47,0);}}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);
})();

function waWireMic() {
  var mic = document.getElementById('wacMic');
  var inp = document.getElementById('wacInput');
  if (!mic || !inp || mic.dataset.wired) return;
  mic.dataset.wired = '1';
  var SR = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
  if (!SR) {
    mic.style.opacity = '.4';
    mic.title = 'Voice input is not supported in this browser';
    mic.addEventListener('click', function () { toast('Voice input is not supported in this browser, try Chrome or Safari'); });
    return;
  }
  var rec = null, active = false, base = '';
  mic.addEventListener('click', function () {
    if (active) { try { rec.stop(); } catch (e) { /* stopping */ } return; }
    rec = new SR();
    rec.lang = navigator.language || 'en-CA';
    rec.interimResults = true;
    rec.continuous = false;
    base = inp.value ? inp.value + ' ' : '';
    rec.onresult = function (ev) {
      var txt = '';
      for (var i = 0; i < ev.results.length; i++) { txt += ev.results[i][0].transcript; }
      inp.value = base + txt;
    };
    rec.onend = function () { active = false; mic.classList.remove('rec'); inp.focus(); };
    rec.onerror = function (ev) {
      active = false; mic.classList.remove('rec');
      if (ev.error === 'not-allowed') toast('Microphone permission was blocked, allow it for this site in the browser');
    };
    try { rec.start(); active = true; mic.classList.add('rec'); } catch (e) { toast('Could not start the microphone'); }
  });
}

(function () {
  var __sa14 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa14();
    try { if (isStaff()) waWireMic(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();

var GL_COMMENTS = [], WA_GL_OPEN = {};
var GL_STATUSES = ['not started', 'in progress', 'in review', 'on track', 'at risk', 'delayed', 'incomplete', 'done'];

var __wlg16 = waLoadGoals;
waLoadGoals = async function () {
  await __wlg16();
  try {
    var ids = GOALS_CACHE.map(function (g) { return g.id; });
    if (ids.length) {
      var c = await db.from('goal_comments').select('*').in('goal_id', ids).order('created_at');
      if (!c.error) GL_COMMENTS = c.data || [];
    } else GL_COMMENTS = [];
  } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
};

waGoalRow = function (g) {
  var linked = (g.task_ids || []).map(waTaskById).filter(Boolean);
  var doneN = linked.filter(function (t) { return t.done; }).length;
  var pct = linked.length ? Math.round(doneN / linked.length * 100)
    : ((g.progress != null && !isNaN(Number(g.progress))) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(g.progress))) : null);
  var cmts = GL_COMMENTS.filter(function (c) { return c.goal_id === g.id; });
  var opts = GL_STATUSES.slice();
  if (g.status && opts.indexOf(g.status) < 0) opts.unshift(g.status);
  var open = WA_GL_OPEN[g.id] || {};
  return '<div class="gl-row" data-gl="' + g.id + '">'
    + '<span class="t">' + esc(g.title) + '</span>'
    + (isStaff()
        ? ' <select data-glst="' + g.id + '">' + opts.map(function (s) { return '<option' + (s === g.status ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(s) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
        : (g.status ? '<span class="gl-st">' + esc(g.status) + '</span>' : ''))
    + (g.measure || g.due_date ? '<div class="m">' + esc(g.measure || '') + (g.due_date ? (g.measure ? ' &middot; ' : '') + 'due ' + esc(g.due_date) : '') + '</div>' : '')
    + (g.detail ? '<div class="m">' + esc(g.detail) + '</div>' : '')
    + linked.map(function (t) {
        return '<div class="ag-task' + (t.done ? ' done' : '') + '"><input type="checkbox" data-gltgl="' + t.id + '" data-gldept="' + esc(t.department || '') + '"' + (t.done ? ' checked' : '') + (isStaff() ? '' : ' disabled') + ' /><span>' + esc(t.title) + '</span><span style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:11px;">' + esc(t.owner || '') + '</span></div>';
      }).join('')
    + (linked.length ? '<div class="m">' + doneN + ' of ' + linked.length + ' linked tasks done</div>' : '')
    + (pct != null ? '<div class="gl-bar"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div>' : '')
    + '<div class="gl-ctl">'
    + '<button data-glcmt="' + g.id + '">&#128172; Comments (' + cmts.length + ')</button>'
    + (isStaff() ? '<button data-gllink="' + g.id + '">&#128279; Link tasks</button>' : '')
    + '</div>'
    + '<div class="gl-sub gl-cmts' + (open.c ? ' open' : '') + '" data-glcbox="' + g.id + '">'
    + (cmts.length ? cmts.map(function (c) {
        var mine = isStaff() && waFirstTok(c.author || '') === waFirstTok(who() || '');
        return '<div class="gl-cmt" data-glc="' + c.id + '">'
          + (mine ? '<span class="ops"><button data-glce="' + c.id + '">edit</button><button data-glcx="' + c.id + '">delete</button></span>' : '')
          + '<span class="who">' + esc(c.author || 'someone') + '</span><span class="when">' + fmtDay(c.created_at) + (c.updated_at ? ' (edited)' : '') + '</span>'
          + '<div data-glcb="' + c.id + '">' + (window.w138Mark ? window.w138Mark(esc(c.body)) : esc(c.body)) + '</div></div>';
      }).join('') : '<div class="mp-empty">No comments yet.</div>')
    + (isStaff() ? '<div class="gl-addc"><input data-glci="' + g.id + '" placeholder="Add a comment" /><button data-glca="' + g.id + '">Post</button></div>' : '')
    + '</div>'
    + '<div class="gl-sub gl-pick' + (open.l ? ' open' : '') + '" data-gllbox="' + g.id + '">'
    + (function () {
        if (!isStaff()) return '';
        var cands = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (t) { return t.department === g.department; })
          .sort(function (a, b) { return (a.done === b.done) ? 0 : (a.done ? 1 : -1); }).slice(0, 30);
        if (!cands.length) return '<div class="mp-empty">No tasks in this department to link.</div>';
        return cands.map(function (t) {
          var on = (g.task_ids || []).indexOf(t.id) >= 0;
          return '<label><input type="checkbox" data-glpick="' + g.id + ':' + t.id + '"' + (on ? ' checked' : '') + ' /><span class="' + (t.done ? 'done-t' : '') + '">' + esc(t.title) + '</span></label>';
        }).join('');
      })()
    + '</div></div>';
};

  try { renderDeptGoalsCards(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
}
function waGlFind(id) { return GOALS_CACHE.find(function (g) { return g.id === id; }); }

document.addEventListener('change', async function (e) {
  var st = e.target.closest('[data-glst]');
  if (st) {
    var gid = Number(st.dataset.glst), g = waGlFind(gid);
    var r = await db.from('goals').update({ status: st.value, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: who() }).eq('id', gid);
    if (r.error) { toast('Could not update status'); return; }
    if (g) g.status = st.value;
    try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'goal', entity_id: String(gid), field: 'status', old_value: null, new_value: st.value, changed_by: who() }); } catch (x) { /* audit */ }
    try {
      db.functions.invoke('goal-sync', { body: { action: 'status', goal_id: gid, status: st.value } }).then(function (s) {
        if (s.error || !(s.data && s.data.ok)) toast('Saved here, but the ClickUp sync did not go through');
      });
    } catch (x) { /* sync best effort */ }
    toast('Goal status: ' + st.value + ', syncing to ClickUp');
    waGlRerender();
    return;
  }
  var tgl = e.target.closest('[data-gltgl]');
  if (tgl) {
    var tid = Number(tgl.dataset.gltgl);
    var ok = await toggleTask(tid, tgl.checked);
    if (!ok) { tgl.checked = !tgl.checked; toast('Could not update the task'); return; }
    var row = waTaskById(tid);
    if (row) row.done = tgl.checked;
    if (tgl.dataset.gldept) { try { renderSection(tgl.dataset.gldept); renderDashboard(); renderNeedsMe(); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } }
    waGlRerender();
    return;
  }
  var pk = e.target.closest('[data-glpick]');
  if (pk) {
    var parts = pk.dataset.glpick.split(':');
    var gid2 = Number(parts[0]), tid2 = Number(parts[1]);
    var g2 = waGlFind(gid2);
    if (!g2) return;
    var arr = (g2.task_ids || []).slice();
    if (pk.checked && arr.indexOf(tid2) < 0) arr.push(tid2);
    if (!pk.checked) arr = arr.filter(function (x) { return x !== tid2; });
    var r2 = await db.from('goals').update({ task_ids: arr, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: who() }).eq('id', gid2);
    if (r2.error) { toast('Could not update links'); pk.checked = !pk.checked; return; }
    g2.task_ids = arr;
    waGlRerender();
  }
});

document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
  var b;
  if ((b = e.target.closest('[data-glcmt]'))) {
    var id = Number(b.dataset.glcmt);
    WA_GL_OPEN[id] = WA_GL_OPEN[id] || {};
    WA_GL_OPEN[id].c = !WA_GL_OPEN[id].c;
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-glcbox="' + id + '"]').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.toggle('open', WA_GL_OPEN[id].c); });
    return;
  }
  if ((b = e.target.closest('[data-gllink]'))) {
    var id2 = Number(b.dataset.gllink);
    WA_GL_OPEN[id2] = WA_GL_OPEN[id2] || {};
    WA_GL_OPEN[id2].l = !WA_GL_OPEN[id2].l;
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-gllbox="' + id2 + '"]').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.toggle('open', WA_GL_OPEN[id2].l); });
    return;
  }
  if ((b = e.target.closest('[data-glca]'))) {
    var gid = Number(b.dataset.glca);
    var inp = b.parentElement.querySelector('[data-glci]');
    var body = (inp.value || '').trim();
    if (!body) { toast('Write a comment first'); return; }
    var r = await db.from('goal_comments').insert({ goal_id: gid, body: body, author: who() }).select('id').single();
    if (r.error) { toast('Could not post'); return; }
    try { db.functions.invoke('goal-sync', { body: { action: 'comment', goal_id: gid, comment_id: r.data ? r.data.id : null, body: body, author: who() } }); } catch (x) { /* sync best effort */ }
    await waLoadGoals();
    waGlRerender();
    return;
  }
  if ((b = e.target.closest('[data-glce]'))) {
    var cid = Number(b.dataset.glce);
    var c = GL_COMMENTS.find(function (x) { return x.id === cid; });
    var now = prompt('Edit your comment:', c ? c.body : '');
    if (now === null || !now.trim()) return;
    var r2 = await db.from('goal_comments').update({ body: now.trim(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', cid);
    if (r2.error) { toast('Could not update'); return; }
    try { db.functions.invoke('goal-sync', { body: { action: 'comment_edit', comment_id: cid, body: now.trim() } }); } catch (x) { /* sync best effort */ }
    await waLoadGoals();
    waGlRerender();
    return;
  }
  if ((b = e.target.closest('[data-glcx]'))) {
    if (!confirm('Delete this comment?')) return;
    var cdid = Number(b.dataset.glcx);
    var crow = GL_COMMENTS.find(function (x) { return x.id === cdid; });
    await db.from('goal_comments').delete().eq('id', cdid);
    try { db.functions.invoke('goal-sync', { body: { action: 'comment_delete', comment_id: cdid, clickup_comment_id: crow ? crow.clickup_comment_id : null } }); } catch (x) { /* sync best effort */ }
    await waLoadGoals();
    waGlRerender();
  }
});

var WA_CD_CUR = null;
var WA_CD_TYPE_LABEL = { email: 'Email', social_post: 'Social post', graphic: 'Social graphic', report: 'Report', newsletter: 'Newsletter', announcement: 'Announcement', letter: 'Letter', other: 'Content' };

function waMdInline(s) {
  return s.replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>').replace(/\*([^*]+)\*/g, '<em>$1</em>');
}
function waMd(md) {
  var lines = String(md || '').split(/\r?\n/);
  var out = [], para = [], list = null;
  function flushPara() { if (para.length) { out.push('<p>' + waMdInline(para.join(' ')) + '</p>'); para = []; } }
  function flushList() { if (list) { out.push('<' + list.tag + '>' + list.items.map(function (i) { return '<li>' + waMdInline(i) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</' + list.tag + '>'); list = null; } }
  lines.forEach(function (raw) {
    var line = esc(raw.trim());
    if (!line) { flushPara(); flushList(); return; }
    var h = line.match(/^(#{1,3})\s+(.*)$/);
    if (h) { flushPara(); flushList(); out.push('<h' + (h[1].length === 1 ? 2 : h[1].length) + '>' + waMdInline(h[2]) + '</h' + (h[1].length === 1 ? 2 : h[1].length) + '>'); return; }
    var ul = line.match(/^[-*]\s+(.*)$/);
    if (ul) { flushPara(); if (!list || list.tag !== 'ul') { flushList(); list = { tag: 'ul', items: [] }; } list.items.push(ul[1]); return; }
    var ol = line.match(/^\d+[.)]\s+(.*)$/);
    if (ol) { flushPara(); if (!list || list.tag !== 'ol') { flushList(); list = { tag: 'ol', items: [] }; } list.items.push(ol[1]); return; }
    flushList(); para.push(line);
  });
  flushPara(); flushList();
  return out.join('');
}

var WA_GFX_CSS = ''
  + '.gfx-card{background:#103F39;color:#F4F2EA;aspect-ratio:1/1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center;align-items:center;text-align:center;padding:7%;box-sizing:border-box;border-radius:14px;font-family:Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;}'
  + '.gfx-brand{font-weight:900;letter-spacing:4px;font-size:13px;}'
  + '.gfx-rule{width:56px;height:5px;background:#6F64AB;border-radius:3px;margin:14px 0 22px;}'
  + '.gfx-head{font-weight:900;text-transform:uppercase;font-size:clamp(20px,5.5vmin,36px);line-height:1.12;letter-spacing:.5px;margin-bottom:16px;}'
  + '.gfx-line{font-size:clamp(12px,2.4vmin,17px);line-height:1.5;opacity:.94;}'
  + '.gfx-cta{margin-top:22px;background:#6F64AB;color:#FFFAF2;font-weight:800;padding:10px 22px;border-radius:999px;font-size:clamp(12px,2.2vmin,15px);}'
  + '.gfx-foot{margin-top:26px;font-size:11px;opacity:.8;}'
  + '.gfx-foot .tag{font-style:italic;}';
function waCdGfxInner(d) {
  var lines = String(d.body_md || '').split(/\r?\n/).map(function (l) { return l.trim(); }).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3);
  return '<div class="gfx-brand">WELFARE AVENUE</div>'
    + '<div class="gfx-rule"></div>'
    + '<div class="gfx-head">' + esc(d.subject_line || d.title || '') + '</div>'
    + lines.map(function (l) { return '<div class="gfx-line">' + esc(l) + '</div>'; }).join('')
    + (d.short_version ? '<div class="gfx-cta">' + esc(d.short_version) + '</div>' : '')
    + '<div class="gfx-foot"><span class="tag">Service is solidarity, not charity.</span><br>welfareavenue.com</div>';
}
function waCdGraphicHtml(d) { /* superseded: wave18 reset without capture; superseded; body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }
function waCdGfxPreview(d) { /* superseded: wave18 reset without capture; superseded; body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }
function waCdBrandHtml(d) {
  if (d.content_type === 'graphic') return waCdGraphicHtml(d);
  var bodyEnd = '</bo' + 'dy>', htmlEnd = '</ht' + 'ml>';
  return '<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">'
    + '<title>' + esc(d.title || 'Welfare Avenue') + '</title>'
    + '<style>body{margin:0;background:#F4F2EA;font-family:Georgia,\'Times New Roman\',serif;color:#1F201E;}'
    + '.wrap{max-width:680px;margin:0 auto;padding:28px 20px;}'
    + '.head{background:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2;padding:22px 26px;border-radius:12px 12px 0 0;}'
    + '.head .b{font-family:Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:3px;font-size:15px;text-transform:uppercase;}'
    + '.head .t{font-size:12px;opacity:.85;margin-top:4px;font-style:italic;}'
    + '.paper{background:#FFFAF2;padding:26px 30px;border-radius:0 0 12px 12px;font-size:15px;line-height:1.65;}'
    + '.paper h2,.paper h3{font-family:Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;color:#103F39;}'
    + '.subj{font-family:Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;font-weight:800;font-size:17px;margin-bottom:14px;border-bottom:3px solid #6F64AB;padding-bottom:8px;}'
    + '.foot{text-align:center;font-family:Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif;font-size:11px;color:#797773;padding:16px 0;}'
    + '@media print{body{background:#FFFAF2;}.wrap{padding:0;}}'
    + '</style></head><body><div class="wrap">'
    + '<div class="head"><div class="b">Welfare Avenue</div><div class="t">Service is solidarity, not charity.</div></div>'
    + '<div class="paper">'
    + (d.subject_line ? '<div class="subj">' + esc(d.subject_line) + '</div>' : '')
    + waMd(d.body_md)
    + '</div>'
    + '<div class="foot">welfareavenue.com</div>'
    + '</div>' + bodyEnd + htmlEnd;
}
function waCdSlug(d) {
  return ((d.title || 'wa-draft').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '').slice(0, 50) || 'wa-draft');
}
function waCdDownload(name, text, mime) {
  var a = document.createElement('a');
  var blob = new Blob([text], { type: mime });
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download = name;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  setTimeout(function () { URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href); a.remove(); }, 400);
}

function waCdRenderPreview() { /* superseded: wave18 reset without capture; superseded; body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }

async function waCdLoadList() {
  var el = document.getElementById('cdList');
  if (!el) return;
  try {
    var r = await db.from('content_drafts').select('id,created_at,created_by,content_type,audience,title').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(12);
    if (r.error) { el.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Could not load drafts.</p>'; return; }
    var rows = r.data || [];
    el.innerHTML = rows.length ? rows.map(function (d) {
      return '<div class="cd-item" data-cdopen="' + d.id + '"><span class="t">' + esc(d.title || 'Untitled') + '</span><div class="m">' + esc(WA_CD_TYPE_LABEL[d.content_type] || d.content_type) + ' &middot; ' + esc((d.audience || '').replace(/_/g, ' ')) + ' &middot; ' + esc(d.created_by || '') + ' &middot; ' + fmtDay(d.created_at) + '</div></div>';
    }).join('') : '<p class="read">Nothing drafted yet. Yours can be the first.</p>';
  } catch (e) { el.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Could not load drafts.</p>'; }
}

function waRenderCreate() {
  var wrap = document.getElementById('cdWrap');
  if (!wrap) return;
  if (!isStaff()) { wrap.innerHTML = '<p class="read">The Create studio is available to staff accounts.</p>'; return; }
  var ds = document.getElementById('cdDept');
  if (ds && ds.options.length < 2 && typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined') {
    DEPT_ORDER.forEach(function (d) {
      var o = document.createElement('option');
      o.value = d;
      o.textContent = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d;
      ds.appendChild(o);
    });
  }
  waCdLoadList();
}

async function waCdGenerate() { /* superseded: wave18 reset without capture; superseded; body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }

async function waCdRefine() {
  if (!WA_CD_CUR) return;
  var inp = document.getElementById('cdRefIn');
  var ask = (inp.value || '').trim();
  if (!ask) { toast('Say what to change first'); return; }
  var go = document.getElementById('cdRefGo');
  go.disabled = true; go.textContent = '...';
  try {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('draft-content', {
      body: {
        draft_id: WA_CD_CUR.id, revision: ask,
        content_type: WA_CD_CUR.content_type, audience: WA_CD_CUR.audience,
        language: WA_CD_CUR.language, department: WA_CD_CUR.department || undefined,
        brief: WA_CD_CUR.brief || ''
      }
    });
    if (r.error || !(r.data && r.data.ok)) { toast('Could not refine: ' + ((r.data && r.data.error) || 'try again')); go.disabled = false; go.textContent = 'Refine'; }
    else { WA_CD_CUR = r.data.draft; waCdRenderPreview(); waCdLoadList(); toast('Refined'); }
  } catch (e) { toast('Could not refine the draft'); go.disabled = false; go.textContent = 'Refine'; }
}

document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
  if (e.target.closest('#cdGo')) { waCdGenerate(); return; }
  if (e.target.closest('#cdRefGo')) { waCdRefine(); return; }
  var op = e.target.closest('[data-cdopen]');
  if (op) {
    var r = await db.from('content_drafts').select('*').eq('id', Number(op.dataset.cdopen)).maybeSingle();
    if (r.data) { WA_CD_CUR = r.data; waCdRenderPreview(); }
    return;
  }
  var act = e.target.closest('[data-cdact]');
  if (act && WA_CD_CUR) {
    var d = WA_CD_CUR, kind = act.dataset.cdact;
    if (kind === 'review') {
      act.disabled = true; act.textContent = 'Sending...';
      try {
        var rr = await db.functions.invoke('draft-content', { body: { action: 'send_review', draft_id: d.id } });
        if (rr.error || !(rr.data && rr.data.ok)) { toast('Could not send: ' + ((rr.data && rr.data.error) || 'try again')); }
        else { toast('Sent to ' + (rr.data.channel || 'Slack') + ' for review'); WA_CD_CUR.status = 'in review'; waCdLoadList(); }
      } catch (x) { toast('Could not send for review'); }
      act.disabled = false; act.innerHTML = '&#128172; Send to Slack for review';
      return;
    }
    if (kind === 'copy') {
      var txt = (d.subject_line ? 'Subject: ' + d.subject_line + '\n\n' : '') + (d.body_md || '');
      try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt); toast('Copied'); } catch (x) { toast('Could not copy on this browser'); }
    } else if (kind === 'md') {
      waCdDownload(waCdSlug(d) + '.md', (d.subject_line ? '# ' + d.subject_line + '\n\n' : '') + (d.body_md || ''), 'text/markdown');
    } else if (kind === 'html') {
      waCdDownload(waCdSlug(d) + '.html', waCdBrandHtml(d), 'text/html');
    } else if (kind === 'print') {
      var w = window.open('', '_blank');
      if (!w) { toast('Allow popups to print'); return; }
      w.document.write(waCdBrandHtml(d));
      w.document.close();
      setTimeout(function () { try { w.print(); } catch (x) { /* user closes */ } }, 350);
    }
    return;
  }
  var t17 = e.target.closest('[data-tab="create"]');
  if (t17) { setTimeout(function () { try { waRenderCreate(); } catch (x) { /* boot */ } }, 60); }
});
document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
  if (e.key === 'Enter' && e.target && e.target.id === 'cdRefIn') { e.preventDefault(); waCdRefine(); }
});

(function () {
  var __ah17 = applyHash;
  applyHash = function () {
    var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
    if (h === 'create') { showPanel('create'); waRenderCreate(); return; }
    __ah17();
  };
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () {
    if ((location.hash || '') === '#create') {
      try { showPanel('create'); waRenderCreate(); } catch (e) { /* boot */ }
    }
  });
  var __sa17 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa17();
    try {
      if (isStaff()) {
        var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
        if (bar && !bar.querySelector('[data-tab="create"]')) {
          var b = document.createElement('button');
          b.className = 'tab';
          b.dataset.tab = 'create';
          b.innerHTML = '<span class="tabdot" style="background:#6F64AB"></span>Create';
          bar.insertBefore(b, bar.querySelector('.tab-group') || null);
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();


/* =====================================================================
   WA v18 UI enhancements (26/07/26)
   Create studio, photo graphics: upload a photo behind the social tile
   (dark overlay keeps the words readable), pick a platform format
   (Square for Instagram, Story 9:16, Landscape 1.91:1 for LinkedIn/X),
   and download a true PNG image via html2canvas alongside the branded
   HTML page. Photos live in the public content-images storage bucket.
   Overrides the v17 graphic renderers via assignment.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.textContent = ''
    + '.gfx-card.f-story{aspect-ratio:9/16;}'
    + '.gfx-card.f-land{aspect-ratio:1.91/1;}'
    + '.gfx-card.f-land .gfx-rule{margin:8px 0 12px;}'
    + '.gfx-card.f-land .gfx-head{margin-bottom:8px;}'
    + '.gfx-card.f-land .gfx-cta{margin-top:12px;}'
    + '.gfx-card.f-land .gfx-foot{margin-top:12px;}'
    + '#cdGfxOpts input[type=file]{border:1px dashed var(--wa-line);border-radius:8px;padding:8px;background:#FFFAF2;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);
  var goBtn = document.getElementById('cdGo');
  if (goBtn && !document.getElementById('cdGfxOpts')) {
    var w = document.createElement('div');
    w.id = 'cdGfxOpts';
    w.style.display = 'none';
    w.innerHTML = '<label>Graphic format</label>'
      + '<select id="cdFmt"><option value="square">Square (Instagram, 1:1)</option><option value="story">Story (Instagram/TikTok, 9:16)</option><option value="landscape">Landscape (LinkedIn/X, 1.91:1)</option></select>'
      + '<label>Photo (optional)</label><input type="file" id="cdImg" accept="image/*" style="width:100%;font-size:12px;box-sizing:border-box;" />'
      + '<div class="cd-hint" id="cdImgHint">A photo sits behind the text with a dark overlay so the words stay readable.</div>';
    goBtn.parentElement.insertBefore(w, goBtn);
  }
})();

var WA_CD_IMG = null;

function waCdSyncGfx() {
  var t = document.getElementById('cdType');
  var o = document.getElementById('cdGfxOpts');
  if (t && o) o.style.display = (t.value === 'graphic') ? 'block' : 'none';
}
(function () { waCdSyncGfx(); })();

function waGfxClass(d) {
  var f = d.format || 'square';
  return 'gfx-card' + (f === 'story' ? ' f-story' : (f === 'landscape' ? ' f-land' : ''));
}
function waGfxCardStyle(d) {
  return d.image_url ? 'background:linear-gradient(rgba(25,22,21,.45),rgba(25,22,21,.62)),url(' + esc(d.image_url) + ') center/cover;' : '';
}

waCdGraphicHtml = function (d) {
  var bodyEnd = '</bo' + 'dy>', htmlEnd = '</ht' + 'ml>';
  var wide = (d.format === 'story') ? 'width:min(50vmin,460px);' : 'width:min(88vmin,720px);';
  return '<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">'
    + '<title>' + esc(d.title || 'Welfare Avenue graphic') + '</title>'
    + '<style>body{margin:0;background:#F4F2EA;min-height:100vh;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:20px;box-sizing:border-box;}'
    + '.gfx-card{' + wide + '}'
    + WA_GFX_CSS
    + '.gfx-card.f-story{aspect-ratio:9/16;}.gfx-card.f-land{aspect-ratio:1.91/1;}'
    + '.gfx-card.f-land .gfx-rule{margin:8px 0 12px;}.gfx-card.f-land .gfx-head{margin-bottom:8px;}.gfx-card.f-land .gfx-cta{margin-top:12px;}.gfx-card.f-land .gfx-foot{margin-top:12px;}'
    + '@media print{body{background:#FFFAF2;}}'
    + '</style></head><body>'
    + '<div class="' + waGfxClass(d) + '" style="' + waGfxCardStyle(d) + '">' + waCdGfxInner(d) + '</div>'
    + bodyEnd + htmlEnd;
};
waCdGfxPreview = function (d) {
  var maxw = (d.format === 'story') ? '260px' : '430px';
  return '<style>' + WA_GFX_CSS + '</style><div class="' + waGfxClass(d) + '" style="max-width:' + maxw + ';margin:0 auto;' + waGfxCardStyle(d) + '">' + waCdGfxInner(d) + '</div>';
};

waCdRenderPreview = function () {
  var box = document.getElementById('cdPrev');
  if (!box) return;
  var d = WA_CD_CUR;
  if (!d) { box.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Your draft will appear here.</p>'; return; }
  var isG = d.content_type === 'graphic';
  box.innerHTML = ''
    + '<div class="cd-prev-head"><div class="brand">Welfare Avenue</div><div class="tag">Service is solidarity, not charity. &middot; ' + esc(WA_CD_TYPE_LABEL[d.content_type] || 'Content') + ' for ' + esc((d.audience || '').replace(/_/g, ' ')) + '</div></div>'
    + '<div class="cd-prev-body">'
    + (d.subject_line && !isG ? '<div class="cd-subject">' + esc(d.subject_line) + '</div>' : '')
    + (isG ? waCdGfxPreview(d) : '<div class="cd-doc">' + waMd(d.body_md) + '</div>')
    + (d.short_version && !isG ? '<div class="cd-sub"><strong>Short version:</strong><br>' + waMd(d.short_version) + '</div>' : '')
    + (d.notes ? '<div class="cd-notes"><strong>Check before sending:</strong> ' + esc(d.notes) + '</div>' : '')
    + '<div class="cd-acts">'
    + '<button data-cdact="copy">&#128203; Copy text</button>'
    + (isG ? '<button data-cdact="png">&#128444; Download PNG</button>' : '')
    + '<button data-cdact="md">&#11015; Markdown</button>'
    + '<button data-cdact="html">&#11015; Branded page</button>'
    + '<button data-cdact="print">&#128424; Print / PDF</button>'
    + '<button data-cdact="review">&#128172; Send to Slack for review</button>'
    + '</div>'
    + '<div class="cd-refine"><input id="cdRefIn" placeholder="Refine it: e.g. warmer, shorter, add the October date" /><button id="cdRefGo">Refine</button></div>'
    + '</div>';
};

async function waCdPng(d) {
  toast('Rendering PNG...');
  if (!window.html2canvas) {
    try {
      await new Promise(function (res, rej) {
        var s = document.createElement('script');
        s.src = 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/html2canvas/1.4.1/html2canvas.min.js';
        s.onload = res; s.onerror = rej;
        document.head.appendChild(s);
      });
    } catch (x) { toast('Could not load the PNG renderer, use the branded page instead'); return; }
  }
  var dims = d.format === 'story' ? [1080, 1920] : (d.format === 'landscape' ? [1200, 628] : [1080, 1080]);
  var land = d.format === 'landscape';
  var fix = land
    ? '.gfx-brand{font-size:22px!important;}.gfx-rule{width:70px!important;height:6px!important;}.gfx-head{font-size:52px!important;}.gfx-line{font-size:24px!important;}.gfx-cta{font-size:22px!important;padding:12px 30px!important;}.gfx-foot{font-size:16px!important;}'
    : '.gfx-brand{font-size:28px!important;letter-spacing:8px!important;}.gfx-rule{width:110px!important;height:9px!important;margin:26px 0 40px!important;}.gfx-head{font-size:68px!important;margin-bottom:30px!important;}.gfx-line{font-size:32px!important;}.gfx-cta{font-size:30px!important;padding:18px 44px!important;margin-top:40px!important;}.gfx-foot{font-size:22px!important;margin-top:46px!important;}';
  var host = document.createElement('div');
  host.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;left:-99999px;top:0;';
  host.innerHTML = '<style>' + WA_GFX_CSS + fix + '</style>'
    + '<div id="waPngNode" class="' + waGfxClass(d) + '" style="width:' + dims[0] + 'px;height:' + dims[1] + 'px;aspect-ratio:auto;border-radius:0;' + waGfxCardStyle(d) + '">' + waCdGfxInner(d) + '</div>';
  document.body.appendChild(host);
  try {
    var canvas = await html2canvas(document.getElementById('waPngNode'), { useCORS: true, backgroundColor: null, scale: 1 });
    canvas.toBlob(function (b) {
      if (!b) { toast('PNG export failed, use the branded page instead'); return; }
      var a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = URL.createObjectURL(b);
      a.download = waCdSlug(d) + '-' + (d.format || 'square') + '.png';
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      setTimeout(function () { URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href); a.remove(); }, 400);
    }, 'image/png');
  } catch (x) { toast('PNG export failed, use the branded page instead'); }
  host.remove();
}

waCdGenerate = async function () {
  var btn = document.getElementById('cdGo');
  var brief = (document.getElementById('cdBrief').value || '').trim();
  if (!brief) { toast('Write a brief first: what is it, the facts, the ask'); return; }
  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Writing in the WA voice...';
  try {
    var type = document.getElementById('cdType').value;
    var payload = {
      content_type: type,
      audience: document.getElementById('cdAud').value,
      language: document.getElementById('cdLang').value,
      brief: brief
    };
    var dep = document.getElementById('cdDept').value;
    if (dep) payload.department = dep;
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('draft-content', { body: payload });
    if (r.error || !(r.data && r.data.ok)) { toast('Could not generate: ' + ((r.data && r.data.error) || 'network issue, try again')); }
    else {
      WA_CD_CUR = r.data.draft;
      if (type === 'graphic') {
        var fmtEl = document.getElementById('cdFmt');
        var fmt = fmtEl ? fmtEl.value : 'square';
        var upd = { format: fmt };
        if (WA_CD_IMG) upd.image_url = WA_CD_IMG;
        try { await db.from('content_drafts').update(upd).eq('id', WA_CD_CUR.id); } catch (x) { /* cosmetic */ }
        WA_CD_CUR.format = fmt;
        WA_CD_CUR.image_url = WA_CD_IMG || null;
        WA_CD_IMG = null;
        var fi = document.getElementById('cdImg');
        if (fi) fi.value = '';
        var hint = document.getElementById('cdImgHint');
        if (hint) hint.textContent = 'A photo sits behind the text with a dark overlay so the words stay readable.';
      }
      waCdRenderPreview(); waCdLoadList(); toast('Draft ready');
    }
  } catch (e) { toast('Could not generate the draft'); }
  btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Write it';
};

document.addEventListener('change', async function (e) {
  if (e.target && e.target.id === 'cdType') { waCdSyncGfx(); return; }
  if (e.target && e.target.id === 'cdImg') {
    var f = e.target.files && e.target.files[0];
    if (!f) return;
    if (f.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { toast('Photo too big (8 MB max)'); e.target.value = ''; return; }
    var hint = document.getElementById('cdImgHint');
    if (hint) hint.textContent = 'Uploading photo...';
    var path = 'gfx/' + Date.now() + '-' + f.name.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, '');
    try {
      var r = await db.storage.from('content-images').upload(path, f, { upsert: false });
      if (r.error) { toast('Upload failed: ' + r.error.message); if (hint) hint.textContent = 'Upload failed, try another photo.'; return; }
      var pu = db.storage.from('content-images').getPublicUrl(path);
      WA_CD_IMG = (pu.data && pu.data.publicUrl) ? pu.data.publicUrl : null;
      if (hint) hint.textContent = 'Photo attached. It will sit behind the words on the tile.';
      toast('Photo attached');
    } catch (x) { toast('Upload failed'); if (hint) hint.textContent = 'Upload failed, try another photo.'; }
  }
});
document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
  var b = e.target.closest('[data-cdact="png"]');
  if (b && WA_CD_CUR) waCdPng(WA_CD_CUR);
});

/* =====================================================================
   WA v19 UI enhancements (27/07/26)
   Shareable agenda links.
   1. Staff: every agenda gets a Share button. It mints a private link,
      writes a ready-to-paste Slack intro (meeting date + summary + the
      item list + "click the link and add yours"), copies either piece,
      posts it to Slack, or stops sharing.
   2. Anyone holding a link opens a clean, branded page for that one
      agenda: no sign in, read the items, add their own. Contributions
      come back into the real agenda tagged with their name.
   3. Staff deep link #agenda/<id> opens a single agenda on the Agendas
      page with the filters cleared.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {

    + 'body.wa-shared > *:not(#agShare){display:none !important;}'
    + '#agShare{display:none;position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9000;overflow:auto;background:#f3f1ea;padding:26px 16px 60px;}'

var WA_AG_ONLY = 0, WA_SHARE = {};

function waCopyText(t) {
  try {
    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(t); return true; }
  } catch (e) { /* fall through */ }
  try {
    var ta = document.createElement('textarea');
    ta.value = t; ta.style.position = 'fixed'; ta.style.opacity = '0';
    document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); document.execCommand('copy'); ta.remove();
    return true;
  } catch (e) { return false; }
}

/* ---------- staff: rows now show contributions and a Share button ---------- */

waAgendaRowHtml = function (a) {
  var items = AG_ITEMS_CACHE.filter(function (i) { return i.agenda_id === a.id; });
  var shared = items.filter(function (i) { return i.source === 'shared'; }).length;
  var openCls = WA_AG_OPEN[a.id] ? ' open' : '';
  var chip = (a.scope || 'department') === 'department'
    ? '<span class="ag-dep" style="background:' + (DEPT_COLOR[a.department] || '#797773') + '">' + (DEPT_LABEL[a.department] || a.department || '') + '</span>'
    : '<span class="ag-dep" style="background:#6F64AB">' + (a.scope === 'leaders' ? 'Leaders' : esc(a.with_person || 'Executive')) + '</span>';
  return '<div class="ag-row' + openCls + '" data-ag="' + a.id + '">'
    + '<div class="ag-head" data-agtog="' + a.id + '">' + chip + '<span class="t">' + esc(a.title) + '</span>'
    + '<span class="ag-st ' + esc(a.status) + '">' + esc(a.status) + '</span>'
    + '<span style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);">' + (a.meeting_date || '') + ' &middot; ' + items.length + ' items'
    + (shared ? ' &middot; ' + shared + ' added by the team' : '')
    + (a.share_token ? ' &middot; &#128279; shared' : '') + '</span></div>'
    + '<div class="ag-body">'
    + items.map(function (i) {
        var tasks = (i.task_ids || []).map(waTaskById).filter(Boolean);
        return '<div class="ag-item' + (i.source === 'shared' ? ' shared' : '') + '" data-agi="' + i.id + '">'
          + '<span class="k" style="background:' + (AG_KIND_COLOR[i.kind] || '#797773') + '">' + esc(i.kind) + '</span>'
          + '<span class="it">' + esc(i.title) + '</span>'
          + (i.source === 'shared' && i.created_by ? '<span class="ag-from">' + esc(i.created_by) + '</span>' : '')
          + (i.notes ? '<div class="nts">' + esc(i.notes) + '</div>' : '')
          + tasks.map(function (t) {
              return '<div class="ag-task' + (t.done ? ' done' : '') + '"><input type="checkbox" data-agtask="' + t.id + '" data-agdept="' + esc(t.department || '') + '"' + (t.done ? ' checked' : '') + ' /><span>' + esc(t.title) + '</span><span style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:11px;">' + esc(t.owner || '') + '</span></div>';
            }).join('')
          + '<div class="ag-ictl"><button data-agup="' + i.id + '">up</button><button data-agdn="' + i.id + '">down</button><button data-agdel="' + i.id + '" style="color:#b3402f;">remove</button></div>'
          + '</div>';
      }).join('')
    + '<div class="ag-add"><input data-agnt="' + a.id + '" placeholder="New item title" /><input data-agnn="' + a.id + '" placeholder="Notes (optional)" /><button class="btn ghost mini" data-agadd="' + a.id + '">Add item</button></div>'
    + '<div class="ag-foot">'
    + '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-agshare="' + a.id + '" style="color:#6F64AB;font-weight:700;">&#128279; ' + (a.share_token ? 'Share link' : 'Get a share link') + '</button>'
    + (a.status === 'draft' ? '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-agfin="' + a.id + '">Mark final</button>' : '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-agdraft="' + a.id + '">Back to draft</button>')
    + (a.status === 'done' ? '' : '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-agdone="' + a.id + '">Archive (done)</button>')
    + '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-agkill="' + a.id + '" style="color:#b3402f;">Delete</button>'
    + '</div>'
    + '<div class="ag-share" data-agsharebox="' + a.id + '"></div>'
    + '</div></div>';
};

function waShareBoxHtml(aid, d) {
  return '<h4>Private link for this agenda</h4>'
    + '<input readonly data-shurl="' + aid + '" value="' + esc(d.url) + '" />'
    + '<div class="row"><button class="btn ghost mini" data-shcopyurl="' + aid + '">Copy link</button>'
    + '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-shopen="' + aid + '">Preview it</button>'
    + '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-shrevoke="' + aid + '" style="color:#b3402f;margin-left:auto;">Stop sharing</button></div>'
    + '<h4>Message for Slack</h4>'
    + '<textarea data-shmsg="' + aid + '">' + esc(d.message) + '</textarea>'
    + '<div class="row"><button class="btn ghost mini" data-shcopymsg="' + aid + '">Copy message</button>'
    + '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-shpost="' + aid + '">Post it to Slack</button></div>'
    + '<div class="hint">Anyone with the link can read this agenda and add items to it without signing in. They cannot see anything else on the dashboard. Edit the message before you copy it if you want.</div>';
}

async function waShareOpen(aid, box) {
  box.classList.add('open');
  box.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Making the link...</p>';
  try {
    const { data, error } = await db.functions.invoke('agenda-share', { body: { action: 'create_link', agenda_id: aid } });
    if (error || !(data && data.ok)) throw (error || new Error('failed'));
    WA_SHARE[aid] = data;
    box.innerHTML = waShareBoxHtml(aid, data);
    var a = AGENDAS_CACHE.find(function (x) { return x.id === aid; });
    if (a) a.share_token = data.token;
    waWireShareBox(box, aid);
  } catch (e) {
    box.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Could not make the link. Try again in a moment.</p>';
  }
}

function waWireShareBox(box, aid) {
  function msg() { var t = box.querySelector('[data-shmsg]'); return t ? t.value : ''; }
  var u = box.querySelector('[data-shcopyurl]');
  if (u) u.addEventListener('click', function () {
    var f = box.querySelector('[data-shurl]');
    if (f) { f.select(); waCopyText(f.value); toast('Link copied'); }
  });
  var m = box.querySelector('[data-shcopymsg]');
  if (m) m.addEventListener('click', function () { waCopyText(msg()); toast('Message copied, paste it into Slack'); });
  var o = box.querySelector('[data-shopen]');
  if (o) o.addEventListener('click', function () {
    var f = box.querySelector('[data-shurl]');
    if (f) window.open(f.value, '_blank');
  });
  var p = box.querySelector('[data-shpost]');
  if (p) p.addEventListener('click', async function () {
    p.disabled = true; p.textContent = 'Posting...';
    try {
      const { data, error } = await db.functions.invoke('agenda-share', { body: { action: 'post_slack', agenda_id: aid, message: msg() } });
      if (error || !(data && data.ok)) throw (error || new Error('failed'));
      toast('Posted to Slack');
    } catch (e) { toast('Slack did not accept it, copy the message instead'); }
    p.disabled = false; p.textContent = 'Post it to Slack';
  });
  var r = box.querySelector('[data-shrevoke]');
  if (r) r.addEventListener('click', async function () {
    if (!confirm('Stop sharing? The link stops working for everyone who has it.')) return;
    try {
      await db.functions.invoke('agenda-share', { body: { action: 'revoke_link', agenda_id: aid } });
      var a = AGENDAS_CACHE.find(function (x) { return x.id === aid; });
      if (a) a.share_token = null;
      delete WA_SHARE[aid];
      box.classList.remove('open');
      box.innerHTML = '';
      toast('Sharing stopped');
    } catch (e) { toast('Could not stop sharing'); }
  });
}

(function () {
  var __wire19 = waWireAgendaCard;
  waWireAgendaCard = function (card, d) {
    __wire19(card, d);
    card.querySelectorAll('[data-agshare]').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var aid = Number(b.dataset.agshare);
        var box = card.querySelector('[data-agsharebox="' + aid + '"]');
        if (!box) return;
        if (box.classList.contains('open')) { box.classList.remove('open'); return; }
        waShareOpen(aid, box);
      });
    });
  };
})();

/* ---------- staff deep link: #agenda/<id> ---------- */

(function () {
  var __rap19 = waRenderAgendasPage;
  waRenderAgendasPage = async function () {
    await __rap19();
    var body = document.getElementById('agPageBody');
    if (!body) return;
    if (!WA_AG_ONLY) return;
    var hit = null;
    body.querySelectorAll('.ag-row').forEach(function (r) {
      if (Number(r.dataset.ag) === WA_AG_ONLY) { hit = r; r.classList.add('open'); }
      else r.style.display = 'none';
    });
    WA_AG_OPEN[WA_AG_ONLY] = true;
    var bar = document.createElement('div');
    bar.className = 'ag-only';
    bar.innerHTML = hit
      ? '<span>Showing one agenda. Use Share to send it to the team.</span>'
      : '<span>That agenda is not here anymore.</span>';
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'btn ghost mini';
    b.textContent = 'Show all agendas';
    b.style.marginLeft = 'auto';
    b.addEventListener('click', function () {
      WA_AG_ONLY = 0;
      if (location.hash !== '#agendas') location.hash = '#agendas';
      else waRenderAgendasPage();
    });
    bar.appendChild(b);
    body.insertBefore(bar, body.firstChild);
  };

  function openOne(h) {
    var m = /^agenda\/(\d+)$/.exec(h);
    if (!m) return false;
    WA_AG_ONLY = Number(m[1]);
    showPanel('agendas');
    try {
      var fd = document.getElementById('agFilterDept'); if (fd) fd.value = 'all';
      var fs = document.getElementById('agFilterStatus'); if (fs) fs.value = 'all';
    } catch (e) { /* not built yet */ }
    waRenderAgendasPage();
    return true;
  }

  var __ah19 = applyHash;
  applyHash = function () {
    var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
    if (openOne(h)) return;
    if (WA_AG_ONLY && h !== 'agendas') WA_AG_ONLY = 0;
    __ah19();
  };
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () {
    var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
    try { openOne(h); } catch (e) { /* boot */ }
  });
})();

/* ---------- the public page anyone with a link lands on ---------- */

(function () {
  var q = new URLSearchParams(location.search || '');
  var token = q.get('a') || '';
  if (!token) return;

  document.body.classList.add('wa-shared');
  var wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.id = 'agShare';
  wrap.innerHTML = '<div class="as-wrap">'
    + '<div class="as-brand"><div class="wa-logo">WA</div><span class="n">Welfare Avenue</span></div>'
    + '<div class="as-card" id="asCard"><p class="as-sum">Opening the agenda...</p></div>'
    + '<div class="as-foot">Shared from the Welfare Avenue dashboard.</div>'
    + '</div>';
  document.body.appendChild(wrap);

  function asEsc(s) {
    return String(s == null ? '' : s)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
  }
  function remembered() {
    try { return localStorage.getItem('wa_share_name') || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; }
  }
  function remember(n) {
    try { localStorage.setItem('wa_share_name', n); } catch (e) { /* private mode */ }
  }
  async function call(payload) {
    const { data, error } = await db.functions.invoke('agenda-share', { body: payload });
    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  }

  function render(d) {
    var card = document.getElementById('asCard');
    if (!card) return;
    var a = d.agenda, items = d.items || [];
    card.innerHTML = '<h1>' + asEsc(a.title) + '</h1>'
      + (a.meeting_date_pretty ? '<div class="as-date">' + asEsc(a.meeting_date_pretty) + '</div>' : '')
      + '<p class="as-sum">' + asEsc(a.summary) + '</p>'
      + '<div class="as-h">' + (items.length ? 'On the agenda' : 'Nothing on it yet') + '</div>'
      + items.map(function (i) {
          return '<div class="as-item' + (i.shared ? ' mine' : '') + '"><div class="t">' + asEsc(i.title) + '</div>'
            + (i.notes ? '<div class="n">' + asEsc(i.notes) + '</div>' : '')
            + (i.created_by ? '<div class="n">Added by ' + asEsc(i.created_by) + '</div>' : '')
            + '</div>';
        }).join('')
      + (a.can_add
        ? '<div class="as-add"><div class="as-h" style="margin-top:0;">Anything you want to add?</div>'
          + '<input id="asName" placeholder="Your name" value="' + asEsc(remembered()) + '" />'
          + '<input id="asWhat" placeholder="What do you want to talk about?" />'
          + '<textarea id="asNotes" placeholder="Any detail worth knowing (optional)"></textarea>'
          + '<button class="as-go" id="asGo">Add it to the agenda</button>'
          + '<div class="as-msg" id="asMsg" style="display:none;"></div></div>'
        : '<div class="as-add"><p class="as-sum">This meeting is closed, so the agenda is read only now.</p></div>');
    var go = document.getElementById('asGo');
    if (!go) return;
    go.addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var name = (document.getElementById('asName').value || '').trim();
      var what = (document.getElementById('asWhat').value || '').trim();
      var notes = (document.getElementById('asNotes').value || '').trim();
      var msg = document.getElementById('asMsg');
      function say(t, bad) {
        msg.textContent = t;
        msg.className = 'as-msg' + (bad ? ' bad' : '');
        msg.style.display = 'block';
      }
      if (!name) { say('Add your name first so the team knows who raised it.', true); return; }
      if (!what) { say('Tell us what you want to talk about.', true); return; }
      go.disabled = true; go.textContent = 'Adding...';
      try {
        var r = await call({ action: 'add', token: token, name: name, title: what, notes: notes });
        if (!(r && r.ok)) throw new Error((r && r.error) || 'failed');
        remember(name);
        var fresh = await call({ action: 'get', token: token });
        render(fresh);
        var m2 = document.getElementById('asMsg');
        if (m2) { m2.textContent = 'Added. Thank you, it is on the agenda now.'; m2.className = 'as-msg'; m2.style.display = 'block'; }
      } catch (e) {
        go.disabled = false; go.textContent = 'Add it to the agenda';
        say('That did not go through. Try again in a moment.', true);
      }
    });
  }

  function boot() {
    call({ action: 'get', token: token }).then(function (d) {
      if (!(d && d.ok)) throw new Error('gone');
      render(d);
    }).catch(function () {
      var card = document.getElementById('asCard');
      if (card) card.innerHTML = '<h1>This link is not active</h1><p class="as-sum">The agenda may have been archived or the link turned off. Ask whoever sent it for a fresh one.</p>';
    });
  }
  if (typeof db !== 'undefined' && db) boot();
  else window.addEventListener('load', boot);
})();

/* =====================================================================
   WA v20 UI enhancements (27/07/26)
   Volunteering: the biweekly Sunday serving programme, end to end.
   1. New staff tab "Volunteering" with five views: the 2026 calendar,
      a single event, the master SOP, the volunteer list and inventory.
   2. Every event gets the identical 94 step SOP checklist, seeded from
      the ClickUp list and kept in sop_event_steps, so the template is
      the same every fortnight and progress is tracked per event.
   3. Each step carries its verbatim ClickUp description, its ClickUp
      link and its screenshots, rehosted permanently in Supabase storage.
   4. Per event roster grouped by role, with contact details and a
      status any staff member can change.
   5. Event specific tasks arriving from operations transcripts land in
      sop_event_extras and show under "Added this event". Ad hoc and
      corporate volunteering stay in the department task lists.
   Overrides applyHash and startApp via assignment.
   ===================================================================== */
