var WA_VOL_EVENTS = [];
var WA_VOL_TMPL = [];
var WA_VOL_SHOTS = {};
var WA_VOL_SHOTBYATT = {};
var WA_VOL_PEOPLE = [];
var WA_VOL_INV = [];
var WA_VOL_LOADED = false;
var WA_VOL_VIEW = 'calendar';
var WA_VOL_EVENT = null;
var WA_VOL_DETAIL = null;
var WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER = '';

var WA_VOL_PHASES = [
  ['t_minus_2w', 'Two weeks out'],
  ['t_minus_1w', 'One week out'],
  ['week_of', 'Week of the event'],
  ['event_day', 'Event day'],
  ['post_event', 'After the event'],
  ['anytime', 'Anytime']
];
var WA_VOL_ROLELABEL = {
  server: 'Servers', cook: 'Cooks', driver: 'Drivers', photographer: 'Photographers', videographer: 'Videographers',
  dishwasher: 'Dishwashers', team_leader: 'Team leaders', community_debrief: 'Community lead',
  community_talker: 'Community lead'
};
/* the vocabulary actually used in the volunteering sheet, plus the two we add when
   somebody drops out. Anything else already in the row is preserved, see waVolRosterSelect. */
var WA_VOL_STATUSES = ['Confirmed', 'Waiting For Response', 'TBD', 'Unavailable', 'Cancelled'];

/* ---------- small helpers ---------- */
function waVolDate(iso, long) {
  if (!iso) return '';
  try {
    var d = new Date(iso + 'T12:00:00Z');
    return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', long
      ? { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' }
      : { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short', timeZone: 'UTC' });
  } catch (e) { return iso; }
}
function waVolMonth(iso) {
  try {
    return new Date(iso + 'T12:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' });
  } catch (e) { return ''; }
}
function waVolToday() { return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
function waVolDaysTo(iso) {
  var a = new Date(waVolToday() + 'T12:00:00Z').getTime();
  var b = new Date(iso + 'T12:00:00Z').getTime();
  return Math.round((b - a) / 86400000);
}
function waVolPhaseNow(iso) {
  var d = waVolDaysTo(iso);
  if (d > 14) return 't_minus_2w';
  if (d > 7) return 't_minus_1w';
  if (d > 0) return 'week_of';
  if (d === 0) return 'event_day';
  return 'post_event';
}
function waVolStatusPill(s) {
  var v = (s || '').toLowerCase();
  var c = 'grey';
  if (v.indexOf('confirm') === 0 || v === 'completed') c = 'green';
  else if (v.indexOf('wait') === 0 || v === 'tentative' || v === 'planned') c = 'amber';
  else if (v.indexOf('cancel') === 0 || v.indexOf('declin') === 0 || v.indexOf('unavail') === 0) c = 'red';
  else if (v === 'in progress') c = 'blue';
  return '<span class="vl-pill ' + c + '">' + esc(s || 'Not set') + '</span>';
}
/* A blank first option matters: a roster row with no status must not read as Confirmed
   just because Confirmed happens to be first in the list. */
function waVolRosterSelect(r) {
  var cur = r.status || '';
  var opts = '<option value=""' + (cur ? '' : ' selected') + '>Not set</option>'
    + WA_VOL_STATUSES.map(function (s) {
      return '<option' + (cur === s ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(s) + '</option>';
    }).join('');
  if (cur && WA_VOL_STATUSES.indexOf(cur) < 0) opts += '<option selected>' + esc(cur) + '</option>';
  return '<select data-vlrstat="' + r.id + '"' + (isStaff() ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + opts + '</select>';
}
function waVolRoleLabel(r) {
  if (WA_VOL_ROLELABEL[r]) return WA_VOL_ROLELABEL[r];
  return (r || 'other').replace(/_/g, ' ').replace(/^./, function (m) { return m.toUpperCase(); });
}

/* ClickUp attachment URLs expire and need a login, so every one of them is
   swapped for our rehosted copy in the sop-shots bucket. The id in the ClickUp
   path is the attachment id, which is exactly what we keyed the rehost on. */
function waVolShotFor(url) {
  var m = /clickup-attachments\.com\/[^/]+\/([0-9a-f-]{36})/i.exec(url || '');
  return (m && WA_VOL_SHOTBYATT[m[1]]) || null;
}

/* Minimal markdown for the verbatim ClickUp descriptions. Escapes first, so
   nothing in the source text can inject markup. It covers the shapes ClickUp
   actually exports: ## headings, _underscore_ emphasis, mailto links, and
   screenshots dropped into the middle of a heading line. */
function waVolMd(src) {
  if (!src) return '';
  var raw = String(src).replace(/https?:\/\/[a-z0-9.\-]*clickup-attachments\.com\/[^\s)"'<>]+/gi, function (u) {
    return waVolShotFor(u) || u;
  });
  var lines = esc(raw).split(/\r?\n/);
  var out = [], inList = false;
  function img(u, alt) {
    if (/clickup-attachments/.test(u)) {
      return '<p class="vl-noimg">A screenshot sits here in ClickUp that could not be copied across. Open the task in ClickUp to see it.</p>';
    }
    return '<img class="vl-mimg" src="' + u + '" alt="' + (alt || 'Screenshot') + '" data-vlshot="' + u + '" loading="lazy" />';
  }
  function inline(t) {
    t = t.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\((mailto:[^)\s]+)\)/g, '<a href="$2">$1</a>');
    t = t.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\((https?:[^)\s]+)\)/g, '<a href="$2" target="_blank" rel="noopener">$1</a>');
    t = t.replace(/(^|[\s(])(https?:\/\/[^\s<)]+)/g, '$1<a href="$2" target="_blank" rel="noopener">$2</a>');
    t = t.replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '<b>$1</b>');
    t = t.replace(/__([^_\n]+)__/g, '<b>$1</b>');
    t = t.replace(/(^|[^*])\*([^*\n]+)\*/g, '$1<i>$2</i>');
    t = t.replace(/(^|[^\w_])_([^_\n]+)_(?![\w_])/g, '$1<i>$2</i>');
    t = t.replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>');
    return t;
  }
  for (var i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    var imgs = [];
    var L = lines[i].replace(/!\[([^\]]*)\]\(([^)\s]*)\)/g, function (_m, alt, u) { imgs.push(img(u, alt)); return ''; });
    var h = /^\s*(#{1,4})\s*(.*)$/.exec(L);
    var m = h ? null : /^\s*([-*+]|\d+[.)])\s+(.*)$/.exec(L);
    if (m) {
      if (!inList) { out.push('<ul>'); inList = true; }
      out.push('<li>' + inline(m[2]) + '</li>');
      if (imgs.length) { out.push('</ul>'); inList = false; out.push(imgs.join('')); }
      continue;
    }
    if (inList) { out.push('</ul>'); inList = false; }
    /* a screenshot wedged into a heading line belongs above the heading it interrupts */
    if (imgs.length) out.push(imgs.join(''));
    var body = h ? h[2] : L;
    if (!body.trim()) continue;
    if (h) out.push('<div class="vl-h vl-h' + h[1].length + '">' + inline(body) + '</div>');
    else out.push('<p>' + inline(body) + '</p>');
  }
  if (inList) out.push('</ul>');
  return out.join('');
}

/* ---------- loaders ---------- */
async function waVolLoad(force) {
  if (WA_VOL_LOADED && !force) return;
  var res = await Promise.all([
    db.from('volunteer_event_summary').select('*').order('event_date', { ascending: true }),
    db.from('sop_template').select('id,parent_id,depth,sort,section,name,description,clickup_url,phase,default_owner,needs_review,review_note,has_secret,stage,hub_signal,hub_note,proposal').eq('active', true).order('sort', { ascending: true }),
    db.from('sop_shots').select('template_id,attachment_id,public_url,title,sort').order('sort', { ascending: true })
  ]);
  WA_VOL_EVENTS = (res[0].data) || [];
  WA_VOL_TMPL = (res[1].data) || [];
  WA_VOL_SHOTS = {};
  WA_VOL_SHOTBYATT = {};
  ((res[2].data) || []).forEach(function (s) {
    (WA_VOL_SHOTS[s.template_id] = WA_VOL_SHOTS[s.template_id] || []).push(s);
    var key = String(s.attachment_id || '').replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '');
    if (key) WA_VOL_SHOTBYATT[key] = s.public_url;
  });
  WA_VOL_LOADED = true;
}
async function waVolLoadEvent(id) {
  var res = await Promise.all([
    db.from('event_roster').select('*').eq('event_id', id).order('role', { ascending: true }).order('sort', { ascending: true }),
    db.from('sop_event_steps').select('*').eq('event_id', id),
    db.from('sop_event_extras').select('*').eq('event_id', id).order('id', { ascending: true })
  ]);
  WA_VOL_DETAIL = {
    id: id,
    roster: (res[0].data) || [],
    steps: (res[1].data) || [],
    extras: (res[2].data) || []
  };
}
async function waVolLoadPeople() {
  if (WA_VOL_PEOPLE.length) return;
  var r = await db.from('volunteer_people').select('*').order('full_name', { ascending: true });
  WA_VOL_PEOPLE = (r.data) || [];
}
async function waVolLoadInv() {
  if (WA_VOL_INV.length) return;
  var r = await db.from('volunteer_inventory').select('*').order('sort', { ascending: true });
  WA_VOL_INV = (r.data) || [];
}

/* ---------- shell ---------- */
function waVolShell(inner) {
  var tabs = [['calendar', 'Calendar'], ['sop', 'The SOP'], ['people', 'Volunteers'], ['inventory', 'Inventory']];
  var chips = tabs.map(function (t) {
    return '<span class="fchip' + (WA_VOL_VIEW === t[0] ? ' on' : '') + '" data-vl="' + t[0] + '">' + t[1] + '</span>';
  }).join('');
  return '<div class="card">'
    + '<h2 style="color:#103F39">Volunteering<span class="deptchip" style="background:#103F39">Montreal</span></h2>'
    + '<p class="read">The Sunday servings in Montreal: this week, the calendar and each serving with its roster and steps, the SOP, the volunteers, the recipes and the inventory.</p>' /* wave191 wording */
    + '<div class="vl-sub">' + chips + '</div>'
    + inner + '</div>';
}
function waVolPaint(inner) {
  var el = document.getElementById('volunteering');
  if (el) el.innerHTML = waVolShell(inner);
}

/* ---------- calendar ---------- */
function waVolNextEvent() {
  var t = waVolToday(), pick = null;
  WA_VOL_EVENTS.forEach(function (e) {
    if (e.kind === 'service' && e.status !== 'cancelled' && e.event_date >= t && !pick) pick = e;
  });
  return pick;
}
function waVolEventCard(e, nextId) {
  var pct = e.steps_total ? Math.round((e.steps_done / e.steps_total) * 100) : 0;
  var past = e.event_date < waVolToday();
  var cls = 'vl-ev' + (past ? ' past' : '') + (e.id === nextId ? ' next' : '');
  var bits = '';
  if (e.roster_count) bits += '<span class="vl-bub">' + e.roster_confirmed + ' of ' + e.roster_count + ' confirmed</span>';
  else bits += '<span class="vl-bub off">No roster yet</span>';
  if (e.extras_open) bits += '<span class="vl-bub warn">' + e.extras_open + ' extra task' + (e.extras_open === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span>';
  return '<div class="' + cls + '" data-vlev="' + e.id + '">'
    + '<h4>' + esc(waVolDate(e.event_date)) + ' &middot; ' + esc(e.title || 'Event') + '</h4>'
    + '<div class="vl-code">' + esc(e.code || '') + ' &middot; ' + esc(e.season || '') + ' ' + waVolStatusPill(e.status) + '</div>'
    + (e.steps_total ? '<div class="vl-bar"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div>'
      + '<div class="vl-nums">' + e.steps_done + ' of ' + e.steps_total + ' SOP steps done</div>' : '')
    + '<div style="margin-top:6px">' + bits + '</div>'
    + '</div>';
}
function waVolRenderCalendar() { /* superseded: wave26 reset without capture; superseded; body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }

/* ---------- one event ---------- */
function waVolStepTree(steps) {
  var byId = {}, tmplById = {};
  WA_VOL_TMPL.forEach(function (t) { tmplById[t.id] = t; });
  steps.forEach(function (s) { byId[s.template_id] = s; });
  var rows = [];
  WA_VOL_TMPL.forEach(function (t) {
    var s = byId[t.id];
    if (!s) return;
    rows.push({ t: t, s: s });
  });
  return rows;
}
function waVolStepHtml(t, s) {
  var desc = t.description || '';
  /* screenshots ClickUp already places inside the description get rendered in
     position by waVolMd, so the gallery underneath only shows the loose ones */
  var shots = (WA_VOL_SHOTS[t.id] || []).filter(function (x) {
    var key = String(x.attachment_id || '').replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '');
    return !(key && desc.indexOf(key) >= 0);
  });
  var hasDet = !!(t.description || shots.length || t.clickup_url || t.needs_review || t.has_secret);
  var det = '';
  if (hasDet) {
    det = '<div class="vl-det" id="vld-' + esc(t.id) + '">'
      + (t.description ? waVolMd(t.description) : '<p style="color:#797773">No description in ClickUp for this step.</p>')
      + (shots.length ? '<div class="vl-shots">' + shots.map(function (x) {
        return '<img src="' + esc(x.public_url) + '" alt="' + esc(x.title || 'Screenshot') + '" data-vlshot="' + esc(x.public_url) + '" loading="lazy" />';
      }).join('') + '</div>' : '')
      + (t.needs_review ? '<div class="vl-flag">Needs review: ' + esc(t.review_note || '') + '</div>' : '')
      + (t.has_secret ? '<div class="vl-flag lock">A password was written into this step in ClickUp. It has been removed here. Ask Steel or Suhaib for the credential.</div>' : '')
      + (t.clickup_url ? '<div style="margin-top:9px"><a href="' + esc(t.clickup_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open this task in ClickUp</a></div>' : '')
      + '</div>';
  }
  return '<div class="vl-step' + (s.done ? ' done' : '') + ' vl-d' + Math.min(t.depth || 0, 3) + '" data-vlstep="' + s.id + '">'
    + '<input type="checkbox" data-vlchk="' + s.id + '"' + (s.done ? ' checked' : '') + (isStaff() ? '' : ' disabled') + ' />'
    + '<div class="vl-t">'
    + '<div class="vl-name">' + esc(t.name || '')
    + (hasDet ? '<button class="vl-more" data-vlmore="' + esc(t.id) + '">details</button>' : '')
    + (t.needs_review ? ' <span class="vl-pill amber">review</span>' : '')
    + '</div>'
    + '<div class="vl-meta">'
    + '<input class="vl-in" style="width:120px" placeholder="owner" value="' + esc(s.owner || t.default_owner || '') + '" data-vlowner="' + s.id + '"' + (isStaff() ? '' : ' disabled') + ' /> '
    + '<input class="vl-in w90" type="date" value="' + esc(s.due_date || '') + '" data-vldue="' + s.id + '"' + (isStaff() ? '' : ' disabled') + ' />'
    + (s.done && s.done_by ? ' <span style="margin-left:6px">done by ' + esc(s.done_by) + '</span>' : '')
    + '</div>'
    + det
    + '</div></div>';
}
function waVolRenderEvent() {
  var ev = null;
  WA_VOL_EVENTS.forEach(function (e) { if (e.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) ev = e; });
  if (!ev || !WA_VOL_DETAIL || WA_VOL_DETAIL.id !== WA_VOL_EVENT) {
    waVolPaint('<p class="vl-empty">Loading the serving...</p>');
    return;
  }
  var D = WA_VOL_DETAIL;
  var pct = D.steps.length ? Math.round((D.steps.filter(function (s) { return s.done; }).length / D.steps.length) * 100) : 0;
  var html = '<button class="vl-back" data-vl="calendar">&larr; All servings</button>';

  html += '<div class="vl-hero"><h3>' + esc(waVolDate(ev.event_date, true)) + ' &middot; ' + esc(ev.title || '') + '</h3>'
    + '<div class="vl-when">' + esc(ev.code || '') + ' &middot; ' + esc(ev.season || '') + ' &middot; ' + esc(ev.location || '') + ' ' + waVolStatusPill(ev.status) + '</div>'
    + (ev.notes ? '<div class="vl-when" style="margin-top:7px">' + esc(ev.notes) + '</div>' : '')
    + '<div class="vl-bar"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div>'
    + '<div class="vl-nums">' + D.steps.filter(function (s) { return s.done; }).length + ' of ' + D.steps.length + ' steps done</div>'
    + '</div>';

  /* roster */
  html += '<h3 class="block">Who is on this serving</h3>';
  if (!D.roster.length) {
    html += '<p class="vl-empty">Nobody is rostered yet for this date.</p>';
  } else {
    var byRole = {};
    D.roster.forEach(function (r) { (byRole[r.role] = byRole[r.role] || []).push(r); });
    Object.keys(byRole).sort().forEach(function (role) {
      var list = byRole[role];
      var conf = list.filter(function (r) { return (r.status || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('confirm') === 0; }).length;
      html += '<div class="vl-role"><h4>' + esc(waVolRoleLabel(role)) + ' &middot; ' + conf + ' of ' + list.length + ' confirmed</h4>';
      list.forEach(function (r) {
        var d = r.detail || {};
        var contact = [];
        if (d.phone) contact.push(esc(String(d.phone)));
        if (d.email) contact.push(esc(String(d.email)));
        if (d.pickup_time) contact.push('pickup ' + esc(String(d.pickup_time)));
        if (d.type_of_food) contact.push(esc(String(d.type_of_food)));
        html += '<div class="vl-per"><b>' + esc(r.person_name || 'Unnamed') + '</b>'
          + waVolRosterSelect(r)
          + (contact.length ? '<span class="vl-c">' + contact.join(' &middot; ') + '</span>' : '')
          + (r.notes ? '<span class="vl-c">' + esc(r.notes) + '</span>' : '')
          + (r.person_id ? '' : '<span class="vl-bub warn">not matched to a volunteer record</span>')
          + '</div>';
      });
      html += '</div>';
    });
  }

  /* extras from transcripts */
  html += '<h3 class="block">Added for this event</h3>'
    + '<p class="vl-empty" style="padding:0 0 8px">Tasks that came out of an operations meeting for this specific date. Ad hoc, corporate and special volunteering stays on the department task lists.</p>';
  if (!D.extras.length) html += '<p class="vl-empty">Nothing extra yet.</p>';
  else {
    html += '<ul class="checklist">' + D.extras.map(function (x) {
      return '<li><input type="checkbox" data-vlxchk="' + x.id + '"' + (x.done ? ' checked' : '') + (isStaff() ? '' : ' disabled') + ' />'
        + '<div class="ci-body"><div class="ci-title"' + (x.done ? ' style="text-decoration:line-through;color:#797773"' : '') + '>' + esc(x.title || '') + '</div>'
        + (x.detail ? '<div class="ci-sub">' + esc(x.detail) + '</div>' : '')
        + '<div class="ci-meta">' + esc(x.owner || 'unassigned') + (x.due_date ? ' &middot; due ' + esc(x.due_date) : '') + ' &middot; from ' + esc(x.source || 'manual') + '</div></div></li>';
    }).join('') + '</ul>';
  }
  if (isStaff()) html += '<div class="add-row"><button class="btn ghost mini" data-vladd="1">+ Add a task for this event</button></div>';

  /* SOP checklist */
  html += '<h3 class="block" style="margin-top:22px">The checklist for this serving</h3>';
  var rows = waVolStepTree(D.steps);
  var nowPhase = waVolPhaseNow(ev.event_date);
  WA_VOL_PHASES.forEach(function (p) {
    var mine = rows.filter(function (r) { return (r.t.phase || 'anytime') === p[0]; });
    if (!mine.length) return;
    var doneN = mine.filter(function (r) { return r.s.done; }).length;
    var isNow = p[0] === nowPhase;
    var body = '', sec = '';
    mine.forEach(function (r) {
      if (r.t.section !== sec) { sec = r.t.section; body += '<div class="vl-sec">' + esc(sec || '') + '</div>'; }
      body += waVolStepHtml(r.t, r.s);
    });
    html += '<details class="vl-phase' + (isNow ? ' now' : '') + '"' + (isNow ? ' open' : '') + '>'
      + '<summary>' + esc(p[1]) + ' <span class="vl-nums">' + doneN + ' of ' + mine.length + '</span>'
      + (isNow ? ' <span class="vl-pill green">now</span>' : '') + '</summary>'
      + '<div class="vl-body">' + body + '</div></details>';
  });
  waVolPaint(html);
}

/* ---------- master SOP ---------- */
function waVolRenderSop() {
  var html = '<p class="vl-empty" style="padding:0 0 12px">The master template, straight from the ClickUp list. Every event gets a copy of exactly this, so editing an event never changes the template. Re-run the ClickUp import to pull changes across.</p>';
  var sec = '';
  WA_VOL_TMPL.forEach(function (t) {
    if (t.section !== sec) {
      if (sec) html += '</div></details>';
      sec = t.section;
      var n = WA_VOL_TMPL.filter(function (x) { return x.section === sec; }).length;
      html += '<details class="vl-phase"><summary>' + esc(sec || '') + ' <span class="vl-nums">' + n + ' steps</span></summary><div class="vl-body">';
    }
    html += waVolStepHtml(t, { id: 'tmpl-' + t.id, done: false, owner: t.default_owner || '', due_date: '' })
      .replace('type="checkbox"', 'type="checkbox" disabled');
  });
  if (sec) html += '</div></details>';
  waVolPaint(html);
}

/* ---------- volunteers ---------- */
function waVolRenderPeople() { /* superseded: wave24/26 reset without capture; superseded; body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }

/* ---------- inventory ---------- */
function waVolRenderInv() {
  var html = '<p class="vl-empty" style="padding:0 0 12px">Counted stock from the inventory tab of the volunteering sheet.</p>'
    + '<div class="vl-tblwrap"><table class="wa-tbl"><thead><tr><th>Category</th><th>Item</th><th>Unit</th><th>In</th><th>Remaining</th><th>Reorder</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
    + WA_VOL_INV.map(function (r) {
      return '<tr><td>' + esc(r.category || '') + '</td><td>' + esc(r.item || '') + '</td><td>' + esc(r.unit || '') + '</td>'
        + '<td>' + esc(r.qty_in || '') + '</td><td>' + esc(r.qty_remaining || '') + '</td>'
        + '<td>' + (r.reorder_needed ? '<span class="vl-bub warn">' + esc(r.reorder_needed) + '</span>' : '') + '</td></tr>';
    }).join('') + '</tbody></table></div>';
  waVolPaint(html);
}

/* ---------- router for the page ---------- */
async function waVolRender() {
  try {
    await waVolLoad(false);
    if (WA_VOL_VIEW === 'event' && WA_VOL_EVENT) {
      if (!WA_VOL_DETAIL || WA_VOL_DETAIL.id !== WA_VOL_EVENT) {
        waVolPaint('<p class="vl-empty">Loading the serving...</p>');
        await waVolLoadEvent(WA_VOL_EVENT);
      }
      waVolRenderEvent();
    } else if (WA_VOL_VIEW === 'sop') {
      waVolRenderSop();
    } else if (WA_VOL_VIEW === 'people') {
      await waVolLoadPeople();
      waVolRenderPeople();
    } else if (WA_VOL_VIEW === 'inventory') {
      await waVolLoadInv();
      waVolRenderInv();
    } else {
      waVolRenderCalendar();
    }
  } catch (e) {
    waVolPaint('<p class="vl-empty">Could not load the volunteering data. ' + esc(String(e && e.message || e)) + '</p>');
  }
}

/* ---------- writes ---------- */
async function waVolToggleStep(id, done, box) {
  var patch = { done: done, done_at: done ? new Date().toISOString() : null, done_by: done ? who() : null, done_source: done ? 'manual' : 'manual_off' }; /* wave191: a person's untick is never overridden by the Hub */
  var r = await db.from('sop_event_steps').update(patch).eq('id', Number(id));
  if (r.error) { toast('Could not save that step'); if (box) box.checked = !done; return; }
  var row = document.querySelector('[data-vlstep="' + id + '"]');
  if (row) row.classList.toggle('done', done);
  if (WA_VOL_DETAIL) {
    WA_VOL_DETAIL.steps.forEach(function (s) { if (String(s.id) === String(id)) { s.done = done; s.done_by = patch.done_by; } });
  }
  WA_VOL_LOADED = false;
}
async function waVolSaveStepField(id, field, value) {
  var patch = {};
  patch[field] = value || null;
  var r = await db.from('sop_event_steps').update(patch).eq('id', Number(id));
  if (r.error) toast('Could not save');
  else toast('Saved');
}
async function waVolSaveRoster(id, status) {
  status = status || null;
  var r = await db.from('event_roster').update({ status: status }).eq('id', Number(id));
  if (r.error) { toast('Could not save'); return; }
  toast('Roster updated');
  if (WA_VOL_DETAIL) WA_VOL_DETAIL.roster.forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.id) === String(id)) x.status = status; });
  WA_VOL_LOADED = false;
}
async function waVolToggleExtra(id, done, box) {
  var r = await db.from('sop_event_extras').update({ done: done }).eq('id', Number(id));
  if (r.error) { toast('Could not save'); if (box) box.checked = !done; return; }
  if (WA_VOL_DETAIL) WA_VOL_DETAIL.extras.forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.id) === String(id)) x.done = done; });
  WA_VOL_LOADED = false;
}
function waVolAddExtraModal() {
  openModal('<h3 style="margin:0 0 12px">Add a task for this event</h3>'
    + '<div class="m-row"><label>Task</label><input id="vlxT" class="vl-in" style="width:100%" placeholder="What needs doing" /></div>'
    + '<div class="m-row"><label>Detail</label><textarea id="vlxD" class="vl-in" style="width:100%;min-height:70px"></textarea></div>'
    + '<div class="m-row"><label>Owner</label><input id="vlxO" class="vl-in" style="width:100%" value="' + esc(who()) + '" /></div>'
    + '<div class="m-row"><label>Due</label><input id="vlxU" class="vl-in" type="date" /></div>'
    + '<div class="m-foot"><button class="btn ghost" data-vlx="cancel">Cancel</button><button class="btn" data-vlx="save">Add task</button></div>');
}
async function waVolSaveExtra() {
  var t = (document.getElementById('vlxT') || {}).value || '';
  if (!t.trim()) { toast('Give the task a name'); return; }
  var row = {
    event_id: WA_VOL_EVENT,
    title: t.trim(),
    detail: ((document.getElementById('vlxD') || {}).value || '').trim() || null,
    owner: ((document.getElementById('vlxO') || {}).value || '').trim() || null,
    due_date: ((document.getElementById('vlxU') || {}).value || '') || null,
    source: 'manual'
  };
  var r = await db.from('sop_event_extras').insert(row);
  if (r.error) { toast('Could not add that'); return; }
  closeModal();
  WA_VOL_LOADED = false;
  WA_VOL_DETAIL = null;
  toast('Task added');
  waVolRender();
}

/* ---------- events ---------- */
document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
  var t = e.target;
  var sub = t.closest && t.closest('[data-vl]');
  if (sub) {
    WA_VOL_VIEW = sub.getAttribute('data-vl');
    WA_VOL_EVENT = null;
    location.hash = WA_VOL_VIEW === 'calendar' ? '#volunteering' : '#volunteering/' + WA_VOL_VIEW;
    return;
  }
  var ev = t.closest && t.closest('[data-vlev]');
  if (ev) {
    location.hash = '#volunteering/e/' + ev.getAttribute('data-vlev');
    return;
  }
  var more = t.closest && t.closest('[data-vlmore]');
  if (more) {
    var box = document.getElementById('vld-' + more.getAttribute('data-vlmore'));
    if (box) box.classList.toggle('open');
    return;
  }
  var shot = t.closest && t.closest('[data-vlshot]');
  if (shot) {
    openModal('<div class="vl-lb"><img src="' + esc(shot.getAttribute('data-vlshot')) + '" alt="Screenshot" /></div>'
      + '<div class="m-foot"><a class="btn ghost" href="' + esc(shot.getAttribute('data-vlshot')) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open full size</a></div>');
    return;
  }
  var role = t.closest && t.closest('[data-vlrole]');
  if (role && document.getElementById('volunteering') && document.getElementById('volunteering').classList.contains('active')) {
    WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER = role.getAttribute('data-vlrole');
    waVolRenderPeople();
    return;
  }
  var add = t.closest && t.closest('[data-vladd]');
  if (add) { waVolAddExtraModal(); return; }
  var xact = t.closest && t.closest('[data-vlx]');
  if (xact) {
    if (xact.getAttribute('data-vlx') === 'save') waVolSaveExtra();
    else closeModal();
    return;
  }
});
document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
  var t = e.target;
  if (!t || !t.getAttribute) return;
  if (t.getAttribute('data-vlchk')) { waVolToggleStep(t.getAttribute('data-vlchk'), t.checked, t); return; }
  if (t.getAttribute('data-vlxchk')) { waVolToggleExtra(t.getAttribute('data-vlxchk'), t.checked, t); return; }
  if (t.getAttribute('data-vlrstat')) { waVolSaveRoster(t.getAttribute('data-vlrstat'), t.value); return; }
  if (t.getAttribute('data-vlowner')) { waVolSaveStepField(t.getAttribute('data-vlowner'), 'owner', t.value); return; }
  if (t.getAttribute('data-vldue')) { waVolSaveStepField(t.getAttribute('data-vldue'), 'due_date', t.value); return; }
});

/* ---------- panel, route, tab ---------- */
(function () {
  var main = document.querySelector('main');
  if (main && !document.getElementById('volunteering')) {
    var sec = document.createElement('section');
    sec.id = 'volunteering';
    sec.className = 'panel';
    main.appendChild(sec);
  }
  function openVol(h) {
    if (h !== 'volunteering' && h.indexOf('volunteering/') !== 0) return false;
    if (!isStaff()) return false;
    var rest = h.slice('volunteering'.length).replace(/^\//, '');
    if (!rest) { WA_VOL_VIEW = 'calendar'; WA_VOL_EVENT = null; }
    else if (rest.indexOf('e/') === 0) { WA_VOL_VIEW = 'event'; WA_VOL_EVENT = Number(rest.slice(2)); }
    else { WA_VOL_VIEW = rest; WA_VOL_EVENT = null; }
    showPanel('volunteering');
    waVolRender();
    return true;
  }
  var __ah20 = applyHash;
  applyHash = function () {
    var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
    if (openVol(h)) return;
    __ah20();
  };
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () {
    try {
      var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
      if (h === 'volunteering' || h.indexOf('volunteering/') === 0) openVol(h);
    } catch (err) { /* boot */ }
  });
  var __sa20 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa20();
    try {
      if (!isStaff()) return;
      var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
      if (bar && !bar.querySelector('[data-tab="volunteering"]')) {
        var b = document.createElement('button');
        b.className = 'tab';
        b.dataset.tab = 'volunteering';
        b.innerHTML = '<span class="tabdot" style="background:#103F39"></span>Volunteering';
        bar.insertBefore(b, bar.querySelector('.tab-group') || null);
      }
      var h0 = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
      if (h0 === 'volunteering' || h0.indexOf('volunteering/') === 0) applyHash();
    } catch (err) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================

   Collapsible per-meeting updates table (meeting, key updates, who was
   speaking), assignee filtering, and board and calendar task views with
   name bubbles, on every department section. Wraps renderSection; the
   list view keeps the original rendering untouched.
   ===================================================================== */

var WA21_WHO = {};
var WA21_MON = {};
var WA21_TX = null;
var WA21_ACTIVE = null;

  st.id = 'wa21css';
  st.textContent = css;

function wa21Initials(name) {
  var p = String(name || '').trim().split(/\s+/);
  var a = (p[0] || '').charAt(0);
  var b = p.length > 1 ? p[p.length - 1].charAt(0) : (p[0] || '').charAt(1);
  return (a + (b || '')).toUpperCase();
}
function wa21Hue(name) {
  var h = 0, s = String(name || '');
  for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) % 360;
  return h;
}
function wa21Bubble(name, small) {
  return '<span class="wa21-bub' + (small ? ' sm' : '') + '" title="' + esc(name) + '" style="background:hsl(' + wa21Hue(name) + ',45%,37%)">' + esc(wa21Initials(name)) + '</span>';
}
function wa21TaskNames(t) {
  var out = [];
  (t.assignees || []).forEach(function (a) { if (a && a.name && out.indexOf(a.name) < 0) out.push(a.name); });
  if (!out.length && t.owner) {
    String(t.owner).split(/[;,]/).forEach(function (x) {
      x = x.replace(/\(.*?\)/g, '').trim();
      if (x && out.indexOf(x) < 0) out.push(x);
    });
  }
  return out;
}
function wa21Trunc(s, n) {
  s = String(s || '');
  return s.length > n ? s.slice(0, n - 1) + '...' : s;
}

async function wa21LoadTx() {
  if (WA21_TX) return;
  try {
    var r = await db.from('transcripts').select('id, title, meeting_date, department, speakers').order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(400);
    WA21_TX = (r && r.data) || [];
  } catch (e) { WA21_TX = []; }
}

function wa21Speakers(id, group) {
  var by = String(group.by || '');
  if (by.indexOf('AI: ') === 0 && WA21_TX) {
    var title = by.slice(4);
    var cands = WA21_TX.filter(function (t) { return t.department === id && t.title === title; });
    if (cands.length) {
      var at = new Date(group.at || 0).getTime();
      cands.sort(function (a, b) {
        var da = Math.abs(new Date((a.meeting_date || '1970-01-01') + 'T12:00:00').getTime() - at);
        var db2 = Math.abs(new Date((b.meeting_date || '1970-01-01') + 'T12:00:00').getTime() - at);
        return da - db2;
      });
      return cands[0].speakers || [];
    }
    return [];
  }
  if (by.indexOf('@') > 0) {
    var nm = by.split('@')[0].replace(/[._]/g, ' ').split(' ').map(function (w) {
      return w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1);
    }).join(' ').trim();
    return nm ? [nm] : [];
  }
  return by ? [by] : [];
}

function wa21UpdatesTable(id, card) {
  var heads = card.querySelectorAll('h3.block');
  var head = null;
  for (var i = 0; i < heads.length; i++) {
    if (heads[i].textContent.trim() === 'Most recent updates') { head = heads[i]; break; }
  }
  if (!head) return;
  var ul = head.nextElementSibling;
  if (!ul || !ul.classList || !ul.classList.contains('updates')) return;

  var rows = (typeof DB_UPDATES !== 'undefined' ? DB_UPDATES : []).filter(function (r) { return r.department === id; });
  if (!rows.length) return;
  rows.sort(function (a, b) { return new Date(b.created_at) - new Date(a.created_at); });

  var groups = [];
  var byLabel = {};
  rows.forEach(function (r) {
    var key = (r.entry_label || 'Undated') + '|' + String(r.added_by || '');
    if (!byLabel[key]) {
      byLabel[key] = { label: r.entry_label || 'Undated', by: r.added_by || '', at: r.created_at, rows: [] };
      groups.push(byLabel[key]);
    }
    byLabel[key].rows.push(r);
  });
  groups.forEach(function (g) {
    g.rows.sort(function (a, b) { return new Date(a.created_at) - new Date(b.created_at); });
  });

  var body = groups.map(function (g, gi) {
    var lis = g.rows.map(function (u, i) {
      var lock = u.confidential && isStaff() ? '<span class="lock-tag">board hidden</span> ' : '';
      var cls = i >= 3 ? ' class="wa21-hid"' : '';
      return '<li' + cls + '>' + lock + u.text + '</li>';
    }).join('');
    var more = g.rows.length > 3
      ? '<a class="wa21-more" data-wa21more="' + gi + '">+' + (g.rows.length - 3) + ' more</a>'
      : '';
    var speakers = wa21Speakers(id, g);
    var who = speakers.length
      ? '<span class="wa21-bubs">' + speakers.slice(0, 6).map(function (n) { return wa21Bubble(n, true); }).join('') +
        (speakers.length > 6 ? '<span class="wa21-names">+' + (speakers.length - 6) + '</span>' : '') + '</span>' +
        '<div class="wa21-names">' + esc(speakers.slice(0, 6).map(function (n) { return n.split(' ')[0]; }).join(', ')) + '</div>'
      : '<span class="wa21-names">&middot;</span>';
    return '<tr><td class="wa21-mt">' + esc(g.label) + '</td>' +
      '<td><ul class="wa21-b" data-wa21g="' + gi + '">' + lis + '</ul>' + more + '</td>' +
      '<td>' + who + '</td></tr>';
  }).join('');

  var det = document.createElement('details');
  det.className = 'wa21-upd';
  det.innerHTML = '<summary>Most recent updates: ' + groups.length + (groups.length === 1 ? ' meeting' : ' meetings') +
    '<span class="wa21-hint">latest ' + esc(groups[0].label) + '</span></summary>' +
    '<div class="wa21-updwrap"><table class="wa21-updtab"><thead><tr><th>Meeting</th><th>Key updates</th><th>Who was speaking</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
    body + '</tbody></table></div>';
  head.style.display = 'none';
  ul.parentNode.insertBefore(det, ul);
  ul.remove();
  det.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var a = e.target.closest('[data-wa21more]');
    if (!a) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    var g = det.querySelector('[data-wa21g="' + a.getAttribute('data-wa21more') + '"]');
    if (!g) return;
    var hid = g.querySelectorAll('.wa21-hid');
    var open = hid.length && hid[0].classList.contains('show');
    for (var i = 0; i < hid.length; i++) hid[i].classList.toggle('show', !open);
    a.textContent = open ? '+' + hid.length + ' more' : 'show fewer';
  });
}

function wa21DueChip(t) {
  if (!t.due) return '';
  var today = new Date(); today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  var d = new Date(t.due + 'T12:00:00');
  var cls = '';
  if (!t.done) {
    var diff = (d - today) / 86400000;
    if (diff < 0) cls = ' late'; else if (diff <= 7) cls = ' soon';
  }
  var lbl = d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
  return '<span class="wa21-due' + cls + '">' + esc(lbl) + '</span>';
}

function wa21Card(t) {
  var flag = t.flag ? '<span title="Flagged">&#9873; </span>' : '';
  var st = t.status && t.status !== 'Open' ? '<span class="wa21-st" title="' + esc(t.status) + '">' + esc(wa21Trunc(t.status, 22)) + '</span>' : '';
  return '<div class="wa21-card" data-wa21t="' + t.ref + '"><div class="wa21-ct">' + flag + esc(t.title) + '</div>' +
    ((t.due || st) ? '<div class="wa21-cm">' + wa21DueChip(t) + st + '</div>' : '') + '</div>';
}

function wa21Board(id, tasks) {
  var open = tasks.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; });
  var cols = [];
  var byName = {};
  open.forEach(function (t) {
    var names = wa21TaskNames(t);
    if (!names.length) names = ['Unassigned'];
    names.forEach(function (n) {
      if (!byName[n]) { byName[n] = []; cols.push(n); }
      byName[n].push(t);
    });
  });
  cols.sort(function (a, b) {
    if (a === 'Unassigned') return 1;
    if (b === 'Unassigned') return -1;
    return byName[b].length - byName[a].length || a.localeCompare(b);
  });
  if (!cols.length) return '<p style="color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);font-size:12.5px;">No open tasks for this view.</p>';
  return '<div class="wa21-board">' + cols.map(function (n) {
    return '<div class="wa21-col"><div class="wa21-colh">' +
      (n === 'Unassigned' ? '' : wa21Bubble(n)) + '<span>' + esc(n) + '</span><span class="n">' + byName[n].length + '</span></div>' +
      byName[n].map(wa21Card).join('') + '</div>';
  }).join('') + '</div>';
}

function wa21Cal(id, tasks) {
  var mon = WA21_MON[id];
  if (!mon) {
    var now = new Date();
    mon = now.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (now.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2);
    WA21_MON[id] = mon;
  }
  var y = Number(mon.slice(0, 4)), m = Number(mon.slice(5, 7)) - 1;
  var first = new Date(y, m, 1);
  var daysIn = new Date(y, m + 1, 0).getDate();
  var lead = first.getDay();
  var todayIso = (function () { var d = new Date(); return d.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' + d.getDate()).slice(-2); })();

  var open = tasks.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; });
  var inMonth = {};
  var other = 0, noDue = [];
  open.forEach(function (t) {
    if (!t.due) { noDue.push(t); return; }
    if (t.due.slice(0, 7) === mon) {
      var day = Number(t.due.slice(8, 10));
      if (!inMonth[day]) inMonth[day] = [];
      inMonth[day].push(t);
    } else { other += 1; }
  });

  var title = first.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
  var html = '<div class="wa21-calh"><button data-wa21m="-1" title="Previous month">&#9664;</button><b>' + esc(title) + '</b><button data-wa21m="1" title="Next month">&#9654;</button></div>';
  html += '<div class="wa21-cal">';
  ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'].forEach(function (d) { html += '<div class="wa21-dow">' + d + '</div>'; });
  var cell;
  for (cell = 0; cell < lead; cell++) html += '<div class="wa21-day out"></div>';
  for (var day = 1; day <= daysIn; day++) {
    var iso = mon + '-' + ('0' + day).slice(-2);
    var chips = (inMonth[day] || []).map(function (t) {
      var names = wa21TaskNames(t);
      return '<div class="wa21-chip" data-wa21t="' + t.ref + '" title="' + esc(t.title + (names.length ? ' (' + names.join(', ') + ')' : '')) + '">' +
        names.slice(0, 2).map(function (n) { return wa21Bubble(n, true); }).join('') +
        '<span>' + esc(wa21Trunc(t.title, 16)) + '</span></div>';
    }).join('');
    html += '<div class="wa21-day' + (iso === todayIso ? ' today' : '') + '"><div class="wa21-dn">' + day + '</div>' + chips + '</div>';
  }
  var trail = (lead + daysIn) % 7;
  if (trail) for (cell = trail; cell < 7; cell++) html += '<div class="wa21-day out"></div>';
  html += '</div>';

  var monthCount = Object.keys(inMonth).reduce(function (n, k) { return n + inMonth[k].length; }, 0);
  html += '<div class="wa21-calfoot">' + monthCount + ' due this month &middot; ' + other + ' due in other months &middot; ' + noDue.length + ' with no due date';
  if (noDue.length) {
    html += '<details><summary>No due date (' + noDue.length + ')</summary><ul>' +
      noDue.map(function (t) {
        var names = wa21TaskNames(t);
        return '<li data-wa21t="' + t.ref + '">' + esc(t.title) + (names.length ? ' <span class="wa21-names" style="display:inline">(' + esc(names.join(', ')) + ')</span>' : '') + '</li>';
      }).join('') + '</ul></details>';
  }
  html += '</div>';
  return html;
}

function wa21EditTask(id, ref) {
  if (!isStaff() || typeof taskModal !== 'function') return;
  try {
    var t = getTasks(id).filter(function (x) { return String(x.ref) === String(ref); })[0];
    if (t) taskModal(id, t);
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
}

function wa21Controls(id, card) {
  var heads = card.querySelectorAll('h3.block');
  var head = null;
  for (var i = 0; i < heads.length; i++) {
    var t = heads[i].textContent.trim();
    if (t === 'Workstreams' || t.indexOf('Open items') === 0) { head = heads[i]; break; }
  }
  if (!head) return;

  var wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.className = 'wa21-list';
  var stop = null;
  var walk = head;
  var toMove = [];
  while (walk) {
    var isStop = (walk.classList && (walk.classList.contains('add-row') || walk.classList.contains('completed'))) ||
      (walk.tagName === 'H3' && walk !== head && walk.textContent.indexOf('Transcripts that contributed') >= 0);
    if (isStop) { stop = walk; break; }
    toMove.push(walk);
    walk = walk.nextElementSibling;
  }
  head.parentNode.insertBefore(wrap, head);
  toMove.forEach(function (n) { wrap.appendChild(n); });

  var tasksAll = getTasks(id);
  var names = [];
  tasksAll.forEach(function (t) {
    wa21TaskNames(t).forEach(function (n) { if (names.indexOf(n) < 0) names.push(n); });
  });
  names.sort(function (a, b) { return a.localeCompare(b); });
  var cur = WA21_WHO[id] || '';
  if (cur && names.indexOf(cur) < 0) { cur = ''; WA21_WHO[id] = ''; }
  var view = WA21_VIEW[id] || 'list';

  var ctl = document.createElement('div');
  ctl.className = 'wa21-ctl';
  ctl.innerHTML = '<span class="wa21-ctlab">Tasks</span>' +
    '<select class="wa21-who"><option value="">Everyone</option>' +
    names.map(function (n) { return '<option value="' + esc(n) + '"' + (n === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(n) + '</option>'; }).join('') +
    '</select>' +
    '<span class="wa21-seg">' +
    [['list', 'List'], ['board', 'Board'], ['cal', 'Calendar']].map(function (v) {
      return '<button data-wa21v="' + v[0] + '"' + (view === v[0] ? ' class="on"' : '') + '>' + v[1] + '</button>';
    }).join('') + '</span>';
  card.insertBefore(ctl, wrap);

  var alt = document.createElement('div');
  alt.className = 'wa21-alt';
  card.insertBefore(alt, wrap.nextSibling);

  var f = WA21_WHO[id] || '';
  var shown = f ? tasksAll.filter(function (t) {
    var fl = f.toLowerCase();
    return wa21TaskNames(t).some(function (n) { return n.toLowerCase() === fl; }) ||
      String(t.owner || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(fl) >= 0;
  }) : tasksAll;

  if (view === 'board') { wrap.style.display = 'none'; alt.innerHTML = wa21Board(id, shown); }
  else if (view === 'cal') { wrap.style.display = 'none'; alt.innerHTML = wa21Cal(id, shown); }

  ctl.querySelector('.wa21-who').addEventListener('change', function () {
    WA21_WHO[id] = this.value;
    renderSection(id);
  });
  ctl.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target.closest('[data-wa21v]');
    if (!b) return;
    WA21_VIEW[id] = b.getAttribute('data-wa21v');
    renderSection(id);
  });
  alt.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var mBtn = e.target.closest('[data-wa21m]');
    if (mBtn) {
      var mon = WA21_MON[id];
      if (!mon) { var now = new Date(); mon = now.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (now.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2); }
      var y = Number(mon.slice(0, 4)), m = Number(mon.slice(5, 7)) - 1 + Number(mBtn.getAttribute('data-wa21m'));
      var d = new Date(y, m, 1);
      WA21_MON[id] = d.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2);
      renderSection(id);
      return;
    }
    var tEl = e.target.closest('[data-wa21t]');
    if (tEl) wa21EditTask(id, tEl.getAttribute('data-wa21t'));
  });
}

function wa21Enhance(id) {
  var el = document.getElementById(id);
  var card = el ? el.querySelector('.dept-card') : null;
  if (!card || card.dataset.wa21) return;
  card.dataset.wa21 = '1';
  try { wa21UpdatesTable(id, card); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  try { wa21Controls(id, card); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
}

(function () {
  var __gt21 = getTasks;
  getTasks = function (id) {
    var rows = __gt21(id);
    if (WA21_ACTIVE !== id) return rows;
    var f = WA21_WHO[id];
    if (!f) return rows;
    var fl = f.toLowerCase();
    return rows.filter(function (t) {
      return wa21TaskNames(t).some(function (n) { return n.toLowerCase() === fl; }) ||
        String(t.owner || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(fl) >= 0;
    });
  };

  var __rs21 = renderSection;
  renderSection = function (id) {
    WA21_ACTIVE = id;
    try { __rs21(id); } finally { WA21_ACTIVE = null; }
    try { wa21Enhance(id); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };

  var __sa21 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa21();
    try {
      await wa21LoadTx();
      if (typeof renderAllSections === 'function') renderAllSections();
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };

  /* If the app somehow booted before this wave installed, enhance in place. */
  try {
    if (document.querySelector('.dept-card')) {
      wa21LoadTx().then(function () {
        try { if (typeof renderAllSections === 'function') renderAllSections(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      });
    }
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v22 finances and budgeting (27/07/26)
   Staff-only Finances page inside the Fundraising department: budget vs
   actual by category (from the Welfare Avenue Budget 2025-2026 sheet),
   spending by department, the full transaction ledger from imported bank
   statements with inline re-allocation and duplicate handling, bank
   statement import (paste or CSV) and manual expense logging.
   Fiscal year Oct 1 to Sep 30. Quarters: Q1 Oct-Dec, Q2 Jan-Mar,
   Q3 Apr-Jun, Q4 Jul-Sep.
   ===================================================================== */

var WA_FIN_LOADED = false;
var WA_FIN_BUD = [];
var WA_FIN_TX = [];
var WA_FIN_STMTS = [];
var WA_FIN_Q = '';
var WA_FIN_DEPT = '';
var WA_FIN_SEARCH = '';
var WA_FIN_EDITS = {};

var WA_FIN_DEPTS = [
  ['opsmtl', 'Ops Montreal'], ['opsto', 'Ops Toronto'], ['marketing', 'Marketing'],
  ['fundraising', 'Fundraising'], ['hr', 'HR'], ['board', 'Board'], ['org', 'Org-wide']
];
var WA_FIN_CATS_IN = ['4100 Donation', '4110 FlagPlus Football', 'Services'];
var WA_FIN_CATS_EX = ['5020 Purchases - COS', 'Supplies and materials - COS', '6010 Transportation fees', '6020 Meal and entertainment expenses', '6030 Subscription fees', '7035 Office expenses', '7090 Telecommunication', '7120 Publicity and Promotion', '7145 Lease payments', 'Other'];

function waFinFYNow() {
  var d = new Date();
  return (d.getMonth() + 1) >= 10 ? d.getFullYear() + 1 : d.getFullYear();
}
function waFinFYLabel() { return 'FY' + String(waFinFYNow()).slice(-2); }
function waFinFYStart() { return (waFinFYNow() - 1) + '-10-01'; }
function waFinFYEnd() { return waFinFYNow() + '-09-30'; }
function waFinQOf(dstr) {
  var m = Number(String(dstr).slice(5, 7));
  if (m >= 10) return 'Q1';
  if (m <= 3) return 'Q2';
  if (m <= 6) return 'Q3';
  return 'Q4';
}
function waFinQNow() {
  var d = new Date();
  return waFinQOf(d.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-01');
}
function waFinMoney(n, dec) {
  var neg = n < 0;
  var v = Math.abs(Number(n) || 0);
  var s = '$' + v.toLocaleString('en-CA', { minimumFractionDigits: dec ? 2 : 0, maximumFractionDigits: dec ? 2 : 0 });
  return neg ? '-' + s : s;
}
function waFinFp(date, amount, desc) {
  var norm = String(desc || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 60);
  return date + '|' + Number(amount).toFixed(2) + '|' + norm;
}

async function waFinLoad(force) {
  if (WA_FIN_LOADED && !force) return;
  var r = await Promise.all([
    db.from('finance_budgets').select('*').eq('fiscal_year', waFinFYLabel()),
    db.from('finance_transactions').select('*').gte('txn_date', waFinFYStart()).lte('txn_date', waFinFYEnd()).order('txn_date', { ascending: false }).order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(1500),
    db.from('finance_statements').select('*').order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(20)
  ]);
  WA_FIN_BUD = r[0].data || [];
  WA_FIN_TX = r[1].data || [];
  WA_FIN_STMTS = r[2].data || [];
  WA_FIN_LOADED = true;
}

function waFinBudget(period, key) {
  var edited = WA_FIN_EDITS[period + '|' + key];
  if (edited !== undefined) return Number(edited) || 0;
  var row = WA_FIN_BUD.filter(function (b) { return b.scope === 'category' && b.period === period && b.key === key; })[0];
  return row ? Number(row.amount) : 0;
}
function waFinActualCat(period, key, income) {
  var sum = 0;
  WA_FIN_TX.forEach(function (t) {
    if (t.duplicate_of !== null && t.duplicate_of !== undefined) return;
    if (t.category !== key) return;
    if (period !== 'FY' && waFinQOf(t.txn_date) !== period) return;
    sum += Number(t.amount);
  });
  return income ? sum : -sum;
}
function waFinDeptSpend(period, dept) {
  var sum = 0;
  WA_FIN_TX.forEach(function (t) {
    if (t.duplicate_of !== null && t.duplicate_of !== undefined) return;
    if (t.department !== dept) return;
    if (WA_FIN_CATS_IN.indexOf(t.category) >= 0) return;
    if (period !== 'FY' && waFinQOf(t.txn_date) !== period) return;
    sum += Number(t.amount);
  });
  return -sum;
}

function waFinBudTable(cats, income, label) {
  var periods = ['Q1', 'Q2', 'Q3', 'Q4', 'FY'];
  var head = '<tr><th>' + label + '</th>' + periods.map(function (p) {
    return '<th>' + p + ' budget</th><th>' + p + ' actual</th>';
  }).join('') + '<th>FY used</th></tr>';
  var totB = { Q1: 0, Q2: 0, Q3: 0, Q4: 0, FY: 0 };
  var totA = { Q1: 0, Q2: 0, Q3: 0, Q4: 0, FY: 0 };
  var rows = cats.map(function (c) {
    var cells = periods.map(function (p) {
      var b = waFinBudget(p, c);
      var a = waFinActualCat(p, c, income);
      totB[p] += b; totA[p] += a;
      var over = !income && b > 0 && a > b;
      var bud = isStaff()
        ? '<input class="fin-bud" data-fbud="' + esc(p + '|' + c) + '" value="' + (b ? b.toFixed(2) : '0') + '" />'
        : waFinMoney(b);
      return '<td>' + bud + '</td><td class="a' + (over ? ' over' : '') + '">' + waFinMoney(a, true) + '</td>';
    }).join('');
    var bFY = waFinBudget('FY', c);
    var aFY = waFinActualCat('FY', c, income);
    var pct = bFY > 0 ? Math.round(aFY / bFY * 100) : (aFY > 0 ? 100 : 0);
    var bar = '<div class="fin-bar" title="' + pct + '% of FY budget"><i class="' + (pct > 100 ? 'over' : '') + '" style="width:' + Math.min(100, Math.max(0, pct)) + '%"></i></div>';
    return '<tr><td>' + esc(c) + '</td>' + cells + '<td>' + bar + ' <span class="a">' + pct + '%</span></td></tr>';
  }).join('');
  var totRow = '<tr class="tot"><td>Total</td>' + periods.map(function (p) {
    return '<td>' + waFinMoney(totB[p]) + '</td><td class="a">' + waFinMoney(totA[p], true) + '</td>';
  }).join('') + '<td></td></tr>';
  return '<div class="fin-wrap"><table class="fin-tab"><thead>' + head + '</thead><tbody>' + rows + totRow + '</tbody></table></div>';
}

function waFinLedger() {
  var q = WA_FIN_Q, d = WA_FIN_DEPT, s = WA_FIN_SEARCH.toLowerCase();
  var rows = WA_FIN_TX.filter(function (t) {
    if (q && waFinQOf(t.txn_date) !== q) return false;
    if (d && t.department !== d) return false;
    if (s && (t.description || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(s) < 0 && (t.category || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(s) < 0) return false;
    return true;
  });
  var shown = rows.slice(0, 250);
  var deptOpts = function (cur) {
    return WA_FIN_DEPTS.map(function (x) {
      return '<option value="' + x[0] + '"' + (x[0] === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + x[1] + '</option>';
    }).join('');
  };
  var catOpts = function (cur) {
    return WA_FIN_CATS_IN.concat(WA_FIN_CATS_EX).map(function (c) {
      return '<option value="' + esc(c) + '"' + (c === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(c) + '</option>';
    }).join('');
  };
  var body = shown.map(function (t) {
    var isDup = t.duplicate_of !== null && t.duplicate_of !== undefined;
    var amt = Number(t.amount);
    var dsel = isStaff()
      ? '<select data-ftd="' + t.id + '">' + deptOpts(t.department) + '</select>'
      : esc((WA_FIN_DEPTS.filter(function (x) { return x[0] === t.department; })[0] || ['', t.department])[1]);
    var csel = isStaff()
      ? '<select data-ftc="' + t.id + '">' + catOpts(t.category) + '</select>'
      : esc(t.category || '');
    var badges = '<span class="fin-badge">' + esc(t.source === 'manual' ? 'manual' : 'statement') + '</span>';
    if (isDup) {
      badges += ' <span class="fin-badge dup" title="Excluded from totals; duplicate of transaction ' + t.duplicate_of + '">duplicate</span>';
      if (isStaff()) badges += ' <a class="fin-lnk" data-fdup="' + t.id + '">not a duplicate</a>';
    } else if (isStaff()) {
      badges += ' <a class="fin-lnk" data-fmarkdup="' + t.id + '" title="Mark as a duplicate and exclude from totals">mark dup</a>';
    }
    var dd = new Date(t.txn_date + 'T12:00:00');
    var dl = dd.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: '2-digit' });
    return '<tr' + (isDup ? ' class="dup"' : '') + '><td>' + esc(dl) + '</td>' +
      '<td class="desc" title="' + esc(t.description) + '">' + esc(String(t.description).slice(0, 46)) + '</td>' +
      '<td class="amt ' + (amt >= 0 ? 'pos' : 'neg') + '">' + waFinMoney(amt, true) + '</td>' +
      '<td>' + dsel + '</td><td>' + csel + '</td><td>' + badges + '</td></tr>';
  }).join('');
  var more = rows.length > shown.length ? '<div class="fin-note">Showing ' + shown.length + ' of ' + rows.length + ' transactions. Narrow the filters to see the rest.</div>' : '';
  return '<div class="fin-wrap"><table class="fin-led"><thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Description</th><th>Amount</th><th>Department</th><th>Category</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
    (body || '<tr><td colspan="6" class="fin-note">No transactions match.</td></tr>') + '</tbody></table></div>' + more;
}

function waFinRender() {
  var sec = document.getElementById('finances');
  if (!sec) return;
  var qNow = waFinQNow();
  var incFY = 0, spendFY = 0, spendQ = 0;
  WA_FIN_TX.forEach(function (t) {
    if (t.duplicate_of !== null && t.duplicate_of !== undefined) return;
    var a = Number(t.amount);
    if (a >= 0) incFY += a; else spendFY += -a;
    if (a < 0 && waFinQOf(t.txn_date) === qNow) spendQ += -a;
  });
  var budExQ = WA_FIN_CATS_EX.reduce(function (n, c) { return n + waFinBudget(qNow, c); }, 0);
  var kpis = '<div class="fin-kpis">' +
    '<div class="fin-kpi"><div class="k">Income FY to date</div><div class="v pos">' + waFinMoney(incFY) + '</div></div>' +
    '<div class="fin-kpi"><div class="k">Spend FY to date</div><div class="v neg">' + waFinMoney(spendFY) + '</div></div>' +
    '<div class="fin-kpi"><div class="k">Net FY to date</div><div class="v ' + (incFY - spendFY >= 0 ? 'pos' : 'neg') + '">' + waFinMoney(incFY - spendFY) + '</div></div>' +
    '<div class="fin-kpi"><div class="k">' + qNow + ' spend vs budget</div><div class="v">' + waFinMoney(spendQ) + ' <span class="a" style="font-size:11px;font-weight:400;">of ' + waFinMoney(budExQ) + '</span></div></div>' +
    '</div>';

  var deptCards = '<div class="fin-dept">' + WA_FIN_DEPTS.map(function (x) {
    var fy = waFinDeptSpend('FY', x[0]);
    var qq = waFinDeptSpend(qNow, x[0]);
    if (!fy && !qq) return '';
    return '<div class="fin-dcard"><div class="n">' + x[1] + '</div><div class="s">' + waFinMoney(fy) + '</div><div class="q">' + qNow + ': ' + waFinMoney(qq) + '</div></div>';
  }).join('') + '</div>';

  var saveBtn = isStaff() && Object.keys(WA_FIN_EDITS).length
    ? ' <button class="btn mini" data-fsave="1">Save budget changes (' + Object.keys(WA_FIN_EDITS).length + ')</button>'
    : '';

  var qOpts = ['', 'Q1', 'Q2', 'Q3', 'Q4'].map(function (p) {
    return '<option value="' + p + '"' + (p === WA_FIN_Q ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + (p || 'All quarters') + '</option>';
  }).join('');
  var dOpts = '<option value="">All departments</option>' + WA_FIN_DEPTS.map(function (x) {
    return '<option value="' + x[0] + '"' + (x[0] === WA_FIN_DEPT ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + x[1] + '</option>';
  }).join('');

  var stmts = WA_FIN_STMTS.length
    ? '<details class="fin-stmts"><summary>Imported statements (' + WA_FIN_STMTS.length + ')</summary><ul>' +
      WA_FIN_STMTS.map(function (s) {
        return '<li>' + esc(s.filename || 'statement') + ': ' + s.txn_count + ' transactions' +
          (s.dup_count ? ' (' + s.dup_count + ' duplicates)' : '') + ', ' + esc(s.status) +
          (s.period_start ? ', ' + esc(s.period_start) + ' to ' + esc(s.period_end || '') : '') + '</li>';
      }).join('') + '</ul></details>'
    : '';

  sec.innerHTML = '<div class="card" style="border-top-color:#103F39">' +
    '<div class="fin-head"><h2 style="color:#103F39">Finances and budgeting</h2>' +
    '<span class="fin-fy">' + waFinFYLabel() + ': Oct 1 to Sep 30 &middot; budgets from the Budget 2025-2026 sheet</span>' +
    (isStaff() ? '<span class="fin-actions"><button class="btn mini" data-fimp="1">Import bank statement</button><button class="btn ghost mini" data-fexp="1">Log an expense</button></span>' : '') +
    '</div>' +
    kpis +
    '<h3 class="block" style="color:#103F39">Budget vs actual: income</h3>' + waFinBudTable(WA_FIN_CATS_IN, true, 'Income line') +
    '<h3 class="block" style="color:#103F39">Budget vs actual: expenses' + saveBtn + '</h3>' + waFinBudTable(WA_FIN_CATS_EX, false, 'Expense line') +
    '<h3 class="block" style="color:#103F39">Spending by department</h3>' +
    '<div class="fin-note">Where the money goes when each transaction is allocated. Duplicates excluded.</div>' + deptCards +
    '<h3 class="block" style="color:#103F39">Transactions</h3>' +
    '<div class="fin-ctl"><select data-fq="1">' + qOpts + '</select><select data-fd="1">' + dOpts + '</select>' +
    '<input type="text" data-fs="1" placeholder="Search description or category" value="' + esc(WA_FIN_SEARCH) + '" /></div>' +
    waFinLedger() + stmts + '</div>';
  waFinWire(sec);
}

function waFinWire(sec) {
  sec.querySelectorAll('[data-fq]').forEach(function (el) {
    el.addEventListener('change', function () { WA_FIN_Q = this.value; waFinRender(); });
  });
  sec.querySelectorAll('[data-fd]').forEach(function (el) {
    el.addEventListener('change', function () { WA_FIN_DEPT = this.value; waFinRender(); });
  });
  sec.querySelectorAll('[data-fs]').forEach(function (el) {
    el.addEventListener('input', function () {
      WA_FIN_SEARCH = this.value;
      clearTimeout(el._t);
      el._t = setTimeout(function () { waFinRender(); }, 350);
    });
  });
  sec.querySelectorAll('input.fin-bud').forEach(function (el) {
    el.addEventListener('change', function () {
      WA_FIN_EDITS[this.getAttribute('data-fbud')] = this.value;
      this.classList.add('chg');
      waFinRender();
    });
  });
  if (!sec.dataset.finWired) {
    sec.dataset.finWired = '1';
    sec.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var t = e.target.closest('[data-fsave],[data-fimp],[data-fexp],[data-fdup],[data-fmarkdup]');
      if (!t) return;
      if (t.hasAttribute('data-fsave')) waFinSaveBudgets();
      else if (t.hasAttribute('data-fimp')) waFinImportModal();
      else if (t.hasAttribute('data-fexp')) waFinExpenseModal();
      else if (t.hasAttribute('data-fdup')) waFinSetDup(Number(t.getAttribute('data-fdup')), null);
      else if (t.hasAttribute('data-fmarkdup')) waFinMarkDup(Number(t.getAttribute('data-fmarkdup')));
    });
  }
  sec.querySelectorAll('[data-ftd]').forEach(function (el) {
    el.addEventListener('change', function () { waFinAlloc(Number(this.getAttribute('data-ftd')), { department: this.value }); });
  });
  sec.querySelectorAll('[data-ftc]').forEach(function (el) {
    el.addEventListener('change', function () { waFinAlloc(Number(this.getAttribute('data-ftc')), { category: this.value }); });
  });
}

async function waFinAlloc(id, patch) {
  if (!isStaff()) return;
  patch.allocated_by = who();
  var r = await db.from('finance_transactions').update(patch).eq('id', id);
  if (r.error) { toast('Save failed'); return; }
  var row = WA_FIN_TX.filter(function (t) { return t.id === id; })[0];
  if (row) { Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { row[k] = patch[k]; }); }
  toast('Reallocated');
  waFinRender();
}

async function waFinSetDup(id, dupOf) {
  if (!isStaff()) return;
  var r = await db.from('finance_transactions').update({ duplicate_of: dupOf }).eq('id', id);
  if (r.error) { toast('Save failed'); return; }
  var row = WA_FIN_TX.filter(function (t) { return t.id === id; })[0];
  if (row) row.duplicate_of = dupOf;
  toast(dupOf === null ? 'Restored to totals' : 'Marked as duplicate');
  waFinRender();
}

async function waFinSaveBudgets() {
  if (!isStaff()) return;
  var keys = Object.keys(WA_FIN_EDITS);
  if (!keys.length) return;
  var rows = keys.map(function (k) {
    var parts = k.split('|');
    var key = parts.slice(1).join('|');
    var kind = WA_FIN_CATS_IN.indexOf(key) >= 0 ? 'income' : 'expense';
    return {
      fiscal_year: waFinFYLabel(), period: parts[0], scope: 'category', key: key,
      kind: kind, amount: Number(WA_FIN_EDITS[k]) || 0, updated_by: who(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };
  });
  var r = await db.from('finance_budgets').upsert(rows, { onConflict: 'fiscal_year,period,scope,key' });
  if (r.error) { toast('Budget save failed'); return; }
  WA_FIN_EDITS = {};
  toast('Budgets saved');
  await waFinLoad(true);
  waFinRender();
}

async function waFinMarkDup(id) {
  var row = WA_FIN_TX.filter(function (t) { return t.id === id; })[0];
  if (!row) return;
  var twin = WA_FIN_TX.filter(function (t) {
    return t.id !== id && t.fingerprint === row.fingerprint && (t.duplicate_of === null || t.duplicate_of === undefined);
  })[0];
  if (!twin) { toast('No matching transaction found to pair it with'); return; }
  waFinSetDup(id, twin.id);
}

function waFinImportModal() {
  openModal('<h3>Import a bank statement</h3>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">Paste the statement text (copy everything from the PDF) or choose a CSV export. The Hub extracts every transaction, allocates each one to a department and budget category, and flags duplicates.</p>' +
    '<label>Statement file name</label><input type="text" id="finFile" placeholder="July 31, 2026.pdf" />' +
    '<label>CSV or text file</label><input type="file" id="finUpload" accept=".csv,.txt" />' +
    '<label>Or paste the statement text</label><textarea id="finText" rows="8" placeholder="Paste the full statement text here"></textarea>' +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="finGo">Import</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="finCancel">Cancel</button></div>');
  var up = document.getElementById('finUpload');
  if (up) up.addEventListener('change', function () {
    var f = this.files && this.files[0];
    if (!f) return;
    var fr = new FileReader();
    fr.onload = function () {
      document.getElementById('finText').value = String(fr.result || '').slice(0, 80000);
      var fn = document.getElementById('finFile');
      if (fn && !fn.value) fn.value = f.name;
    };
    fr.readAsText(f);
  });
  var cancel = document.getElementById('finCancel');
  if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  var go = document.getElementById('finGo');
  if (go) go.addEventListener('click', async function () {
    var text = (document.getElementById('finText').value || '').trim();
    var fname = (document.getElementById('finFile').value || '').trim() || 'pasted statement';
    if (text.length < 60) { toast('Paste the statement text first'); return; }
    go.disabled = true;
    go.textContent = 'Importing (about a minute)...';
    try {
      var r = await db.functions.invoke('finance-import', { body: { statement_text: text, filename: fname } });
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      var d = r.data || {};
      closeModal();
      toast('Imported ' + (d.imported || 0) + ' transactions' + (d.duplicates ? ', ' + d.duplicates + ' duplicates flagged' : ''));
      await waFinLoad(true);
      waFinRender();
    } catch (err) {
      go.disabled = false;
      go.textContent = 'Import';
      toast('Import failed');
    }
  });
}

function waFinExpenseModal() {
  var today = new Date();
  var iso = today.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (today.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' + today.getDate()).slice(-2);
  openModal('<h3>Log an expense</h3>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">For cash spends and anything that never hits the bank account.</p>' +
    '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Date</label><input type="date" id="feDate" value="' + iso + '" /></div>' +
    '<div><label>Amount (CAD)</label><input type="number" step="0.01" min="0" id="feAmt" placeholder="25.00" /></div></div>' +
    '<label>Description</label><input type="text" id="feDesc" placeholder="What was it for" />' +
    '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Department</label><select id="feDept">' +
    WA_FIN_DEPTS.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x[0] + '">' + x[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') +
    '</select></div><div><label>Category</label><select id="feCat">' +
    WA_FIN_CATS_EX.map(function (c) { return '<option value="' + esc(c) + '">' + esc(c) + '</option>'; }).join('') +
    '</select></div></div>' +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="feGo">Save expense</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="feCancel">Cancel</button></div>');
  var cancel = document.getElementById('feCancel');
  if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  var go = document.getElementById('feGo');
  if (go) go.addEventListener('click', async function () {
    var date = document.getElementById('feDate').value;
    var amt = Math.abs(Number(document.getElementById('feAmt').value || 0));
    var desc = (document.getElementById('feDesc').value || '').trim();
    if (!date || !amt || !desc) { toast('Date, amount and description are needed'); return; }
    var row = {
      txn_date: date, description: desc, amount: -amt,
      department: document.getElementById('feDept').value,
      category: document.getElementById('feCat').value,
      source: 'manual', allocated_by: who(),
      fingerprint: waFinFp(date, -amt, desc)
    };
    var r = await db.from('finance_transactions').insert(row);
    if (r.error) { toast('Save failed'); return; }
    closeModal();
    toast('Expense logged');
    await waFinLoad(true);
    waFinRender();
  });
}

    }
    return true;
  }

  var __ah22 = applyHash;
  applyHash = function () {
    var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');

    __ah22();
  };
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', function () {
    try {
      var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
      if (h === 'finances') openFin(h);
    } catch (err) { /* boot */ }
  });

  var __rs22 = renderSection;
  renderSection = function (id) {
    __rs22(id);
    try {
      return;   /* wave164: Finance is not part of Fundraising, so the Fundraising page no longer links to it */

      if (!card || card.querySelector('[data-fin-open]')) return;

      btn.className = 'btn mini';
      btn.setAttribute('data-fin-open', '1');
      btn.style.margin = '10px 0 0';
      btn.innerHTML = 'Finances and budgeting &raquo;';
      btn.addEventListener('click', function () { location.hash = '#finances'; });
      var h2 = card.querySelector('h2');
      if (h2 && h2.parentNode === card) card.insertBefore(btn, h2.nextSibling);
      else card.insertBefore(btn, card.firstChild);
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };

  var __sa22 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa22();
    try {
      var h0 = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
      if (h0 === 'finances') applyHash();
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v23 volunteer texting (27/07/26)
   "Text volunteers" on the Volunteering pages: pick an audience (this
   event's roster with a status filter, a tag like cooks or drivers, or
   everyone with a phone), write the message with {{first_name}} and
   {{event_date}} placeholders, PREVIEW the exact personalized texts
   (dry run, nothing sends), then send for real from the Welfare Phone.
   Backed by the quo-send edge function; every send is logged.
   ===================================================================== */

var WA_TXT_PAYLOAD = null;

function waTxtEventDate(ev) {
  if (!ev || !ev.event_date) return '';
  var d = new Date(ev.event_date + 'T12:00:00');
  return d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' });
}

function waTxtCurrentEvent() {
  if (WA_VOL_VIEW === 'event' && WA_VOL_EVENT) {
    var hit = null;
    WA_VOL_EVENTS.forEach(function (e) { if (e.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) hit = e; });
    return hit;
  }
  return null;
}

function waTxtAudiences(ev) {
  var a = [];
  if (ev) a.push(['roster', 'This event roster']);
  a.push(['cook', 'All cooks'], ['driver', 'All drivers'], ['server', 'All servers'],
    ['dishwasher', 'All dishwashers'], ['photographer', 'All photographers'], ['videographer', 'All videographers'],
    ['all', 'Everyone with a phone']);
  return a;
}

async function waTxtResolve(aud, stat) {
  var ids = [], phones = [], names = 0;
  if (aud === 'roster' && WA_VOL_DETAIL) {
    WA_VOL_DETAIL.roster.forEach(function (r) {
      if (stat && (String(r.status || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(stat) !== 0)) return;
      if (r.person_id) { ids.push(r.person_id); }
      else {
        var d = r.detail || {};
        var digits = String(d.phone || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
        if (digits.length >= 10) phones.push(digits.slice(-10));
      }
      names += 1;
    });
  } else {
    await waVolLoadPeople();
    WA_VOL_PEOPLE.forEach(function (p) {
      if (!p.phones || !p.phones.length) return;
      if (aud === 'all' || (p.tags || []).indexOf(aud) >= 0 || (p.roles || []).indexOf(aud) >= 0) ids.push(p.id);
    });
  }
  return { ids: ids, phones: phones, names: names };
}

function waTxtModal() {
  var ev = waTxtCurrentEvent();
  var dateStr = waTxtEventDate(ev || (typeof waVolNextEvent === 'function' ? waVolNextEvent() : null));
  var defMsg = ev
    ? 'Hi {{first_name}}, confirming you for the {{event_date}} serving event with Welfare Avenue. Reply YES to confirm or NO if you cannot make it. Thank you!'
    : 'Hi {{first_name}}, it is Welfare Avenue. We would love your help at our Sunday serving event on {{event_date}}. Can you make it? Reply YES or NO. Thank you!';
  var audOpts = waTxtAudiences(ev).map(function (x) {
    return '<option value="' + x[0] + '">' + x[1] + '</option>';
  }).join('');
  openModal('<h3>Text volunteers</h3>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">From the Welfare Phone (+1 438 808 7754). Preview first: nothing sends until you hit the send button. People sharing a phone number get one text.</p>' +
    '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Who</label><select id="vtAud">' + audOpts + '</select></div>' +
    '<div id="vtStatWrap"' + (ev ? '' : ' style="display:none"') + '><label>Roster status</label><select id="vtStat">' +
    '<option value="">Everyone on the roster</option><option value="confirm">Confirmed</option>' +
    '<option value="waiting">Waiting for response</option><option value="tbd">TBD</option></select></div></div>' +
    '<label>Message ({{first_name}} and {{event_date}} fill in automatically)</label>' +
    '<textarea id="vtMsg" rows="5">' + esc(defMsg) + '</textarea>' +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="vtPrev">Preview</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="vtCancel">Cancel</button></div>' +
    '<div id="vtOut"></div>');
  WA_TXT_PAYLOAD = null;
  var cancel = document.getElementById('vtCancel');
  if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  var audSel = document.getElementById('vtAud');
  if (audSel) audSel.addEventListener('change', function () {
    var w = document.getElementById('vtStatWrap');
    if (w) w.style.display = this.value === 'roster' ? '' : 'none';
  });
  var prev = document.getElementById('vtPrev');
  if (prev) prev.addEventListener('click', function () { waTxtPreview(ev, dateStr); });
}

async function waTxtPreview(ev, dateStr) { /* superseded: wave31 reset without capture; superseded; body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }

async function waTxtSend() {
  if (!WA_TXT_PAYLOAD) return;
  var btn = document.getElementById('vtSend');
  if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Sending...'; }
  try {
    var payload = {};
    Object.keys(WA_TXT_PAYLOAD).forEach(function (k) { payload[k] = WA_TXT_PAYLOAD[k]; });
    payload.dry_run = false;
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('quo-send', { body: payload });
    if (r.error) throw r.error;
    var d = r.data || {};
    closeModal();
    toast(d.queued ? 'Queued ' + d.queued + (d.queued === 1 ? ' text' : ' texts') + ': they go at 9 am' : 'Sent ' + (d.sent || 0) + (d.sent === 1 ? ' text' : ' texts') + (d.failed ? ', ' + d.failed + ' failed' : ''));
  } catch (e) {
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Send now'; }
    toast('Send failed');
  }
}

        s.setAttribute('data-vtxt', '1');
        s.style.background = '#103F39';
        s.style.color = '#FFFAF2';
        s.textContent = 'Text volunteers';
        sub.appendChild(s);
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };

  function wire() {

    if (!sec || sec.dataset.txtWired) return;
    sec.dataset.txtWired = '1';

      try { waTxtModal(); } catch (err) { /* non-fatal */ }
    });
  }
  wire();
  var __sa23 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa23();
    try { wire(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();

function wa24Nav() {
  var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
  if (!bar) return;
  ['volunteering', 'create'].forEach(function (id) {
    var b = bar.querySelector('[data-tab="' + id + '"]');
    if (b) b.remove();
  });
}

function wa24Links(id) {
  var map = {
    opsmtl: [['#volunteering', 'Volunteering programme']],
    marketing: [['#create', 'Create content']]   /* wave164: Fundraising no longer links to Finance (they are separate) */
  };
  var links = map[id];
  if (!links) return;
  if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
  var card = document.querySelector('#' + id + ' .dept-card');
  if (!card || card.querySelector('.wa24-links')) return;
  var old = card.querySelector('[data-fin-open]');
  if (old) old.remove();
  var row = document.createElement('div');
  row.className = 'wa24-links';
  links.forEach(function (l) {
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.innerHTML = l[1] + ' &raquo;';
    b.addEventListener('click', function () { location.hash = l[0]; });
    row.appendChild(b);
  });
  var anchor = card.querySelector('.dept-banner') || card.querySelector('h2');
  if (anchor) anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', row);
  else card.insertBefore(row, card.firstChild);
}

function wa24SopModal(tid) {
  var t = null;
  WA_VOL_TMPL.forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.id) === String(tid)) t = x; });
  if (!t) return;
  openModal('<h3>Edit this SOP step</h3>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">This edits the master template. The name, notes and help link change everywhere immediately. The assignee applies to all upcoming planned events; events already in progress keep their own assignments.</p>' +
    '<label>Step name</label><input type="text" id="w24Name" value="' + esc(t.name || '') + '" />' +
    '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Assignee</label><input type="text" id="w24Owner" value="' + esc(t.default_owner || '') + '" placeholder="Who owns this step" /></div>' +
    '<div><label>Help link (Google Drive or doc)</label><input type="text" id="w24Help" value="' + esc(t.help_url || '') + '" placeholder="https://..." /></div></div>' +
    '<label>Notes and how-to (shown under the step)</label><textarea id="w24Desc" rows="5">' + esc(t.description || '') + '</textarea>' +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w24Save">Save to the master SOP</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w24Cancel">Cancel</button></div>');
  var cancel = document.getElementById('w24Cancel');
  if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  var save = document.getElementById('w24Save');
  if (save) save.addEventListener('click', async function () {
    var patch = {
      name: (document.getElementById('w24Name').value || '').trim(),
      default_owner: (document.getElementById('w24Owner').value || '').trim() || null,
      help_url: (document.getElementById('w24Help').value || '').trim() || null,
      description: (document.getElementById('w24Desc').value || '').trim() || null
    };
    if (!patch.name) { toast('The step needs a name'); return; }
    save.disabled = true;
    var r = await db.from('sop_template').update(patch).eq('id', t.id);
    if (r.error) { save.disabled = false; toast('Save failed'); return; }
    t.name = patch.name;
    t.default_owner = patch.default_owner;
    t.help_url = patch.help_url;
    t.description = patch.description;
    closeModal();
    toast('Saved. Assignee pushed to upcoming planned events.');
    try { waVolRender(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  });
}
