    try { wire(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
  try { if (document.querySelector('.dept-card') || document.getElementById('tabBar')) wire(); } catch (e) { /* boot */ }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v25 master SOP structural editing (29/07/26)
   The SOP tab is the source of truth for event checklists. On top of
   v24's per-step editing (name, assignee, help link, notes), staff can
   now add steps and subtasks, remove steps, move steps (with their
   subtasks) up and down, reorder whole sections, add new sections and
   rename sections. Structure changes propagate to every upcoming
   planned event through database triggers; events in progress or
   completed keep their records. Section open/closed state survives
   re-renders.
   ===================================================================== */

(function () {
  if (document.getElementById('wa25css')) return;
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa25css';
  st.textContent = '.wa24-sx.w25row{margin-top:-4px;opacity:.75;}' +
    '.wa24-sx.w25row:hover{opacity:1;}' +
    '.wa25-secadd{margin:14px 0 2px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);
})();

function wa25Subtree(id) {
  var ids = [String(id)], grew = true;
  while (grew) {
    grew = false;
    WA_VOL_TMPL.forEach(function (t) {
      if (t.parent_id && ids.indexOf(String(t.parent_id)) >= 0 && ids.indexOf(String(t.id)) < 0) {
        ids.push(String(t.id));
        grew = true;
      }
    });
  }
  return ids;
}

function wa25Find(id) {
  var hit = null;
  WA_VOL_TMPL.forEach(function (t) { if (String(t.id) === String(id)) hit = t; });
  return hit;
}

async function wa25Reload(openSecs) {
  try {
    await waVolLoad(true);
    await waVolRender();
    if (openSecs && openSecs.length) {
      document.querySelectorAll('#volunteering details').forEach(function (d) {
        var s = d.querySelector('summary');
        var label = s ? (s.textContent || '').trim() : '';
        openSecs.forEach(function (o) { if (label.indexOf(o) === 0) d.open = true; });
      });
    }
  } catch (e) { toast('Could not refresh. Reload the page.'); }
}

function wa25OpenSecs() {
  var out = [];
  document.querySelectorAll('#volunteering details[open]').forEach(function (d) {
    var s = d.querySelector('summary');
    if (s) out.push((s.textContent || '').replace(/\d+ steps$/, '').trim());
  });
  return out;
}

/* ---------- add step or subtask ---------- */
function wa25AddModal(anchorId) {
  var a = wa25Find(anchorId);
  if (!a) return;
  var defPlace = (a.depth === 0) ? 'child' : 'after';
  openModal('<h3>Add a step to the master SOP</h3>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">The new step appears in the master SOP and on the checklist of every upcoming planned event. Events already in progress are not touched.</p>' +
    '<label>Where</label><select id="w25Place">' +
    '<option value="child"' + (defPlace === 'child' ? ' selected' : '') + '>As a subtask of &quot;' + esc(a.name || '') + '&quot;</option>' +
    '<option value="after"' + (defPlace === 'after' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Right after &quot;' + esc(a.name || '') + '&quot;, same level</option>' +
    '</select>' +
    '<label>Step name</label><input type="text" id="w25Name" placeholder="What needs to happen" />' +
    '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Assignee</label><input type="text" id="w25Owner" placeholder="Who owns this step" /></div>' +
    '<div><label>Help link (Google Drive or doc)</label><input type="text" id="w25Help" placeholder="https://..." /></div></div>' +
    '<label>Notes and how-to</label><textarea id="w25Desc" rows="4"></textarea>' +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w25Save">Add the step</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w25Cancel">Cancel</button></div>');
  var cancel = document.getElementById('w25Cancel');
  if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  var save = document.getElementById('w25Save');
  if (save) save.addEventListener('click', async function () {
    var name = (document.getElementById('w25Name').value || '').trim();
    if (!name) { toast('The step needs a name'); return; }
    save.disabled = true;
    var open = wa25OpenSecs();
    if (open.indexOf(a.section) < 0) open.push(a.section);
    var r = await db.rpc('sop_step_insert', {
      p_anchor: String(a.id),
      p_mode: document.getElementById('w25Place').value,
      p_name: name,
      p_owner: (document.getElementById('w25Owner').value || '').trim() || null,
      p_help: (document.getElementById('w25Help').value || '').trim() || null,
      p_desc: (document.getElementById('w25Desc').value || '').trim() || null
    });
    if (r.error) { save.disabled = false; toast('Could not add the step'); return; }
    closeModal();
    toast('Added to the master SOP and all upcoming planned events.');
    wa25Reload(open);
  });
}

/* ---------- add section ---------- */
function wa25SecModal() {
  openModal('<h3>Add a section</h3>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">A section groups related steps, like Itineraries or Post Event. It lands at the bottom of the SOP; use the move links to place it. Add steps to it with &quot;add a step&quot;.</p>' +
    '<label>Section name</label><input type="text" id="w25SecName" placeholder="Name the section" />' +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w25SecSave">Add the section</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w25SecCancel">Cancel</button></div>');
  var cancel = document.getElementById('w25SecCancel');
  if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  var save = document.getElementById('w25SecSave');
  if (save) save.addEventListener('click', async function () {
    var name = (document.getElementById('w25SecName').value || '').trim();
    if (!name) { toast('The section needs a name'); return; }
    save.disabled = true;
    var open = wa25OpenSecs();
    open.push(name);
    var r = await db.rpc('sop_step_insert', { p_anchor: null, p_mode: 'section', p_name: name, p_owner: null, p_help: null, p_desc: null });
    if (r.error) { save.disabled = false; toast('Could not add the section'); return; }
    closeModal();
    toast('Section added at the bottom of the SOP.');
    wa25Reload(open);
  });
}

/* ---------- remove ---------- */
function wa25DelModal(tid) {
  var t = wa25Find(tid);
  if (!t) return;
  var n = wa25Subtree(tid).length;
  var what = (t.depth === 0)
    ? 'the whole &quot;' + esc(t.name || '') + '&quot; section (' + n + ' steps)'
    : '&quot;' + esc(t.name || '') + '&quot;' + (n > 1 ? ' and its ' + (n - 1) + ' subtasks' : '');
  openModal('<h3>Remove from the master SOP</h3>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">This removes ' + what + ' from the master SOP and from the checklist of every upcoming planned event. Events already in progress or completed keep their records. Steel or Suhaib can restore it from the database if needed.</p>' +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w25DelGo" style="background:#b3402f">Remove</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w25DelCancel">Cancel</button></div>');
  var cancel = document.getElementById('w25DelCancel');
  if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  var go = document.getElementById('w25DelGo');
  if (go) go.addEventListener('click', async function () {
    go.disabled = true;
    var open = wa25OpenSecs();
    var r = await db.rpc('sop_step_deactivate', { p_id: String(tid) });
    if (r.error) { go.disabled = false; toast('Could not remove it'); return; }
    closeModal();
    toast('Removed from the master SOP and upcoming planned events.');
    wa25Reload(open);
  });
}

/* ---------- move ---------- */
async function wa25Move(tid, dir) {
  var open = wa25OpenSecs();
  var r = await db.rpc('sop_step_move', { p_id: String(tid), p_dir: dir });
  if (r.error) { toast('Could not move it'); return; }
  if (r.data !== true) { toast('Already at the edge of its group'); return; }
  wa25Reload(open);
}

/* ---------- render hooks ---------- */
if (typeof waVolStepHtml === 'function') {
  (function () {
    var __sh25 = waVolStepHtml;
    waVolStepHtml = function (t, s) {
      var html = __sh25(t, s);
      try {
        if (WA24_EDIT && typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff()) {
          html += '<div class="wa24-sx w25row">' +
            '<a class="ed" data-w25a="' + t.id + '">add a step</a> ' +
            '<a class="ed" data-w25u="' + t.id + '">move up</a> ' +
            '<a class="ed" data-w25d="' + t.id + '">move down</a> ' +
            '<a class="ed" data-w25x="' + t.id + '">remove</a></div>';
        }
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return html;
    };
  })();
}

if (typeof waVolRenderSop === 'function') {
  (function () {
    var __rs25 = waVolRenderSop;
    waVolRenderSop = function () {
      /* keep local section labels in step with renamed section headers */
      try {
        WA_VOL_TMPL.forEach(function (h) {
          if (h.depth === 0 && h.section !== h.name) {
            var old = h.section;
            WA_VOL_TMPL.forEach(function (x) { if (x.section === old) x.section = h.name; });
          }
        });
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      var open = wa25OpenSecs();
      __rs25();
      try {
        document.querySelectorAll('#volunteering details').forEach(function (d) {
          var s = d.querySelector('summary');
          var label = s ? (s.textContent || '').trim() : '';
          open.forEach(function (o) { if (o && label.indexOf(o) === 0) d.open = true; });
        });
        var intro = document.querySelector('#volunteering .vl-empty');
        if (intro) intro.textContent = 'This is the master SOP, the source of truth for every event. Edit a step or change its assignee, add or remove steps, or reorder them: every upcoming planned event updates to match. Events already in progress keep their own checklist.';
        if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff() && !document.querySelector('#volunteering [data-w25sec]')) {
          var all = document.querySelectorAll('#volunteering details');
          var last = all.length ? all[all.length - 1] : null;
          if (last) {
            var row = document.createElement('div');
            row.className = 'wa24-links wa25-secadd';
            var b = document.createElement('button');
            b.setAttribute('data-w25sec', '1');
            b.textContent = 'Add a section';
            row.appendChild(b);
            last.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', row);
          }
        }
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  })();
}

/* ---------- wiring ---------- */
(function () {
  function wire() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering');
    if (!sec || sec.dataset.wa25Wired) return;
    sec.dataset.wa25Wired = '1';
    sec.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var el = e.target.closest('[data-w25a],[data-w25u],[data-w25d],[data-w25x],[data-w25sec]');
      if (!el) return;
      if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
      try {
        if (el.hasAttribute('data-w25a')) wa25AddModal(el.getAttribute('data-w25a'));
        else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w25u')) wa25Move(el.getAttribute('data-w25u'), -1);
        else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w25d')) wa25Move(el.getAttribute('data-w25d'), 1);
        else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w25x')) wa25DelModal(el.getAttribute('data-w25x'));
        else if (el.hasAttribute('data-w25sec')) wa25SecModal();
      } catch (err) { /* non-fatal */ }
    });
  }
  wire();
  var __sa25 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa25();
    try { wire(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v26 volunteering usability wave (29/07/26)
   1. Calendar: upcoming events at the top, past events tucked into a
      dropdown, most recent first.
   2. Volunteers: search by name, email or phone; every record editable
      (name, phones, emails, address, car, tags, notes) with a one-click
      clear of the review flag.
   3. Event checklists: steps about texting or confirming volunteers get
      a "Text volunteers" shortcut that opens the texting tool for that
      event.
   4. Master SOP: "add a photo" on any step; photos land in the step
      details on the master and on every event checklist.
   5. ClickUp-style tasks: parents show a chevron and subtask count,
      collapse and expand their subtree, and depth guides make the
      hierarchy readable.
   ===================================================================== */

var WA26_Q = '';

(function () {
  if (document.getElementById('wa26css')) return;
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa26css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w26search{width:100%;max-width:340px;padding:8px 11px;border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.35);border-radius:9px;font-size:13px;margin:0 0 10px;}' +
    '.w26c{border:0;background:none;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px;padding:0 5px 0 0;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);}' +
    '.w26n{display:inline-block;margin-left:6px;font-size:10.5px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.35);border-radius:8px;padding:0 6px;}' +
    '.w26hide{display:none !important;}' +
    '.vl-d1,.vl-d2,.vl-d3{border-left:2px solid rgba(16,63,57,.14);padding-left:10px;}' +
    '.w26p > .vl-t > .vl-name{font-weight:600;}' +
    '.vl-ev.past{opacity:.82;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);
})();

/* ---------- 1. calendar: upcoming first, past in a dropdown ---------- */
if (typeof waVolRenderCalendar === 'function') {
  waVolRenderCalendar = function () {
    var nxt = waVolNextEvent();
    var nextId = nxt ? nxt.id : -1;
    var today = waVolToday();
    var html = '';
    if (nxt) {
      var d = waVolDaysTo(nxt.event_date);
      var ph = waVolPhaseNow(nxt.event_date);
      var phLabel = '';
      WA_VOL_PHASES.forEach(function (p) { if (p[0] === ph) phLabel = p[1]; });
      html += '<div class="vl-hero">'
        + '<h3>Next event: ' + esc(waVolDate(nxt.event_date, true)) + '</h3>'
        + '<div class="vl-when">' + (d === 0 ? 'Today' : (d === 1 ? 'Tomorrow' : 'In ' + d + ' days')) + '. '
        + 'You are in the "' + esc(phLabel) + '" part of the checklist. '
        + nxt.roster_confirmed + ' of ' + nxt.roster_count + ' roster slots confirmed, '
        + nxt.steps_done + ' of ' + nxt.steps_total + ' steps done.</div>'
        + '<div style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn mini" data-vlev="' + nxt.id + '">Open this event</button></div>'
        + '</div>';
    }
    var up = WA_VOL_EVENTS.filter(function (e) { return e.event_date >= today; });
    var past = WA_VOL_EVENTS.filter(function (e) { return e.event_date < today; });
    past.sort(function (a, b) { return a.event_date < b.event_date ? 1 : -1; });
    var month = '';
    if (up.length) {
      html += '<h3 class="block" style="color:#103F39">Upcoming</h3>';
      up.forEach(function (e) {
        var m = waVolMonth(e.event_date);
        if (m !== month) {
          if (month) html += '</div>';
          html += '<div class="vl-month">' + esc(m) + '</div><div class="vl-grid">';
          month = m;
        }
        html += waVolEventCard(e, nextId);
      });
      if (month) html += '</div>';
    }
    if (past.length) {
      html += '<details class="vl-phase" style="margin-top:16px"><summary>Past events <span class="vl-nums">' + past.length + '</span></summary><div class="vl-body">';
      month = '';
      past.forEach(function (e) {
        var m = waVolMonth(e.event_date);
        if (m !== month) {
          if (month) html += '</div>';
          html += '<div class="vl-month">' + esc(m) + '</div><div class="vl-grid">';
          month = m;
        }
        html += waVolEventCard(e, nextId);
      });
      if (month) html += '</div>';
      html += '</div></details>';
    }
    if (!WA_VOL_EVENTS.length) html += '<p class="vl-empty">No events loaded yet.</p>';
    waVolPaint(html);
  };
}

/* ---------- 2. volunteers: search plus editable records ---------- */
function wa26PersonRowData(p) {
  var bits = [p.full_name || ''].concat(p.emails || []).concat(p.tags || []).concat(p.roles || []);
  var digits = (p.phones || []).map(function (x) { return String(x).replace(/\D/g, ''); });
  return (bits.join(' ') + ' ' + digits.join(' ')).toLowerCase();
}

function wa26ApplySearch() {
  var q = (WA26_Q || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  var qd = q.replace(/\D/g, '');
  document.querySelectorAll('#volunteering tbody tr[data-p26]').forEach(function (tr) {
    var hay = tr.getAttribute('data-search') || '';
    var hit = !q || hay.indexOf(q) >= 0 || (qd.length >= 3 && hay.indexOf(qd) >= 0);
    tr.style.display = hit ? '' : 'none';
  });
}

if (typeof waVolRenderPeople === 'function') {
  waVolRenderPeople = function () {
    var counts = {};
    WA_VOL_PEOPLE.forEach(function (p) {
      var set = {};
      (p.roles || []).forEach(function (r) { set[r] = 1; });
      (p.tags || []).forEach(function (r) { set[r] = 1; });
      Object.keys(set).forEach(function (r) { counts[r] = (counts[r] || 0) + 1; });
    });
    var chips = '<span class="fchip' + (WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER ? '' : ' on') + '" data-vlrole="">Everyone (' + WA_VOL_PEOPLE.length + ')</span>'
      + Object.keys(counts).sort().map(function (r) {
        return '<span class="fchip' + (WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER === r ? ' on' : '') + '" data-vlrole="' + esc(r) + '">' + esc(waVolRoleLabel(r)) + ' (' + counts[r] + ')</span>';
      }).join('');
    var list = WA_VOL_PEOPLE.filter(function (p) {
      if (!WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER) return true;
      return (p.roles || []).indexOf(WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER) >= 0 || (p.tags || []).indexOf(WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER) >= 0;
    });
    var showAddr = WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER === 'cook';
    var showCar = WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER === 'driver';
    var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();
    var flagged = WA_VOL_PEOPLE.filter(function (p) { return p.needs_review; }).length;
    var html = '<input class="w26search" id="w26q" type="search" placeholder="Search name, email or phone" value="' + esc(WA26_Q) + '" />';
    html += '<div class="filters" style="margin-bottom:12px">' + chips + '</div>';
    if (flagged) html += '<div class="vl-flag" style="margin:0 0 14px">' + flagged + ' records need a human eye before any bulk message goes out. They are marked below; open one to clear its flag.</div>';
    html += '<div class="vl-tblwrap"><table class="wa-tbl"><thead><tr><th>Name</th><th>Tags</th><th>Phone</th><th>Email</th>'
      + (showAddr ? '<th>Address</th>' : '') + (showCar ? '<th>Car</th>' : '')
      + '<th>Notes</th>' + (staff ? '<th></th>' : '') + '</tr></thead><tbody>'
      + list.map(function (p) {
        var tagset = {};
        (p.roles || []).forEach(function (r) { tagset[r] = 1; });
        (p.tags || []).forEach(function (r) { tagset[r] = 1; });
        return '<tr data-p26="' + p.id + '" data-search="' + esc(wa26PersonRowData(p)) + '"><td><b>' + esc(p.full_name || '') + '</b>'
          + (p.quo_contact_id ? ' <span class="vl-bub" title="Linked to Quo contacts">Quo</span>' : '')
          + (p.needs_review ? '<br /><span class="vl-bub warn">' + esc(p.review_note || 'needs review') + '</span>' : '') + '</td>'
          + '<td>' + Object.keys(tagset).sort().map(function (r) { return '<span class="vl-bub">' + esc(waVolRoleLabel(r)) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</td>'
          + '<td>' + esc((p.phones || []).join(', ')) + '</td>'
          + '<td>' + esc((p.emails || []).join(', ')) + '</td>'
          + (showAddr ? '<td style="max-width:220px">' + esc(p.address || '') + '</td>' : '')
          + (showCar ? '<td>' + esc(p.car_type || '') + '</td>' : '')
          + '<td style="max-width:220px">' + esc((p.notes || '').slice(0, 120)) + '</td>'
          + (staff ? '<td><a class="ed" data-w26e="' + p.id + '" style="color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;font-size:12px">edit</a></td>' : '')
          + '</tr>';
      }).join('') + '</tbody></table></div>';
    waVolPaint(html);
    var q = document.getElementById('w26q');
    if (q) q.addEventListener('input', function () { WA26_Q = this.value; wa26ApplySearch(); });
    wa26ApplySearch();
  };
}

function wa26PersonModal(pid) {
  var p = null;
  WA_VOL_PEOPLE.forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.id) === String(pid)) p = x; });
  if (!p) return;
  /* wave176: one record: the person is their CRM contact (the same name, emails, phones, address, tags and notes in both apps) */
  openModal('<h3>Edit volunteer</h3>' +
    '<p class="read" style="font-size:12.5px;margin:0 0 8px">One record: these details are also this person\u2019s contact in the CRM, so a change here shows there too, and the other way round.' + (p.contact_id ? ' <a href="#crm/c/' + esc(String(p.contact_id)) + '" data-w176crm="1" style="color:#103F39;font-weight:600">Open in the CRM</a>' : '') + '</p>' +
    '<label>Full name</label><input type="text" id="p26Name" value="' + esc(p.full_name || '') + '" />' +
    '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Phones (comma separated)</label><input type="text" id="p26Phones" value="' + esc((p.phones || []).join(', ')) + '" /></div>' +
    '<div><label>Emails (comma separated)</label><input type="text" id="p26Emails" value="' + esc((p.emails || []).join(', ')) + '" /></div></div>' +
    '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Address</label><input type="text" id="p26Addr" value="' + esc(p.address || '') + '" placeholder="For cook pickups" /></div>' +
    '<div><label>Car</label><input type="text" id="p26Car" value="' + esc(p.car_type || '') + '" placeholder="For drivers" /></div></div>' +
    '<label>Tags (comma separated: cook, driver, server...)</label><input type="text" id="p26Tags" value="' + esc((p.tags || []).join(', ')) + '" />' +
    '<label>Notes</label><textarea id="p26Notes" rows="3">' + esc(p.notes || '') + '</textarea>' +
    (p.needs_review ? '<label style="display:flex;gap:7px;align-items:center;margin-top:8px"><input type="checkbox" id="p26Clr" style="width:15px;height:15px" /> Clear the review flag (' + esc(p.review_note || 'needs review') + ')</label>' : '') +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="p26Save">Save</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="p26Cancel">Cancel</button></div>');
  var cancel = document.getElementById('p26Cancel');
  var w176 = document.querySelector('[data-w176crm]');
  if (w176) w176.addEventListener('click', function () { try { closeModal(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
  if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  var save = document.getElementById('p26Save');
  if (save) save.addEventListener('click', async function () {
    function splitList(id) {
      return (document.getElementById(id).value || '').split(/[,;]/).map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
    }
    var patch = {
      full_name: (document.getElementById('p26Name').value || '').trim(),
      phones: splitList('p26Phones'),
      emails: splitList('p26Emails'),
      address: (document.getElementById('p26Addr').value || '').trim() || null,
      car_type: (document.getElementById('p26Car').value || '').trim() || null,
      tags: splitList('p26Tags').map(function (x) { return x.toLowerCase(); }),
      notes: (document.getElementById('p26Notes').value || '').trim() || null
    };
    if (!patch.full_name) { toast('The volunteer needs a name'); return; }
    var clr = document.getElementById('p26Clr');
    if (clr && clr.checked) { patch.needs_review = false; patch.review_note = null; }
    save.disabled = true;
    var r = await db.from('volunteer_people').update(patch).eq('id', p.id);
    if (r.error) { save.disabled = false; toast('Save failed'); return; }
    Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { p[k] = patch[k]; });
    closeModal();
    toast('Saved');
    try { waVolRender(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  });
}

/* ---------- 3 to 5. step decorations: texting shortcut, photos, nesting ---------- */
var WA26_TXT_RE = /\b(text|texts|texting|sms|whatsapp|message|messages|remind|reminder|follow.?up|contact|confirm|confirming|reach out)\b/i;

if (typeof waVolStepHtml === 'function') {
  (function () {
    var __sh26 = waVolStepHtml;
    waVolStepHtml = function (t, s) {
      var html = __sh26(t, s);
      try {
        /* depth marker for the nesting pass (class caps at vl-d3) */
        html = html.replace('data-vlstep="', 'data-w26d="' + (t.depth || 0) + '" data-vlstep="');
        var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();
        /* photo upload link joins the structural row on the master SOP */
        if (WA24_EDIT && staff) {
          html = html.replace('data-w25x="' + t.id + '">remove</a>', 'data-w25x="' + t.id + '">remove</a> <a class="ed" data-w26ph="' + t.id + '">add a photo</a>');
        }
        /* texting shortcut on event checklists for steps about contacting people */
        if (!WA24_EDIT && staff && s && String(s.id).indexOf('tmpl-') !== 0 && WA26_TXT_RE.test(t.name || '')) {
          html += '<div class="wa24-sx"><a class="ed" data-w26tx="1" style="color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline">Text volunteers for this step</a></div>';
        }
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return html;
    };
  })();
}

function wa26Nest() {
  document.querySelectorAll('#volunteering .vl-body').forEach(function (body) {
    var kids = Array.prototype.slice.call(body.children);
    var steps = kids.filter(function (el) { return el.classList && el.classList.contains('vl-step'); });
    steps.forEach(function (el, i) {
      if (el.dataset.w26nested) return;
      el.dataset.w26nested = '1';
      var d = parseInt(el.getAttribute('data-w26d') || '0', 10);
      var count = 0;
      for (var j = i + 1; j < steps.length; j++) {
        var dj = parseInt(steps[j].getAttribute('data-w26d') || '0', 10);
        if (dj <= d) break;
        count++;
      }
      if (!count) return;
      el.classList.add('w26p');
      var name = el.querySelector('.vl-name');
      if (!name) return;
      var c = document.createElement('button');
      c.className = 'w26c';
      c.innerHTML = '&#9662;';
      c.setAttribute('data-w26c', '1');
      c.title = 'Collapse or expand subtasks';
      name.insertBefore(c, name.firstChild);
      var badge = document.createElement('span');
      badge.className = 'w26n';
      badge.textContent = count + (count === 1 ? ' subtask' : ' subtasks');
      name.appendChild(badge);
    });
  });
}

function wa26Toggle(btn) {
  var step = btn.closest('.vl-step');
  if (!step) return;
  var d = parseInt(step.getAttribute('data-w26d') || '0', 10);
  var collapse = btn.dataset.open !== '0';
  btn.dataset.open = collapse ? '0' : '1';
  btn.innerHTML = collapse ? '&#9656;' : '&#9662;';
  var el = step.nextElementSibling;
  var inSub = false;
  while (el) {
    if (el.classList.contains('vl-step')) {
      var dj = parseInt(el.getAttribute('data-w26d') || '0', 10);
      if (dj <= d) break;
      inSub = true;
      el.classList.toggle('w26hide', collapse);
      if (!collapse) {
        var cc = el.querySelector('.w26c');
        if (cc) { cc.innerHTML = '&#9662;'; cc.dataset.open = '1'; }
      }
    } else if (inSub) {
      el.classList.toggle('w26hide', collapse);
    }
    el = el.nextElementSibling;
  }
}

/* ---------- photo upload ---------- */
function wa26Photo(tid) {
  var input = document.getElementById('w26file');
  if (!input) {
    input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = 'image/*';
    input.id = 'w26file';
    input.style.display = 'none';
    document.body.appendChild(input);
  }
  input.onchange = async function () {
    var file = input.files && input.files[0];
    input.value = '';
    if (!file) return;
    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { toast('That photo is over 8 MB. Pick a smaller one.'); return; }
    toast('Uploading the photo...');
    try {
      var ext = (file.name.split('.').pop() || 'png').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '') || 'png';
      var path = tid + '/wa-' + Date.now() + '.' + ext;
      var up = await db.storage.from('sop-shots').upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type || 'image/png' });
      if (up.error) throw up.error;
      var pub = db.storage.from('sop-shots').getPublicUrl(path);
      var url = pub && pub.data ? pub.data.publicUrl : '';
      if (!url) throw new Error('no url');
      var ins = await db.from('sop_shots').insert({ template_id: tid, attachment_id: 'wa-upload-' + Date.now(), public_url: url, title: file.name, sort: 500 });
      if (ins.error) throw ins.error;
      toast('Photo added. It shows on this step in the master SOP and every event.');
      var open = (typeof wa25OpenSecs === 'function') ? wa25OpenSecs() : [];
      if (typeof wa25Reload === 'function') wa25Reload(open);
    } catch (e) { toast('Could not upload the photo'); }
  };
  input.click();
}

/* ---------- render wrappers and wiring ---------- */
(function () {
  ['waVolRenderSop', 'waVolRenderEvent'].forEach(function (fn) {
    if (typeof window[fn] === 'function') {
      var inner = window[fn];
      window[fn] = function () {
        inner();
        try { wa26Nest(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      };
    }
  });

  function wire() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering');
    if (!sec || sec.dataset.wa26Wired) return;
    sec.dataset.wa26Wired = '1';
    sec.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var c = e.target.closest('[data-w26c]');
      if (c) { wa26Toggle(c); return; }
      if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
      var ed = e.target.closest('[data-w26e]');
      if (ed) { try { wa26PersonModal(ed.getAttribute('data-w26e')); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } return; }
      var ph = e.target.closest('[data-w26ph]');
      if (ph) { try { wa26Photo(ph.getAttribute('data-w26ph')); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } return; }
      var tx = e.target.closest('[data-w26tx]');
      if (tx && typeof waTxtModal === 'function') { try { waTxtModal(); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } return; }
    });
  }
  wire();
  var __sa26 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa26();
    try { wire(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v27 projects and goals flow (29/07/26)
   Tasks flow into projects; projects flow into quarterly goals.
   1. Every department page gets a Projects card: General tasks plus
      each project with its open task count. Click through to a project
      page with the description at the top, the linked goal, open and
      completed tasks, quick add, and per task moves between projects.
   2. Transcript uploads now file new tasks into the right project
      automatically (process-transcript v6); anything unmatched lands in
      General tasks.
   3. Goal rows show their linked projects and a "Suggest missing tasks"
      action: AI gap analysis against everything already open, with one
      click add of any suggestion.
   ===================================================================== */

var WA27_PROJECTS = [];
var WA27_COUNTS = {};
var WA27_LOADED = false;

(function () {
  if (document.getElementById('wa27css')) return;
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa27css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w27-chips{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:10px 0 2px;}' +
    '.w27-chip{border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.35);border-radius:9px;padding:7px 12px;font-size:12.5px;cursor:pointer;background:rgba(16,63,57,.06);}' +
    '.w27-chip b{font-weight:600;}' +
    '.w27-chip .n{opacity:.65;margin-left:5px;}' +
    '.w27-chip.gen{background:transparent;}' +
    '.w27-desc{font-size:13.5px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);margin:4px 0 12px;max-width:720px;}' +
    '.w27-goal{font-size:12.5px;border:1px solid rgba(168,127,44,.4);background:rgba(168,127,44,.07);border-radius:9px;padding:8px 12px;margin:0 0 14px;max-width:720px;}' +
    '.w27-task{display:flex;gap:9px;align-items:flex-start;padding:7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.15);font-size:13.5px;}' +
    '.w27-task input[type=checkbox]{width:16px;height:16px;margin-top:2px;flex:0 0 auto;cursor:pointer;}' +

    '.w27gp{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;margin:2px 0 10px;font-size:11.5px;}' +

    '.w27gp a.sg{color:#a87f2c;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;}' +
    '.w27-sug{border-top:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.2);padding:8px 0;font-size:13px;}' +
    '.w27-sug .why{font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);margin-top:2px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);
})();

async function wa27Load(force) {
  if (WA27_LOADED && !force) return;
  try {
    var res = await Promise.all([
      db.from('projects').select('*').eq('status', 'active').order('sort').order('id'),
      db.from('dept_tasks').select('id, department, project_id').eq('done', false)
    ]);
    WA27_PROJECTS = (res[0].data) || [];
    WA27_COUNTS = {};
    ((res[1].data) || []).forEach(function (t) {
      var k = t.project_id ? 'p' + t.project_id : 'g' + t.department;
      WA27_COUNTS[k] = (WA27_COUNTS[k] || 0) + 1;
    });
    WA27_LOADED = true;
  } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
}

function wa27Find(id) {
  var hit = null;
  WA27_PROJECTS.forEach(function (p) { if (String(p.id) === String(id)) hit = p; });
  return hit;
}

function wa27Name(p) {
  if (!p) return '';
  var par = p.parent_id ? wa27Find(p.parent_id) : null;
  return (par ? par.name + ' > ' : '') + p.name;
}

function wa27Goal(gid) {
  var hit = null;
  if (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined') GOALS_CACHE.forEach(function (g) { if (String(g.id) === String(gid)) hit = g; });
  return hit;
}

/* ---------- projects card on department pages ---------- */
function wa27DeptCard(id) {
  if (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' && DEPT_ORDER.indexOf(id) < 0) return;
  var panel = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!panel) return;
  var old = panel.querySelector('.w27-card');
  if (old) old.remove();
  var projs = WA27_PROJECTS.filter(function (p) { return p.department === id; });
  var card = document.createElement('div');
  card.className = 'card w27-card';
  var chips = '<span class="w27-chip gen" data-w27o="general/' + id + '"><b>General tasks</b><span class="n">' + (WA27_COUNTS['g' + id] || 0) + ' open</span></span>';
  projs.forEach(function (p) {
    chips += '<span class="w27-chip" data-w27o="' + p.id + '"><b>' + esc(wa27Name(p)) + '</b><span class="n">' + (WA27_COUNTS['p' + p.id] || 0) + ' open</span></span>';
  });
  var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();
  card.innerHTML = '<h2 style="color:#103F39">Projects</h2>'
    + '<p class="read" style="margin:0 0 4px">Tasks from transcripts file themselves into these projects; everything else sits in General tasks. Projects roll up into the quarterly goals.</p>'
    + '<div class="w27-chips">' + chips + '</div>'
    + (staff ? '<div class="wa24-links" style="margin-top:10px"><button data-w27add="' + id + '">Add a project</button></div>' : '');
  var anchor = panel.querySelector('.dept-card');
  if (anchor) anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', card);
  else panel.appendChild(card);
}

/* ---------- project page ---------- */
var WA27_VIEW = null;

async function wa27RenderProject(ref, deptForGeneral) {
  var sec = document.getElementById('projectpage');
  if (!sec) return;
  await wa27Load(false);
  var isGen = ref === 'general';
  var p = isGen ? null : wa27Find(ref);
  var dept = isGen ? deptForGeneral : (p ? p.department : null);
  if (!dept) { sec.innerHTML = '<div class="card"><p class="read">Project not found.</p></div>'; return; }
  WA27_VIEW = { ref: ref, dept: dept };
  var label = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[dept]) || dept;
  var q = db.from('dept_tasks').select('*').eq('department', dept).order('done').order('id', { ascending: false });
  q = isGen ? q.is('project_id', null) : q.eq('project_id', p.id);
  var r = await q;
  var tasks = (r.data) || [];
  if (isGen) tasks = tasks.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; });
  var open = tasks.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; });
  var done = tasks.filter(function (t) { return t.done; });
  var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();
  var g = p && p.goal_id ? wa27Goal(p.goal_id) : null;

  function row(t) {
    return '<div class="w27-task' + (t.done ? ' done' : '') + '">'
      + '<input type="checkbox" data-w27t="' + t.id + '"' + (t.done ? ' checked' : '') + (staff ? '' : ' disabled') + ' />'
      + '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><span class="tt">' + esc(t.title || '') + '</span>'
      + '<div class="meta">' + esc(t.owner || 'unassigned')
      + (t.due_date ? ' &middot; due ' + esc(t.due_date) : '')
      + (t.flag ? ' &middot; flagged' : '')
      + (staff ? ' &middot; <a class="mv" data-w27mv="' + t.id + '">move</a>' : '')
      + '</div></div></div>';
  }

  var html = '<div class="card">'
    + '<button class="vl-back" data-w27back="' + dept + '">&larr; ' + esc(label) + '</button>'
    + '<h2 style="color:#103F39">' + esc(isGen ? 'General tasks' : wa27Name(p)) + '<span class="deptchip" style="background:#103F39">' + esc(label) + '</span></h2>'
    + (isGen
      ? '<p class="w27-desc">Open tasks with no project yet. Move one into a project with its move link.</p>'
      : '<p class="w27-desc">' + esc(p.description || 'No description yet.') + (staff ? ' <a class="mv" data-w27edit="' + p.id + '" style="color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline">edit project</a>' : '') + '</p>')
    + (g ? '<div class="w27-goal">Rolls up to the goal: <b>' + esc(g.title) + '</b>' + (g.status ? ' <span style="opacity:.7">(' + esc(g.status) + ')</span>' : '') + '</div>' : '')
    + '<h3 class="block">Open (' + open.length + ')</h3>'
    + (open.length ? open.map(row).join('') : '<p class="read">Nothing open here right now.</p>')
    + (staff ? '<div class="add-row" style="margin-top:10px"><input type="text" id="w27nt" placeholder="Add a task to ' + esc(isGen ? 'General' : p.name) + '" style="max-width:340px;padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.35);border-radius:8px;font-size:13px" /> <button class="btn mini" id="w27ntgo">Add</button></div>' : '')
    + (done.length ? '<details class="completed" style="margin-top:14px"><summary>Completed (' + done.length + ')</summary>' + done.map(row).join('') + '</details>' : '')
    + '</div>';
  sec.innerHTML = html;
}

function wa27OpenProject(ref) {
  location.hash = '#project/' + ref;
}

/* ---------- modals ---------- */
function wa27ProjectModal(dept, existing) {
  var p = existing || null;
  var goals = (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []);
  var goalOpts = '<option value="">No goal link</option>' + goals.map(function (g) {
    return '<option value="' + g.id + '"' + (p && String(p.goal_id) === String(g.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc((g.department || '') + ': ' + String(g.title).slice(0, 70)) + '</option>';
  }).join('');
  var parents = WA27_PROJECTS.filter(function (x) { return x.department === dept && !x.parent_id && (!p || x.id !== p.id); });
  var parOpts = '<option value="">Top level</option>' + parents.map(function (x) {
    return '<option value="' + x.id + '"' + (p && String(p.parent_id) === String(x.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(x.name) + '</option>';
  }).join('');
  openModal('<h3>' + (p ? 'Edit project' : 'Add a project') + '</h3>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">The description matters: transcript uploads use it to decide which tasks belong here. Say what the project covers in plain words.</p>' +
    '<label>Project name</label><input type="text" id="w27pn" value="' + esc(p ? p.name : '') + '" />' +
    '<label>Description (routing hint)</label><textarea id="w27pd" rows="3">' + esc(p ? (p.description || '') : '') + '</textarea>' +
    '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Lives under</label><select id="w27pp">' + parOpts + '</select></div>' +
    '<div><label>Rolls up to goal</label><select id="w27pg">' + goalOpts + '</select></div></div>' +
    (p ? '<label style="display:flex;gap:7px;align-items:center;margin-top:8px"><input type="checkbox" id="w27parch" style="width:15px;height:15px" /> Archive this project (tasks keep their tag, chip disappears)</label>' : '') +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w27ps">' + (p ? 'Save' : 'Add the project') + '</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w27pc">Cancel</button></div>');
  var cancel = document.getElementById('w27pc');
  if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  var save = document.getElementById('w27ps');
  if (save) save.addEventListener('click', async function () {
    var name = (document.getElementById('w27pn').value || '').trim();
    if (!name) { toast('The project needs a name'); return; }
    save.disabled = true;
    var patch = {
      name: name,
      description: (document.getElementById('w27pd').value || '').trim() || null,
      parent_id: Number(document.getElementById('w27pp').value) || null,
      goal_id: Number(document.getElementById('w27pg').value) || null
    };
    var arch = document.getElementById('w27parch');
    if (arch && arch.checked) patch.status = 'archived';
    var r;
    if (p) r = await db.from('projects').update(patch).eq('id', p.id);
    else {
      patch.department = dept;
      patch.status = 'active';
      r = await db.from('projects').insert(patch);
    }
    if (r.error) { save.disabled = false; toast('Could not save the project'); return; }
    closeModal();
    toast(p ? 'Project saved' : 'Project added');
    await wa27Load(true);
    if (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined') DEPT_ORDER.forEach(function (d) { try { wa27DeptCard(d); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } });
    if (WA27_VIEW && !document.getElementById('projectpage').classList.contains('on')) return;
    if (WA27_VIEW) wa27RenderProject(WA27_VIEW.ref, WA27_VIEW.dept);
  });
}

function wa27MoveModal(taskId) {
  var byDept = {};
  WA27_PROJECTS.forEach(function (p) { (byDept[p.department] = byDept[p.department] || []).push(p); });
  var opts = '<option value="">General tasks (no project)</option>';
  Object.keys(byDept).sort().forEach(function (d) {
    var label = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d;
    opts += '<optgroup label="' + esc(label) + '">' + byDept[d].map(function (p) {
      return '<option value="' + p.id + '">' + esc(wa27Name(p)) + '</option>';
    }).join('') + '</optgroup>';
  });
  openModal('<h3>Move this task</h3>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">Pick the project this task belongs to. General means no project.</p>' +
    '<label>Project</label><select id="w27mvp">' + opts + '</select>' +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w27mvgo">Move</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w27mvc">Cancel</button></div>');
  var cancel = document.getElementById('w27mvc');
  if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  var go = document.getElementById('w27mvgo');
  if (go) go.addEventListener('click', async function () {
    go.disabled = true;
    var v = document.getElementById('w27mvp').value;
    var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').update({ project_id: v ? Number(v) : null }).eq('id', taskId);
    if (r.error) { go.disabled = false; toast('Could not move it'); return; }
    closeModal();
    toast('Moved');
    await wa27Load(true);
    if (WA27_VIEW) wa27RenderProject(WA27_VIEW.ref, WA27_VIEW.dept);
  });
}

/* ---------- goal rows: linked projects and gap suggestions ---------- */
if (typeof waGoalRow === 'function') {
  (function () {
    var __gr27 = waGoalRow;
    waGoalRow = function (g) {
      var html = __gr27(g);
      try {
        var linked = WA27_PROJECTS.filter(function (p) { return String(p.goal_id) === String(g.id); });
        var bits = linked.map(function (p) {
          return '<span class="pj" data-w27o="' + p.id + '">' + esc(wa27Name(p)) + ' (' + (WA27_COUNTS['p' + p.id] || 0) + ' open)</span>';
        });
        if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff()) bits.push('<a class="sg" data-w27sg="' + g.id + '">Suggest missing tasks</a>');
        if (bits.length) html += '<div class="w27gp">' + bits.join(' ') + '</div>';
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return html;
    };
  })();
}

async function wa27Suggest(goalId, forceRefresh) {
  var g = wa27Goal(goalId);
  if (!g) return;
  var cached = g.suggestions && !forceRefresh ? g.suggestions : null;
  openModal('<h3>What is missing for this goal</h3>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">' + esc(String(g.title).slice(0, 140)) + '</p>' +
    '<div id="w27sout">' + (cached ? '' : '<p class="m-sub">Reading the goal, its projects and every open task, then looking for gaps. Twenty seconds or so.</p>') + '</div>' +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn ghost" id="w27sref">' + (cached ? 'Refresh the analysis' : 'Working...') + '</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w27sc">Close</button></div>');
  var closeB = document.getElementById('w27sc');
  if (closeB) closeB.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  var refB = document.getElementById('w27sref');
  if (refB) refB.addEventListener('click', function () { wa27Suggest(goalId, true); });

  function paint(payload) {
    var out = document.getElementById('w27sout');
    if (!out) return;
    var sugs = (payload && payload.suggestions) || [];
    var html = payload && payload.note ? '<p class="m-sub">' + esc(payload.note) + '</p>' : '';
    if (!sugs.length) html += '<p class="m-sub">No gaps found: the open tasks already cover this goal.</p>';
    sugs.forEach(function (s, i) {
      var pj = s.project_id ? wa27Find(s.project_id) : null;
      html += '<div class="w27-sug"><b>' + esc(s.title || '') + '</b>'
        + '<div class="why">' + esc(s.why || '') + (pj ? ' Files under ' + esc(wa27Name(pj)) + '.' : ' Files under General tasks.') + '</div>'
        + '<div style="margin-top:5px"><button class="btn mini" data-w27sa="' + i + '">Add as a task</button></div></div>';
    });
    out.innerHTML = html;
    out.querySelectorAll('[data-w27sa]').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', async function () {
        var s = sugs[Number(b.getAttribute('data-w27sa'))];
        if (!s) return;
        b.disabled = true;
        var ins = await db.from('dept_tasks').insert({
          department: g.department, title: String(s.title || '').slice(0, 300),
          owner: '', status: 'Open', flag: false, priority: false, done: false, sort: 999,
          project_id: s.project_id || null,
          reason_note: 'Goal gap suggestion from the Hub: ' + String(s.why || '').slice(0, 300),
          updated_by: 'AI: goal gap analysis', opened_at: new Date().toISOString()
        });
        if (ins.error) { b.disabled = false; toast('Could not add it'); return; }
        b.textContent = 'Added';
        toast('Task added');
        wa27Load(true);
      });
    });
  }

  if (cached) { paint(cached); return; }
  try {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('goal-suggest', { body: { goal_id: Number(goalId) } });
    if (r.error) throw r.error;
    g.suggestions = r.data;
    if (refB) { refB.textContent = 'Refresh the analysis'; }
    paint(r.data);
  } catch (e) {
    var out = document.getElementById('w27sout');
    if (out) out.innerHTML = '<p class="m-sub">The analysis did not come back. Try again in a minute.</p>';
    if (refB) refB.textContent = 'Try again';
  }
}

/* ---------- wiring ---------- */
(function () {
  function ensureSection() {
    if (document.getElementById('projectpage')) return;
    var main = document.querySelector('main');
    if (!main) return;
    var sec = document.createElement('section');
    sec.id = 'projectpage';
    sec.className = 'panel';
    main.appendChild(sec);
  }

  function wire() {
    ensureSection();
    if (document.body.dataset.wa27Wired) return;
    document.body.dataset.wa27Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
      var o = e.target.closest('[data-w27o]');
      if (o) { wa27OpenProject(o.getAttribute('data-w27o')); return; }
      var back = e.target.closest('[data-w27back]');
      if (back) { location.hash = '#dept/' + back.getAttribute('data-w27back'); return; }
      if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
      var add = e.target.closest('[data-w27add]');
      if (add) { try { wa27ProjectModal(add.getAttribute('data-w27add'), null); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } return; }
      var ed = e.target.closest('[data-w27edit]');
      if (ed) { try { wa27ProjectModal(wa27Find(ed.getAttribute('data-w27edit')).department, wa27Find(ed.getAttribute('data-w27edit'))); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } return; }
      var mv = e.target.closest('[data-w27mv]');
      if (mv) { try { wa27MoveModal(Number(mv.getAttribute('data-w27mv'))); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } return; }
      var sg = e.target.closest('[data-w27sg]');
      if (sg) { try { wa27Suggest(Number(sg.getAttribute('data-w27sg')), false); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } return; }
      var chk = e.target.closest('input[data-w27t]');
      if (chk) {
        var tid = Number(chk.getAttribute('data-w27t'));
        var nowDone = chk.checked;
        var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').update({ done: nowDone, closed_at: nowDone ? new Date().toISOString() : null, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: (typeof who === 'function' ? who() : 'dashboard') }).eq('id', tid);
        if (r.error) { chk.checked = !nowDone; toast('Could not update the task'); return; }
        try {
          await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'task', entity_id: String(tid), field: 'done', old_value: String(!nowDone), new_value: String(nowDone) + ' (project page)', changed_by: (typeof who === 'function' ? who() : 'dashboard') });
        } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ }
        toast(nowDone ? 'Closed' : 'Reopened');
        wa27Load(true);
        if (WA27_VIEW) wa27RenderProject(WA27_VIEW.ref, WA27_VIEW.dept);
        return;
      }
      var ntgo = e.target.closest('#w27ntgo');
      if (ntgo && WA27_VIEW) {
        var inp = document.getElementById('w27nt');
        var title = (inp && inp.value || '').trim();
        if (!title) { toast('Write the task first'); return; }
        ntgo.disabled = true;
        var isGen = WA27_VIEW.ref === 'general';
        var ins = await db.from('dept_tasks').insert({
          department: WA27_VIEW.dept, title: title.slice(0, 300), owner: '', status: 'Open',
          flag: false, priority: false, done: false, sort: 999,
          project_id: isGen ? null : Number(WA27_VIEW.ref),
          updated_by: (typeof who === 'function' ? who() : 'dashboard'), opened_at: new Date().toISOString()
        });
        ntgo.disabled = false;
        if (ins.error) { toast('Could not add the task'); return; }
        toast('Task added');
        await wa27Load(true);
        wa27RenderProject(WA27_VIEW.ref, WA27_VIEW.dept);
        return;
      }
    });
  }

  if (typeof renderSection === 'function') {
    var __rsec27 = renderSection;
    renderSection = function (id) {
      __rsec27(id);
      try { wa27DeptCard(id); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  if (typeof applyHash === 'function') {
    var __ah27 = applyHash;
    applyHash = function () {
      var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
      if (h.indexOf('project/') === 0) {
        var bits = h.split('/');
        ensureSection();
        try { wa27RenderProject(bits[1], bits[2] || null); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
        showPanel('projectpage');
        return;
      }
      __ah27();
    };
  }

  var __sa27 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa27();
    try {
      wire();
      await wa27Load(false);
      if (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined') DEPT_ORDER.forEach(function (d) { try { wa27DeptCard(d); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } });
      if (typeof renderDeptGoalsCards === 'function') { try { renderDeptGoalsCards(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
      var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
      if (h.indexOf('project/') === 0) applyHash();
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
  try { if (document.querySelector('main')) wire(); } catch (e) { /* boot */ }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v28 one people table everywhere (29/07/26)
   1. Assignees are dropdowns fed by the team_members table, color coded
      per person, on: dept task rows, project pages, the master SOP edit
      and add modals, and every event checklist step.
   2. Task status is a click-to-change dropdown (Open, In progress,
      Blocked, Done), color coded; Done closes the task properly.
   3. Team tags on department pages open a profile: role, email, color
      and the person's open tasks across the whole organization.
   4. Signed-in staff get a personalized "My work" card on the home
      page: their open tasks everywhere plus their upcoming SOP steps.
   5. Planned event dates are editable on the event page; moving the
      date re-anchors every step due date by phase (two weeks before,
      one week before, week of, event day, post event) via the database.
   ===================================================================== */

var WA28_PEOPLE = [];
var WA28_TASKS = [];
var WA28_ME = null;
var WA28_LOADED = false;

(function () {
  if (document.getElementById('wa28css')) return;
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa28css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w28sel{border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.35);border-radius:8px;padding:2px 6px;font-size:11.5px;background:#FFFAF2;color:#333;max-width:150px;cursor:pointer;border-left-width:4px;}' +
    '.w28st-Open{border-left-color:#797773 !important;}' +
    '.w28st-Inprogress{border-left-color:#3a6ea5 !important;background:rgba(58,110,165,.08) !important;}' +
    '.w28st-Blocked{border-left-color:#b3402f !important;background:rgba(179,64,47,.08) !important;}' +
    '.w28st-Done{border-left-color:#103F39 !important;background:rgba(16,63,57,.1) !important;}' +
    '.w28row{display:flex;gap:7px;align-items:center;margin-top:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;}' +
    '.team-tag{cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w28dot{display:inline-block;width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;margin-right:6px;vertical-align:baseline;}' +
    '.w28my{border-top:4px solid #103F39;}' +
    '.w28my .t{font-size:13.5px;padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.15);display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;}' +
    '.w28my .dep{font-size:10.5px;border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.35);border-radius:8px;padding:0 7px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773);}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);
})();

async function wa28Load(force) {
  if (WA28_LOADED && !force) return;
  try {
    var res = await Promise.all([
      db.from('team_members').select('id, name, role, department, email, color').eq('active', true).order('name'),
      db.from('dept_tasks').select('id, title, owner, status, done, department, due_date, project_id').eq('done', false)
    ]);
    WA28_PEOPLE = (res[0].data) || [];
    WA28_TASKS = (res[1].data) || [];
    WA28_LOADED = true;
  } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
}

function wa28Person(name) {
  var hit = null;
  WA28_PEOPLE.forEach(function (p) { if (p.name.toLowerCase() === String(name || '').toLowerCase()) hit = p; });
  return hit;
}

function wa28Me() {
  if (WA28_ME) return WA28_ME;
  try {
    var email = (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) ? String(USER.email).toLowerCase() : '';
    if (!email) return null;
    WA28_PEOPLE.forEach(function (p) {
      if (p.email && p.email.toLowerCase() === email) WA28_ME = p;
    });
    if (!WA28_ME) {
      var local = email.split('@')[0].split('.')[0];
      WA28_PEOPLE.forEach(function (p) {
        if (!WA28_ME && p.name.split(' ')[0].toLowerCase() === local) WA28_ME = p;
      });
    }
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  return WA28_ME;
}

/* ---------- select builders ---------- */
function wa28OwnSel(current, tid) {
  var cur = String(current || '');
  var p = wa28Person(cur);
  var inList = !!p;
  var html = '<select class="w28sel" data-w28own="' + tid + '" style="border-left-color:' + esc((p && p.color) || '#797773') + '">';
  html += '<option value=""' + (cur ? '' : ' selected') + '>Unassigned</option>';
  WA28_PEOPLE.forEach(function (x) {
    html += '<option value="' + esc(x.name) + '"' + (x.name === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(x.name) + ' (' + esc(x.department) + ')</option>';
  });
  if (cur && !inList) html += '<option value="' + esc(cur) + '" selected>' + esc(cur) + '</option>';
  return html + '</select>';
}

function wa28StatSel(current, tid) {
  var cur = String(current || 'Open');
  var opts = ['Open', 'In progress', 'Blocked', 'Done'];
  var cls = 'w28st-' + cur.replace(/ /g, '');
  var html = '<select class="w28sel ' + esc(cls) + '" data-w28st="' + tid + '">';
  opts.forEach(function (s) { html += '<option value="' + s + '"' + (s === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + s + '</option>'; });
  if (opts.indexOf(cur) < 0) html += '<option value="' + esc(cur) + '" selected>' + esc(cur) + '</option>';
  return html + '</select>';
}

/* swap a text input for a people select, keeping id or data attribute */
function wa28SwapInput(el, keepAttr) {
  if (!el || el.tagName === 'SELECT') return;
  var cur = el.value || '';
  var p = wa28Person(cur);
  var sel = document.createElement('select');
  sel.className = (el.className || '') + ' w28sel';
  sel.style.borderLeftColor = (p && p.color) || '#797773';
  if (el.id) sel.id = el.id;
  (keepAttr || []).forEach(function (a) { if (el.hasAttribute(a)) sel.setAttribute(a, el.getAttribute(a)); });
  if (el.disabled) sel.disabled = true;
  var o0 = document.createElement('option');
  o0.value = ''; o0.textContent = 'Unassigned';
  sel.appendChild(o0);
  var seen = false;
  WA28_PEOPLE.forEach(function (x) {
    var o = document.createElement('option');
    o.value = x.name; o.textContent = x.name;
    if (x.name === cur) { o.selected = true; seen = true; }
    sel.appendChild(o);
  });
  if (cur && !seen) {
    var ox = document.createElement('option');
    ox.value = cur; ox.textContent = cur; ox.selected = true;
    sel.appendChild(ox);
  }
  sel.addEventListener('change', function () {
    var np = wa28Person(sel.value);
    sel.style.borderLeftColor = (np && np.color) || '#797773';
  });
  el.parentNode.replaceChild(sel, el);
}

/* ---------- profile modal ---------- */
function wa28Profile(name) {
  var p = wa28Person(name);
  if (!p) return;
  var mine = WA28_TASKS.filter(function (t) { return String(t.owner || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(p.name.toLowerCase()) >= 0; });
  var label = function (d) { return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d; };
  var rows = mine.slice(0, 12).map(function (t) {
    return '<div class="w28my-r" style="padding:5px 0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.15);font-size:13px">' + esc(t.title)
      + ' <span class="dep" style="font-size:10.5px;border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.35);border-radius:8px;padding:0 6px;color:#797773">' + esc(label(t.department)) + '</span>'
      + (t.due_date ? ' <span style="font-size:11px;color:#797773">due ' + esc(t.due_date) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>';
  }).join('');
  openModal('<h3><span class="w28dot" style="background:' + esc(p.color || '#797773') + '"></span>' + esc(p.name) + '</h3>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">' + esc(p.role || '') + ' &middot; ' + esc(label(p.department)) + (p.email ? ' &middot; ' + esc(p.email) : ' &middot; no login email on file yet') + '</p>' +
    '<h4 style="margin:10px 0 2px">Open tasks across the organization (' + mine.length + ')</h4>' +
    (rows || '<p class="m-sub">Nothing assigned right now.</p>') +
    (mine.length > 12 ? '<p class="m-sub">plus ' + (mine.length - 12) + ' more.</p>' : '') +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn ghost" id="w28pc">Close</button></div>');
  var c = document.getElementById('w28pc');
  if (c) c.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
}

/* ---------- my work card on the home page ---------- */
function wa28MyCard() {
  var me = wa28Me();
  var host = document.getElementById('overview');
  if (!host) return;
  var old = host.querySelector('.w28my');
  if (old) old.remove();
  if (!me) return;
  if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
  var mine = WA28_TASKS.filter(function (t) { return String(t.owner || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(me.name.toLowerCase()) >= 0; });
  mine.sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.due_date || '9999') < String(b.due_date || '9999') ? -1 : 1; });
  var label = function (d) { return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d; };
  var rows = mine.slice(0, 10).map(function (t) {
    return '<div class="t"><span style="flex:1;min-width:0">' + esc(t.title) + '</span>'
      + '<span class="dep">' + esc(label(t.department)) + '</span>'
      + (t.due_date ? '<span class="dep">due ' + esc(t.due_date) + '</span>' : '')
      + wa28StatSel(t.status, t.id) + '</div>';
  }).join('');
  var card = document.createElement('div');
  card.className = 'card w28my';
  card.innerHTML = '<h2 style="color:#103F39"><span class="w28dot" style="background:' + esc(me.color || '#103F39') + '"></span>Your plate, ' + esc(me.name) + '</h2>'
    + '<p class="read" style="margin:0 0 6px">' + mine.length + (mine.length === 1 ? ' open task' : ' open tasks') + ' assigned to you across the organization, soonest due first. Change a status right here.</p>'
    + (rows || '<p class="read">Nothing on your plate. Enjoy it.</p>')
    + (mine.length > 10 ? '<p class="read" style="margin-top:6px">plus ' + (mine.length - 10) + ' more on the department pages.</p>' : '');
  host.insertBefore(card, host.firstChild);
}

/* ---------- dept board rows: inline assignee + status ---------- */
function wa28DeptRows(id) {
  var panel = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!panel) return;
  if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
  var byId = {};
  WA28_TASKS.forEach(function (t) { byId[String(t.id)] = t; });
  panel.querySelectorAll('li[data-task]').forEach(function (li) {
    if (li.querySelector('.w28row')) return;
    var t = byId[String(li.getAttribute('data-task'))];
    if (!t) return;
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'w28row';
    row.innerHTML = wa28OwnSel(t.owner, t.id) + wa28StatSel(t.status, t.id);
    li.appendChild(row);
  });
}

/* ---------- writes ---------- */
async function wa28SetOwner(tid, name) {
  var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').update({ owner: name || '', updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: (typeof who === 'function' ? who() : 'dashboard') }).eq('id', Number(tid));
  if (r.error) { toast('Could not reassign'); return false; }
  WA28_TASKS.forEach(function (t) { if (String(t.id) === String(tid)) t.owner = name || ''; });
  toast(name ? 'Assigned to ' + name : 'Unassigned');
  return true;
}

async function wa28SetStatus(tid, status, selEl) {
  var isDone = status === 'Done';
  var patch = { status: status, done: isDone, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: (typeof who === 'function' ? who() : 'dashboard') };
  patch.closed_at = isDone ? new Date().toISOString() : null;
  var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').update(patch).eq('id', Number(tid));
  if (r.error) { toast('Could not change the status'); return false; }
  try {
    await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'task', entity_id: String(tid), field: isDone ? 'done' : 'status', old_value: '', new_value: status + ' (dropdown)', changed_by: (typeof who === 'function' ? who() : 'dashboard') });
  } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ }
  WA28_TASKS = WA28_TASKS.filter(function (t) { return isDone ? String(t.id) !== String(tid) : true; });
  WA28_TASKS.forEach(function (t) { if (String(t.id) === String(tid)) t.status = status; });
  if (selEl) {
    selEl.className = 'w28sel w28st-' + status.replace(/ /g, '');
    if (isDone) {
      var li = selEl.closest('li[data-task], .w27-task, .t');
      if (li) li.style.opacity = '.45';
    }
  }
  toast(isDone ? 'Closed' : 'Status: ' + status);
  return true;
}

/* ---------- event date editing ---------- */
function wa28EventDate() {
  if (typeof WA_VOL_VIEW === 'undefined' || WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'event' || !WA_VOL_EVENT) return;
  if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
  var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering');
  if (!sec || sec.querySelector('[data-w28evd]')) return;
  var ev = null;
  WA_VOL_EVENTS.forEach(function (e) { if (e.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) ev = e; });
  if (!ev || ev.status === 'completed' || ev.status === 'cancelled') return;
  var back = sec.querySelector('.vl-back');
  if (!back) return;
  var wrap = document.createElement('span');
  wrap.style.cssText = 'margin-left:12px;font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773)';
  wrap.innerHTML = 'Event date: <input type="date" class="vl-in w90" value="' + esc(ev.event_date) + '" data-w28evd="' + ev.id + '" /> <span style="opacity:.8">moving it re-anchors every step due date</span>';
  back.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', wrap);
}

/* ---------- render hooks ---------- */
(function () {
  if (typeof waVolRenderEvent === 'function') {
    var __ev28 = waVolRenderEvent;
    waVolRenderEvent = function () {
      __ev28();
      try {
        document.querySelectorAll('#volunteering input[data-vlowner]').forEach(function (el) {
          wa28SwapInput(el, ['data-vlowner']);
        });
        wa28EventDate();
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  if (typeof wa24SopModal === 'function') {
    var __m24 = wa24SopModal;
    wa24SopModal = function (tid) {
      __m24(tid);
      try { wa28SwapInput(document.getElementById('w24Owner'), []); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof wa25AddModal === 'function') {
    var __m25 = wa25AddModal;
    wa25AddModal = function (a) {
      __m25(a);
      try { wa28SwapInput(document.getElementById('w25Owner'), []); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  if (typeof wa27RenderProject === 'function') {
    var __pr28 = wa27RenderProject;
    wa27RenderProject = async function (ref, dept) {
      await __pr28(ref, dept);
      try {
        if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
        var byId = {};
        WA28_TASKS.forEach(function (t) { byId[String(t.id)] = t; });
        document.querySelectorAll('#projectpage .w27-task input[data-w27t]').forEach(function (chk) {
          var task = byId[String(chk.getAttribute('data-w27t'))];
          if (!task) return;
          var meta = chk.parentNode.querySelector('.meta');
          if (!meta || meta.querySelector('.w28sel')) return;
          var row = document.createElement('span');
          row.style.cssText = 'display:inline-flex;gap:6px;margin-left:8px;vertical-align:middle';
          row.innerHTML = wa28OwnSel(task.owner, task.id) + wa28StatSel(task.status, task.id);
          meta.appendChild(row);
        });
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  if (typeof renderSection === 'function') {
    var __rs28 = renderSection;
    renderSection = function (id) {
      __rs28(id);
      try { wa28DeptRows(id); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  function wire() {
    if (document.body.dataset.wa28Wired) return;
    document.body.dataset.wa28Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
      var own = e.target.closest('select[data-w28own]');
      if (own) { wa28SetOwner(own.getAttribute('data-w28own'), own.value); return; }
      var st = e.target.closest('select[data-w28st]');
      if (st) { wa28SetStatus(st.getAttribute('data-w28st'), st.value, st); return; }
      var evd = e.target.closest('input[data-w28evd]');
      if (evd && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(evd.value)) {
        (async function () {
          var r = await db.from('volunteer_events').update({ event_date: evd.value }).eq('id', Number(evd.getAttribute('data-w28evd')));
          if (r.error) { toast('Could not move the event'); return; }
          toast('Event moved to ' + evd.value + '. Step due dates re-anchored.');
          try {
            WA_VOL_LOADED = false;
            WA_VOL_DETAIL = null;
            await waVolRender();
          } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ }
        })();
        return;
      }
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var tag = e.target.closest('.team-tag');
      if (tag) {
        var b = tag.querySelector('b');
        if (b) { try { wa28Profile(b.textContent.trim()); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } }
      }
    });
  }

  var __sa28 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa28();
    try {
      wire();
      await wa28Load(false);
      wa28MyCard();
      if (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined') DEPT_ORDER.forEach(function (d) { try { wa28DeptRows(d); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
  try { if (document.querySelector('main')) wire(); } catch (e) { /* boot */ }
})();

   1. The goals page is organized as dropdowns: quarters first (newest
      open), then departments inside each quarter, then every goal. Each
      department goals card links through to its full list.
   2. Goals are editable: add, edit, archive. Every goal carries a
      description (used to match projects automatically), a measure, a
      quarter, a status and a due date.
   3. Projects attach to goals from the goal side too: link and unlink
      in the goal editor.
   4. Creating or editing a project suggests the best-matching goal from
      the project name and description.
   ===================================================================== */

function wa29QKey(q) {
  var m = String(q || '').match(/FY\s*(\d{4})/i);
  var fy = m ? Number(m[1]) : 0;
  var qm = String(q || '').match(/Q(\d)/i);
  return fy * 10 + (qm ? Number(qm[1]) : 0);
}

function wa29Goals() {
  return (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).filter(function (g) { return g.status !== 'archived'; });
}

/* ---------- project modal: suggest the best goal ---------- */
function wa29Score(text, g) {
  var stop = { the: 1, and: 1, for: 1, with: 1, that: 1, this: 1, from: 1, into: 1, our: 1, all: 1, are: 1 };
  var toks = String(text || '').toLowerCase().split(/[^a-z0-9]+/).filter(function (t) { return t.length > 3 && !stop[t]; });
  var hay = (String(g.title || '') + ' ' + String(g.detail || '') + ' ' + String(g.measure || '')).toLowerCase();
  var score = 0;
  var seen = {};
  toks.forEach(function (t) { if (!seen[t] && hay.indexOf(t) >= 0) { score += 1; seen[t] = 1; } });
  return score;
}

function wa29SuggestGoal() {
  var name = (document.getElementById('w27pn') || { value: '' }).value || '';
  var desc = (document.getElementById('w27pd') || { value: '' }).value || '';
  var sel = document.getElementById('w27pg');
  var hint = document.getElementById('w29gh');
  if (!sel || !hint) return;
  var text = name + ' ' + desc;
  if (text.trim().length < 6) { hint.innerHTML = ''; return; }
  var best = null, bestScore = 1;
  wa29Goals().forEach(function (g) {
    var s = wa29Score(text, g);
    if (s > bestScore) { bestScore = s; best = g; }
  });
  if (!best || String(sel.value) === String(best.id)) { hint.innerHTML = ''; return; }
  hint.innerHTML = 'Suggested goal: ' + esc(String(best.title).slice(0, 80)) + ' <a data-w29use="' + best.id + '">use it</a>';
  var use = hint.querySelector('[data-w29use]');
  if (use) use.addEventListener('click', function () {
    sel.value = String(best.id);
    hint.innerHTML = 'Linked to: ' + esc(String(best.title).slice(0, 80));
  });
}

if (typeof wa27ProjectModal === 'function') {
  (function () {
    var __pm29 = wa27ProjectModal;
    wa27ProjectModal = function (dept, existing) {
      __pm29(dept, existing);
      try {
        var sel = document.getElementById('w27pg');
        if (!sel || document.getElementById('w29gh')) return;
        var hint = document.createElement('div');
        hint.className = 'w29hint';
        hint.id = 'w29gh';
        sel.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', hint);
        ['w27pn', 'w27pd'].forEach(function (id) {
          var el = document.getElementById(id);
          if (el) el.addEventListener('input', wa29SuggestGoal);
        });
        wa29SuggestGoal();
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  })();
}

/* ---------- wiring ---------- */
(function () {
  function wire() {
    if (document.body.dataset.wa29Wired) return;
    document.body.dataset.wa29Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var all = e.target.closest('[data-w29all]');
      if (all) {
        WA29_FOCUS = all.getAttribute('data-w29all');
        location.hash = '#goals';
        try { if (typeof renderGoalsPage === 'function') renderGoalsPage(); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ }
        return;
      }
      if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
      var ge = e.target.closest('[data-w29ge]');
      if (ge) { try { wa29GoalModal(Number(ge.getAttribute('data-w29ge'))); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } return; }
      var ga = e.target.closest('[data-w29ga]');
      if (ga) {
        var bits = (ga.getAttribute('data-w29ga') || '|').split('|');
        try { wa29GoalModal(null, bits[0] || null, bits[1] || null); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ }
        return;
      }
    });
  }
  wire();
  var __sa29 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa29();
    try { wire(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();

   1. The goals page now shows the upcoming FY2027 quarters (fiscal year

      before any exist. Current quarter opens first, then the future in
      order, then the past.

      updates, tasks and projects. Suggestions are clearly marked, never
      auto added: one click opens the add form prefilled for review.
   3. An Organizational goals block sits at the top: the high level goals
      that department quarterly goals and projects roll up to, linked via
      a "rolls up to" field on every department goal.
