/* ---------- fiscal quarters (Oct 1 to Sep 30) ---------- */
function wa30Now() {
  var d = new Date();
  var m = d.getMonth() + 1;
  var y = d.getFullYear();
  if (m >= 10) return { q: 1, fy: y + 1 };
  if (m <= 3) return { q: 2, fy: y };
  if (m <= 6) return { q: 3, fy: y };
  return { q: 4, fy: y };
}

function wa30Quarters() {
  var cur = wa30Now();
  var out = [];
  var q = cur.q, fy = cur.fy;
  for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    out.push('Q' + q + ' FY' + fy);
    q += 1;
    if (q > 4) { q = 1; fy += 1; }
  }
  return out;
}

function wa30QRange(qstr) {
  var qm = String(qstr || '').match(/Q(\d)/i);
  var fm = String(qstr || '').match(/FY\s*(\d{4})/i);
  if (!qm || !fm) return '';
  var q = Number(qm[1]), fy = Number(fm[1]);
  if (q === 1) return 'Oct 1 to Dec 31, ' + (fy - 1);
  if (q === 2) return 'Jan 1 to Mar 31, ' + fy;
  if (q === 3) return 'Apr 1 to Jun 30, ' + fy;
  return 'Jul 1 to Sep 30, ' + fy;
}

async function wa30LoadIdeas() {
  try {
    var r = await db.from('goal_ideas').select('*');
    WA30_IDEAS = {};
    ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (row) { WA30_IDEAS[row.department] = row.payload || {}; });
  } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
}

/* ---------- goals page: org tier, quarters ahead, suggestions ---------- */
if (typeof renderGoalsPage === 'function') {
  renderGoalsPage = async function () {
    var body = document.getElementById('glBody');
    if (!body) return;
    if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) {
      body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Goals are available to staff accounts.</p>';
      return;
    }
    if (typeof waLoadGoals === 'function') await waLoadGoals();
    await wa30LoadIdeas();
    var all = wa29Goals();
    var head = document.querySelector('#goals .card h2');
    if (head) head.textContent = 'Goals';
    var intro = document.querySelector('#goals .card p.read');
    if (intro) intro.textContent = 'Organizational goals at the top, then quarter by quarter. Department quarterly goals and projects roll up into the organizational tier.';

    var org = all.filter(function (g) { return g.department === 'org'; });
    var dept = all.filter(function (g) { return g.department !== 'org'; });
    var html = '<div class="w30org"><h3>Organizational goals</h3>'
      + '<div class="sub">The high level direction of Welfare Avenue. Department quarterly goals and projects roll up here.</div>';
    if (!org.length) html += '<p class="read" style="margin:0 0 8px">None defined yet. Add the first one.</p>';
    org.forEach(function (g) {
      var kids = dept.filter(function (x) { return String(x.parent_goal_id) === String(g.id); }).length;
      html += waGoalRow(g);
      html += '<span class="w30kids">' + kids + (kids === 1 ? ' quarterly goal rolls' : ' quarterly goals roll') + ' up here</span>';
      html += '<div class="w29ge"><a data-w29ge="' + g.id + '">edit goal</a></div>';
    });
    html += '<div class="wa24-links"><button data-w29ga="org|FY2027">Add an organizational goal</button></div></div>';

    var upcoming = wa30Quarters();
    var quarters = upcoming.slice();
    dept.forEach(function (g) { var q = g.quarter || 'No quarter'; if (quarters.indexOf(q) < 0) quarters.push(q); });
    var curKey = wa29QKey(upcoming[0]);
    quarters.sort(function (a, b) {
      var ka = wa29QKey(a), kb = wa29QKey(b);
      var fa = ka >= curKey, fb = kb >= curKey;
      if (fa && fb) return ka - kb;
      if (!fa && !fb) return kb - ka;
      return fa ? -1 : 1;
    });

    quarters.forEach(function (q, qi) {
      var inQ = dept.filter(function (g) { return (g.quarter || 'No quarter') === q; });
      var isUpcoming = wa29QKey(q) >= curKey;
      if (!inQ.length && !isUpcoming) return;
      var range = wa30QRange(q);
      html += '<details class="w29q"' + (qi === 0 ? ' open' : '') + '><summary>' + esc(q)
        + (range ? '<span class="w30qr">' + esc(range) + '</span>' : '')
        + ' <span style="font-weight:400;font-size:12px;opacity:.65">' + inQ.length + (inQ.length === 1 ? ' goal' : ' goals') + '</span></summary>';
      var depts = (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined') ? DEPT_ORDER.slice() : [];
      inQ.forEach(function (g) { if (depts.indexOf(g.department) < 0) depts.push(g.department); });
      depts.forEach(function (d) {
        var gs = inQ.filter(function (g) { return g.department === d; });
        var ideas = (WA30_IDEAS[d] && WA30_IDEAS[d].suggestions) || [];
        var here = ideas.filter(function (s, i) {
          var sq = s.quarter && wa29QKey(s.quarter) >= curKey ? s.quarter : upcoming[1];
          s._idx = i;
          return sq === q;
        });
        if (!gs.length && !isUpcoming) return;
        if (!gs.length && !here.length && qi !== 0 && !isUpcoming) return;
        var label = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d;
        var col = (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[d]) || '#797773';
        html += '<details class="w29d" style="border-left-color:' + col + '"><summary style="color:' + col + '">' + esc(label) + '<span class="n">' + gs.length + (gs.length === 1 ? ' goal' : ' goals') + (here.length ? ' &middot; ' + here.length + ' suggested' : '') + '</span></summary>';
        gs.forEach(function (g) {
          html += waGoalRow(g);
          html += '<div class="w29ge"><a data-w29ge="' + g.id + '">edit goal</a></div>';
        });
        if (!gs.length) html += '<p class="read" style="margin:4px 0">No goals here yet.</p>';
        if (here.length) {
          html += '<details class="w30i" open><summary>Suggested goals (from the Hub, drawn from transcripts and the board; nothing is added until you say so)</summary>';
          here.forEach(function (s) {
            html += '<div class="w30sug"><b>' + esc(s.title || '') + '</b>'
              + '<div class="m">' + esc(s.detail || '') + (s.measure ? ' Measure: ' + esc(s.measure) : '') + '</div>'
              + '<div class="why">' + esc(s.why || '') + '</div>'
              + '<div style="margin-top:5px"><button class="btn mini" data-w30a="' + esc(d) + '|' + s._idx + '">Review and add</button></div></div>';
          });
          html += '<div style="margin-top:6px;font-size:11px;opacity:.6">Generated ' + esc(String((WA30_IDEAS[d] && WA30_IDEAS[d].generated) || '').slice(0, 10)) + ' &middot; <a data-w30r="' + esc(d) + '" style="cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline">refresh suggestions</a></div>';
          html += '</details>';
        } else if (qi === 0) {
          html += '<div style="font-size:11px;opacity:.6;margin:2px 0 6px"><a data-w30r="' + esc(d) + '" style="cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline">Generate suggested goals for ' + esc(label) + '</a></div>';
        }
        html += '<div class="wa24-links w29add"><button data-w29ga="' + esc(d) + '|' + esc(q) + '">Add a goal</button></div>';
        html += '</details>';
      });
      html += '</details>';
    });
    html += '<div class="wa24-links" style="margin-top:6px"><button data-w29ga="|">Add a goal in any department or quarter</button></div>';
    body.innerHTML = html;
    WA29_FOCUS = null;
  };
}

/* ---------- goal editor with the organizational tier ---------- */
if (typeof wa29GoalModal === 'function') {
  wa29GoalModal = function (gid, presetDept, presetQ, prefill) {
    var g = null;
    if (gid) (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.id) === String(gid)) g = x; });
    var isOrg = g ? g.department === 'org' : presetDept === 'org';
    var depts = ((typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined') ? DEPT_ORDER.slice() : ['opsmtl', 'opsto', 'marketing', 'fundraising', 'hr', 'board']);
    depts.push('org');
    var deptOpts = depts.map(function (d) {
      var label = d === 'org' ? 'Organization (overarching)' : ((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d);
      var sel = g ? g.department === d : presetDept === d;
      return '<option value="' + esc(d) + '"' + (sel ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(label) + '</option>';
    }).join('');
    var stats = ['not started', 'in progress', 'in review', 'delayed', 'closed'];
    var statOpts = stats.map(function (s) {
      var sel = g ? g.status === s : s === 'not started';
      return '<option value="' + s + '"' + (sel ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + s + '</option>';
    }).join('');
    var quarters = wa30Quarters();
    (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).forEach(function (x) { if (x.quarter && quarters.indexOf(x.quarter) < 0) quarters.push(x.quarter); });
    var orgGoals = wa29Goals().filter(function (x) { return x.department === 'org'; });
    var parentOpts = '<option value="">No organizational goal link</option>' + orgGoals.map(function (x) {
      var sel = g && String(g.parent_goal_id) === String(x.id);
      return '<option value="' + x.id + '"' + (sel ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(String(x.title).slice(0, 75)) + '</option>';
    }).join('');
    var linked = g ? WA27_PROJECTS.filter(function (p) { return String(p.goal_id) === String(g.id); }) : [];
    var linkedHtml = linked.length ? '<div class="w29lp">' + linked.map(function (p) {
      return '<span class="pjx">' + esc(wa27Name(p)) + '<a data-w29ul="' + p.id + '" title="Unlink">&times;</a></span>';
    }).join('') + '</div>' : (g ? '<p class="m-sub" style="margin:4px 0">No projects linked yet.</p>' : '');
    var attachable = WA27_PROJECTS.filter(function (p) { return !g || String(p.goal_id) !== String(g.id); });
    var attachOpts = '<option value="">Attach a project...</option>' + attachable.map(function (p) {
      return '<option value="' + p.id + '">' + esc(p.department + ': ' + wa27Name(p)) + '</option>';
    }).join('');
    openModal('<h3>' + (g ? 'Edit goal' : (isOrg ? 'Add an organizational goal' : 'Add a goal')) + '</h3>' +
      '<p class="m-sub">' + (isOrg ? 'The overarching direction. Department quarterly goals and projects roll up to this.' : 'The description helps match projects to this goal automatically, so say what winning looks like in plain words.') + '</p>' +
      '<label>Goal title</label><input type="text" id="w29t" value="' + esc(g ? g.title : '') + '" />' +
      '<label>Description</label><textarea id="w29de" rows="3">' + esc(g ? (g.detail || '') : '') + '</textarea>' +
      '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Measure of success</label><input type="text" id="w29m" value="' + esc(g ? (g.measure || '') : '') + '" placeholder="What number or outcome counts" /></div>' +
      '<div><label>Due date</label><input type="date" id="w29dd" value="' + esc(g ? (g.due_date || '') : '') + '" /></div></div>' +
      '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Department</label><select id="w29dp"' + (g ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + deptOpts + '</select></div>' +
      '<div><label>' + (isOrg ? 'Horizon (FY2027, Long term...)' : 'Quarter') + '</label><input type="text" id="w29q" list="w29ql" value="' + esc(g ? (g.quarter || '') : (presetQ || 'Q1 FY2027')) + '" placeholder="Q1 FY2027" /><datalist id="w29ql">' + quarters.map(function (q) { return '<option value="' + esc(q) + '"></option>'; }).join('') + '</datalist></div></div>' +
      '<label>Status</label><select id="w29st">' + statOpts + '</select>' +
      (!isOrg ? '<label style="margin-top:8px">Rolls up to organizational goal</label><select id="w30pg">' + parentOpts + '</select>' : '') +
      (g ? '<label style="margin-top:8px">Linked projects</label>' + linkedHtml +
        '<select id="w29at">' + attachOpts + '</select>' +
        '<label style="display:flex;gap:7px;align-items:center;margin-top:10px"><input type="checkbox" id="w29ar" style="width:15px;height:15px" /> Archive this goal (removes it from every view)</label>' : '') +
      '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w29sv">' + (g ? 'Save the goal' : 'Add the goal') + '</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w29cx">Cancel</button></div>');
    if (prefill) {
      try {
        if (prefill.title) document.getElementById('w29t').value = prefill.title;
        if (prefill.detail) document.getElementById('w29de').value = prefill.detail;
        if (prefill.measure) document.getElementById('w29m').value = prefill.measure;
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    }
    var cancel = document.getElementById('w29cx');
    if (cancel) cancel.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    var at = document.getElementById('w29at');
    if (at && g) at.addEventListener('change', async function () {
      var pid = Number(at.value);
      if (!pid) return;
      var r = await db.from('projects').update({ goal_id: g.id }).eq('id', pid);
      if (r.error) { toast('Could not link it'); return; }
      toast('Project linked');
      await wa27Load(true);
      wa29GoalModal(g.id);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-w29ul]').forEach(function (a) {
      a.addEventListener('click', async function () {
        var r = await db.from('projects').update({ goal_id: null }).eq('id', Number(a.getAttribute('data-w29ul')));
        if (r.error) { toast('Could not unlink it'); return; }
        toast('Project unlinked');
        await wa27Load(true);
        wa29GoalModal(g.id);
      });
    });
    var save = document.getElementById('w29sv');
    if (save) save.addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var title = (document.getElementById('w29t').value || '').trim();
      if (!title) { toast('The goal needs a title'); return; }
      save.disabled = true;
      var patch = {
        title: title,
        detail: (document.getElementById('w29de').value || '').trim() || null,
        measure: (document.getElementById('w29m').value || '').trim() || null,
        due_date: (document.getElementById('w29dd').value || '') || null,
        quarter: (document.getElementById('w29q').value || '').trim() || 'Q1 FY2027',
        status: document.getElementById('w29st').value,
        updated_by: (typeof who === 'function' ? who() : 'dashboard'),
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      };
      var pg = document.getElementById('w30pg');
      if (pg) patch.parent_goal_id = Number(pg.value) || null;
      var ar = document.getElementById('w29ar');
      if (ar && ar.checked) patch.status = 'archived';
      var r;
      if (g) r = await db.from('goals').update(patch).eq('id', g.id);
      else {
        patch.department = document.getElementById('w29dp').value;
        patch.source = 'dashboard';
        patch.sort = 999;
        r = await db.from('goals').insert(patch);
      }
      if (r.error) { save.disabled = false; toast('Could not save the goal'); return; }
      closeModal();
      toast(g ? 'Goal saved' : 'Goal added');
      try {
        if (typeof waLoadGoals === 'function') await waLoadGoals();
        if (typeof renderGoalsPage === 'function') renderGoalsPage();
        if (typeof renderDeptGoalsCards === 'function') renderDeptGoalsCards();
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    });
  };
}

/* ---------- wiring ---------- */
(function () {
  function wire() {
    if (document.body.dataset.wa30Wired) return;
    document.body.dataset.wa30Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
      if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
      var a = e.target.closest('[data-w30a]');
      if (a) {
        var bits = (a.getAttribute('data-w30a') || '|').split('|');
        var idea = ((WA30_IDEAS[bits[0]] || {}).suggestions || [])[Number(bits[1])];
        if (idea) {
          try { wa29GoalModal(null, bits[0], idea.quarter || 'Q1 FY2027', idea); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ }
        }
        return;
      }
      var rf = e.target.closest('[data-w30r]');
      if (rf) {
        var d = rf.getAttribute('data-w30r');
        rf.textContent = 'Thinking about ' + d + '... twenty seconds or so';
        try {
          var r = await db.functions.invoke('goal-propose', { body: { department: d } });
          if (r.error) throw r.error;
          toast('Fresh suggestions ready');
          if (typeof renderGoalsPage === 'function') renderGoalsPage();
        } catch (x) {
          toast('Could not generate suggestions. Try again in a minute.');
          rf.textContent = 'refresh suggestions';
        }
        return;
      }
    });
  }
  wire();
  var __sa30 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa30();
    try { wire(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v31 confirmations, templates, event summary, recipes, requests
   (29/07/26)
   1. Public confirmation page at #confirm/<token>: no login needed.
      Role-aware questions: servers say what they are bringing; cooks say
      where they cook, whose food, and if they need containers; drivers
      give vehicle type and model; photographers give their camera; team
      leaders just confirm. Answers land on the event roster instantly
      (confirmed, declined, or needs attention).
   2. Text volunteers gains templates per audience, all carrying a
      personal {{confirm_link}}.
   3. Every event page gets a live summary: confirmations by role, the
      cars coming, cook locations and addresses, containers needed, a
      Google Maps pickup route, the server itinerary link (editable),
      and checklist progress.
   4. A Recipes tab in Volunteering (backed by the Google Drive folder)
      and a recipe dropdown per cook on the event summary.
   5. Requests reach people: open requests for you or your department
      show on your home card, assignable to a person.
   6. The dashboard is now the Welfare Avenue Hub: new title and the
      Welfare Avenue sign logo in the header, the sign-in card, and the
      volunteer confirmation page.
   7. The Text volunteers window shows the Quo web login for staff
      (stored behind staff-only row security, never in the page source).
   ===================================================================== */

/* ---------- 1. public confirmation page ---------- */
(function () {
  var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '');
  if (h.indexOf('#confirm/') !== 0) return;
  var token = h.slice(9).split('?')[0].trim();
  /* wave171 (planned as wave169a): the confirm page lives at hub.welfareavenue.com/c/<token> now (branded, phone-first, what to expect);
     old #confirm/ links from earlier texts go there */
  if (/^[A-Za-z0-9]{12,40}$/.test(token) && /welfareavenue\.com$/.test(location.hostname)) { location.replace('/c/' + token); return; }
  window.__wa31Confirm = true;
  if (typeof showGate === 'function') {
    var __sg31 = showGate;
    showGate = function (x, y) { if (window.__wa31Confirm) return; __sg31(x, y); };
  }
  var wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.id = 'wa31c';
  wrap.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9999;background:#f4f7f6;overflow:auto;font-family:system-ui,-apple-system,Segoe UI,Roboto,sans-serif;color:#103F39;';
  wrap.innerHTML = '<div style="max-width:460px;margin:0 auto;padding:28px 18px"><img src="' + WA31_LOGO + '" alt="Welfare Avenue" style="height:66px;display:block;margin:0 0 8px"><div style="font-weight:800;color:#103F39;font-size:19px;margin-bottom:4px">Welfare Avenue</div><div id="wa31body" style="background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:14px;padding:18px;box-shadow:0 2px 14px rgba(16,63,57,.08)">Loading your invitation...</div></div>';
  function mount() { if (document.body) { document.body.appendChild(wrap); } else { setTimeout(mount, 30); } }
  mount();

  function el(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
  function val(id) { var e = el(id); return e ? e.value : ''; }
  function checked(name) {
    var e = document.querySelector('input[name="' + name + '"]:checked');
    return e ? e.value : '';
  }
  function esc31(s) { var d = document.createElement('div'); d.textContent = s == null ? '' : String(s); return d.innerHTML; }

  function form(info) {
    var role = String(info.role || '').toLowerCase();
    var a = info.answers || {};
    var d = new Date(String(info.event_date) + 'T12:00:00');
    var dateStr = isNaN(d) ? String(info.event_date) : d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' });
    var html = '<h2 style="margin:0 0 4px;font-size:18px">Hi ' + esc31(String(info.name || '').split(' ')[0]) + '!</h2>'
      + '<p style="margin:0 0 12px;font-size:14px;color:#797773">You are on the volunteer list as a <b>' + esc31(info.role || 'volunteer') + '</b> for our serving event on <b>' + esc31(dateStr) + '</b> in ' + esc31(info.location || 'Montreal') + '.</p>';
    if (info.already) html += '<p style="font-size:12.5px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);border-radius:8px;padding:7px 10px">You already answered (' + esc31(info.already) + '). You can update your answer below.</p>';
    var radio = function (name, v, label, cur) {
      return '<label style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;padding:7px 0;font-size:14.5px;cursor:pointer"><input type="radio" name="' + name + '" value="' + v + '"' + (cur === v ? ' checked' : '') + ' style="width:17px;height:17px" /> ' + label + '</label>';
    };
    var input = function (id, label, cur, ph) {
      return '<label style="display:block;font-size:12.5px;color:#797773;margin-top:10px">' + label + '</label><input id="' + id + '" value="' + esc31(cur || '') + '" placeholder="' + (ph || '') + '" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;padding:9px 10px;border:1px solid #cdd8d4;border-radius:9px;font-size:14px" />';
    };
    html += '<div style="border-top:1px solid #e4ebe8;margin-top:6px;padding-top:6px"><b style="font-size:14px">Can you make it?</b>'
      + radio('att', 'yes', 'Yes, I will be there', a.attending || '')
      + radio('att', 'no', 'Sorry, I cannot make it', a.attending || '') + '</div>';
    html += '<div id="wa31x">';
    if (role.indexOf('server') === 0 || role.indexOf('dish') === 0) {
      html += input('x_bringing', 'What are you bringing to the event? (water, serving spoons, nothing is fine too)', a.bringing, 'What you are bringing');
    } else if (role.indexOf('cook') === 0) {
      html += '<div style="margin-top:10px"><b style="font-size:13.5px">Where are you cooking?</b>'
        + radio('loc', 'our kitchen', 'At the Welfare Avenue kitchen', a.cook_location || '')
        + radio('loc', 'home', 'From home', a.cook_location || '') + '</div>'
        + '<div style="margin-top:6px"><b style="font-size:13.5px">Whose ingredients?</b>'
        + radio('food', 'own food', 'I am cooking my own ingredients', a.food_source || '')
        + radio('food', 'provided', 'Welfare Avenue provides the food', a.food_source || '') + '</div>'
        + '<div style="margin-top:6px"><b style="font-size:13.5px">Containers?</b>'
        + radio('cont', 'needs containers', 'I need containers for the food', a.containers || '')
        + radio('cont', 'has containers', 'I have my own containers', a.containers || '') + '</div>';
    } else if (role.indexOf('driver') === 0) {
      html += '<label style="display:block;font-size:12.5px;color:#797773;margin-top:10px">What type of car will you drive?</label>'
        + '<select id="x_vehicle" style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;padding:9px 10px;border:1px solid #cdd8d4;border-radius:9px;font-size:14px">'
        + ['', 'Sedan', 'Hatchback', 'SUV', 'Truck', 'Van'].map(function (v) {
          return '<option value="' + v + '"' + ((a.vehicle || '') === v ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + (v || 'Pick one') + '</option>';
        }).join('') + '</select>'
        + input('x_model', 'Car model (for example Toyota Corolla)', a.model, 'Make and model');
    } else if (role.indexOf('photo') === 0) {
      html += input('x_camera', 'What camera will you use?', a.camera, 'Camera make and model');
    }
    html += input('x_note', 'Anything else we should know? (optional)', a.note, '');
    html += '</div>';
    html += '<button id="wa31go" style="margin-top:16px;width:100%;padding:12px;background:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2;border:0;border-radius:10px;font-size:15px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer">Send my answer</button>'
      + '<p style="font-size:11px;color:#8b9a95;margin-top:10px">Questions? Text the Welfare Phone: 438 808 7754.</p>';
    el('wa31body').innerHTML = html;
    el('wa31go').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var att = checked('att');
      if (!att) { el('wa31go').textContent = 'Pick yes or no first'; return; }
      el('wa31go').disabled = true;
      el('wa31go').textContent = 'Sending...';
      var p = { attending: att, note: val('x_note') || null };
      if (el('x_bringing')) p.bringing = val('x_bringing') || null;
      if (document.querySelector('input[name="loc"]')) {
        p.cook_location = checked('loc') || null;
        p.food_source = checked('food') || null;
        p.containers = checked('cont') || null;
      }
      if (el('x_vehicle')) { p.vehicle = val('x_vehicle') || null; p.model = val('x_model') || null; }
      if (el('x_camera')) p.camera = val('x_camera') || null;
      try {
        var r = await db.rpc('volunteer_confirm_submit', { p_token: token, p: p });
        if (r.error || !(r.data && r.data.ok)) throw new Error('save failed');
        el('wa31body').innerHTML = '<h2 style="margin:0 0 6px;font-size:18px">' + (att === 'yes' ? 'You are confirmed. Thank you!' : 'Thanks for letting us know.') + '</h2>'
          + '<p style="font-size:14px;color:#797773">' + (att === 'yes' ? 'We have everything we need. See you at the event, and watch for the itinerary text a couple of days before.' : 'No worries at all. We hope to see you at a future serving event.') + '</p>';
      } catch (e) {
        el('wa31go').disabled = false;
        el('wa31go').textContent = 'Try again';
      }
    });
  }

  var tries = 0;
  var iv = setInterval(async function () {
    tries += 1;
    if (typeof db === 'undefined' || !db || !db.rpc) { /* audit 26/09/26: db is a script-level let, never on window */
      if (tries > 100) { clearInterval(iv); el('wa31body').innerHTML = 'Could not load. Refresh the page.'; }
      return;
    }
    clearInterval(iv);
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('volunteer_confirm_get', { p_token: token });
      if (r.error || !r.data) {
        el('wa31body').innerHTML = '<b>This link is not valid.</b><p style="font-size:13px;color:#797773">It may have been replaced by a newer text. Reply to our message and we will sort it out.</p>';
        return;
      }
      form(r.data);
    } catch (e) {
      el('wa31body').innerHTML = 'Could not load. Refresh the page.';
    }
  }, 100);
})();

/* ---------- 2. texting templates with confirm links ---------- */
var WA31_TPL = {
  cook: 'Hi {{first_name}}, it is Welfare Avenue. Confirming you as a COOK for our {{event_date}} serving event. Tap here to confirm and tell us where you are cooking and if you need containers: {{confirm_link}}',
  server: 'Hi {{first_name}}, it is Welfare Avenue. Confirming you as a SERVER for {{event_date}}. Tap here to confirm and tell us what you are bringing: {{confirm_link}}',
  driver: 'Hi {{first_name}}, it is Welfare Avenue. Confirming you as a DRIVER for {{event_date}}. Tap here to confirm and tell us which car you will drive: {{confirm_link}}',
  photographer: 'Hi {{first_name}}, it is Welfare Avenue. Confirming you as our PHOTOGRAPHER for {{event_date}}. Confirm and tell us your camera here: {{confirm_link}}',
  team_lead: 'Hi {{first_name}}, it is Welfare Avenue. Confirming you as a TEAM LEADER for {{event_date}}. One tap to confirm: {{confirm_link}}',
  availability: 'Hi {{first_name}}, it is Welfare Avenue. We would love your help at our Sunday serving event on {{event_date}}. Can you make it? Reply YES or NO. Thank you!'
};

if (typeof waTxtModal === 'function') {
  (function () {
    var __tm31 = waTxtModal;
    waTxtModal = function () {
      __tm31();
      try {
        var msg = document.getElementById('vtMsg');
        if (!msg || document.getElementById('vtTpl')) return;
        var sel = document.createElement('select');
        sel.id = 'vtTpl';
        sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Templates...</option>'
          + '<option value="cook">Confirm cooks (with confirm link)</option>'
          + '<option value="server">Confirm servers (with confirm link)</option>'
          + '<option value="driver">Confirm drivers (with confirm link)</option>'
          + '<option value="photographer">Confirm photographer (with confirm link)</option>'
          + '<option value="team_lead">Confirm team leaders (with confirm link)</option>'
          + '<option value="availability">Availability outreach (reply YES or NO)</option>';
        sel.style.marginBottom = '6px';
        msg.parentNode.insertBefore(sel, msg);
        var note = document.createElement('p');
        note.className = 'm-sub';
        note.textContent = '{{confirm_link}} becomes a personal confirmation page per volunteer; their answers land on the event roster automatically.';
        msg.parentNode.insertBefore(note, msg);
        try {
          db.from('staff_notes').select('v').eq('k', 'quo_login').maybeSingle().then(function (r) {
            if (r && r.data && r.data.v && !document.getElementById('w31quo') && msg.parentNode) {
              var q = document.createElement('p');
              q.className = 'm-sub';
              q.id = 'w31quo';
              q.textContent = r.data.v;
              msg.parentNode.insertBefore(q, msg);
            }
          });
        } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ }
        sel.addEventListener('change', function () {
          if (WA31_TPL[sel.value]) msg.value = WA31_TPL[sel.value];
          var aud = document.getElementById('vtAud');
          if (aud && ['cook', 'driver', 'server', 'photographer'].indexOf(sel.value) >= 0) {
            var has = false;
            aud.querySelectorAll('option').forEach(function (o) { if (o.value === sel.value) has = true; });
            if (has && aud.value !== 'roster') aud.value = sel.value;
          }
        });
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  })();
}

/* replace the preview builder so the audience role travels with the batch */
if (typeof waTxtPreview === 'function') {
  waTxtPreview = async function (ev, dateStr) {
    var out = document.getElementById('vtOut');
    var msg = (document.getElementById('vtMsg').value || '').trim();
    if (msg.length < 5) { toast('Write the message first'); return; }
    var aud = document.getElementById('vtAud').value;
    var stat = aud === 'roster' ? document.getElementById('vtStat').value : '';
    out.innerHTML = '<p class="m-sub">Building the preview...</p>';
    try {
      var who = await waTxtResolve(aud, stat);
      if (!who.ids.length && !who.phones.length) {
        out.innerHTML = '<p class="m-sub">Nobody matches that audience with a phone on file.</p>';
        return;
      }
      WA_TXT_PAYLOAD = {
        message: msg,
        person_ids: who.ids,
        phones: who.phones,
        event_id: ev ? ev.id : null,
        event_date: dateStr,
        role: ['cook', 'driver', 'server', 'dishwasher', 'photographer'].indexOf(aud) >= 0 ? aud : null,
        dry_run: true
      };
      var r = await db.functions.invoke('quo-send', { body: WA_TXT_PAYLOAD });
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      var d = r.data || {};
      var rows = (d.previews || []).slice(0, 8).map(function (p) {
        return '<div style="border-top:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.25);padding:6px 0;font-size:12px;">' +
          '<b>' + esc(p.name || p.phone) + '</b> <span style="opacity:.6">' + esc(p.phone) + '</span><br />' + esc(p.text) + '</div>';
      }).join('');
      var more = d.recipients > 8 ? '<p class="m-sub">plus ' + (d.recipients - 8) + ' more with the same wording.</p>' : '';
      out.innerHTML = '<h4 style="margin:12px 0 4px">' + d.recipients + (d.recipients === 1 ? ' text ready' : ' texts ready') + '</h4>' +
        (d.skipped_no_phone ? '<p class="m-sub">' + d.skipped_no_phone + ' skipped with no phone on file.</p>' : '') +
        (d.skipped_no_link ? '<p class="m-sub">' + d.skipped_no_link + ' skipped: no volunteer record to attach a confirm link to.</p>' : '') +
        rows + more +
        (d.window_open === false ? '<p class="m-sub">It is outside 9 am to 7 pm in Montreal, so these texts wait and go at 9 am.</p>' : '') +
        '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="vtSend">' + (d.window_open === false ? 'Queue ' + d.recipients + (d.recipients === 1 ? ' text' : ' texts') + ' for 9 am' : 'Send ' + d.recipients + (d.recipients === 1 ? ' text' : ' texts') + ' now') + '</button></div>';
      var send = document.getElementById('vtSend');
      if (send) send.addEventListener('click', waTxtSend);
    } catch (e) {
      out.innerHTML = '<p class="m-sub">Preview failed. Try again.</p>';
    }
  };
}

/* ---------- recipes cache ---------- */
async function wa31LoadRecipes(force) {
  if (WA31_RLOADED && !force) return;
  try {
    var r = await db.from('recipes').select('*').eq('active', true).order('sort').order('id');
    WA31_RECIPES = (r.data) || [];
    WA31_RLOADED = true;
  } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
}

/* ---------- 3. event summary ---------- */
async function wa31Summary() {
  if (typeof WA_VOL_VIEW === 'undefined' || WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'event' || !WA_VOL_DETAIL) return;
  var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering');
  if (!sec || sec.querySelector('.w31sum')) return;
  var anchor = sec.querySelector('.vl-back');
  if (!anchor) return;
  try { await waVolLoadPeople(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  await wa31LoadRecipes(false);
  var ev = null;
  WA_VOL_EVENTS.forEach(function (e) { if (e.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) ev = e; });
  var roster = WA_VOL_DETAIL.roster || [];
  var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();
  var byPerson = {};
  (typeof WA_VOL_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_PEOPLE : []).forEach(function (p) { byPerson[String(p.id)] = p; });

  var counts = { confirmed: 0, declined: 0, waiting: 0 };
  roster.forEach(function (r) {
    var s = String(r.status || '').toLowerCase();
    if (s.indexOf('confirm') === 0) counts.confirmed += 1;
    else if (s.indexOf('declin') === 0 || s === 'unavailable') counts.declined += 1; /* wave171 (planned as wave169a): a NO or I can't make it sets Unavailable */
    else counts.waiting += 1;
  });
  var cooks = roster.filter(function (r) { return String(r.role || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('cook') === 0; });
  var drivers = roster.filter(function (r) { return String(r.role || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('driver') === 0; });
  var servers = roster.filter(function (r) { return ['server', 'dishwasher'].indexOf(String(r.role || '').toLowerCase()) >= 0; });
  var photos = roster.filter(function (r) { return String(r.role || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('photo') === 0; });

  var homeAddrs = [];
  var cookRows = cooks.map(function (r) {
    var c = r.confirm || {};
    var p = r.person_id ? byPerson[String(r.person_id)] : null;
    var addr = (p && p.address) || '';
    if (String(c.cook_location || '') === 'home' && addr) homeAddrs.push(addr);
    var bits = [];
    if (c.cook_location) bits.push('<span class="b">' + esc(c.cook_location) + '</span>');
    if (c.food_source) bits.push('<span class="b">' + esc(c.food_source) + '</span>');
    if (c.containers) bits.push('<span class="b' + (c.containers === 'needs containers' ? ' no' : '') + '">' + esc(c.containers) + '</span>');
    if (addr) bits.push('<span class="b">' + esc(addr) + '</span>');
    var recSel = '';
    if (staff) {
      recSel = '<select class="w28sel" data-w31r="' + r.id + '"><option value="">Assign a recipe</option>'
        + WA31_RECIPES.map(function (x) {
          return '<option value="' + x.id + '"' + (String(r.recipe_id) === String(x.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(x.name) + '</option>';
        }).join('') + '</select>';
    }
    var rec = null;
    WA31_RECIPES.forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.id) === String(r.recipe_id)) rec = x; });
    return '<div class="w31cook"><b>' + esc(r.person_name || '') + '</b>'
      + '<span class="b' + (String(r.status || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('confirm') === 0 ? ' ok' : '') + '">' + esc(r.status || 'TBD') + '</span>'
      + bits.join('')
      + recSel
      + (rec && rec.drive_url ? ' <a href="' + esc(rec.drive_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">recipe</a>' : '')
      + '</div>';
  }).join('');

  var carList = drivers.map(function (r) {
    var c = r.confirm || {};
    return '<span class="b' + (String(r.status || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('confirm') === 0 ? ' ok' : '') + '">' + esc(r.person_name || '')
      + (c.vehicle ? ': ' + esc(c.vehicle) + (c.model ? ' (' + esc(c.model) + ')' : '') : ': car TBD') + '</span>';
  }).join(' ');
  var bringing = servers.filter(function (r) { return r.confirm && r.confirm.bringing; }).map(function (r) {
    return '<span class="b">' + esc(r.person_name.split(' ')[0]) + ': ' + esc(r.confirm.bringing) + '</span>';
  }).join(' ');
  var cams = photos.filter(function (r) { return r.confirm && r.confirm.camera; }).map(function (r) {
    return '<span class="b">' + esc(r.person_name.split(' ')[0]) + ': ' + esc(r.confirm.camera) + '</span>';
  }).join(' ');
  var needCont = cooks.filter(function (r) { return r.confirm && r.confirm.containers === 'needs containers'; }).length;
  var route = homeAddrs.length
    ? 'https://www.google.com/maps/dir/' + homeAddrs.map(function (a) { return encodeURIComponent(a); }).join('/')
    : null;

  var div = document.createElement('div');
  div.className = 'w31sum';
  div.innerHTML = '<h4>Event summary</h4>'
    + '<div class="row"><span class="b ok">' + counts.confirmed + ' confirmed</span><span class="b no">' + counts.declined + ' declined</span><span class="b">' + counts.waiting + ' waiting</span>'
    + (ev && ev.steps_total ? '<span class="b">' + ev.steps_done + ' of ' + ev.steps_total + ' checklist steps done</span>' : '')
    + (needCont ? '<span class="b no">' + needCont + (needCont === 1 ? ' cook needs containers' : ' cooks need containers') + '</span>' : '') + '</div>'
    + (carList ? '<div class="row"><b>Cars:</b> ' + carList + '</div>' : '')
    + (bringing ? '<div class="row"><b>Servers bringing:</b> ' + bringing + '</div>' : '')
    + (cams ? '<div class="row"><b>Cameras:</b> ' + cams + '</div>' : '')
    + (route ? '<div class="row"><a href="' + esc(route) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Google Maps pickup route for the home cooks (' + homeAddrs.length + ' stops)</a></div>' : '')
    + '<div class="row">' + (ev && ev.itinerary_url ? '<a href="' + esc(ev.itinerary_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open the server and photographer itinerary</a>' : 'No itinerary link yet.')
    + (staff ? ' <input type="text" class="vl-in" style="width:230px" placeholder="Paste the Canva itinerary link" value="' + esc((ev && ev.itinerary_url) || '') + '" data-w31itin="' + (ev ? ev.id : '') + '" />' : '') + '</div>'
    + (cooks.length ? '<div style="margin-top:6px"><b style="font-size:13px">Cooks and recipes</b>' + cookRows + '</div>' : '');
  var after = anchor.nextElementSibling && anchor.nextElementSibling.tagName === 'SPAN' ? anchor.nextElementSibling : anchor;
  after.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', div);
}

/* ---------- 4. recipes tab ---------- */
async function wa31Recipes() {
  await wa31LoadRecipes(true);
  var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();
  var html = '<p class="vl-empty" style="padding:0 0 12px">The recipe library, linked to the Google Drive recipes folder. Assign one to each cook from any event page summary.</p>';
  WA31_RECIPES.forEach(function (x) {
    html += '<div class="w31rec"><b>' + esc(x.name) + '</b>'
      + (x.drive_url ? ' <a href="' + esc(x.drive_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:#103F39">open in Drive</a>' : '')
      + (x.notes ? '<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted,#797773)">' + esc(x.notes) + '</div>' : '')
      + (staff ? '<div style="font-size:11.5px;margin-top:3px"><a data-w31re="' + x.id + '" style="color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline">edit</a></div>' : '')
      + '</div>';
  });
  if (staff) html += '<div class="wa24-links" style="margin-top:12px"><button data-w31ra2="1">Add a recipe</button></div>';
  waVolPaint(html);
}

function wa31RecipeModal(rid) {
  var x = null;
  WA31_RECIPES.forEach(function (r) { if (String(r.id) === String(rid)) x = r; });
  openModal('<h3>' + (x ? 'Edit recipe' : 'Add a recipe') + '</h3>' +
    '<label>Recipe name</label><input type="text" id="w31rn" value="' + esc(x ? x.name : '') + '" />' +
    '<label>Google Drive link</label><input type="text" id="w31rl" value="' + esc(x ? (x.drive_url || '') : '') + '" placeholder="https://drive.google.com/..." />' +
    '<label>Notes</label><textarea id="w31rno" rows="2">' + esc(x ? (x.notes || '') : '') + '</textarea>' +
    (x ? '<label style="display:flex;gap:7px;align-items:center;margin-top:8px"><input type="checkbox" id="w31rar" style="width:15px;height:15px" /> Remove from the library</label>' : '') +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w31rs">Save</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w31rc">Cancel</button></div>');
  var c = document.getElementById('w31rc');
  if (c) c.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  var s = document.getElementById('w31rs');
  if (s) s.addEventListener('click', async function () {
    var name = (document.getElementById('w31rn').value || '').trim();
    if (!name) { toast('The recipe needs a name'); return; }
    s.disabled = true;
    var patch = {
      name: name,
      drive_url: (document.getElementById('w31rl').value || '').trim() || null,
      notes: (document.getElementById('w31rno').value || '').trim() || null
    };
    var ar = document.getElementById('w31rar');
    if (ar && ar.checked) patch.active = false;
    var r;
    if (x) r = await db.from('recipes').update(patch).eq('id', x.id);
    else { patch.sort = 99; r = await db.from('recipes').insert(patch); }
    if (r.error) { s.disabled = false; toast('Could not save it'); return; }
    closeModal();
    toast('Saved');
    wa31Recipes();
  });
}

/* ---------- 5. requests on the home card ---------- */
async function wa31Reqs() {
  var me = (typeof wa28Me === 'function') ? wa28Me() : null;
  var card = document.querySelector('#overview .w28my');
  if (!me || !card || card.querySelector('.w31req')) return;
  try {
    var r = await db.from('dept_requests').select('*').eq('done', false).order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(30);
    var reqs = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (q) {
      return (q.assignee && q.assignee.toLowerCase() === me.name.toLowerCase()) || q.department === me.department;
    });
    if (!reqs.length) return;
    var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();
    var html = '<h3 class="block" style="margin-top:14px;color:#103F39">Requests for you and your department (' + reqs.length + ')</h3>';
    reqs.slice(0, 8).forEach(function (q) {
      html += '<div class="w31req"><span style="flex:1;min-width:0"><b>' + esc(q.title || q.type_key || 'Request') + '</b>'
        + ' <span style="font-size:11px;opacity:.6">' + esc(q.status || '') + '</span></span>'
        + '<span class="dep" style="font-size:10.5px;border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.35);border-radius:8px;padding:0 7px;color:#797773">' + esc(q.department || '') + '</span>'
        + (staff ? '<select class="w28sel" data-w31ra="' + q.id + '"><option value="">' + (q.assignee ? esc(q.assignee) : 'Assign to...') + '</option>'
          + (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []).map(function (p) {
            return '<option value="' + esc(p.name) + '">' + esc(p.name) + '</option>';
          }).join('') + '</select>' : '')
        + '</div>';
    });
    var wrap = document.createElement('div');
    wrap.innerHTML = html;
    card.appendChild(wrap);
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
}

/* ---------- wiring ---------- */
(function () {
  if (typeof waVolShell === 'function') {
    var __sh31 = waVolShell;
    waVolShell = function (inner) {
      var html = __sh31(inner);
      try {
        html = html.replace('Inventory</span>', 'Inventory</span><span class="fchip' + (typeof WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'undefined' && WA_VOL_VIEW === 'recipes' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-vl="recipes">Recipes</span>');
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return html;
    };
  }
  if (typeof waVolRender === 'function') {
    var __vr31 = waVolRender;
    waVolRender = async function () {
      if (typeof WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'undefined' && WA_VOL_VIEW === 'recipes') {
        try { await waVolLoad(false); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
        await wa31Recipes();
        return;
      }
      await __vr31();
    };
  }
  if (typeof waVolRenderEvent === 'function') {
    var __ev31 = waVolRenderEvent;
    waVolRenderEvent = function () {
      __ev31();
      try { wa31Summary(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  function wire() {
    if (document.body.dataset.wa31Wired) return;
    document.body.dataset.wa31Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('change', async function (e) {
      if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
      var rr = e.target.closest('select[data-w31r]');
      if (rr) {
        var r1 = await db.from('event_roster').update({ recipe_id: Number(rr.value) || null }).eq('id', Number(rr.getAttribute('data-w31r')));
        if (r1.error) { toast('Could not assign the recipe'); return; }
        (WA_VOL_DETAIL && WA_VOL_DETAIL.roster || []).forEach(function (x) {
          if (String(x.id) === String(rr.getAttribute('data-w31r'))) x.recipe_id = Number(rr.value) || null;
        });
        toast('Recipe assigned');
        return;
      }
      var it = e.target.closest('input[data-w31itin]');
      if (it) {
        var r2 = await db.from('volunteer_events').update({ itinerary_url: (it.value || '').trim() || null }).eq('id', Number(it.getAttribute('data-w31itin')));
        if (r2.error) { toast('Could not save the itinerary link'); return; }
        WA_VOL_EVENTS.forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.id) === String(it.getAttribute('data-w31itin'))) x.itinerary_url = (it.value || '').trim() || null; });
        toast('Itinerary link saved');
        return;
      }
      var ra = e.target.closest('select[data-w31ra]');
      if (ra && ra.value) {
        var r3 = await db.from('dept_requests').update({ assignee: ra.value, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: (typeof who === 'function' ? who() : 'dashboard') }).eq('id', Number(ra.getAttribute('data-w31ra')));
        if (r3.error) { toast('Could not assign it'); return; }
        toast('Request assigned to ' + ra.value);
        return;
      }
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
      var re = e.target.closest('[data-w31re]');
      if (re) { try { wa31RecipeModal(re.getAttribute('data-w31re')); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } return; }
      var ra2 = e.target.closest('[data-w31ra2]');
      if (ra2) { try { wa31RecipeModal(null); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } return; }
    });
  }
  wire();
  var __sa31 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa31();
    try {
      wire();
      wa31Reqs();
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v32 assignee dropdowns on the master SOP
   (29/07/26)
   Every step on the Volunteering > SOP master template now carries the
   same color-coded people dropdown used everywhere else. Changing it
   saves to the master template and flows straight through to every
   upcoming planned event; events already in progress or done keep
   their own assignments.
   Also fixed underneath: owner edits on master rows used to be sent
   to the per-event table with a broken id, so they never saved. They
   now write to the template itself. The due date box is hidden on
   master rows, because dues belong to real events and anchor to each
   event date.
   ===================================================================== */

(function () {
  /* master rows carry ids like "tmpl-42"; route their saves to the template */
  if (typeof waVolSaveStepField === 'function') {
    var __sf32 = waVolSaveStepField;
    waVolSaveStepField = async function (id, field, value) {
      var sid = String(id || '');
      if (sid.indexOf('tmpl-') !== 0) return __sf32(id, field, value);
      if (field !== 'owner') return;
      /* sop_template ids are text (ClickUp ids), never coerce to Number */
      var tid = sid.slice(5);
      if (!tid) return;
      var r = await db.from('sop_template').update({ default_owner: value || null }).eq('id', tid);
      if (r.error) { toast('Could not save the assignee'); return; }
      if (typeof WA_VOL_TMPL !== 'undefined') {
        WA_VOL_TMPL.forEach(function (t) {
          if (String(t.id) === String(tid)) t.default_owner = value || null;
        });
      }
      toast('Saved to the master and all upcoming planned events');
    };
  }

  /* swap the owner inputs on the master SOP for people dropdowns */
  if (typeof waVolRenderSop === 'function') {
    var __rs32 = waVolRenderSop;
    waVolRenderSop = function () {
      __rs32();
      try {
        var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering');
        if (!sec) return;
        sec.querySelectorAll('input[data-vldue^="tmpl-"]').forEach(function (el) {
          el.style.display = 'none';
        });
        if (typeof wa28SwapInput !== 'function' || typeof WA28_PEOPLE === 'undefined' || !WA28_PEOPLE.length) return;
        sec.querySelectorAll('input[data-vlowner^="tmpl-"]').forEach(function (el) {
          wa28SwapInput(el, ['data-vlowner']);
        });
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v33 event plans, driver itineraries, person pages, Quo replies
   (29/07/26)
   1. Every event summary opens with the plan: how many meals we are
      planning to serve, where, and what (editable in place; revisions
      show up for everyone).
   2. Build the team leader and driver itinerary from live data: leader
      and driver phones, meeting time at the storage unit, pickup start,
      home cook stops with addresses and phones, the Google Maps route,
      and the instructions link. Copy it or open it in an email.
   3. Click anyone on the org chart (or their profile card) to open a
      full person page: an AI job description grounded in their actual
      Hub work, fiscal year stats, open tasks, and what they finished.
   4. Check Quo replies on any event: the latest inbound text from each
      rostered volunteer, pulled straight from the Welfare Phone.
   ===================================================================== */

var WA33_STORAGE = '888 Rue Saint-Antoine W, Montreal, QC H3C 4G3';

(function () {
  if (document.getElementById('wa33css')) return;
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa33css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w33plan{border-bottom:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.25);padding:2px 0 8px;margin:0 0 6px;font-size:13px;}' +
    '.w33plan input{margin:0 4px;}' +
    '.w33qr-r{border-top:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.15);padding:5px 0;font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '.w33qr-r b{color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w33head{display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:4px;}' +
    '.w33dot{display:inline-block;width:14px;height:14px;border-radius:50%;}' +
    '.w33chip{display:inline-block;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.45);color:#103F39;border-radius:9px;padding:2px 10px;font-size:12px;margin:2px 6px 2px 0;}' +
    '.w33bio{background:rgba(16,63,57,.06);border-radius:10px;padding:10px 13px;font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.5;}' +
    '.w33task{border-bottom:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.15);padding:5px 0;font-size:13px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);
})();

/* ---------- 1. the plan, on the event summary ---------- */
function wa33Ev() {
  var ev = null;
  if (typeof WA_VOL_EVENTS !== 'undefined') {
    WA_VOL_EVENTS.forEach(function (e) { if (e.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) ev = e; });
  }
  return ev;
}

function wa33Augment() {
  var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering');
  if (!sec) return;
  var sum = sec.querySelector('.w31sum');
  if (!sum || sum.querySelector('.w33plan')) return;
  var ev = wa33Ev();
  if (!ev) return;
  var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();
  var plan = document.createElement('div');
  plan.className = 'w33plan';
  if (staff) {
    plan.innerHTML = '<b>The plan:</b> serving'
      + ' <input type="number" min="0" class="vl-in" style="width:70px" value="' + (ev.planned_meals == null ? '' : esc(String(ev.planned_meals))) + '" placeholder="how many" data-w33pm="' + ev.id + '" /> meals at'
      + ' <input type="text" class="vl-in" style="width:210px" value="' + esc(ev.location || '') + '" placeholder="location" data-w33loc="' + ev.id + '" />'
      + ' <input type="text" class="vl-in" style="width:250px" value="' + esc(ev.plan_note || '') + '" placeholder="what we plan to serve (menu, notes)" data-w33pn="' + ev.id + '" />';
  } else {
    plan.innerHTML = '<b>The plan:</b> ' + (ev.planned_meals ? 'serving ' + esc(String(ev.planned_meals)) + ' meals' : 'meal count TBD')
      + (ev.location ? ' at ' + esc(ev.location) : '')
      + (ev.plan_note ? ' &middot; ' + esc(ev.plan_note) : '');
  }
  var h4 = sum.querySelector('h4');
  if (h4) h4.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', plan);
  else sum.insertBefore(plan, sum.firstChild);
  if (staff && !sum.querySelector('[data-w33it]')) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'row';
    row.style.marginTop = '7px';
    row.innerHTML = '<button class="btn ghost" style="font-size:12px" data-w33it="' + ev.id + '">Build the driver and team leader itinerary</button> '
      + '<button class="btn ghost" style="font-size:12px" data-w33qr="' + ev.id + '">Check Quo replies</button>'
      + '<div id="w33qr"></div>';
    sum.appendChild(row);
  }
}

if (typeof wa31Summary === 'function') {
  (function () {
    var __sum33 = wa31Summary;
    wa31Summary = async function () {
      await __sum33();
      try { wa33Augment(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  })();
}

/* ---------- 2. driver and team leader itinerary ---------- */
function wa33People() {
  var by = {};
  (typeof WA_VOL_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_PEOPLE : []).forEach(function (p) { by[String(p.id)] = p; });
  return by;
}

function wa33Compose(ev) {
  var by = wa33People();
  var roster = (WA_VOL_DETAIL && WA_VOL_DETAIL.roster) || [];
  var pick = function (id) { return (document.getElementById(id) || {}).value || ''; };
  var d = new Date(String(ev.event_date) + 'T12:00:00');
  var dateStr = isNaN(d) ? String(ev.event_date) : d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' });
  var phone = function (r) {
    var p = r.person_id ? by[String(r.person_id)] : null;
    return p && p.phones && p.phones.length ? ' ' + p.phones[0] : '';
  };
  var leads = roster.filter(function (r) { return String(r.role || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('team') === 0; });
  var drivers = roster.filter(function (r) { return String(r.role || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('driver') === 0; });
  var homeCooks = roster.filter(function (r) {
    return String(r.role || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('cook') === 0 && r.confirm && r.confirm.cook_location === 'home';
  });
  var lines = [];
  lines.push('TEAM LEADER & DRIVER ITINERARY');
  lines.push(dateStr);
  lines.push('');
  leads.forEach(function (r) { lines.push('Team leader: ' + r.person_name + phone(r)); });
  if (!leads.length) lines.push('Team leader: TBD');
  drivers.forEach(function (r) {
    var c = r.confirm || {};
    lines.push('Driver: ' + r.person_name + phone(r) + (c.vehicle ? ' - ' + c.vehicle + (c.model ? ' (' + c.model + ')' : '') : ''));
  });
  if (!drivers.length) lines.push('Driver: TBD');
  lines.push('');
  lines.push('Pickup driver start time: ' + pick('w33t1'));
  lines.push('Meeting time with the team leader: ' + pick('w33t2') + ' at the storage unit: ' + pick('w33mp'));
  lines.push('');
  if (homeCooks.length) {
    lines.push('Cook pickups (cooking from home):');
    var addrs = [];
    homeCooks.forEach(function (r) {
      var p = r.person_id ? by[String(r.person_id)] : null;
      var addr = (p && p.address) || 'address TBD';
      if (p && p.address) addrs.push(p.address);
      lines.push('  - ' + r.person_name + phone(r) + ': ' + addr + (r.confirm && r.confirm.containers === 'needs containers' ? ' (bring containers)' : ''));
    });
    if (addrs.length) {
      var stops = [pick('w33mp')].concat(addrs);
      if (ev.location) stops.push(ev.location);
      lines.push('');
      lines.push('Google Maps route: https://www.google.com/maps/dir/' + stops.map(function (a) { return encodeURIComponent(a); }).join('/'));
    }
  } else {
    lines.push('No home cook pickups confirmed yet. Check back after cooks confirm.');
  }
  lines.push('');
  lines.push('The event starts at ' + (ev.location || 'the serving location') + ' at ' + pick('w33t3') + '.');
  if (ev.planned_meals) lines.push('We are planning to serve ' + ev.planned_meals + ' meals.');
  var il = pick('w33il');
  if (il) { lines.push(''); lines.push('Full event instructions for the team leader and driver: ' + il); }
  lines.push('');
  lines.push('THANK YOU FOR BEING A CRUCIAL PART OF THIS EVENT!');
  lines.push('Questions? Text the Welfare Phone: 438 808 7754.');
  return lines.join('\n');
}

function wa33Itin(evId) {
  var ev = wa33Ev();
  if (!ev || String(ev.id) !== String(evId)) return;
  openModal('<h3>Team leader and driver itinerary</h3>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">Built from the live roster and confirmations. Adjust the times, rebuild, then copy it into a text or email.</p>' +
    '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Pickup driver starts</label><input type="text" id="w33t1" value="10:00 AM" /></div>' +
    '<div><label>Meet the team leader</label><input type="text" id="w33t2" value="11:00 AM" /></div>' +
    '<div><label>Event starts</label><input type="text" id="w33t3" value="12:00 PM" /></div></div>' +
    '<label>Meeting place</label><input type="text" id="w33mp" value="' + esc(WA33_STORAGE) + '" />' +
    '<label>Instructions link (Canva)</label><input type="text" id="w33il" value="' + esc(ev.itinerary_url || '') + '" placeholder="https://..." />' +
    '<label>The itinerary</label><textarea id="w33txt" rows="13" style="font-size:12px"></textarea>' +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w33cp">Copy it</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w33rb">Rebuild</button> <a class="btn ghost" id="w33em" style="text-decoration:none">Open in email</a> <button class="btn ghost" id="w33cl">Close</button></div>');
  var fill = function () {
    var txt = wa33Compose(ev);
    var ta = document.getElementById('w33txt');
    if (ta) ta.value = txt;
    var em = document.getElementById('w33em');
    if (em) {
      var dd = new Date(String(ev.event_date) + 'T12:00:00');
      var ds = isNaN(dd) ? String(ev.event_date) : dd.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { weekday: 'long', month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' });
      em.href = 'mailto:?subject=' + encodeURIComponent('Welfare Avenue: Team Leader & Driver Itinerary - ' + ds) + '&body=' + encodeURIComponent(txt);
    }
  };
  fill();
  var rb = document.getElementById('w33rb');
  if (rb) rb.addEventListener('click', fill);
  var cp = document.getElementById('w33cp');
  if (cp) cp.addEventListener('click', function () {
    var ta = document.getElementById('w33txt');
    if (!ta) return;
    ta.select();
    try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(ta.value); toast('Copied'); } catch (e) { document.execCommand('copy'); toast('Copied'); }
  });
  var cl = document.getElementById('w33cl');
  if (cl) cl.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
}

/* ---------- 3. person pages ---------- */
function wa33Section() {
  var main = document.querySelector('main');
  if (main && !document.getElementById('personpage')) {
    var sec = document.createElement('section');
    sec.id = 'personpage';
    sec.className = 'panel';
    main.appendChild(sec);
  }
}

function wa33FyStart() {
  var now = new Date();
  var y = now.getMonth() + 1 >= 10 ? now.getFullYear() : now.getFullYear() - 1;
  return y + '-10-01';
}

async function wa33Person(mid) {
  wa33Section();
  var sec = document.getElementById('personpage');
  if (!sec) return;
  sec.innerHTML = '<div class="card"><p class="read">Opening the profile...</p></div>';
  var r = await db.from('team_members').select('*').eq('id', Number(mid)).maybeSingle();
  var m = r && r.data;
  if (!m) { sec.innerHTML = '<div class="card"><p class="read">Person not found.</p></div>'; return; }
  var label = function (d) { return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; };
  var open = (typeof WA28_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? WA28_TASKS : []).filter(function (t) {
    return !t.done && String(t.owner || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(String(m.name).toLowerCase()) >= 0;
  });
  var fy = wa33FyStart();
  var doneQ = await db.from('dept_tasks').select('id, title, department, closed_at').eq('done', true)
    .gte('closed_at', fy).ilike('owner', '%' + m.name + '%').order('closed_at', { ascending: false }).limit(100);
  var doneRows = (doneQ && doneQ.data) || [];
  var fyLabel = 'FY' + (Number(fy.slice(0, 4)) + 1);
  var html = '<div class="card">'
    + '<p class="read" style="margin:0 0 8px"><a href="#orgchart" style="color:#103F39">&larr; Back to the org chart</a></p>'
    + '<div class="w33head"><span class="w33dot" style="background:' + esc(m.color || '#797773') + '"></span>'
    + '<h2 style="margin:0">' + esc(m.name) + '</h2>'
    + '<span class="read" style="margin:0">' + esc(m.role || 'Team member') + ' &middot; ' + esc(label(m.department)) + (m.email ? ' &middot; ' + esc(m.email) : '') + '</span></div>'
    + '<div id="w33stats" style="margin:6px 0 10px"><span class="w33chip">' + open.length + ' open now</span><span class="w33chip">' + doneRows.length + ' finished in ' + esc(fyLabel) + '</span></div>'
    + '<h3 class="block" style="color:#103F39">What they do here</h3>'
    + '<div class="w33bio" id="w33bio">' + (m.ai_bio ? esc(m.ai_bio) : 'Writing the job description from their actual work...') + '</div>'
    + '<p class="m-sub" style="margin:4px 0 0"><a data-w33bref="' + m.id + '" style="color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline">Rewrite it from the latest work</a></p>'
    + '<h3 class="block" style="color:#103F39;margin-top:14px">On their plate (' + open.length + ')</h3>'
    + (open.slice(0, 15).map(function (t) {
      return '<div class="w33task">' + esc(t.title) + ' <span class="dep" style="font-size:10.5px;border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.35);border-radius:8px;padding:0 6px;color:#797773">' + esc(label(t.department)) + '</span></div>';
    }).join('') || '<p class="m-sub">Nothing assigned right now.</p>')
    + (open.length > 15 ? '<p class="m-sub">plus ' + (open.length - 15) + ' more.</p>' : '')
    + '<h3 class="block" style="color:#103F39;margin-top:14px">Finished this fiscal year (' + doneRows.length + ')</h3>'
    + (doneRows.slice(0, 15).map(function (t) {
      return '<div class="w33task">' + esc(t.title) + (t.closed_at ? ' <span style="font-size:11px;color:#797773">' + esc(String(t.closed_at).slice(0, 10)) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>';
    }).join('') || '<p class="m-sub">Nothing closed out yet this fiscal year.</p>')
    + (doneRows.length > 15 ? '<p class="m-sub">plus ' + (doneRows.length - 15) + ' more.</p>' : '')
    + '</div>';
  sec.innerHTML = html;
  try {
    var pr = await db.functions.invoke('person-profile', { body: { member_id: m.id } });
    if (!pr.error && pr.data) {
      var bio = document.getElementById('w33bio');
      if (bio && pr.data.bio) bio.textContent = pr.data.bio;
      var stx = document.getElementById('w33stats');
      if (stx && pr.data.stats && pr.data.stats.sop_steps_upcoming) {
        stx.innerHTML += '<span class="w33chip">' + pr.data.stats.sop_steps_upcoming + ' checklist steps across ' + pr.data.stats.sop_events + ' upcoming events</span>';
      }
    } else if (!m.ai_bio) {
      var b2 = document.getElementById('w33bio');
      if (b2) b2.textContent = (m.role || 'Team member') + ', ' + label(m.department) + '.';
    }
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
}

/* ---------- 4. wiring ---------- */
(function () {
  if (typeof applyHash === 'function') {
    var __ah33 = applyHash;
    applyHash = function () {
      var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
      if (h.indexOf('person/') === 0 && typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff()) {
        wa33Section();
        try { wa33Person(h.slice(7)); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
        showPanel('personpage');
        return;
      }
      __ah33();
    };
  }

  function memberIdByName(name) {
    var list = typeof WA_ROSTER !== 'undefined' && WA_ROSTER.length ? WA_ROSTER
      : (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []);
    var hit = null;
    list.forEach(function (m) { if (String(m.name).toLowerCase() === String(name).toLowerCase()) hit = m; });
    return hit && hit.id ? hit.id : null;
  }

  function wire() {
    if (document.body.dataset.wa33Wired) return;
    document.body.dataset.wa33Wired = '1';
    /* org chart people open the full page */
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var oc = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-ocp]');
      if (oc) {
        var id = memberIdByName(oc.getAttribute('data-ocp'));
        if (id) {
          e.preventDefault();
          e.stopPropagation();
          location.hash = '#person/' + id;
        }
        return;
      }
      var pp = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w33pp]');
      if (pp) {
        var id2 = memberIdByName(pp.getAttribute('data-w33pp'));
        if (id2) { try { closeModal(); } catch (x) { } location.hash = '#person/' + id2; }
        return;
      }
      var it = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w33it]');
      if (it) { try { wa33Itin(it.getAttribute('data-w33it')); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } return; }
      var qr = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w33qr]');
      if (qr) {
        var box = document.getElementById('w33qr');
        if (box) box.innerHTML = '<p class="m-sub">Reading the Welfare Phone... about ten seconds.</p>';
        qr.disabled = true;
        db.functions.invoke('quo-pull', { body: { event_id: Number(qr.getAttribute('data-w33qr')) } }).then(function (r) {
          qr.disabled = false;
          if (!box) return;
          if (r.error || !r.data) { box.innerHTML = '<p class="m-sub">Could not reach Quo. Try again.</p>'; return; }
          var reps = r.data.replies || [];
          if (!reps.length) { box.innerHTML = '<p class="m-sub">No replies in the last month from the ' + (r.data.checked || 0) + ' rostered people checked.</p>'; return; }
          box.innerHTML = reps.map(function (x) {
            var days = Math.round((Date.now() - new Date(x.at).getTime()) / 86400000);
            return '<div class="w33qr-r"><b>' + esc(x.name || x.phone) + ':</b> ' + esc(x.text)
              + ' <span style="opacity:.55">' + (days <= 0 ? 'today' : days + (days === 1 ? ' day ago' : ' days ago')) + (x.more ? ', ' + x.more + ' more' : '') + '</span></div>';
          }).join('') + '<p class="m-sub">Checked ' + (r.data.checked || 0) + ' people with phones on the roster.</p>';
        });
        return;
      }
      var br = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w33bref]');
      if (br) {
        var bio = document.getElementById('w33bio');
        if (bio) bio.textContent = 'Rereading their tasks and events... about fifteen seconds.';
        db.functions.invoke('person-profile', { body: { member_id: Number(br.getAttribute('data-w33bref')), refresh: true } }).then(function (r) {
          if (bio) bio.textContent = (r.data && r.data.bio) || 'Could not write it just now. Try again in a minute.';
        });
        return;
      }
    }, true);

    /* the plan saves as you type it */
    document.body.addEventListener('change', async function (e) {
      if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
      var pm = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w33pm]');
      var lo = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w33loc]');
      var pn = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w33pn]');
      var el = pm || lo || pn;
      if (!el) return;
      var evId = Number(el.getAttribute(pm ? 'data-w33pm' : (lo ? 'data-w33loc' : 'data-w33pn')));
      var patch = {};
      if (pm) patch.planned_meals = el.value === '' ? null : Number(el.value);
      if (lo) patch.location = el.value.trim() || null;
      if (pn) patch.plan_note = el.value.trim() || null;
      var r = await db.from('volunteer_events').update(patch).eq('id', evId);
      if (r.error) { toast('Could not save the plan'); return; }
      (typeof WA_VOL_EVENTS !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_EVENTS : []).forEach(function (x) {
        if (String(x.id) === String(evId)) { Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { x[k] = patch[k]; }); }
      });
      toast('Plan updated');
    });
  }
  wire();

  /* profile modal gains a full page button */
  if (typeof wa28Profile === 'function') {
    var __pf33 = wa28Profile;
    wa28Profile = function (name) {
      __pf33(name);
      try {
        var c = document.getElementById('w28pc');
        if (c && !document.querySelector('[data-w33pp]')) {
          c.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', '<button class="btn" data-w33pp="' + esc(name) + '">Open their full page</button> ');
        }
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  var __sa33 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa33();
    try { wire(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v34 goals explainer, cleaner header, dashboard feel
   (29/07/26)
   1. A "How this fits together" button at the top of the Goals page:
      a flow diagram from meeting transcripts, to tasks, to projects,
      to department quarterly goals, to the organizational goals tier.
      Anyone new to the Hub can onboard from that one screen.
   2. The header sheds its clutter: the feed and legend lines are gone
      (the transcript page already explains the automation where you
      actually use it) and the subtitle is one calm line.
   3. A visual pass across the whole Hub: compact header, sticky pill
      navigation that follows you down the page, softer cards, cleaner
      tables and buttons. Same bones, dashboard feel.
   ===================================================================== */

(function () {
  if (document.getElementById('wa34css')) return;
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa34css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    /* header: compact and calm */
    'header.wa-header{padding:15px 24px 13px;}' +
    '.wa-meta,.wa-legend{display:none !important;}' +
    '.wa-brandrow h1{font-size:18px;letter-spacing:.1px;}' +
    '.wa-brandrow .sub{font-size:12.5px;opacity:.85;}' +
    '.wa-toolbar{padding:6px 24px;}' +
    /* sticky pill navigation */
    'nav.wa-nav{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:60;background:rgba(255,250,242,.94);' +
    '-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(9px) saturate(1.15);backdrop-filter:blur(9px) saturate(1.15);' +
    'border-bottom:1px solid var(--wa-line);}' +
    'nav.wa-nav .wa-wrap{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:2px;padding:6px 0;}' +
    '.tab{padding:7px 13px;border-radius:999px;border-bottom:none;margin:2px 1px;font-weight:600;font-size:13px;transition:background .15s,color .15s;}' +
    '.tab:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.07);color:var(--wa-ink);}' +
    '.tab.active{background:rgba(16,63,57,.11);color:var(--wa-green);border-bottom:none;}' +
    '@media (max-width:760px){nav.wa-nav .wa-wrap{flex-wrap:nowrap;overflow-x:auto;scrollbar-width:none;}nav.wa-nav .wa-wrap::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none;}.tab{flex:0 0 auto;}}' +
    /* cards and content */
    '.card{border-radius:16px;border-color:#e9e4d7;box-shadow:0 1px 2px rgba(16,63,57,.03),0 14px 34px -24px rgba(16,63,57,.18);}' +
    '.card > h2{font-size:17px;}' +
    'main{padding-top:22px;}' +
    '.wa-tbl thead th{font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;color:var(--wa-muted);font-weight:700;}' +
    '.wa-tbl tbody tr:hover td{background:rgba(16,63,57,.035);}' +
    '.btn{border-radius:10px;}' +
    'input,select,textarea{border-radius:9px;}' +
    '.fchip{border-radius:999px;}' +
    /* the goals flow diagram */
    '.w34flow{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px;margin:10px 0;}' +
    '.w34box{border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.3);border-left:4px solid #103F39;border-radius:11px;padding:9px 13px;background:#FFFAF2;}' +
    '.w34box b{display:block;font-size:13.5px;color:#103F39;margin-bottom:1px;}' +
    '.w34box span{font-size:12.5px;color:#797773;line-height:1.45;display:block;}' +
    '.w34arr{text-align:center;color:#103F39;font-size:15px;line-height:1.3;}' +
    '.w34org{border-left-color:#DCA527;background:rgba(220,165,39,.06);}' +
    '.w34org b{color:#8a6414;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* declutter the header now */
  try {
    document.querySelectorAll('.wa-meta, .wa-legend').forEach(function (el) { el.remove(); });
    var sub = document.querySelector('.wa-brandrow .sub');
    if (sub) sub.textContent = 'The Welfare Avenue operating hub';
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
})();

    '<div class="w34box"><b>2. Tasks</b><span>Every action item from the meeting lands on the right department board, with an owner, a status, and a due date.</span></div>' +

    '<div class="w34box"><b>3. Projects</b><span>Related tasks group into named projects, like Flag Football 2026 or the donor program. New tasks file themselves into the right project; anything that does not fit sits in General tasks.</span></div>' +

    '<div class="w34box"><b>4. Department quarterly goals</b><span>Each department sets SMART goals for the quarter and attaches its projects to them, so goal progress reflects the real work underneath.</span></div>' +
    '<div class="w34arr">&darr;</div>' +
    '<div class="w34box w34org"><b>5. Organizational goals</b><span>The overarching tier at the top of this page. Department goals and their projects roll up here, so anyone can see how the daily work ladders to the mission.</span></div>' +
    '</div>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">The Hub helps at every level: it files tasks into projects, suggests which goal a new project serves, proposes department goals from your meetings, and runs gap analysis on any goal (open one and ask for the missing tasks).</p>' +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w34hc">Got it</button></div>');
  var c = document.getElementById('w34hc');

}

/* ---------- the home stat strip ---------- */
async function wa35Stats() {
  var panel = document.getElementById('overview');
  if (!panel || document.getElementById('w35stats')) return;
  function fy() {
    var now = new Date();
    var y = now.getMonth() + 1 >= 10 ? now.getFullYear() : now.getFullYear() - 1;
    return y + '-10-01';
  }
  var today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  try {
    var qs = await Promise.all([
      db.from('dept_tasks').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('done', false),
      db.from('dept_tasks').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('done', true).gte('closed_at', fy()),
      db.from('volunteer_events').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('status', 'planned').gte('event_date', today),
      db.from('volunteer_people').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
    ]);
    var n = qs.map(function (r) { return (r && r.count) || 0; });
    var fyLabel = 'FY' + (Number(fy().slice(0, 4)) + 1);
    var div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'w35stats';
    div.id = 'w35stats';
    div.innerHTML = ''
      + '<div class="w35stat acc"><b>' + n[0] + '</b><span>open tasks across the organization</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w35stat"><b>' + n[1] + '</b><span>finished so far in ' + fyLabel + '</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w35stat"><b>' + n[2] + '</b><span>upcoming serving events planned</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w35stat"><b>' + n[3] + '</b><span>volunteers on file</span></div>';
    panel.insertBefore(div, panel.firstChild);
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
}

(function () {
  var __sa35 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa35();
    try { wa35Stats(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v36 Training and onboarding
   (29/07/26)
   1. A Training tab: the full catalog pulled from your Loom library and
      the ClickUp training paths, grouped by area (Governance, Keela,
      Marketing Strategy Package, Operations, Excel, OpenPhone, General).
      Each item opens the video or resource. Staff can assign a training
      to a person and mark it done; each person's progress shows on
      their profile page, so you can see who is trained on what.
   2. An onboarding block at the top of the tab: the onboarding SOP and
      the recommended path for a new member, plus who is mid-onboarding.
   3. Inventory gains a Create purchase order button that files a PO
      request straight into the requests portal.
   ===================================================================== */

var WA36_TRAININGS = [];
var WA36_PROG = [];
var WA36_LOADED = false;

  st.id = 'wa36css';
  st.textContent = '' +

/* ---------- panel + tab ---------- */

  var main = document.querySelector('main');

    sec.id = 'training';

      + '<p class="read">Your Loom trainings and the Learning Avenues in one catalog. Assign a training to someone and mark it done; each person\'s progress shows on their profile.</p>'
      + '<div id="w36body">Loading the catalog...</div></div>';
    main.appendChild(sec);
  }
  var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
  if (bar && !bar.querySelector('[data-tab="training"]')) {
    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'tab';
    btn.setAttribute('data-tab', 'training');
    btn.innerHTML = '<span class="tabdot" style="background:#103F39"></span>Training';

    if (before) bar.insertBefore(btn, before); else bar.appendChild(btn);

      try { location.hash = 'training'; } catch (e) { }
    });
