async function wa36Load(force) {
  if (WA36_LOADED && !force) return;
  try {
    var t = await db.from('trainings').select('*').eq('active', true).order('category').order('sort').order('id');
    WA36_TRAININGS = ((t && t.data) || []).filter(function (x) { return !(x && x.body && x.body.planned); }); /* wave141: planned trainings stay in the Academy, for leaders */
    var p = await db.from('training_progress').select('*');
    WA36_PROG = (p && p.data) || [];
    WA36_LOADED = true;
  } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
}

function wa36Dur(s) {
  if (!s) return '';
  var m = Math.round(s / 60);
  return m >= 60 ? (Math.floor(m / 60) + 'h ' + (m % 60) + 'm') : (m + ' min');
}
function wa36ProgFor(tid) {
  return WA36_PROG.filter(function (p) { return String(p.training_id) === String(tid); });
}

async function wa36Render() {
  var body = document.getElementById('w36body');
  if (!body) return;
  if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Training is available to staff accounts.</p>'; return; }
  await wa36Load(false);
  var people = (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []);
  var me = (typeof wa28Me === 'function') ? wa28Me() : null;

  var onb = '<div class="w36onb"><h3>New here? Start with onboarding</h3>'
    + '<p class="read" style="margin:0 0 6px">Follow the '
    + '<a href="https://docs.google.com/document/d/1OWeVlCHCSLPMCDD7DbfpO_OidcuBvPRvRNE5VueqWpg/edit" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:#103F39">Onboarding SOP</a>'
    + ', then work through the training path for your team below. Mark each item done as you go.</p>'
    + '<div class="w36row">' + (me ? '<span class="w36pill">You are signed in as ' + esc(me.name) + '</span>' : '') + '</div></div>';

  var cats = {};
  WA36_TRAININGS.forEach(function (t) { (cats[t.category] = cats[t.category] || []).push(t); });
  var order = ['General', 'Operations', 'Marketing', 'Keela (donor CRM)', 'Charity governance & accounting', 'Excel', 'OpenPhone'];
  var keys = Object.keys(cats).sort(function (a, b) {
    var ia = order.indexOf(a); var ib = order.indexOf(b);
    return (ia < 0 ? 99 : ia) - (ib < 0 ? 99 : ib);
  });

  var html = onb;
  keys.forEach(function (cat) {
    html += '<div class="w36cat">' + esc(cat) + '</div><div class="w36grid">';
    cats[cat].forEach(function (t) {
      var pr = wa36ProgFor(t.id);
      var doneN = pr.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'done'; }).length;
      var mine = me ? pr.filter(function (x) { return x.person_name.toLowerCase() === me.name.toLowerCase(); })[0] : null;
      html += '<div class="w36card"><h4>' + esc(t.title) + '</h4>'
        + '<div class="w36meta">' + (t.path_group ? esc(t.path_group) + ' &middot; ' : '') + (t.duration_sec ? wa36Dur(t.duration_sec) + ' &middot; ' : '') + esc(t.source === 'clickup' ? 'ClickUp' : 'Loom') + '</div>'
        + (t.description ? '<div class="w36meta">' + esc(t.description) + '</div>' : '')
        + '<div class="w36row">'
        + (t.resource_url ? '<a class="w36watch" href="' + esc(t.resource_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open the training &raquo;</a>' : '')
        + (doneN ? '<span class="w36pill done">' + doneN + ' done</span>' : '')
        + '</div>'
        + '<div class="w36row">'
        + (mine ? '<span class="w36pill ' + (mine.status === 'done' ? 'done' : 'prog') + '">You: ' + esc(mine.status) + '</span>' : '')
        + '<button class="w36sel" data-w36me="' + t.id + '" data-st="' + (mine && mine.status === 'done' ? 'done' : 'todo') + '">' + (mine && mine.status === 'done' ? 'Mark not done' : 'Mark it done for me') + '</button>'
        + '<select class="w36sel" data-w36assign="' + t.id + '"><option value="">Assign to...</option>'
        + people.map(function (pp) { return '<option value="' + esc(pp.name) + '">' + esc(pp.name) + '</option>'; }).join('')
        + '</select>'
        + '</div></div>';
    });
    html += '</div>';
  });
  body.innerHTML = html;
}

/* progress writes */
async function wa36SetMine(tid, done) {
  var me = (typeof wa28Me === 'function') ? wa28Me() : null;
  if (!me) { toast('Sign in to track your training'); return; }
  /* wave150: a lesson with a hard knowledge check is completed only by passing it in the Avenue Academy */
  if (done) {
    var tr = WA36_TRAININGS.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(tid); })[0], bd = tr && tr.body;
    if (typeof bd === 'string') { try { bd = JSON.parse(bd); } catch (e) { bd = null; } }
    if (bd && bd.quiz && bd.quiz.hard) { toast('Pass its knowledge check in the Avenue Academy to complete this one'); return; }
  }
  await wa36Assign(tid, me.name, done ? 'done' : 'assigned');
}
async function wa36Assign(tid, name, status) {
  var patch = { training_id: Number(tid), person_name: name, status: status, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
  if (status === 'done') patch.done_at = new Date().toISOString();
  patch.assigned_by = (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'dashboard';
  var r = await db.from('training_progress').upsert(patch, { onConflict: 'training_id,person_name' });
  if (r.error) { toast('Could not save the training status'); return; }
  await wa36Load(true);
  wa36Render();
  toast(status === 'done' ? 'Marked done' : 'Assigned to ' + name);
}

/* profile page training block (wraps v33 wa33Person) */
async function wa36ProfileBlock(mid) {
  var sec = document.getElementById('personpage');
  if (!sec) return;
  try {
    var r = await db.from('team_members').select('name').eq('id', Number(mid)).maybeSingle();
    var nm = r && r.data && r.data.name;
    if (!nm) return;
    await wa36Load(false);
    var mine = WA36_PROG.filter(function (p) { return p.person_name.toLowerCase() === String(nm).toLowerCase(); });
    var byId = {};
    WA36_TRAININGS.forEach(function (t) { byId[String(t.id)] = t; });
    var card = sec.querySelector('.card');
    if (!card || card.querySelector('.w36prof')) return;
    var done = mine.filter(function (m) { return m.status === 'done'; });
    var html = '<div class="w36prof"><h3 class="block" style="color:#103F39">Training (' + done.length + ' of ' + mine.length + ' done)</h3>';
    if (!mine.length) html += '<p class="m-sub">No training assigned yet. Assign some from the Training tab.</p>';
    mine.forEach(function (m) {
      var t = byId[String(m.training_id)];
      html += '<div class="w36prog-row"><span class="w36pill ' + (m.status === 'done' ? 'done' : 'prog') + '">' + esc(m.status) + '</span> ' + esc(t ? t.title : 'Training') + (t && t.resource_url ? ' <a href="' + esc(t.resource_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:#103F39;font-size:12px">open</a>' : '') + '</div>';
    });
    html += '</div>';
    card.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
}

if (typeof wa33Person === 'function') {
  (function () {
    var __pp36 = wa33Person;
    wa33Person = async function (mid) {
      await __pp36(mid);
      try { await wa36ProfileBlock(mid); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  })();
}

/* ---------- inventory: create purchase order ---------- */
function wa36PoModal() {
  openModal('<h3>Create a purchase order</h3>' +
    '<p class="m-sub">This files a purchase order request into the requests portal for fundraising to action.</p>' +
    '<label>What are you ordering</label><input type="text" id="w36item" placeholder="Item or supplies" />' +
    '<div class="m-row"><div><label>Estimated amount</label><input type="text" id="w36amt" placeholder="e.g. 120.00$" /></div>' +
    '<div><label>Vendor</label><input type="text" id="w36vendor" placeholder="Where from" /></div></div>' +
    '<label>Reason</label><input type="text" id="w36reason" placeholder="What it is for" />' +
    '<label>Needed by</label><input type="date" id="w36need" />' +
    '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w36posave">File the PO request</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w36pocancel">Cancel</button></div>');
  var c = document.getElementById('w36pocancel');
  if (c) c.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  var s = document.getElementById('w36posave');
  if (s) s.addEventListener('click', async function () {
    var item = (document.getElementById('w36item').value || '').trim();
    if (!item) { toast('Say what you are ordering'); return; }
    s.disabled = true;
    var details = [
      'Amount: ' + ((document.getElementById('w36amt').value || '').trim() || 'TBD'),
      'Vendor: ' + ((document.getElementById('w36vendor').value || '').trim() || 'TBD'),
      'Reason: ' + ((document.getElementById('w36reason').value || '').trim() || ''),
      'Needed by: ' + ((document.getElementById('w36need').value || '').trim() || 'TBD')
    ].join('\n');
    var row = {
      type_key: 'purchase-order', department: 'fundraising',
      title: 'PO: ' + item, details: details, status: 'requested', done: false,
      requested_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'dashboard'
    };
    var r = await db.from('dept_requests').insert(row);
    if (r.error) { s.disabled = false; toast('Could not file the PO'); return; }
    closeModal();
    toast('Purchase order filed');
  });
}

var WA37 = { tmpl: [], onbs: [], steps: [], loaded: false };
var WA37_WEEK_ORDER = { 'WEEK 1': 1, 'WEEK 2': 2, 'WEEK 3': 3, 'WEEK 4': 4, '60 DAYS': 5, '90 DAYS': 6 };   /* wave279: 90 DAYS is part of every plan */

async function wa37Load(force) {
  if (WA37.loaded && !force) return;
  try {
    var t = await db.from('onboarding_template').select('*').eq('active', true).order('sort').order('id');
    WA37.tmpl = (t && t.data) || [];
    var o = await db.from('onboardings').select('*').order('started_at', { ascending: false });
    WA37.onbs = (o && o.data) || [];
    var act = WA37.onbs.map(function (x) { return x.id; });
    if (act.length) {
      var s = await db.from('onboarding_steps').select('*').in('onboarding_id', act);
      WA37.steps = (s && s.data) || [];
    } else WA37.steps = [];
    WA37.loaded = true;
  } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
}

function wa37Md(s) {
  if (!s) return '';
  if (typeof waVolMd === 'function') { try { return waVolMd(s); } catch (e) { } }
  return '<p>' + esc(s) + '</p>';
}
function wa37Weeks() {
  var by = {};
  WA37.tmpl.forEach(function (t) { (by[t.section] = by[t.section] || []).push(t); });
  return Object.keys(by).sort(function (a, b) { return (WA37_WEEK_ORDER[a] || 9) - (WA37_WEEK_ORDER[b] || 9); })
    .map(function (k) { return { name: k, steps: by[k] }; });
}
function wa37StepsFor(onbId) {
  var m = {};
  WA37.steps.forEach(function (s) { if (String(s.onboarding_id) === String(onbId)) m[s.template_id] = s; });
  return m;
}
function wa37Pct(onbId) {
  var m = wa37StepsFor(onbId);
  var ids = Object.keys(m);
  if (!ids.length) return { done: 0, total: 0, pct: 0 };
  var d = ids.filter(function (k) { return m[k].done; }).length;
  return { done: d, total: ids.length, pct: Math.round(100 * d / ids.length) };
}

function wa37Checklist(onb, canTick) { /* superseded: wave44 reset without capture; superseded; body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }

/* ---------- the block on the Training tab ---------- */
async function wa37Mount() {
  var host = document.querySelector('#training .w36onb');
  if (!host) return;
  await wa37Load(false);
  var me = (typeof wa28Me === 'function') ? wa28Me() : null;
  var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();
  var mine = me ? WA37.onbs.filter(function (o) {
    return o.status !== 'cancelled' && o.person_name.toLowerCase() === me.name.toLowerCase();
  })[0] : null;

  var html = '';
  if (mine) {
    var p = wa37Pct(mine.id);
    html += '<h3>' + (mine.status === 'completed' ? 'Your onboarding: complete!' : 'Your onboarding') + '</h3>'
      + '<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin:4px 0 10px"><span class="w37bar"><i style="width:' + p.pct + '%"></i></span>'
      + '<b style="font-size:13px;color:#103F39">' + p.pct + '%</b><span style="font-size:12px;color:#797773">' + p.done + ' of ' + p.total + ' steps</span></div>'
      + '<p class="read" style="margin:0 0 8px">Each module below opens up. Work top to bottom, tick things off as you go, and lean on your onboarding channel whenever something is unclear. The '
      + '<a href="https://docs.google.com/document/d/1OWeVlCHCSLPMCDD7DbfpO_OidcuBvPRvRNE5VueqWpg/edit" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:#103F39">Onboarding SOP</a> is the companion doc.</p>'
      + wa37Checklist(mine, true);
  } else {
    html += '<h3>New here? Start with onboarding</h3>'
      + '<p class="read" style="margin:0 0 6px">Follow the <a href="https://docs.google.com/document/d/1OWeVlCHCSLPMCDD7DbfpO_OidcuBvPRvRNE5VueqWpg/edit" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:#103F39">Onboarding SOP</a>, then work through the training path for your team below.</p>';
  }

  if (staff) {
    var people = (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []);
    var activeNames = WA37.onbs.filter(function (o) { return o.status === 'active'; }).map(function (o) { return o.person_name.toLowerCase(); });
    html += '<div style="border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.15);margin-top:12px;padding-top:10px">'
      + '<h3 style="font-size:13.5px">Onboarding board (HR)</h3>'
      + '<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:4px 0 8px">'
      + '<select class="w36sel" id="w37assign"><option value="">Assign an onboarding to...</option>'
      + people.filter(function (pp) { return activeNames.indexOf(pp.name.toLowerCase()) < 0; })
        .map(function (pp) { return '<option value="' + esc(String(pp.id)) + '">' + esc(pp.name) + ' (' + esc(pp.department) + ')</option>'; }).join('')
      + '</select>'
      + '<a class="w37edit" data-w37master="1">Edit the master onboarding</a></div>';
    var act = WA37.onbs.filter(function (o) { return o.status === 'active'; });
    var done = WA37.onbs.filter(function (o) { return o.status === 'completed'; });
    if (!act.length) html += '<p class="m-sub">Nobody is mid-onboarding right now.</p>';
    act.forEach(function (o) {
      var p = wa37Pct(o.id);
      html += '<div class="w37hr-row"><b>' + esc(o.person_name) + '</b>'
        + '<span class="w37bar"><i style="width:' + p.pct + '%"></i></span>'
        + '<span style="font-size:12px;color:#103F39;font-weight:700">' + p.pct + '%</span>'
        + '<span class="mini">' + p.done + ' of ' + p.total + ' &middot; started ' + esc(String(o.started_at)) + '</span>'
        + '<a class="w37edit" data-w37open="' + o.id + '">open their checklist</a></div>';
    });
    if (done.length) html += '<p class="m-sub" style="margin-top:6px">' + done.length + ' completed onboarding' + (done.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' on record.</p>';
    html += '</div>';
  }
  host.innerHTML = html;
}

/* staff view of one person's checklist */
function wa37OpenModal(onbId) {
  var o = WA37.onbs.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(onbId); })[0];
  if (!o) return;
  var p = wa37Pct(o.id);
  openModal('<h3>' + esc(o.person_name) + '\'s onboarding (' + p.pct + '%)</h3>'
    + '<p class="m-sub">Started ' + esc(String(o.started_at)) + (o.assigned_by ? ' &middot; assigned by ' + esc(o.assigned_by) : '') + '. You can tick steps on their behalf.</p>'
    + '<div id="w37modal">' + wa37Checklist(o, true) + '</div>'
    + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn ghost" id="w37close">Close</button></div>');
  var c = document.getElementById('w37close');
  if (c) c.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); wa37Mount(); });
}

async function wa37Profile(mid) {
  var sec = document.getElementById('personpage');
  if (!sec) return;
  try {
    await wa37Load(false);
    var o = WA37.onbs.filter(function (x) { return String(x.member_id) === String(mid) && x.status !== 'cancelled'; })[0];
    if (!o) return;
    var card = sec.querySelector('.card');
    if (!card || card.querySelector('.w37profbar')) return;
    var p = wa37Pct(o.id);
    var stats = document.getElementById('w33stats');
    var html = '<div class="w37profbar" style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin:2px 0 10px;max-width:520px">'
      + '<span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;white-space:nowrap">Onboarding' + (o.status === 'completed' ? ': complete' : '') + '</span>'
      + '<span class="w37bar"><i style="width:' + p.pct + '%"></i></span>'
      + '<span style="font-size:12px;color:#797773;white-space:nowrap">' + p.done + ' of ' + p.total + '</span></div>';
    if (stats) stats.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', html);
    else card.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', html);
  } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
}

/* ---------- wiring ---------- */
(function () {
  function wire() {
    if (document.body.dataset.wa37Wired) return;
    document.body.dataset.wa37Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('change', async function (e) {
      var chk = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w37chk]');
      if (chk) {
        var sid = Number(chk.getAttribute('data-w37chk'));
        var patch = { done: chk.checked, done_at: chk.checked ? new Date().toISOString() : null, done_by: chk.checked ? ((typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'dashboard') : null };
        var r = await db.from('onboarding_steps').update(patch).eq('id', sid);
        if (r.error) { toast('Could not save that'); chk.checked = !chk.checked; return; }
        var row = null;
        WA37.steps.forEach(function (s) { if (String(s.id) === String(sid)) { s.done = patch.done; s.done_at = patch.done_at; s.done_by = patch.done_by; row = s; } });
        if (row) {
          var p = (typeof window.wa37PctRaw === 'function' ? window.wa37PctRaw : wa37Pct)(row.onboarding_id);
          var o = WA37.onbs.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(row.onboarding_id); })[0];
          /* wave285: complete when every row is ticked, or when the one progress rule's facts are all known and every real step is done (window.w285.completeKnown) */
          var W285 = window.w285, full = W285 && typeof W285.completeKnown === 'function' ? W285.completeKnown(row.onboarding_id) : (p.total > 0 && p.done === p.total);
          if (o && full && o.status === 'active') {
            /* wave285: the rows the rule counted (a header whose steps are done, a step setup covered or done in the Academy) are written done first, so the table says what people saw */
            try {
              var fill = W285 && typeof W285.derivedRows === 'function' ? W285.derivedRows(row.onboarding_id) : [];
              for (var fi = 0; fi < fill.length; fi++) {
                var fp = { done: true, done_at: new Date().toISOString(), done_by: fill[fi].by };
                var fr = await db.from('onboarding_steps').update(fp).eq('id', fill[fi].id);
                if (!fr.error) WA37.steps.forEach(function (s) { if (String(s.id) === String(fill[fi].id)) { s.done = true; s.done_at = fp.done_at; s.done_by = fp.done_by; } });
              }
            } catch (e3) { /* the plan is still complete by the rule */ }
            await db.from('onboardings').update({ status: 'completed', completed_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', o.id);
            o.status = 'completed';
            toast('Onboarding complete. Congratulations!');
          } else if (o && o.status === 'completed' && !full && p.done < p.total) {
            await db.from('onboardings').update({ status: 'active', completed_at: null }).eq('id', o.id);
            o.status = 'active';
            toast('Saved');
          } else toast('Saved');
        }
        if (!document.getElementById('w37modal')) wa37Mount();
        return;
      }
      var as = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w37assign');
      if (as && as.value) {
        var mid = Number(as.value);
        as.disabled = true;
        try {
          var r2 = await db.rpc('onboarding_assign', { p_member_id: mid, p_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'dashboard' });
          if (r2.error) throw r2.error;
          await wa37Load(true);
          toast('Onboarding assigned');
          wa37Mount();
        } catch (x) { toast('Could not assign it'); as.disabled = false; }
        return;
      }
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var op = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w37open]');
      if (op) { wa37OpenModal(op.getAttribute('data-w37open')); return; }
      var ms = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w37master]');
      if (ms) { wa37Master(); return; }
      var ed = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w37e]');
      if (ed) { wa37EditStep(ed.getAttribute('data-w37e')); return; }
      var ad = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w37add]');
      if (ad) { wa37AddStep(ad.getAttribute('data-w37add')); return; }
      var xx = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w37x]');
      if (xx) {
        var tid = xx.getAttribute('data-w37x');
        db.from('onboarding_template').update({ active: false, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', tid).then(async function (r) {
          if (r.error) { toast('Could not retire it'); return; }
          await wa37Load(true);
          toast('Retired from the master and active onboardings');
          wa37Master();
        });
        return;
      }
    });
  }
  wire();
  var __sa37 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa37();
    try { wire(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();

var WA38_PENDING = {};
var WA38_N = 0;

function wa38FieldLines(p) {
  var i = p.input || {};
  var lines = [];
  var L = { opsmtl: 'Operations Montreal', opsto: 'Operations Toronto', marketing: 'Marketing', fundraising: 'Fundraising', finance: 'Finance', hr: 'Human Resources', board: 'Board', progdev: 'Program Development' };
  if (p.name === 'create_request') {
    lines.push('To: ' + (L[i.department] || i.department));
    if (i.type_key) lines.push('Type: ' + i.type_key);
    if (i.assignee) lines.push('For: ' + i.assignee);
    if (i.details) lines.push(String(i.details));
  } else if (p.name === 'create_task') {
    lines.push('Board: ' + (L[i.department] || i.department));
    if (i.owner) lines.push('Owner: ' + i.owner);
    if (i.due_date) lines.push('Due: ' + i.due_date);
    if (i.detail) lines.push(String(i.detail));
  } else if (p.name === 'set_request_status') {
    lines.push('New status: ' + i.status);
  } else if (p.name === 'bank_task') {
    if (i.bank_until) lines.push('Comes back: ' + i.bank_until);
    if (i.reason) lines.push('Why it waits: ' + i.reason);
  } else if (p.name === 'close_task' && i.note) {
    lines.push('Note: ' + i.note);
  }
  return lines.join('\n');
}

function wa38Card(p) {
  var m = document.getElementById('wacMsgs');
  if (!m) return;
  WA38_N += 1;
  var key = 'p' + WA38_N;
  WA38_PENDING[key] = p;
  var d = document.createElement('div');
  d.className = 'wac-card';
  d.id = 'wac-' + key;
  d.innerHTML = '<b>' + esc(p.summary || p.name) + '</b>'
    + (wa38FieldLines(p) ? '<div class="f">' + esc(wa38FieldLines(p)) + '</div>' : '')
    + '<div class="act"><button class="go" data-w38go="' + key + '">Confirm, do it</button>'
    + '<button class="no" data-w38no="' + key + '">Cancel</button></div>';
  m.appendChild(d);
  m.scrollTop = m.scrollHeight;
}

async function wa38Execute(key) {
  var p = WA38_PENDING[key];
  var card = document.getElementById('wac-' + key);
  if (!p || !card) return;
  var go = card.querySelector('.go');
  if (go) { go.disabled = true; go.textContent = 'Doing it...'; }
  try {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('ask', { body: { execute: { name: p.name, input: p.input } } });
    if (r.error) throw r.error;
    var out = (r.data && r.data.executed) || {};
    card.classList.add('decided');
    card.querySelector('.act').remove();
    var res = document.createElement('div');
    res.className = 'res' + (out.ok ? '' : ' err');
    res.textContent = out.ok ? (out.detail || 'Done.') : ('Could not do it: ' + (out.error || 'unknown problem'));
    card.appendChild(res);
    if (out.ok) {
      toast('Done');
      try {
        if (typeof loadAll === 'function') { await loadAll(); renderAllSections(); renderDashboard(); }
        if (typeof waLoadRequests === 'function') { await waLoadRequests(); renderDeptRequestCards(); }
      } catch (e2) { /* refresh best effort */ }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    card.classList.add('decided');
    var res2 = document.createElement('div');
    res2.className = 'res err';
    res2.textContent = 'Could not reach the Hub. Nothing was changed. Try again.';
    card.appendChild(res2);
    if (go) { go.disabled = false; go.textContent = 'Confirm, do it'; }
  }
  delete WA38_PENDING[key];
}

function wa38Cancel(key) {
  var card = document.getElementById('wac-' + key);
  if (card) {
    card.classList.add('decided');
    var act = card.querySelector('.act');
    if (act) act.remove();
    var res = document.createElement('div');
    res.className = 'res';
    res.style.color = '#797773';
    res.textContent = 'Cancelled. Nothing was filed.';
    card.appendChild(res);
  }
  delete WA38_PENDING[key];
}

/* replace the send pipeline: hide the old input row, mount ours */
function wa38Patch() {
  var panel = document.getElementById('waChatPanel');
  if (!panel || document.getElementById('w38in')) return;
  var oldRow = panel.querySelector('.wac-in');
  if (oldRow) oldRow.style.display = 'none';
  var h = panel.querySelector('.wac-h small');
  if (h) h.textContent = 'Ask anything, put in requests, add tasks. You confirm before anything is saved.';
  var row = document.createElement('div');
  row.className = 'wac-in';
  row.innerHTML = '<input id="w38in" placeholder="Ask, or: put in a request to HR to..." />'
    + '<button id="w38send" title="Send">&#10148;</button>';
  panel.appendChild(row);

  function addMsg(cls, text, sources) {
    var m = document.getElementById('wacMsgs');
    var d = document.createElement('div');
    d.className = 'wac-m ' + cls;
    d.innerHTML = '<span>' + esc(text) + '</span>' + (sources && sources.length ? '<div class="wac-src">Sources: ' + esc(sources.join('; ')) + '</div>' : '');
    m.appendChild(d);
    m.scrollTop = m.scrollHeight;
    return d;
  }
  async function send() {
    var inp = document.getElementById('w38in');
    var q = (inp.value || '').trim();
    if (!q) return;
    inp.value = '';
    addMsg('user', q);
    WA_CHAT_HIST.push({ role: 'user', content: q });
    var thinking = addMsg('bot', 'Thinking...');
    try {
      var r = await db.functions.invoke('ask', { body: { question: q, history: WA_CHAT_HIST.slice(-6, -1) } });
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      thinking.remove();
      var data = r.data || {};
      var ans = data.answer || 'I could not find that in the dashboard data.';
      addMsg('bot', ans, data.sources);
      (data.proposals || []).forEach(function (p) { wa38Card(p); });
      WA_CHAT_HIST.push({ role: 'assistant', content: ans });
    } catch (e) {
      thinking.remove();
      addMsg('bot', 'Something went wrong, try again.');
    }
  }
  document.getElementById('w38send').addEventListener('click', send);
  document.getElementById('w38in').addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Enter') send(); });
}

(function () {
  if (typeof waInitChat === 'function') {
    var __ic38 = waInitChat;
    waInitChat = function () {
      __ic38();
      try { wa38Patch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var go = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w38go]');
    if (go) { wa38Execute(go.getAttribute('data-w38go')); return; }
    var no = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w38no]');
    if (no) { wa38Cancel(no.getAttribute('data-w38no')); return; }
  });
  /* if the widget already exists (wave loaded after boot), patch now */
  try { wa38Patch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
})();

      and onboarding for HR, the org chart for board, projects and
      goals everywhere).
   4. Then the familiar card: meetings, updates, summary, and tasks.
   Also across the Hub: action links now look like actual buttons, and
   task rows are cleaner, with the owner dropdown right on the row for
   quick reassignment.
   ===================================================================== */

function wa39DeptDecisions(id) {
  var list = (typeof DB_DECISIONS !== 'undefined' ? DB_DECISIONS : []).filter(function (d) {
    if (d.done) return false;
    var a = String(d.area || '').toLowerCase();
    var lbl = String((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[id]) || id).toLowerCase();
    return a === id || a.indexOf(id) >= 0 || (a && lbl.indexOf(a) >= 0) || a.split(/[ ,\/]+/).indexOf(id) >= 0;
  });
  if (!list.length) return '';
  return '<details class="w39dd w39top"><summary>Executive decisions'
    + '<span class="cnt">' + list.length + ' open</span><span class="chev">&#9662;</span></summary><div class="bd">'
    + list.map(function (d) {
      return '<div class="w39dec"><span class="fl">&#9873;</span><span style="flex:1">' + esc(d.title || '')
        + (d.owner ? ' <span style="font-size:11px;color:#797773">(' + esc(d.owner) + ')</span>' : '') + '</span>'
        + '<a class="w37edit" href="#decision/' + d.id + '">open</a></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div></details>';
}

function wa39DeptGoals(id) {
  var goals = (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).filter(function (g) {
    return g.department === id && g.status !== 'archived' && g.status !== 'done';
  });
  if (!goals.length) return '';
  var nowQ = (typeof wa30Now === 'function') ? wa30Now() : '';
  goals.sort(function (a, b) {
    var ac = a.quarter === nowQ ? 0 : 1, bc = b.quarter === nowQ ? 0 : 1;
    return ac - bc || String(a.quarter).localeCompare(String(b.quarter));
  });
  var col = (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[id]) || '#103F39';
  var projs = (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_PROJECTS : []);
  var html = '<div class="w39goals w39top"><h3 style="color:' + col + '">Quarterly goals</h3>';
  goals.slice(0, 6).forEach(function (g) {
    var linked = projs.filter(function (p) { return String(p.goal_id) === String(g.id) && p.status === 'active'; });
    html += '<div class="w39goal"><div class="t"><span class="w39q">' + esc(g.quarter || '') + '</span>' + esc(g.title || '')
      + '<span style="margin-left:auto"><a class="w37edit" data-w39ge="' + g.id + '">edit and link projects</a></span></div>'
      + (g.measure ? '<div class="m">Measured by: ' + esc(g.measure) + '</div>' : '')
      + '<div>' + (linked.length
        ? linked.map(function (p) { return '<span class="w39pchip" data-w39pp="' + p.id + '">' + esc(p.name) + '</span>'; }).join('')
        : '<span style="font-size:11.5px;color:#797773">No projects linked yet.</span>') + '</div>'
      + '</div>';
  });
  html += '<div style="margin-top:8px"><a class="w37edit" href="#goals">Open the full goals page &raquo;</a></div></div>';
  return html;
}

function wa39DeptTools(id) {
  var tools = [];
  if (id === 'opsmtl' || id === 'opsto') {
    tools.push(['&#129387;', 'Volunteering manager', '#volunteering']);
    tools.push(['&#128214;', 'Recipes', '#volunteering/recipes']);
  }
  if (id === 'hr') {
    tools.push(['&#127891;', 'Avenue Academy', '#training']);
    tools.push(['&#128202;', 'Onboarding board', '#training']);
  }
  if (id === 'board') tools.push(['&#128101;', 'Org chart', '#orgchart']);
  if (id === 'marketing') tools.push(['&#127891;', 'Learning Avenues', '#training']);
  tools.push(['&#127919;', 'Goals', '#goals']);
  tools.push(['&#128194;', 'Projects', 'scroll:projects']);
  if (!tools.length) return '';
  return '<div class="w39tools w39top">' + tools.map(function (t) {
    return '<button class="w39tool" data-w39t="' + esc(t[2]) + '"><span class="ic">' + t[0] + '</span>' + t[1] + '</button>';
  }).join('') + '</div>';
}

function wa39Recompose(id) {
  var panel = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!panel || !panel.querySelector('.dept-card')) return;
  panel.querySelectorAll('.w39top').forEach(function (x) { x.remove(); });
  var html = wa39DeptDecisions(id) + wa39DeptGoals(id) + wa39DeptTools(id);
  if (html) panel.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', html);
}

/* ---------- onboarding: pace, help, and the HR setup checklist ---------- */
var WA39_HELP = [];
var WA39_HRT = [];
var WA39_HRS = [];

async function wa39OnbLoad() {
  try {
    var h = await db.from('onboarding_help').select('*').eq('status', 'open');
    WA39_HELP = (h && h.data) || [];
    var t = await db.from('hr_setup_template').select('*').eq('active', true).order('sort');
    WA39_HRT = (t && t.data) || [];
    var s = await db.from('hr_setup_steps').select('*');
    WA39_HRS = (s && s.data) || [];
  } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
}

function wa39Pace(o) {
  if (o.status === 'completed') return null;
  var days = Math.max(0, (Date.now() - new Date(String(o.started_at) + 'T12:00:00').getTime()) / 86400000);
  var expected = Math.min(100, Math.round(days / 90 * 100));   /* wave279: the plan runs 90 days */
  var p = wa37Pct(o.id).pct;
  if (p >= Math.min(99, expected + 10)) return ['ahead', '#103F39'];
  if (p + 15 < expected) return ['falling behind', '#b3402f'];
  return ['on track', '#797773'];
}

async function wa39OnbAugment() {
  var host = document.querySelector('#training .w36onb');
  if (!host) return;
  await wa39OnbLoad();
  /* the onboardee's ask-for-help button */
  var me = (typeof wa28Me === 'function') ? wa28Me() : null;
  var mine = me ? WA37.onbs.filter(function (o) { return o.status === 'active' && o.person_name.toLowerCase() === me.name.toLowerCase(); })[0] : null;
  if (mine && !host.querySelector('[data-w39ask]')) {
    var anchor = host.querySelector('.w37week');
    if (anchor) anchor.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin',
      '<div style="margin:0 0 8px"><button class="w39tool" data-w39ask="' + mine.id + '">&#9995; Stuck on a step? Ask for help in Slack</button></div>');
  }
  /* HR rows: pace chip, help badge, HR setup progress */
  host.querySelectorAll('.w37hr-row').forEach(function (row) {
    if (row.querySelector('.w39pace')) return;
    var open = row.querySelector('[data-w37open]');
    if (!open) return;
    var oid = open.getAttribute('data-w37open');
    var o = WA37.onbs.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(oid); })[0];
    if (!o) return;
    var pace = wa39Pace(o);
    var helpN = WA39_HELP.filter(function (x) { return String(x.onboarding_id) === String(oid); }).length;
    var hrMine = WA39_HRS.filter(function (x) { return String(x.onboarding_id) === String(oid); });
    var hrDone = hrMine.filter(function (x) { return x.done; }).length;
    var bits = '';
    if (pace) bits += '<span class="w39pace" style="font-size:10.5px;border:1px solid ' + pace[1] + ';color:' + pace[1] + ';border-radius:999px;padding:1px 9px;font-weight:700">' + pace[0] + '</span>';
    if (helpN) bits += '<span class="w39pace" style="font-size:10.5px;background:#b3402f;color:#FFFAF2;border-radius:999px;padding:2px 9px;font-weight:700">' + helpN + ' asking for help</span>';
    bits += '<a class="w37edit w39pace" data-w39hr="' + oid + '">HR setup ' + hrDone + ' of ' + hrMine.length + '</a>';
    bits += '<a class="w37edit w39pace" style="color:#b3402f !important" data-w39del="' + oid + '">remove</a>';
    open.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', bits);
  });
}

function wa39HelpModal(onbId) {
  var m = wa37StepsFor(onbId);
  var undone = WA37.tmpl.filter(function (t) { return m[t.id] && !m[t.id].done; });
  openModal('<h3>Ask for help</h3>'
    + '<p class="m-sub">HR and your lead see it in the Hub right away (the onboarding board, and your lead\'s Home) and reach out.</p>'
    + '<label>Which step are you stuck on?</label><select id="w39hs">'
    + undone.map(function (t) { return '<option value="' + esc(t.name) + '">' + esc(t.section + ': ' + t.name) + '</option>'; }).join('')
    + '</select>'
    + '<label>What is the problem? (optional)</label><textarea id="w39hn" rows="3" placeholder="What you tried, what happened"></textarea>'
    + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w39hgo">Send it</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w39hc">Cancel</button></div>');   /* wave166: the Hub, not Slack (it never reached Slack) */
  document.getElementById('w39hc').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  document.getElementById('w39hgo').addEventListener('click', async function () {
    var me = (typeof wa28Me === 'function') ? wa28Me() : null;
    var btn = document.getElementById('w39hgo');
    btn.disabled = true;
    var r = await db.from('onboarding_help').insert({
      onboarding_id: Number(onbId),
      person_name: (me && me.name) || 'A team member',
      step_name: (document.getElementById('w39hs').value || 'an onboarding step'),
      note: (document.getElementById('w39hn').value || '').trim() || null
    });
    if (r.error) { btn.disabled = false; toast('Could not send it'); return; }
    closeModal();
    toast('Sent. HR and your lead will see it in the Hub.');
  });
}

function wa39HrModal(onbId) {
  var o = WA37.onbs.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(onbId); })[0];
  if (!o) return;
  var mine = {};
  WA39_HRS.forEach(function (s) { if (String(s.onboarding_id) === String(onbId)) mine[s.template_id] = s; });
  var phases = [];
  WA39_HRT.forEach(function (t) { if (phases.indexOf(t.phase) < 0) phases.push(t.phase); });
  var html = '<h3>HR setup for ' + esc(o.person_name) + '</h3>'
    + '<p class="m-sub">The end to end setup from the Onboarding SOP 2025/26: accounts, invites, the onboarding call, and the manager follow-through.</p>';
  phases.forEach(function (ph) {
    html += '<div style="margin:10px 0 2px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;font-size:13px">' + esc(ph) + '</div>';
    WA39_HRT.filter(function (t) { return t.phase === ph; }).forEach(function (t) {
      var s = mine[t.id];
      if (!s) return;
      html += '<div class="w37step' + (s.done ? ' done' : '') + '" style="border-bottom:1px solid #F4F2EA">'
        + '<input type="checkbox" data-w39hrchk="' + s.id + '"' + (s.done ? ' checked' : '') + ' />'
        + '<div style="flex:1"><div class="nm">' + esc(t.name) + '</div>'
        + (t.description ? '<div class="ds">' + esc(t.description) + '</div>' : '')
        + (s.done && s.done_by ? '<div class="ds" style="opacity:.6">done by ' + esc(s.done_by) + '</div>' : '')
        + '</div></div>';
    });
  });
  html += '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn ghost" id="w39hrc">Close</button></div>';
  openModal(html);
  document.getElementById('w39hrc').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); wa37Mount(); });
}

if (typeof wa37Mount === 'function') {
  (function () {
    var __m39 = wa37Mount;
    wa37Mount = async function () {
      await __m39();
      try { await wa39OnbAugment(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  })();
}

(function () {
  if (typeof renderSection === 'function') {
    var __rs39 = renderSection;
    renderSection = function (id) {
      __rs39(id);
      try { wa39Recompose(id); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  function wire() {
    if (document.body.dataset.wa39Wired) return;
    document.body.dataset.wa39Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var t = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w39t]');
      if (t) {
        var dest = t.getAttribute('data-w39t');
        if (dest === 'scroll:projects') {
          var panel = t.closest('.panel');
          var card = panel && panel.querySelector('.w27-chips');
          if (card) card.closest('.card').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
          else location.hash = '#goals';
        } else location.hash = dest;
        return;
      }
      var ge = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w39ge]');
      if (ge && typeof wa29GoalModal === 'function') {
        try { wa29GoalModal(Number(ge.getAttribute('data-w39ge'))); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ }
        return;
      }
      var pp = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w39pp]');
      if (pp) { location.hash = '#project/' + pp.getAttribute('data-w39pp'); return; }
      var ask = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w39ask]');
      if (ask) { try { wa39HelpModal(ask.getAttribute('data-w39ask')); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } return; }
      var hr = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w39hr]');
      if (hr) { try { wa39HrModal(hr.getAttribute('data-w39hr')); } catch (x) { /* non-fatal */ } return; }
      var del = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w39del]');
      if (del) {
        var oid2 = del.getAttribute('data-w39del');
        var o2 = WA37.onbs.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(oid2); })[0];
        openModal('<h3>Remove this onboarding?</h3><p class="m-sub">This deletes ' + esc(o2 ? o2.person_name : 'this person') + '\'s onboarding, their checklist progress, and the HR setup checklist. It cannot be undone.</p>'
          + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w39dgo" style="background:#b3402f">Yes, remove it</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w39dc">Keep it</button></div>');
        document.getElementById('w39dc').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
        document.getElementById('w39dgo').addEventListener('click', async function () {
          var r = await db.from('onboardings').delete().eq('id', Number(oid2));
          if (r.error) { toast('Could not remove it'); return; }
          closeModal();
          await wa37Load(true);
          toast('Onboarding removed');
          wa37Mount();
        });
        return;
      }
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('change', async function (e) {
      var chk = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w39hrchk]');
      if (!chk) return;
      var patch = { done: chk.checked, done_at: chk.checked ? new Date().toISOString() : null, done_by: chk.checked ? ((typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'dashboard') : null };
      var r = await db.from('hr_setup_steps').update(patch).eq('id', Number(chk.getAttribute('data-w39hrchk')));
      if (r.error) { toast('Could not save that'); chk.checked = !chk.checked; return; }
      WA39_HRS.forEach(function (s) { if (String(s.id) === String(chk.getAttribute('data-w39hrchk'))) { s.done = patch.done; s.done_by = patch.done_by; } });
      toast('Saved');
    });
  }
  wire();
  var __sa39 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa39();
    try {
      wire();
      /* goals and projects load async; recompose once they are in */
      setTimeout(function () {
        try {
          if (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined') DEPT_ORDER.forEach(function (d) { wa39Recompose(d); });
        } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      }, 1200);
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v40 roles, permissions, and the admin panel
   (29/07/26)
   Guardrails ahead of the wider rollout. Every signed-in person now
   carries a role: admin, leader, member, or viewer. Roles decide which
   tabs exist for them; per-person overrides are possible. Admins
   (Steel, Suhaib, Ian, Valex, Michael, Tuna) get an Admin tab: set
   anyone's role, override their visible tabs, see who has access.
   Defaults: admins and leaders see everything; members see their work
   surfaces (no Board & Governance, no Admin); viewers see the overview
   only. This governs navigation; board-only data stays protected
   server side as before.
   ===================================================================== */

var WA40_ROLES = [];
var WA40_MINE = null;
var WA40_TABS = ['overview', 'dashboard', 'opsmtl', 'opsto', 'marketing', 'fundraising', 'hr', 'board', 'training', 'transcripts', 'activity'];
var WA40_DEFAULTS = {
  admin: WA40_TABS.concat(['adminpanel']),
  leader: WA40_TABS.slice(),
  member: ['overview', 'dashboard', 'opsmtl', 'opsto', 'marketing', 'fundraising', 'hr', 'training', 'transcripts'],
  viewer: ['overview']
};

async function wa40Load() {
  try {
    var r = await db.from('hub_roles').select('*');
    WA40_ROLES = (r && r.data) || [];
    var em = (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email ? USER.email : '').toLowerCase();
    WA40_MINE = WA40_ROLES.filter(function (x) { return x.email === em; })[0]
      || { email: em, role: em.indexOf('@welfareavenue.com') > 0 ? 'member' : 'viewer' };
  } catch (e) { WA40_MINE = { role: 'member' }; }
}
function wa40Allowed() {
  if (!WA40_MINE) return WA40_TABS;
  if (Array.isArray(WA40_MINE.tabs) && WA40_MINE.tabs.length) return WA40_MINE.tabs;
  return WA40_DEFAULTS[WA40_MINE.role] || WA40_DEFAULTS.member;
}
function wa40Enforce() {
  var allowed = wa40Allowed();
  document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .tab').forEach(function (t) {
    var id = t.dataset.tab;
    t.style.display = allowed.indexOf(id) >= 0 ? '' : 'none';
  });
}

/* admin tab + panel */
(function () {
  var main = document.querySelector('main');
  if (main && !document.getElementById('adminpanel')) {
    var sec = document.createElement('section');
    sec.id = 'adminpanel';
    sec.className = 'panel';
    sec.innerHTML = '<div class="card"><h2>Admin: who sees what</h2>'
      + '<p class="read">Set each person\'s role. Admins manage everything; leaders see every page; members see the work surfaces (no board page, no admin); viewers see the overview only. Tab overrides win over the role default.</p>'
      + '<div id="w40body">Loading...</div></div>';
    main.appendChild(sec);
  }
  var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
  if (bar && !bar.querySelector('[data-tab="adminpanel"]')) {
    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'tab';
    btn.setAttribute('data-tab', 'adminpanel');
    btn.innerHTML = '<span class="tabdot" style="background:#44403c"></span>Admin';
    bar.appendChild(btn);
    btn.addEventListener('click', function () { if (typeof showPanel === 'function') showPanel('adminpanel'); });
  }
})();

async function wa40Render() {
  var body = document.getElementById('w40body');
  if (!body) return;
  if (!WA40_MINE || WA40_MINE.role !== 'admin') { body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Only admins can manage roles.</p>'; return; }
  var people = (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []).filter(function (p) { return p.email; });
  var byEmail = {};
  WA40_ROLES.forEach(function (r) { byEmail[r.email] = r; });
  var rows = [];
  var seen = {};
  people.forEach(function (p) {
    var em = String(p.email).toLowerCase();
    seen[em] = 1;
    rows.push({ email: em, name: p.name, row: byEmail[em] });
  });
  WA40_ROLES.forEach(function (r) { if (!seen[r.email]) rows.push({ email: r.email, name: r.note || '', row: r }); });
  var html = '<table class="wa-tbl" style="width:100%"><thead><tr><th>Person</th><th>Email</th><th>Role</th><th>Tab override</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  rows.forEach(function (x, i) {
    var role = x.row ? x.row.role : 'member';
    var ov = x.row && Array.isArray(x.row.tabs) && x.row.tabs.length ? x.row.tabs.join(', ') : '';
    html += '<tr><td><b>' + esc(x.name || '') + '</b></td><td style="font-size:12px">' + esc(x.email) + '</td>'
      + '<td><select class="w36sel" data-w40r="' + esc(x.email) + '">'
      + ['admin', 'leader', 'member', 'viewer'].map(function (r) { return '<option value="' + r + '"' + (r === role ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + r + '</option>'; }).join('')
      + '</select></td>'
      + '<td><a class="w37edit" data-w40t="' + esc(x.email) + '">' + (ov ? esc(ov.slice(0, 40)) : 'role default') + '</a></td></tr>';
  });
  html += '</tbody></table>'
    + '<p class="m-sub" style="margin-top:10px">People appear here once their email is on their team profile. Roles apply the next time they load the Hub.</p>';
  body.innerHTML = html;
}

function wa40TabModal(email) { /* superseded: wave72 rebinds this to w72TabModal (grouped access modal); body removed in Phase 0 compaction */ }

(function () {
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp40 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      var allowed = wa40Allowed();
      if (WA40_MINE && allowed.indexOf(id) < 0 && id !== 'needsme' && id !== 'personpage' && id !== 'projectpage' && id !== 'volunteering' && id !== 'importantinfo') {
        /* wave260 (16/10/26): the Dashboard is for the admins; everyone else lands on Home (directors on the Board overview) */
        id = (window.w260 && typeof window.w260.fallback === 'function') ? window.w260.fallback(id) : 'overview';
      }
      __sp40(id);
      if (id === 'adminpanel') { try { wa40Render(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
    };
  }
  function wire() {
    if (document.body.dataset.wa40Wired) return;
    document.body.dataset.wa40Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('change', async function (e) {
      var r = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w40r]');
      if (!r) return;
      var em = r.getAttribute('data-w40r');
      var res = await db.from('hub_roles').upsert({
        email: em, role: r.value,
        updated_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'admin', updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      }, { onConflict: 'email' });
      if (res.error) { toast('Could not change the role'); return; }
      await wa40Load();
      toast(em.split('@')[0] + ' is now ' + r.value);
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var t = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w40t]');
      if (t) { wa40TabModal(t.getAttribute('data-w40t')); }
    });
  }
  wire();
  var __sa40 = startApp;
  startApp = async function () {
    await __sa40();
    try {
      wire();
      await wa40Load();
      wa40Enforce();
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    /* wave278 (04/11/26): the "Sign in to the Hub" tick of a new hire's setup page (/o/<token>?setup). Once per page load, after the role is
       known (WA40_MINE); wa278_hub_seen() ticks rd_hub only on the onboarding flow whose wa_email is this sign-in, so for everyone else it does
       nothing. Not awaited, errors ignored, the result unused. */
    if (!window.__wa278Seen) {
      window.__wa278Seen = true;
      try { db.rpc('wa278_hub_seen').then(function () {}, function () {}); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    }
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v41 the department dropdown returns
   (30/07/26)
   The hover menu on department tabs (Overview, Executive decisions,
   Agendas, Transcripts) was opening UNDERNEATH the new floating sticky
   navigation (its z-index predates the redesign), so it looked gone.
   It now paints above everything, opens just below the nav pill even
   when the tabs wrap to two rows, and matches the new aesthetic.
   ===================================================================== */

(function () {
  if (document.getElementById('wa41css')) return;
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa41css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.wa-dd{z-index:220 !important;border:none !important;border-radius:14px !important;' +
    'box-shadow:0 18px 50px -12px rgba(16,63,57,.3) !important;overflow:hidden;padding:5px;min-width:210px;}' +
    '.wa-dd a{border-radius:9px;font-size:12.8px;padding:8px 12px;}' +
    '.wa-dd a:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);color:#103F39;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* after the original hover code positions the menu, snap it below the
     whole nav pill so first-row tabs do not open it behind the second row */
  /* v40's tab gating hid every nav button it did not recognize, which
     swallowed the Workspace menu (agendas, important info, requests).
     Gate only the tabs the role system actually knows about. */
  if (typeof wa40Enforce === 'function' && typeof WA40_TABS !== 'undefined') {
    var __en41 = wa40Enforce;
    wa40Enforce = function () {
      __en41();
      var known = WA40_TABS.concat(['adminpanel']);
      document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .tab').forEach(function (t) {
        var id = t.dataset.tab;
        if (!id || known.indexOf(id) < 0) t.style.display = '';
      });
    };
  }

  var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
  if (bar && !bar.dataset.wa41) {
    bar.dataset.wa41 = '1';
    bar.addEventListener('mouseover', function (e) {
      var tab = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.tab[data-tab]');
      if (!tab) return;
      setTimeout(function () {
        var dd = document.querySelector('.wa-dd');
        var nav = document.querySelector('nav.wa-nav');
        if (!dd || !nav || dd.style.display === 'none' || !dd.style.display) return;
        var nr = nav.getBoundingClientRect();
        var tr = tab.getBoundingClientRect();
        dd.style.position = 'fixed';
        dd.style.top = Math.round(nr.bottom + 4) + 'px';
        var left = Math.round(tr.left);
        var maxLeft = window.innerWidth - 230;
        dd.style.left = Math.max(8, Math.min(left, maxLeft)) + 'px';
      }, 0);
    });
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v42 one navigation
   (30/07/26)
   The Hub gets one organized menu and a nav registry.
   - Restores the pages v40's gate was bouncing to the overview
     (My page, Goals, Agendas, Requests, Create, Finances, Org chart)
     and makes the Admin panel deep-linkable at #adminpanel.
   - Rebuilds the bar: Home, Decisions, Requests, Agendas, Important
     info, Departments, Work, People, Volunteering, Archive, Admin.
     Existing buttons are MOVED, never recreated, so all delegated
     routing keeps working. Volunteering, Create and Finances get
     their nav entries back (v24 had buried them in department cards;
     those quick links stay too).
   - Rebuilds the per-department flyout (Overview, Executive
     decisions, Agendas, Transcripts) inside the Departments menu;
     the old v4 hover menu had been dead CSS since v13.
   - WA_NAV registry: from now on waves call WA_NAV.register() to add
     a page (tab placement, route, role gating in one declaration)
     instead of touching the tab bar or wrapping applyHash.
   Role semantics preserved per v40's banner: admins and leaders see
   everything (leaders minus Admin); members lose Board, Activity and
   Finances; viewers see the overview only; per-person tab overrides
   still win. The always-reachable exemptions are untouched.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.WA_NAV) return;

  /* ---- permissions: enrol every real page in the v40 role system ---- */
  var WA42_EXTRA = ['mypage', 'goals', 'agendas', 'requests', 'importantinfo', 'create', 'finances', 'orgchart', 'volunteering'];
  try {
    if (typeof WA40_TABS !== 'undefined' && typeof WA40_DEFAULTS !== 'undefined') {
      WA42_EXTRA.forEach(function (id) { if (WA40_TABS.indexOf(id) < 0) WA40_TABS.push(id); });
      WA40_DEFAULTS.admin = WA40_TABS.concat(['adminpanel']);
      WA40_DEFAULTS.leader = WA40_TABS.slice();
      WA40_DEFAULTS.member = WA40_TABS.filter(function (id) { return id !== 'board' && id !== 'activity' && id !== 'finances'; });
    }
  } catch (e) { /* role system absent; nav still works */ }

  function wa42Enrol(id, gate) {
    try {
      if (typeof WA40_TABS === 'undefined' || typeof WA40_DEFAULTS === 'undefined') return;
      if (WA40_TABS.indexOf(id) < 0) WA40_TABS.push(id);
      var g = gate || 'member';
      if (g === 'viewer' && WA40_DEFAULTS.viewer.indexOf(id) < 0) WA40_DEFAULTS.viewer.push(id);
      if ((g === 'viewer' || g === 'member') && WA40_DEFAULTS.member.indexOf(id) < 0) WA40_DEFAULTS.member.push(id);
      if (g !== 'admin' && WA40_DEFAULTS.leader.indexOf(id) < 0) WA40_DEFAULTS.leader.push(id);
      if (WA40_DEFAULTS.admin.indexOf(id) < 0) WA40_DEFAULTS.admin.push(id);
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  /* ---- styles ---- */
  if (!document.getElementById('wa42css')) {
    var st = document.createElement('style');
    st.id = 'wa42css';
    st.textContent = '' +
      '.tab-group-h{border-radius:999px;padding:10px 14px;}' +
      '.w42d{position:relative;display:block;}' +
      '.w42d > .tab{display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;}' +
      '.w42sub{display:none;position:absolute;left:100%;top:0;background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:12px;' +
      'box-shadow:0 18px 50px -12px rgba(16,63,57,.3);min-width:200px;padding:5px;z-index:230;}' +
      '.w42d:hover > .w42sub{display:block;}' +
      '.w42sub a{display:block;padding:8px 12px;border-radius:9px;font-size:12.8px;font-weight:600;' +
      'color:var(--wa-text);cursor:pointer;text-decoration:none;}' +
      '.w42sub a:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);color:#103F39;}' +
      '@media (max-width:740px){.w42sub{display:none !important;}.w42d > .tab{padding:12px 14px;}}';
    document.head.appendChild(st);
  }

  /* ---- small helpers ---- */
  function wa42Tab(id, label, dot) {
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'tab';
    b.setAttribute('data-tab', id);
    b.innerHTML = '<span class="tabdot" style="background:' + (dot || '#103F39') + '"></span>' + label;
    return b;
  }
  function wa42Label(btn, label) {
    if (!btn) return;
    var dot = btn.querySelector('.tabdot');
    btn.innerHTML = (dot ? dot.outerHTML : '') + label;
  }
  function wa42Group(key, label, items) {
    var g = document.createElement('span');
    g.className = 'tab-group';
    g.setAttribute('data-w42g', key);
    var h = document.createElement('button');
    h.className = 'tab-group-h';
    h.innerHTML = label + ' <span class="caret">&#9660;</span>';
    var p = document.createElement('div');
    p.className = 'tab-group-p';
    items.forEach(function (el) { if (el) p.appendChild(el); });
    g.appendChild(h);
    g.appendChild(p);
    h.addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
      ev.stopPropagation();
      var was = g.classList.contains('open');
      document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .tab-group.open').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('open'); });
      if (!was) g.classList.add('open');
    });
    return g;
  }
  function wa42DeptRow(btn, d) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'w42d';
    row.appendChild(btn);
    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'w42sub';
    sub.innerHTML = '<a data-w42a="overview" data-w42d="' + d + '">Overview</a>' +
      '<a data-w42a="exec" data-w42d="' + d + '">Executive decisions</a>' +
      '<a data-w42a="agenda" data-w42d="' + d + '">Agendas</a>' +
      '<a data-w42a="tx" data-w42d="' + d + '">Transcripts</a>';
    row.appendChild(sub);
    return row;
  }

  /* ---- the registry ---- */
  window.WA_NAV = {
    pages: [],
    groups: ['departments', 'work', 'people', 'archive'],
    /* def: {id, label, group, dot, gate: viewer|member|leader|admin, onShow, panel:false, noRoute:true} */
    register: function (def) {
      if (!def || !def.id) return;
      wa42Enrol(def.id, def.gate);
      /* audit 26/09/26: a page registered twice (wave87 did) is kept once */
      if (!def.noRoute && !this.pages.some(function (p) { return p.id === def.id; })) this.pages.push(def);
      if (def.panel !== false && !document.getElementById(def.id)) {
        var main = document.querySelector('main');
        if (main) {
          var s = document.createElement('section');
          s.id = def.id;
          s.className = 'panel';
          main.appendChild(s);
        }
      }
      var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
      if (bar && def.label && !bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + def.id + '"]')) {
        var b = wa42Tab(def.id, def.label, def.dot);
        var host = null;
        if (def.group) {
          var g = bar.querySelector('.tab-group[data-w42g="' + def.group + '"]');
          if (g) host = g.querySelector('.tab-group-p');
        }
        if (host) host.appendChild(b);
        else bar.insertBefore(b, bar.querySelector(':scope > .tab[data-tab="adminpanel"]') || null); /* the menu layout (wave134) may hold Admin inside a group */
        if (window.WA_NAV && WA_NAV.layout && WA_NAV.applyLayout) setTimeout(function () { try { WA_NAV.applyLayout(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 0);
      }
      if (typeof wa40Enforce === 'function') { try { wa40Enforce(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
    },
    rebuild: function () { try { wa42Nav(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
  };

  /* ---- one route dispatcher for registry pages ---- */
  if (typeof applyHash === 'function') {
    var __ah42 = applyHash;
    applyHash = function () {
      var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
      var root = h.split('/')[0];
      var pg = null;
      for (var i = 0; i < WA_NAV.pages.length; i++) { if (WA_NAV.pages[i].id === root) { pg = WA_NAV.pages[i]; break; } }
      if (pg) {
        showPanel(pg.id);
        if (pg.onShow) { try { pg.onShow(h); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
        return;
      }
      __ah42();
    };
  }
  /* the admin panel becomes deep-linkable; its tab and panel already exist */
  WA_NAV.pages.push({ id: 'adminpanel' });

  /* ---- hide group headers whose every tab is role-hidden ---- */
  function wa42Groups() {
    document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .tab-group').forEach(function (g) {
      var any = false;
      g.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(function (t) { if (t.style.display !== 'none') any = true; });
      var h = g.querySelector('.tab-group-h');
      if (h) h.style.display = any ? '' : 'none';
    });
  }
  if (typeof wa40Enforce === 'function') {
    var __en42 = wa40Enforce;
    wa40Enforce = function () {
      __en42();
      try { wa42Groups(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  /* ---- the rebuild ---- */
  function wa42Nav() {
    var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar');
    if (!bar || bar.dataset.wa42) return;
    bar.dataset.wa42 = '1';
    /* dissolve prior groups; buttons move back to the bar with their wiring intact */
    Array.prototype.slice.call(bar.querySelectorAll('.tab-group')).forEach(function (g) {
      Array.prototype.slice.call(g.querySelectorAll('.tab')).forEach(function (t) { bar.appendChild(t); });
      if (g.parentNode) g.parentNode.removeChild(g);
    });
    function grab(id) { return bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + id + '"]'); }
    function need(id, label, dot) { return grab(id) || wa42Tab(id, label, dot); }
    wa42Label(grab('mypage'), 'Home');
    wa42Label(grab('overview'), 'Decisions');
    var order = [];
    ['mypage', 'overview', 'requests', 'agendas', 'importantinfo'].forEach(function (id) {
      var b = grab(id);
      if (b) order.push(b);
    });
    var depts = (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' ? DEPT_ORDER : []).map(function (d) {
      var b = grab(d);
      return b ? wa42DeptRow(b, d) : null;
    });
    order.push(wa42Group('departments', 'Teams', depts));
    order.push(wa42Group('work', 'Work', [grab('dashboard'), grab('goals'),
      need('create', 'Create studio', '#6F64AB'), need('finances', 'Finance', '#B9851B')]));
    order.push(wa42Group('people', 'People', [grab('orgchart'), grab('training')]));
    order.push(need('volunteering', 'Volunteering', '#103F39'));
    order.push(wa42Group('archive', 'Archive', [grab('transcripts'), grab('activity')]));
    var adm = grab('adminpanel');
    if (adm) order.push(adm);
    order.forEach(function (el) { if (el) bar.appendChild(el); });
    if (typeof wa40Enforce === 'function') { try { wa40Enforce(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } }
  }

  /* ---- department flyout actions (same destinations as the old v4 menu) ---- */
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa42Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa42Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w42sub a');
      if (!a) return;
      var d = a.getAttribute('data-w42d');
      var act = a.getAttribute('data-w42a');
      document.querySelectorAll('#tabBar .tab-group.open').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('open'); });
      document.body.classList.remove('wa-mob-open');
      if (act === 'overview') { location.hash = '#dept/' + d; }
      if (act === 'exec') {
        location.hash = '#dept/' + d;
        setTimeout(function () {
          var c = document.querySelector('#' + d + ' .exec-card');
          if (c) c.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
        }, 380);
      }
      if (act === 'agenda') {
        location.hash = '#agendas';
        setTimeout(function () {
          try {
            var f = document.getElementById('agFilterDept');
            if (f) { f.value = d; waRenderAgendasPage(); }
          } catch (e2) { /* non-fatal */ }
        }, 300);
      }
      if (act === 'tx') {
        location.hash = '#transcripts';
        setTimeout(function () {
          try {
            var f = document.getElementById('txFilter');
            if (f) {
              var opt = Array.prototype.slice.call(f.options).find(function (o) {
                return txDeptKey(o.value) === d || txDeptKey(o.text) === d;
              });
              if (opt) { f.value = opt.value; renderTranscripts(); }
            }
          } catch (e3) { /* non-fatal */ }
        }, 380);
      }
    });
  }

  /* ---- run after boot, once everything else has assembled the bar ---- */
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa42 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa42();
      try { wa42Nav(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();

      admin links, and friends). One consistent look everywhere.

      in the goals block. Nothing loses functionality; things stop
      appearing twice.

      its title that opens a plain-language explainer.
   4. The training catalog becomes editable for admins and leaders
      (add a training, edit any card), completing the item deferred
      from v39.

  function wa43PlaceExec(id) {
    try {
      var panel = document.getElementById(id);
      if (!panel) return;
      var card = panel.querySelector('.exec-card');
      if (card) {
        var dd = panel.querySelector('.w39dd');
        if (!dd) {
          dd = document.createElement('details');
          dd.className = 'w39dd w39top';
          var n = card.querySelectorAll('.exec-item').length;
          dd.innerHTML = '<summary>Executive decisions<span class="cnt">' + n + ' open</span><span class="chev">&#9662;</span></summary><div class="bd"></div>';
          panel.insertBefore(dd, panel.firstChild);
        }
        var bd = dd.querySelector('.bd');
        if (bd && card.parentElement !== bd) {
          bd.innerHTML = '';
          bd.appendChild(card);
        }
      }
      /* retire the compact goals card on department pages only */
      panel.querySelectorAll('.card.gl-card').forEach(function (el) { el.remove(); });
      /* keep the "All goals and quarters" jump inside the goals block */

  function wa43Key(id) {
    if (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' && DEPT_ORDER.indexOf(id) >= 0) return 'dept';
    return WA43_HELP[id] ? id : null;
  }
  function wa43HelpBtn(id) {
    try {
      var key = wa43Key(id);
      if (!key) return;
      var panel = document.getElementById(id);
      if (!panel || panel.querySelector('[data-w43help]')) return;
      var host = key === 'dept' ? null : (panel.querySelector('.card h2') || panel.querySelector('h2'));
      var b = document.createElement('button');
      b.className = 'w43help';
      b.setAttribute('data-w43help', key);
      b.innerHTML = '&#63; How this page works';
      if (host) host.appendChild(b);
      else {
        var row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'w43helprow';
        row.appendChild(b);
        panel.insertBefore(row, panel.firstChild);
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp43 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      __sp43(id);
      try {
        var act = document.querySelector('.panel.active');
        if (act) {
          wa43HelpBtn(act.id);
          /* some pages rewrite their own header right after showPanel */
          setTimeout(function () { wa43HelpBtn(act.id); }, 500);
        }
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof renderGoalsPage === 'function') {
    var __gp43 = renderGoalsPage;

  /* ---- 4. editable training catalog (deferred from v39) ---- */
  function wa43CanEdit() {
    try {
      if (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) {
        return WA40_MINE.role === 'admin' || WA40_MINE.role === 'leader';
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fall through */ }
    return false;
  }
  function wa43Decorate() {
    try {
      if (!wa43CanEdit()) return;
      var body = document.getElementById('w36body');
      if (!body) return;
      if (!body.querySelector('[data-w43ta]')) {
        var onb = body.querySelector('.w36onb');
        var bar = document.createElement('div');
        bar.style.cssText = 'margin:4px 0 10px;text-align:right;';
        bar.innerHTML = '<a data-w43ta="1">+ Add a training</a>';
        if (onb && onb.nextSibling) body.insertBefore(bar, onb.nextSibling);
        else body.insertBefore(bar, body.firstChild);
      }
      body.querySelectorAll('.w36card').forEach(function (card) {
        if (card.querySelector('[data-w43te]')) return;
        var me = card.querySelector('[data-w36me]');
        if (!me) return;
        var row = card.querySelector('.w36row');
        if (row) row.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<a data-w43te="' + me.getAttribute('data-w36me') + '">Edit</a>');
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  if (typeof wa36Render === 'function') {
    var __tr43 = wa36Render;
    wa36Render = async function () {
      await __tr43.apply(this, arguments);
      wa43Decorate();
    };
  }
  function wa43TrainModal(tid) {
    var t = null;
    try {
      (WA36_TRAININGS || []).forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.id) === String(tid)) t = x; });
    } catch (e) { /* new */ }
    var cats = [];
    try {
      (WA36_TRAININGS || []).forEach(function (x) { if (x.category && cats.indexOf(x.category) < 0) cats.push(x.category); });
    } catch (e) { /* empty */ }
    function v(x) { return t && t[x] != null ? String(t[x]) : ''; }
    openModal('<h3>' + (t ? 'Edit training' : 'Add a training') + '</h3>' +
      '<label class="m-sub">Title</label><input id="w43tt" style="width:100%" value="' + esc(v('title')) + '" />' +
      '<label class="m-sub" style="margin-top:8px;display:block">Category</label>' +
      '<input id="w43tc" style="width:100%" list="w43cl" value="' + esc(v('category')) + '" />' +
      '<datalist id="w43cl">' + cats.map(function (c) { return '<option value="' + esc(c) + '"></option>'; }).join('') + '</datalist>' +
      '<label class="m-sub" style="margin-top:8px;display:block">Link (Loom, Slack, doc...)</label><input id="w43tu" style="width:100%" value="' + esc(v('resource_url')) + '" />' +
      '<label class="m-sub" style="margin-top:8px;display:block">Length in minutes (optional)</label><input id="w43td" type="number" style="width:120px" value="' + (t && t.duration_sec ? Math.round(t.duration_sec / 60) : '') + '" />' +
      '<label class="m-sub" style="margin-top:8px;display:block">Description (optional)</label><textarea id="w43tds" style="width:100%;min-height:60px">' + esc(v('description')) + '</textarea>' +
      '<label class="m-sub" style="margin-top:8px;display:block">Path group (optional, e.g. Marketing path)</label><input id="w43tg" style="width:100%" value="' + esc(v('path_group')) + '" />' +
      (t ? '<label style="display:flex;gap:7px;align-items:center;margin-top:10px;font-size:13px"><input type="checkbox" id="w43tar" style="width:15px;height:15px" /> Retire this training (hides it from the catalog)</label>' : '') +
      '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w43tsv">Save</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w43tcx">Cancel</button></div>');
    document.getElementById('w43tcx').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w43tsv').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var title = document.getElementById('w43tt').value.trim();
      if (!title) { toast('The training needs a title'); return; }
      var mins = parseInt(document.getElementById('w43td').value, 10);
      var patch = {
        title: title,
        category: document.getElementById('w43tc').value.trim() || 'General',
        resource_url: document.getElementById('w43tu').value.trim() || null,
        duration_sec: isNaN(mins) ? null : mins * 60,
        description: document.getElementById('w43tds').value.trim() || null,
        path_group: document.getElementById('w43tg').value.trim() || null
      };
      var ar = document.getElementById('w43tar');
      if (ar && ar.checked) patch.active = false;
      var r;
      if (t) r = await db.from('trainings').update(patch).eq('id', t.id);
      else { patch.source = 'hub'; patch.active = true; r = await db.from('trainings').insert(patch); }
      if (r.error) { toast('Could not save the training'); return; }
      closeModal();
      try { await wa36Load(true); wa36Render(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      toast('Saved');
    });
  }

  /* ---- delegated clicks ---- */
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa43Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa43Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var h = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w43help]');
      if (h) {
        var key = h.getAttribute('data-w43help');
        openModal('<h3>' + esc(WA43_TITLE[key] || 'This page') + '</h3><div class="w43hm">' + (WA43_HELP[key] || '') + '</div>' +
          '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w43hc">Got it</button></div>');
        var c = document.getElementById('w43hc');
        if (c) c.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
        return;
      }
      var ta = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w43ta]');
      if (ta) { if (wa43CanEdit()) wa43TrainModal(null); return; }
      var te = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w43te]');
      if (te) { if (wa43CanEdit()) wa43TrainModal(te.getAttribute('data-w43te')); return; }
    });
  }

  /* ---- boot ---- */
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa43 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa43();
      try {
        var act = document.querySelector('.panel.active');
        if (act) wa43HelpBtn(act.id);
        if (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined') DEPT_ORDER.forEach(wa43PlaceExec);
        wa43Decorate();
        /* the v39 delayed recompose runs 1.2s after boot; sweep again after it */
        setTimeout(function () {
          try {
            if (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined') DEPT_ORDER.forEach(wa43PlaceExec);
            document.querySelectorAll('section.panel:not(#goals) .card.gl-card').forEach(function (el) { el.remove(); });
          } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
        }, 1800);
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v44 the training centre
   (01/08/26)
   The Training page grows up.
   1. Your training, first: a personal dashboard at the top: to do,
      done, assigned counts, onboarding progress, overdue onboarding
      steps, and your outstanding trainings as a working list.
   2. An HR dashboard (admins and leaders): the onboarding board and
      master editor in one place, plus a per-person training table
      with one-click "Assign trainings" (checkbox the catalog for any
      person; unticking removes an assignment unless it is done).
   3. The master onboarding editor now edits everything: name,
      instructions, a link field, the week it belongs to, its position,
      priority (shows the "important" badge), and where new steps sit
      (top level or under a parent). Retire still works.
   4. Checklists now show due dates: an amber chip when a step has a
      date, red when it is overdue.
   Everything rides the existing tables; no schema changes.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (document.getElementById('wa44css')) return;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa44css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w44me,.w44hr{background:#F4F2EA;border-radius:16px;padding:14px 16px;margin:0 0 14px;}' +
    '.w44me h3,.w44hr h3{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:12px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.7px;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w44tiles{display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 0 10px;}' +
    '.w44tile{background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:14px;padding:10px 16px;min-width:96px;box-shadow:0 1px 2px rgba(16,63,57,.04);}' +
    '.w44tile b{display:block;font-size:20px;color:#103F39;line-height:1.2;}' +
    '.w44tile span{font-size:11.5px;color:#797773;font-weight:600;}' +

    '.w44due.late{background:rgba(179,64,47,.12);color:#b3402f;}' +
    '[data-w44as],[data-w44mark]{display:inline-block;background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:999px;padding:3px 11px;' +
    'font-size:11.5px;color:#103F39 !important;text-decoration:none !important;cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;line-height:1.5;margin:1px 2px;}' +
    '[data-w44as]:hover,[data-w44mark]:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.4);}' +
    '.w44cat{margin:6px 0 2px;font-size:12px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.7px;color:#797773;font-weight:700;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  function wa44CanEdit() {
    try {
      if (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) {
        return WA40_MINE.role === 'admin' || WA40_MINE.role === 'leader';
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fall through */ }
    return false;
  }
  function wa44Today() { return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
  function wa44Sections() {
    var secs = [];
    try {
      Object.keys(WA37_WEEK_ORDER).forEach(function (k) { secs.push(k); });
      WA37.tmpl.forEach(function (t) { if (t.section && secs.indexOf(t.section) < 0) secs.push(t.section); });
    } catch (e) { /* defaults */ }
    if (!secs.length) secs = ['WEEK 1', 'WEEK 2', 'WEEK 3', 'WEEK 4', '60 DAYS'];
    return secs;
  }

  /* ---- 4. checklists show due dates ---- */
  if (typeof wa37Checklist === 'function' && typeof wa37StepsFor === 'function' && typeof wa37Weeks === 'function') {
    wa37Checklist = function (onb, canTick) {
      var m = wa37StepsFor(onb.id);
      var today = wa44Today();
      var html = '';
      wa37Weeks().forEach(function (wk, i) {
        var wkSteps = wk.steps.filter(function (t) { return m[t.id]; });
        if (!wkSteps.length) return;
        var d = wkSteps.filter(function (t) { return m[t.id].done; }).length;
        html += '<details class="w37week"' + (i === 0 && d < wkSteps.length ? ' open' : '') + '><summary>' + esc(wk.name)
          + ' <span class="w37bar" style="max-width:140px"><i style="width:' + Math.round(100 * d / wkSteps.length) + '%"></i></span>'
          + '<span style="font-size:11.5px;color:#797773;font-weight:500">' + d + ' of ' + wkSteps.length + '</span></summary><div class="w37wbody">';
        wkSteps.forEach(function (t) {
          var s = m[t.id];
          var due = '';
          if (!s.done && s.due_date) {
            due = '<span class="w44due' + (String(s.due_date) < today ? ' late' : '') + '">due ' + esc(String(s.due_date)) + '</span>';
          }
          html += '<div class="w37step' + (t.parent_id ? ' child' : '') + (s.done ? ' done' : '') + '">'
            + '<input type="checkbox" data-w37chk="' + s.id + '"' + (s.done ? ' checked' : '') + (canTick ? '' : ' disabled') + ' />'
            + '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><div class="nm">' + esc(t.name)
            + (t.priority === 'high' ? '<span class="pr">important</span>' : '') + due + '</div>'
            + (t.description ? '<div class="ds">' + wa37Md(t.description) + '</div>' : '')
