            + (t.content_pending ? '<div class="ds" style="opacity:.7">Full instructions are syncing from ClickUp. <a href="' + esc(t.clickup_url || '#') + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open the ClickUp task</a> in the meantime.</div>' : '')
            + (s.done && s.done_by ? '<div class="ds" style="opacity:.6">done by ' + esc(s.done_by) + '</div>' : '')
            + '</div></div>';
        });
        html += '</div></details>';
      });
      return html;
    };
  }

  /* ---- 3. the master editor edits everything ---- */
  function wa44SecSelect(id, current) {
    return '<select id="' + id + '" class="w36sel">' + wa44Sections().map(function (s) {
      return '<option value="' + esc(s) + '"' + (s === current ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(s) + '</option>';
    }).join('') + '</select>';
  }
  if (typeof wa37EditStep === 'function') {
    wa37EditStep = function (tid) {
      var t = WA37.tmpl.filter(function (x) { return x.id === tid; })[0];
      if (!t) return;
      openModal('<h3>Edit this onboarding step</h3>'
        + '<p class="m-sub">Changes flow instantly to everyone, including onboardings already in flight.</p>'
        + '<label>Step name</label><input type="text" id="w44en" value="' + esc(t.name) + '" />'
        + '<label>Instructions (links welcome, markdown works)</label><textarea id="w44ed" rows="6">' + esc(t.description || '') + '</textarea>'
        + '<label>Add a link (appended to the instructions)</label><input type="text" id="w44el" placeholder="https://..." />'
        + '<div style="display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:8px;align-items:end">'
        + '<div><label>Week</label><br/>' + wa44SecSelect('w44ew', t.section) + '</div>'
        + '<div><label>Position</label><br/><input type="number" id="w44eo" style="width:80px" value="' + (t.sort || 0) + '" /></div>'
        + '<div><label>Priority</label><br/><select id="w44ep" class="w36sel"><option value="">Normal</option><option value="high"' + (t.priority === 'high' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Important</option></select></div>'
        + '</div>'
        + '<p class="m-sub" style="margin-top:6px">Position orders steps within the week (smaller numbers first). Subtasks keep their parent.</p>'
        + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w44es">Save</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w44ec">Cancel</button></div>');
      document.getElementById('w44ec').addEventListener('click', function () { wa37Master(); });
      document.getElementById('w44es').addEventListener('click', async function () {
        var nm = (document.getElementById('w44en').value || '').trim();
        if (!nm) { toast('The step needs a name'); return; }
        var desc = (document.getElementById('w44ed').value || '').trim();
        var link = (document.getElementById('w44el').value || '').trim();
        if (link) {
          if (link.indexOf('http') !== 0) link = 'https://' + link;
          if (desc.indexOf(link) < 0) desc = (desc ? desc + '\n\n' : '') + '[Open the link](' + link + ')';
        }
        var srt = parseInt(document.getElementById('w44eo').value, 10);
        var r = await db.from('onboarding_template').update({
          name: nm,
          description: desc || null,
          section: document.getElementById('w44ew').value,
          sort: isNaN(srt) ? (t.sort || 0) : srt,
          priority: document.getElementById('w44ep').value || null,
          content_pending: false,
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
        }).eq('id', tid);
        if (r.error) { toast('Could not save it'); return; }
        await wa37Load(true);
        toast('Saved to the master and every active onboarding');
        wa37Master();
      });
    };
  }
  if (typeof wa37AddStep === 'function') {
    wa37AddStep = function (section) {
      var parents = [];
      try {
        parents = WA37.tmpl.filter(function (t) { return t.section === section && !t.parent_id; });
      } catch (e) { /* none */ }
      openModal('<h3>Add a step to ' + esc(section) + '</h3>'
        + '<p class="m-sub">Everyone still mid-onboarding gets it immediately, unticked.</p>'
        + '<label>Step name</label><input type="text" id="w44an" placeholder="What they need to do" />'
        + '<label>Instructions (optional, markdown works)</label><textarea id="w44ad" rows="5"></textarea>'
        + '<label>Add a link (appended to the instructions)</label><input type="text" id="w44al" placeholder="https://..." />'
        + '<div style="display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:8px;align-items:end">'
        + '<div><label>Week</label><br/>' + wa44SecSelect('w44aw', section) + '</div>'
        + '<div><label>Priority</label><br/><select id="w44app" class="w36sel"><option value="">Normal</option><option value="high">Important</option></select></div>'
        + '<div><label>Sits under</label><br/><select id="w44ap" class="w36sel"><option value="">Top level</option>'
        + parents.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + esc(p.id) + '">' + esc(p.name) + '</option>'; }).join('')
        + '</select></div>'
        + '</div>'
        + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w44as2">Add it</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w44ac">Cancel</button></div>');
      document.getElementById('w44ac').addEventListener('click', function () { wa37Master(); });
      document.getElementById('w44as2').addEventListener('click', async function () {
        var nm = (document.getElementById('w44an').value || '').trim();
        if (!nm) { toast('Give the step a name'); return; }
        var sec = document.getElementById('w44aw').value;
        var desc = (document.getElementById('w44ad').value || '').trim();
        var link = (document.getElementById('w44al').value || '').trim();
        if (link) {
          if (link.indexOf('http') !== 0) link = 'https://' + link;
          desc = (desc ? desc + '\n\n' : '') + '[Open the link](' + link + ')';
        }
        var parent = document.getElementById('w44ap').value || null;
        var maxSort = 0;
        WA37.tmpl.forEach(function (t) { if (t.section === sec && t.sort > maxSort) maxSort = t.sort; });
        var r = await db.from('onboarding_template').insert({
          id: 'wa' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10),
          section: sec, name: nm,
          description: desc || null,
          parent_id: parent,
          priority: document.getElementById('w44app').value || null,
          sort: maxSort + 10, active: true, content_pending: false
        });
        if (r.error) { toast('Could not add it'); return; }
        await wa37Load(true);
        toast('Added to the master and every active onboarding');
        wa37Master();
      });
    };
  }

  /* ---- 1 + 2. personal dashboard and HR dashboard ---- */
  function wa44MyProg() {
    var me = (typeof wa28Me === 'function') ? wa28Me() : null;
    if (!me) return { me: null, rows: [] };
    var rows = (typeof WA36_PROG !== 'undefined' ? WA36_PROG : []).filter(function (p) {
      return String(p.person_name || '').toLowerCase() === me.name.toLowerCase();
    });
    return { me: me, rows: rows };
  }
  async function wa44Compose() {
    try {
      var body = document.getElementById('w36body');
      if (!body || !body.querySelector('.w36onb')) return;
      if (typeof wa37Load === 'function') { try { await wa37Load(false); } catch (e) { /* offline */ } }
      var onbEl = body.querySelector('.w36onb');

      /* personal dashboard */
      var old = body.querySelector('.w44me');
      if (old) old.remove();
      var mp = wa44MyProg();
      var byId = {};
      (typeof WA36_TRAININGS !== 'undefined' ? WA36_TRAININGS : []).forEach(function (t) { byId[String(t.id)] = t; });
      var done = mp.rows.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'done'; });
      var todo = mp.rows.filter(function (p) { return p.status !== 'done'; });
      var mine = null, overdue = 0, onbPct = null;
      try {
        if (mp.me && typeof WA37 !== 'undefined') {
          mine = WA37.onbs.filter(function (o) { return o.status === 'active' && o.person_name.toLowerCase() === mp.me.name.toLowerCase(); })[0];
          if (mine) {
            onbPct = wa37Pct(mine.id).pct;
            var today = wa44Today();
            WA37.steps.forEach(function (s) {
              if (s.onboarding_id === mine.id && !s.done && s.due_date && String(s.due_date) < today) overdue++;
            });
          }
        }
      } catch (e) { /* no onboarding data */ }
      var pct = mp.rows.length ? Math.round(100 * done.length / mp.rows.length) : 0;
      var html = '<div class="w44me"><h3>Your training</h3><div class="w44tiles">'
        + '<div class="w44tile' + (todo.length ? '' : '') + '"><b>' + todo.length + '</b><span>to do</span></div>'
        + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + done.length + '</b><span>done</span></div>'
        + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + mp.rows.length + '</b><span>assigned to you</span></div>'
        + (onbPct !== null ? '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + onbPct + '%</b><span>onboarding</span></div>' : '')
        + (overdue ? '<div class="w44tile warn"><b>' + overdue + '</b><span>onboarding steps overdue</span></div>' : '')
        + '</div>';
      if (mp.rows.length) {
        html += '<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin:0 0 8px"><span class="w44bar"><i style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></span>'
          + '<span style="font-size:12px;color:#797773;font-weight:600">' + pct + '% of your trainings done</span></div>';
      }
      if (todo.length) {
        html += todo.slice(0, 12).map(function (p) {
          var t = byId[String(p.training_id)];
          if (!t) return '';
          return '<div class="w44row"><span class="t">' + esc(t.title) + '</span>'
            + (t.resource_url ? '<a class="go" href="' + esc(t.resource_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open &raquo;</a>' : '')
            + '<a data-w36me="' + t.id + '" data-st="todo" data-w44mark="1">Mark done</a></div>';
        }).join('');
        if (todo.length > 12) html += '<p class="m-sub">And ' + (todo.length - 12) + ' more below in the catalog.</p>';
      } else if (mp.rows.length) {
        html += '<p class="m-sub" style="margin:0">All caught up. Nice.</p>';
      } else {
        html += '<p class="m-sub" style="margin:0">Nothing assigned to you yet. Browse the catalog below, or ask your lead.</p>';
      }
      html += '</div>';
      onbEl.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', html);

      /* HR dashboard */
      var oldHr = body.querySelector('.w44hr');
      var rescued = null;
      if (oldHr) {
        var ob = oldHr.querySelector('.w44hrblock');
        if (ob && ob.firstElementChild) { rescued = ob.firstElementChild; rescued.remove(); }
        oldHr.remove();
      }
      if (wa44CanEdit()) {
        var hr = document.createElement('div');
        hr.className = 'w44hr';
        hr.innerHTML = '<h3>HR dashboard</h3><div class="w44hrblock"></div><div class="w44hrtrain"></div>';
        onbEl.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', hr);
        /* move the onboarding board (assign select, master editor, live rows) in here;
           a fresh render inside .w36onb wins, else keep the block moved last time */
        var assign = body.querySelector('.w36onb #w37assign');
        var block = assign ? (assign.parentElement && assign.parentElement.parentElement) : rescued;
        if (block) hr.querySelector('.w44hrblock').appendChild(block);
        /* per-person training table */
        var people = (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []).slice();
        var byPerson = {};
        (typeof WA36_PROG !== 'undefined' ? WA36_PROG : []).forEach(function (p) {
          var k = String(p.person_name || '').toLowerCase();
          byPerson[k] = byPerson[k] || { a: 0, d: 0 };
          byPerson[k].a++;
          if (p.status === 'done') byPerson[k].d++;
        });
        people.sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
        var rows = people.map(function (p) {
          var s = byPerson[String(p.name).toLowerCase()] || { a: 0, d: 0 };
          return '<tr><td><b>' + esc(p.name) + '</b> <span style="color:#797773;font-size:11px">' + esc(p.department || '') + '</span></td>'
            + '<td>' + s.a + '</td><td>' + s.d + '</td><td>' + (s.a - s.d) + '</td>'
            + '<td><a data-w44as="' + esc(p.name) + '">Assign trainings</a></td></tr>';
        }).join('');
        hr.querySelector('.w44hrtrain').innerHTML = '<h3 style="margin-top:12px">Training by person</h3>'
          + '<div class="w44wrap"><table class="w44tbl"><thead><tr><th>Person</th><th>Assigned</th><th>Done</th><th>To do</th><th></th></tr></thead>'
          + '<tbody>' + rows + '</tbody></table></div>';
      }

      /* catalog heading */
      if (!body.querySelector('.w44cat')) {
        var firstCat = body.querySelector('.w36cat');
        if (firstCat) firstCat.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', '<div class="w44cat">The full catalog</div>');
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  function wa44AssignModal(name) {
    var rows = (typeof WA36_PROG !== 'undefined' ? WA36_PROG : []).filter(function (p) {
      return String(p.person_name || '').toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase();
    });
    var have = {};
    rows.forEach(function (p) { have[String(p.training_id)] = p.status; });
    var cats = {};
    (typeof WA36_TRAININGS !== 'undefined' ? WA36_TRAININGS : []).forEach(function (t) {
      (cats[t.category] = cats[t.category] || []).push(t);
    });
    var html = '<h3>Trainings for ' + esc(name) + '</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">Tick to assign, untick to remove. Completed trainings stay on their record.</p>'
      + '<div style="max-height:320px;overflow:auto">';
    Object.keys(cats).forEach(function (c) {
      html += '<div style="font-weight:700;font-size:12px;color:#103F39;margin:8px 0 2px">' + esc(c) + '</div>';
      cats[c].forEach(function (t) {
        var stt = have[String(t.id)];
        html += '<label style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;padding:3px 0;font-size:13px">'
          + '<input type="checkbox" class="w44cb" value="' + t.id + '"' + (stt ? ' checked' : '') + (stt === 'done' ? ' disabled' : '') + ' style="width:15px;height:15px" /> '
          + esc(t.title) + (stt === 'done' ? ' <span class="w36pill done">done</span>' : '') + '</label>';
      });
    });
    html += '</div><div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w44asv">Save</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w44acx">Cancel</button></div>';
    openModal(html);
    document.getElementById('w44acx').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w44asv').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var picked = {};
      Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.w44cb:checked')).forEach(function (c) { picked[c.value] = 1; });
      var adds = [], removals = [];
      Object.keys(picked).forEach(function (tid) { if (!have[tid]) adds.push(Number(tid)); });
      Object.keys(have).forEach(function (tid) { if (!picked[tid] && have[tid] !== 'done') removals.push(Number(tid)); });
      var by = (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'HR';
      var ok = true;
      if (adds.length) {
        var r1 = await db.from('training_progress').upsert(adds.map(function (tid) {
          return { training_id: tid, person_name: name, status: 'assigned', assigned_by: by, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
        }), { onConflict: 'training_id,person_name' });
        if (r1.error) ok = false;
      }
      for (var i = 0; i < removals.length; i++) {
        var r2 = await db.from('training_progress').delete().eq('training_id', removals[i]).eq('person_name', name).neq('status', 'done');
        if (r2.error) ok = false;
      }
      if (!ok) { toast('Some changes did not save'); return; }
      closeModal();
      try { await wa36Load(true); wa36Render(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      toast('Saved: ' + adds.length + ' assigned, ' + removals.length + ' removed');
    });
  }

  /* ---- wiring ---- */
  if (typeof wa36Render === 'function') {
    var __r44 = wa36Render;
    wa36Render = async function () {
      await __r44.apply(this, arguments);
      await wa44Compose();
    };
  }
  if (typeof wa37Mount === 'function') {
    var __m44 = wa37Mount;
    wa37Mount = async function () {
      await __m44.apply(this, arguments);
      await wa44Compose();
    };
  }
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa44Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa44Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w44as]');
      if (a && wa44CanEdit()) wa44AssignModal(a.getAttribute('data-w44as'));
    });
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v45 the onboarding SOP doc is HR-only
   (02/08/26)
   The Google Doc "Onboarding SOP" linked from the Training page is an
   HR document, not something regular members should be sent to. The
   link disappears from the member-facing onboarding intro (the
   sentences are reworded, not left broken) and moves into the HR
   dashboard next to the master editor, admins and leaders only.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa45) return;
  window.__wa45 = 1;
  var DOC = '1OWeVlCHCSLPMCDD7DbfpO_OidcuBvPRvRNE5VueqWpg';
  var DOC_URL = 'https://docs.google.com/document/d/1OWeVlCHCSLPMCDD7DbfpO_OidcuBvPRvRNE5VueqWpg/edit';

  function wa45IsHr() {
    try {
      if (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) {
        return WA40_MINE.role === 'admin' || WA40_MINE.role === 'leader';
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fall through */ }
    return false;
  }

  function wa45Scrub() {
    try {
      var panel = document.getElementById('training');
      if (!panel) return;
      /* reword the intro sentences that carried the link */
      panel.querySelectorAll('.w36onb p').forEach(function (p) {
        if (p.innerHTML.indexOf(DOC) < 0) return;
        var h = p.innerHTML;
        h = h.replace(/\s*The <a[^>]*>Onboarding SOP<\/a> is the companion doc\./, '');
        h = h.replace(/Follow the <a[^>]*>Onboarding SOP<\/a>, then work/, 'Work');
        p.innerHTML = h;
      });
      /* remove any leftover anchors to the doc outside the HR dashboard */
      panel.querySelectorAll('a[href*="' + DOC + '"]').forEach(function (a) {
        if (a.closest && a.closest('.w44hr')) return;
        if (a.classList.contains('w45sop')) return;
        a.remove();
      });
      /* give HR the link where their tools live */
      if (wa45IsHr()) {
        var hr = panel.querySelector('.w44hr');
        if (hr && !hr.querySelector('.w45sop')) {
          var master = hr.querySelector('[data-w37master]');
          var a = document.createElement('a');
          a.className = 'w37edit w45sop';
          a.href = DOC_URL;
          a.target = '_blank';
          a.rel = 'noopener';
          a.textContent = 'Open the HR onboarding SOP (doc)';
          if (master && master.parentElement) master.parentElement.appendChild(a);
          else hr.querySelector('.w44hrblock').appendChild(a);
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  if (typeof wa36Render === 'function') {
    var __r45 = wa36Render;
    wa36Render = async function () {
      await __r45.apply(this, arguments);
      wa45Scrub();
    };
  }
  if (typeof wa37Mount === 'function') {
    var __m45 = wa37Mount;
    wa37Mount = async function () {
      await __m45.apply(this, arguments);
      wa45Scrub();
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v46 the HR centre
   (02/08/26)
   HR gets its own page; Training goes back to being personal.
   1. Training page now shows ONLY your own training: your dashboard,
      your onboarding checklist, the catalog. Every HR tool leaves it.
   2. New page: HR centre (People menu, leaders and admins).
      - Onboarding board: tiles (active, completed, running late, avg
        progress), assign an onboarding, live rows with pace, help
        flags, HR-setup progress, open-checklist, remove, and a
        Welcome kit per person.
      - Master onboarding editor, inline, SOP-style: weeks as
        dropdowns, every step row with edit / add subtask / retire pills,
        add a step per week. Uses the rich v44 editors underneath.
      - Trainings: org tiles, the training-by-person table with
        Assign trainings, and catalog editing (add and edit moved
        here from the Training page).
      - Recruitment tracker: the hiring-request pipeline with days in
        stage and total days per position (stage history from the
        change log), average days to hire, plus the member pipeline
        (onboarding-request). Stage changes made here are logged so
        the timing data keeps building.
   3. Welcome kit automation: assigning an onboarding opens a kit with
      the onboarding email, the Slack channel message, and the
      create-onboarding checklist from the HR SOP doc, name and dates
      prefilled, with copy buttons.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa46) return;
  window.__wa46 = 1;

  var SOP_DOC = 'https://docs.google.com/document/d/1OWeVlCHCSLPMCDD7DbfpO_OidcuBvPRvRNE5VueqWpg/edit';

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa46css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w46sec{background:#F4F2EA;border-radius:16px;padding:14px 16px;margin:0 0 14px;}' +
    '.w46sec > h3{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:12px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.7px;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w46wk{background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:12px;margin:6px 0;padding:2px 12px;}' +
    '.w46wk > summary{list-style:none;cursor:pointer;display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;padding:9px 2px;font-weight:700;font-size:13px;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w46wk > summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none;}' +
    '.w46wk > summary .chev{margin-left:auto;transition:transform .25s;color:#8a958f;font-size:12px;}' +
    '.w46wk[open] > summary .chev{transform:rotate(180deg);}' +
    '.w46st{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;padding:5px 0;border-top:1px solid #F4F2EA;font-size:12.8px;}' +
    '.w46st.sub{margin-left:22px;font-size:12.3px;color:#1F201E;}' +
    '.w46st .nm{flex:1;min-width:0;}' +
    '.w46st .pr{font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:#b3402f;border:1px solid #b3402f;border-radius:999px;padding:0 7px;margin-left:6px;}' +
    '[data-w46kit],[data-w46sub],[data-w46addtr]{display:inline-block;background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:999px;padding:3px 11px;' +
    'font-size:11.5px;color:#103F39 !important;text-decoration:none !important;cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;line-height:1.5;margin:1px 2px;}' +
    '[data-w46kit]:hover,[data-w46sub]:hover,[data-w46addtr]:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.4);}' +
    '.w46row{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;padding:7px 0;border-top:1px solid #e8edea;font-size:13px;flex-wrap:wrap;}' +
    '.w46row:first-of-type{border-top:none;}' +
    '.w46stage{font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;border-radius:999px;padding:2px 10px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w46days{font-size:11.5px;color:#797773;}' +
    '.w46days.late{color:#b3402f;font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w46kittxt{width:100%;min-height:110px;font-size:12px;font-family:inherit;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:10px;padding:8px;}' +
    '.w46tl{display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:10.5px;color:#797773;margin-top:2px;}' +
    '.w46tl span{background:#ECEBE3;border-radius:999px;padding:1px 8px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  function wa46IsHr() {
    try {
      if (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) {
        /* wave169: and HR's own access profile, as that profile says ("Use the HR centre: onboarding plans, hours, recruitment and policies") */
        return WA40_MINE.role === 'admin' || WA40_MINE.role === 'leader' || (WA40_MINE.profile === 'hr' && WA40_MINE.role !== 'blocked' && WA40_MINE.role !== 'viewer');
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fall through */ }
    return false;
  }
  window.wa46IsHr = wa46IsHr; /* audit 26/09/26: wave116p's canHr() asks this */
  function wa46Days(a, b) {
    try { return Math.max(0, Math.round((new Date(b || Date.now()) - new Date(a)) / 86400000)); } catch (e) { return 0; }
  }
  function wa46Plus7() {
    var d = new Date(Date.now() + 7 * 86400000);
    return d.toDateString();
  }

  /* ---- 1. the Training page becomes personal only ---- */
  function wa46Scrub() {
    try {
      var p = document.getElementById('training');
      if (!p) return;
      var hr = p.querySelector('.w44hr');
      if (hr) hr.remove();
      p.querySelectorAll('[data-w43ta]').forEach(function (el) {
        var wrap = el.parentElement;
        if (wrap && wrap.children.length === 1) wrap.remove(); else el.remove();
      });
      p.querySelectorAll('[data-w43te]').forEach(function (el) { el.remove(); });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  if (typeof wa36Render === 'function') {
    var __r46 = wa36Render;
    wa36Render = async function () {
      await __r46.apply(this, arguments);
      wa46Scrub();
    };
  }
  if (typeof wa37Mount === 'function') {
    var __m46 = wa37Mount;
    wa37Mount = async function () {
      await __m46.apply(this, arguments);
      wa46Scrub();
    };
  }

  /* ---- welcome kit templates (from the HR SOP doc) ---- */
  function wa46EmailBody(first) {
    return 'Dear ' + first + ',\n\n' +
      'Congratulations and welcome to the team at Welfare Avenue!\n\n' +
      'We are excited you have accepted the [job title] within our organization. You will be starting [start date], and we cannot wait to have you on board. To get started, please take the following steps:\n\n' +
      '1. Confirm your availability for two meetings: a 1-on-1 introductory meeting with the manager you will report to, [manager name and position], and your in-person group onboarding session with [manager name] at [date]. This step is crucial.\n' +
      '2. Activate your Welfare Avenue Google account. Watch the setup video: https://www.loom.com/share/522090b198054eb4aab13e0365efadf6\n' +
      '   Email: [new WA email]\n   Your temporary password comes to you separately; you will be asked to change it when you first sign in.\n' +
      '3. Join the Slack workspace using the invitation sent to your new Welfare Avenue Gmail.\n' +
      '4. Open the Hub (https://hub.welfareavenue.com) and sign in with your Welfare Avenue Google account: your onboarding plan is in the Avenue Academy.\n' +
      '5. Sign the contract, sent via DocuSign to your personal email.\n' +
      '6. Review your job description and prepare questions to ask your director.\n\n' +
      'If you run into any technical issues or have any questions, do not hesitate to reach out. We are here to make your onboarding experience smooth and welcoming.\n\n' +
      'Looking forward to seeing all the great work you will do with us!\n\n[your signature]';
  }
  function wa46SlackMsg(first) {
    return 'Hi ' + first + '!\n\n' +
      'This channel is solely for your onboarding journey over the next month. I will be here to assist you with anything that you need during this process. Please let us know if you encounter any problems during your onboarding tasks; we will help as quickly as we can.\n\n' +
      '[Their direct manager] and I will be your direct managers, so we will be completing your 30-day eval together.\n\n' +
      'Week 1 will need to be completed by ' + wa46Plus7() + '. If you need more time, please let us know.\n\n' +
      'Good luck! We are here by your side.';
  }
  function wa46Kit(name) {
    var first = String(name || '').split(' ')[0];
    var subj = 'Welcome to Welfare Avenue! Your Next Steps';
    var body = wa46EmailBody(first);
    openModal('<h3>Welcome kit for ' + esc(name) + '</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">Everything from the HR SOP for a new assignment: send the email once they have accepted, set up their accounts, then post the channel message. <a href="' + SOP_DOC + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:#103F39">Open the full HR SOP</a>.</p>'
      + '<details open><summary style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer">1. Onboarding email</summary>'
      + '<p class="m-sub" style="margin:4px 0">Subject: ' + esc(subj) + '</p>'
      + '<textarea class="w46kittxt" id="w46em">' + esc(body) + '</textarea>'
      + '<div style="margin:6px 0"><button class="btn ghost mini" data-w46copy="w46em">Copy the email</button> '
      + '<a class="btn ghost mini" style="text-decoration:none" href="mailto:?subject=' + encodeURIComponent(subj) + '&body=' + encodeURIComponent(body) + '">Open in your mail app</a></div></details>'
      + '<details><summary style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer">2. Set up their accounts</summary>'
      + '<ul style="font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.7;margin:6px 0 4px 18px;padding:0">'
      + '<li>Create their Welfare Avenue Gmail and share the first password with them one to one</li>'
      + '<li>Send the Slack invitation to their WA Gmail</li>'
      + '<li>Create their onboarding channel: #onboarding-' + esc(first.toLowerCase()) + '</li>'
      + '<li>Post the channel message below (their plan is already in the Hub, in the Avenue Academy)</li>'
      + '<li>On the onboarding call: follow the call checklist in the <a href="' + SOP_DOC + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:#103F39">HR SOP doc</a> (Loom recording, bookmarks, Slack groups, Loom and Canva invites, drives, weekly meeting, and a first look at the Hub and their plan in the Avenue Academy)</li>'
      + '</ul></details>'
      + '<details><summary style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer">3. Onboarding channel message</summary>'
      + '<textarea class="w46kittxt" id="w46sm">' + esc(wa46SlackMsg(first)) + '</textarea>'
      + '<div style="margin:6px 0"><button class="btn ghost mini" data-w46copy="w46sm">Copy the message</button></div></details>'
      + '<details><summary style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer">4. Their lead\'s moments</summary>'
      + '<p class="m-sub" style="margin:4px 0">Their lead (who they report to in the org chart) sees them on Home in the Hub from today: their progress, what is late, their help requests, and these moments with their dates, ticked there.</p>'
      + '<ul style="font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.7;margin:6px 0 4px 18px;padding:0">'
      + '<li>Introduce them at the team\'s meeting (first week)</li>'
      + '<li>Give them their first tasks in a meeting (first week)</li>'
      + '<li>Check in at two weeks</li>'
      + '<li>Review their quizzes and share the correct answers (around day 30)</li>'
      + '<li>The 30-day evaluation, with HR</li>'
      + '<li>Probation and evaluation complete (four months)</li>'
      + '</ul></details>'
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w46kc">Done</button></div>');
    var c = document.getElementById('w46kc');
    if (c) c.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
  }

  /* ---- data ---- */
  var WA46 = { reqs: [], log: [], types: {}, loaded: false };
  window.WA46 = WA46; /* audit 26/09/26: waves 116o and 116p read it (request types, open hiring requests) */
  async function wa46Load(force) {
    if (WA46.loaded && !force) return;
    try {
      var r1 = await db.from('dept_requests').select('*').in('type_key', ['hiring-request', 'onboarding-request']).order('created_at');
      WA46.reqs = (r1 && r1.data) || [];
      var r2 = await db.from('change_log').select('entity_id,old_value,new_value,changed_at').eq('entity_type', 'request').eq('field', 'status').order('changed_at');
      WA46.log = (r2 && r2.data) || [];
      var r3 = await db.from('request_types').select('key,label,statuses').in('key', ['hiring-request', 'onboarding-request']);
      ((r3 && r3.data) || []).forEach(function (t) { WA46.types[t.key] = t; });
      WA46.loaded = true;
    } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
  }
  function wa46Trans(id) {
    return WA46.log.filter(function (l) { return String(l.entity_id) === String(id); });
  }

  /* ---- section builders ---- */
  function wa46OnbSection() {
    var onbs = (typeof WA37 !== 'undefined' ? WA37.onbs : []).filter(function (o) { return o.status !== 'cancelled'; });
    var act = onbs.filter(function (o) { return o.status === 'active'; });
    var done = onbs.filter(function (o) { return o.status === 'completed'; });
    var late = 0, pctSum = 0;
    act.forEach(function (o) {
      var p = wa37Pct(o.id).pct;
      pctSum += p;
      var expected = Math.min(100, Math.round(wa46Days(o.started_at) / 90 * 100));   /* wave279: the plan runs 90 days */
      if (p < expected - 15) late++;
    });
    var people = (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []);
    var activeNames = act.map(function (o) { return o.person_name.toLowerCase(); });
    var html = '<div class="w46sec"><h3>Onboarding</h3><div class="w44tiles">'
      + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + act.length + '</b><span>mid-onboarding</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + done.length + '</b><span>completed</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w44tile' + (late ? ' warn' : '') + '"><b>' + late + '</b><span>running late</span></div>'
      + (act.length ? '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + Math.round(pctSum / act.length) + '%</b><span>avg progress</span></div>' : '')
      + '</div>'
      + '<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:2px 0 8px">'
      + '<select class="w36sel" data-w46assign="1"><option value="">Assign an onboarding to...</option>'
      + people.filter(function (pp) { return activeNames.indexOf(String(pp.name).toLowerCase()) < 0; })
        .map(function (pp) { return '<option value="' + esc(String(pp.id)) + '" data-name="' + esc(pp.name) + '">' + esc(pp.name) + ' (' + esc(pp.department || '') + ')</option>'; }).join('')
      + '</select>'
      + '<a class="w37edit" href="' + SOP_DOC + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open the HR onboarding SOP (doc)</a></div>';
    if (!act.length) html += '<p class="m-sub">Nobody is mid-onboarding right now.</p>';
    act.forEach(function (o) {
      var p = wa37Pct(o.id);
      var expected = Math.min(100, Math.round(wa46Days(o.started_at) / 90 * 100));   /* wave279: the plan runs 90 days */
      var isLate = p.pct < expected - 15;
      var helpN = (typeof WA39_HELP !== 'undefined' ? WA39_HELP : []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.onboarding_id) === String(o.id); }).length;
      var hrMine = (typeof WA39_HRS !== 'undefined' ? WA39_HRS : []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.onboarding_id) === String(o.id); });
      var hrDone = hrMine.filter(function (x) { return x.done; }).length;
      html += '<div class="w46row"><b>' + esc(o.person_name) + '</b>'
        + '<span class="w37bar" style="max-width:150px"><i style="width:' + p.pct + '%"></i></span>'
        + '<span style="font-size:12px;color:#103F39;font-weight:700">' + p.pct + '%</span>'
        + '<span class="w46days' + (isLate ? ' late' : '') + '">day ' + wa46Days(o.started_at) + (isLate ? ' &middot; behind pace' : '') + '</span>'
        + '<span class="w46days" data-w279acad="' + o.id + '">' + esc(window.wa279Acad ? window.wa279Acad.text(o.id) : '') + '</span>'   /* wave279: Academy trainings d of t (parts279/b_hooks.js) */
        + (helpN ? '<span class="w46stage" style="background:#b3402f;color:#FFFAF2">' + helpN + ' asking for help</span>' : '')
        + '<a class="w37edit" data-w37open="' + o.id + '">checklist</a>'
        + (hrMine.length ? '<a class="w37edit" data-w39hr="' + o.id + '">HR setup ' + hrDone + ' of ' + hrMine.length + '</a>' : '')
        + '<a data-w46kit="' + esc(o.person_name) + '">Welcome kit</a>'
        + '<a class="w37edit" style="color:#b3402f !important" data-w39del="' + o.id + '">remove</a>'
        + '</div>';
    });
    html += '</div>';
    return html;
  }

  function wa46MasterHtml() {
    var html = '<div class="w46sec"><h3>Master onboarding</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub" style="margin:0 0 6px">Changes flow to everyone still mid-onboarding, and to what they see in the Avenue Academy. Edit a step for its name, instructions, link, where it is done in the Avenue Academy, week, position and priority. Retiring removes it from active onboardings.</p><div id="w46master">';
    html += wa46MasterInner();
    html += '</div></div>';
    return html;
  }
  function wa46MasterInner() {
    if (typeof wa37Weeks !== 'function') return '';
    var html = '';
    wa37Weeks().forEach(function (wk) {
      html += '<details class="w46wk"><summary>' + esc(wk.name)
        + '<span style="font-size:11px;color:#797773;font-weight:600">' + wk.steps.length + ' steps</span>'
        + '<a class="w37edit" data-w37add="' + esc(wk.name) + '" style="margin-left:8px">+ add a step</a>'
        + '<span class="chev">&#9662;</span></summary>';
      wk.steps.forEach(function (t) {
        html += '<div class="w46st' + (t.parent_id ? ' sub' : '') + '"><span class="nm">' + esc(t.name)
          + (t.priority === 'high' ? '<span class="pr">important</span>' : '') + '</span>'
          + '<a class="w37edit" data-w37e="' + esc(t.id) + '">edit</a>'
          + (!t.parent_id ? '<a data-w46sub="' + esc(t.id) + '">+ subtask</a>' : '')
          + '<a class="w37edit" style="color:#b3402f !important" data-w37x="' + esc(t.id) + '">retire</a>'
          + '</div>';
      });
      html += '</details>';
    });
    return html;
  }

  function wa46TrainSection() {
    var trainings = (typeof WA36_TRAININGS !== 'undefined' ? WA36_TRAININGS : []);
    var prog = (typeof WA36_PROG !== 'undefined' ? WA36_PROG : []);
    var doneN = prog.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'done'; }).length;
    var peopleCovered = {};
    prog.forEach(function (p) { peopleCovered[String(p.person_name).toLowerCase()] = 1; });
    var byPerson = {};
    prog.forEach(function (p) {
      var k = String(p.person_name || '').toLowerCase();
      byPerson[k] = byPerson[k] || { a: 0, d: 0 };
      byPerson[k].a++;
      if (p.status === 'done') byPerson[k].d++;
    });
    var people = (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []).slice();
    people.sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    var html = '<div class="w46sec"><h3>Trainings across the team</h3><div class="w44tiles">'   /* wave169: a title starting "Trainings", which wave47's Training view looks for (it showed under Onboarding); not the bare "Trainings" that would bring back wave67's whole catalog here (the Academy is the catalog) */
      + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + trainings.length + '</b><span>in the catalog</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + Object.keys(peopleCovered).length + '</b><span>people with assignments</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + (prog.length ? Math.round(100 * doneN / prog.length) : 0) + '%</b><span>org completion</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + (prog.length - doneN) + '</b><span>outstanding</span></div>'
      + '</div>'
      + '<div style="margin:0 0 8px"><a data-w43ta="1" data-w46addtr="1">+ Add a training</a></div>'
      + '<div class="w44wrap"><table class="w44tbl"><thead><tr><th>Person</th><th>Assigned</th><th>Done</th><th>To do</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>'
      + people.map(function (p) {
        var s = byPerson[String(p.name).toLowerCase()] || { a: 0, d: 0 };
        return '<tr><td><b>' + esc(p.name) + '</b> <span style="color:#797773;font-size:11px">' + esc(p.department || '') + '</span></td>'
          + '<td>' + s.a + '</td><td>' + s.d + '</td><td>' + (s.a - s.d) + '</td>'
          + '<td><a data-w44as="' + esc(p.name) + '">Assign trainings</a></td></tr>';
      }).join('')
      + '</tbody></table></div>'
      + '<details style="margin-top:8px"><summary style="cursor:pointer;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39">Edit the catalog (' + trainings.length + ')</summary><div style="max-height:260px;overflow:auto;margin-top:4px">'
      + trainings.map(function (t) {
        return '<div class="w46row"><span style="flex:1;font-size:12.5px"><b>' + esc(t.title) + '</b> <span style="color:#797773;font-size:11px">' + esc(t.category || '') + '</span></span>'
          + '<a data-w43te="' + t.id + '">Edit</a></div>';
      }).join('')
      + '</div></details></div>';
    return html;
  }

  function wa46Pipeline(typeKey, title) {
    var t = WA46.types[typeKey] || { statuses: [] };
    var reqs = WA46.reqs.filter(function (r) { return r.type_key === typeKey; });
    var open = reqs.filter(function (r) { return !r.done; });
    var html = '<h3 style="margin-top:12px">' + esc(title) + '</h3>';
    if (!reqs.length) return html + '<p class="m-sub">Nothing here yet. These arrive through the Requests page.</p>';
    reqs.slice().reverse().forEach(function (r) {
      var tr = wa46Trans(r.id);
      var lastChange = tr.length ? tr[tr.length - 1].changed_at : r.created_at;
      var inStage = wa46Days(lastChange);
      var total = wa46Days(r.created_at, r.done ? r.done_at : null);
      /* per-stage durations */
      var tl = '';
      if (tr.length) {
        var prevAt = r.created_at, prevStage = tr[0].old_value;
        tr.forEach(function (x) {
          tl += '<span>' + esc(prevStage || '') + ': ' + wa46Days(prevAt, x.changed_at) + 'd</span>';
          prevAt = x.changed_at; prevStage = x.new_value;
        });
        tl += '<span>' + esc(prevStage || '') + ': ' + wa46Days(prevAt) + 'd and counting</span>';
      }
      html += '<div class="w46row"><b style="flex:1;min-width:140px">' + esc(r.title) + '</b>'
        + '<select class="w36sel" data-w46st="' + r.id + '">'
        + (t.statuses || []).map(function (s) { return '<option value="' + esc(s) + '"' + (s === r.status ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(s) + '</option>'; }).join('')
        + '</select>'
        + '<span class="w46days' + (inStage > 14 && !r.done ? ' late' : '') + '">' + inStage + 'd in stage</span>'
        + '<span class="w46days">' + total + 'd total</span>'
        + (r.done ? '<span class="w46stage">closed</span>' : '')
        + (tl ? '<div class="w46tl" style="flex-basis:100%">' + tl + '</div>' : '')
        + '</div>';
    });
    return html + '<p class="m-sub" style="margin-top:4px">' + open.length + ' open. New positions come in as requests (Requests page, "' + esc((WA46.types[typeKey] || {}).label || typeKey) + '").</p>';
  }

  function wa46RecruitSection() {
    var hires = WA46.reqs.filter(function (r) { return r.type_key === 'hiring-request'; });
    var open = hires.filter(function (r) { return !r.done; });
    /* time to hire: created -> first transition into offer accepted or official member */
    var times = [];
    hires.forEach(function (r) {
      var hit = wa46Trans(r.id).filter(function (x) { return x.new_value === 'offer accepted' || x.new_value === 'official member'; })[0];
      if (hit) times.push(wa46Days(r.created_at, hit.changed_at));
    });
    var avg = times.length ? Math.round(times.reduce(function (a, b) { return a + b; }, 0) / times.length) : null;
    var longest = 0;
    open.forEach(function (r) { var d = wa46Days(r.created_at); if (d > longest) longest = d; });
    var interviewing = open.filter(function (r) { return r.status === 'interviewing'; }).length;
    var html = '<div class="w46sec"><h3>Recruitment</h3><div class="w44tiles">'
      + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + open.length + '</b><span>open positions</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + interviewing + '</b><span>interviewing</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + (avg === null ? '-' : avg + 'd') + '</b><span>avg days to hire</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w44tile' + (longest > 30 ? ' warn' : '') + '"><b>' + longest + 'd</b><span>longest open</span></div>'
      + '</div>'
      + wa46Pipeline('hiring-request', 'Positions (hiring requests)')
      + wa46Pipeline('onboarding-request', 'New member pipeline (onboarding requests)')
      + '</div>';
    return html;
  }

  /* ---- the page ---- */
  async function wa46Render() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!panel) return;
    if (!wa46IsHr()) { panel.innerHTML = '<div class="card"><h2>HR centre</h2><p class="read">This page is for the HR team, and for admins and leaders.</p></div>'; return; }
    panel.innerHTML = '<div class="card"><h2>HR centre <button class="w43help" data-w46help="1">&#63; How this page works</button></h2>'
      + '<p class="read">Onboarding, trainings and recruitment for the whole organization. Personal training lives in the Avenue Academy.</p>'
      + '<div id="w46body">Loading...</div></div>';
    try {
      await Promise.all([
        (typeof wa37Load === 'function' ? wa37Load(false) : null),
        (typeof wa36Load === 'function' ? wa36Load(false) : null),
        (typeof wa39OnbLoad === 'function' ? wa39OnbLoad() : null),
        wa46Load(true)
      ]);
    } catch (e) { /* partial data is fine */ }
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body');
    if (!body) return;
    body.innerHTML = wa46OnbSection() + wa46MasterHtml() + wa46TrainSection() + wa46RecruitSection();
  }

  /* the master modal becomes a page refresh */
  if (typeof wa37Master === 'function') {
    wa37Master = function () {
      try { closeModal(); } catch (e) { /* none open */ }
      var host = document.getElementById('w46master');
      if (host) host.innerHTML = wa46MasterInner();
    };
  }

  /* ---- wiring ---- */
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa46Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa46Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var k = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w46kit]');
      if (k) { wa46Kit(k.getAttribute('data-w46kit')); return; }
      var sb = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w46sub]');
      if (sb) {
        var tid = sb.getAttribute('data-w46sub');
        var t = (typeof WA37 !== 'undefined' ? WA37.tmpl : []).filter(function (x) { return x.id === tid; })[0];
        if (t && typeof wa37AddStep === 'function') {
          wa37AddStep(t.section);
          setTimeout(function () {
            var sel = document.getElementById('w44ap');
            if (sel) sel.value = tid;
          }, 60);
        }
        return;
      }
      var cp = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w46copy]');
      if (cp) {
        var ta = document.getElementById(cp.getAttribute('data-w46copy'));
        if (ta) {
          ta.select();
          try { document.execCommand('copy'); toast('Copied'); } catch (e2) { toast('Select and copy manually'); }
        }
        return;
      }
      var h = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w46help]');
      if (h) {
        openModal('<h3>HR centre</h3><div class="w43hm">'
          + '<p>The organization view of people operations. Regular members never see this page; their own training and onboarding live in the Avenue Academy.</p>'
          + '<p>Onboarding: who is mid-onboarding, their progress and pace, who is asking for help. Assign an onboarding from the dropdown; the Welcome kit opens with the onboarding email, channel message and setup checklist prefilled.</p>'
          + '<p>Master onboarding: the program itself, and what people see in the Avenue Academy. Each week shows what people see for it (its title, photo, goals, why it matters and the lesson that opens it): Edit what people see changes it. Edit any step (name, instructions, link, where it is done in the Avenue Academy, week, position, priority), add steps and subtasks, retire what no longer fits. Changes reach everyone mid-onboarding instantly. Preview what a new person sees shows the journey as it looks on day one; Journey, next to each person on the board, shows theirs.</p>'
          + '<p>Trainings: Assign a training gives a module or a Learning Avenue to everyone, a team or a few people, with a due date and a note; it shows at the top of their Avenue Academy, and people who join later get it too. The Training view lists every assignment with who is done, late or not started. Catalog editing and per-person assignment are there too.</p>'
          + '<p>Recruitment: every hiring request with its stage, days in stage and total days open, plus average days to hire. Change stages here; every change is logged, which is what powers the timing data.</p>'
          + '</div><div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w46hc">Got it</button></div>');
        var c = document.getElementById('w46hc');
        if (c) c.addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
        return;
      }
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('change', async function (e) {
      var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w46assign]');
      if (a && a.value) {
        if (!wa46IsHr()) return;
        var opt = a.options[a.selectedIndex];
        var nm = opt ? opt.getAttribute('data-name') : '';
        var r = await db.rpc('onboarding_assign', { p_member_id: Number(a.value), p_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'HR' });
        if (r.error) { toast('Could not assign it'); return; }
        toast('Onboarding assigned' + (nm ? ' to ' + nm : ''));
        try { await wa37Load(true); } catch (e2) { /* non-fatal */ }
        wa46Render();
        if (nm) wa46Kit(nm);
        return;
      }
      var s = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w46st]');
      if (s) {
        if (!wa46IsHr()) return;
        var id = Number(s.getAttribute('data-w46st'));
        var req = WA46.reqs.filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0];
        if (!req || req.status === s.value) return;
        var old = req.status;
        var r1 = await db.from('dept_requests').update({ status: s.value, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'HR' }).eq('id', id);
        if (r1.error) { toast('Could not change the stage'); return; }
        await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'request', entity_id: String(id), field: 'status', old_value: old, new_value: s.value, changed_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'HR' });
        toast('Stage: ' + s.value);
        await wa46Load(true);
        wa46Render();
        return;
      }
    });
  }

  /* ---- register the page after the nav is rebuilt ---- */
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa46 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa46();
      try {
        if (window.WA_NAV && typeof WA_NAV.register === 'function') {
          WA_NAV.register({ id: 'hrcentre', label: 'HR centre', group: 'people', dot: '#b3402f', gate: 'leader', onShow: function () { wa46Render(); } });
          if ((location.hash || '') === '#hrcentre') { try { applyHash(); } catch (e) { /* boot */ } }
        }
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v47 HR views, recruitment depth, SOP roles, agenda groups
   (02/08/26)
   1. The HR centre splits into three toggled dashboards: Onboarding,
      Trainings, Recruitment. Deep links: #hrcentre/onboarding,
      #hrcentre/training, #hrcentre/recruitment.
   2. Each dashboard gains bar charts: average completion by module
      (onboarding), completion by category (trainings), average days
      per stage and days open per position (recruitment).
   3. Hiring requests open into a detail view: candidates with their
      interview stage and notes, the job description link, and linked
      HR tasks. Stored on the request itself (details), so it rides
      the existing requests plumbing.
   4. Master onboarding: sections can be added (90 DAYS, 6 MONTHS,
      anything), ordered sensibly.
   5. Assigning trainings gets a search box.
   6. Volunteering: "The SOP" chip becomes "Master program SOP", and
      the master gains ROLES: named bundles of steps (for example
      Coordinating and confirming volunteers). Assign a person to a
      role and every linked step gets that owner at once, propagating
      to upcoming events through the existing trigger. Roles live in
      the new sop_roles table.
   7. Agendas: when viewing all departments, the list groups under
      Executive and leaders first, then each department, newest first
      within its status.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa47) return;
  window.__wa47 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa47css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w47tabs{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 0 12px;}' +
    '.w47tab{border:1px solid #DEE0D8;background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:999px;padding:6px 16px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;color:#1F201E;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w47tab.on{background:#103F39;border-color:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2;}' +
    '.w47tab:hover{border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.4);}' +
    '.w47chart{background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:12px;padding:10px 14px;margin:10px 0 4px;}' +
    '.w47chart h4{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:11.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;color:#797773;}' +
    '.w47bar{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin:5px 0;font-size:12px;color:#1F201E;}' +
    '.w47bar .lb{width:170px;flex:none;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;}' +
    '.w47bar .tr{flex:1;height:10px;background:#ECEBE3;border-radius:0 4px 4px 0;position:relative;}' +
    '.w47bar .tr i{display:block;height:100%;background:#103F39;border-radius:0 4px 4px 0;min-width:2px;}' +
    '.w47bar.warn .tr i{background:#b3402f;}' +
    '.w47bar .vl{flex:none;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;min-width:44px;}' +
    '[data-w47open],[data-w47as],[data-w47sec],[data-w47ra],[data-w47re],[data-w47rd]{display:inline-block;background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:999px;' +
    'padding:3px 11px;font-size:11.5px;color:#103F39 !important;text-decoration:none !important;cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;line-height:1.5;margin:1px 2px;}' +
    '[data-w47open]:hover,[data-w47as]:hover,[data-w47sec]:hover,[data-w47ra]:hover,[data-w47re]:hover,[data-w47rd]:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.4);}' +
    '[data-w47rd]{color:#b3402f !important;}' +
    '.w47cand{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;padding:5px 0;border-top:1px solid #F4F2EA;font-size:12.5px;flex-wrap:wrap;}' +
    '.w47cnum{font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;border-radius:999px;padding:2px 9px;background:rgba(111,100,171,.12);color:#6F64AB;}' +
    '.w47roles{background:#F4F2EA;border-radius:14px;padding:12px 14px;margin:0 0 12px;}' +
    '.w47roles h4{margin:0 0 4px;font-size:11.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w47agh{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:800;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;color:#FFFAF2;border-radius:999px;padding:4px 14px;margin:14px 0 6px;width:fit-content;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  function wa47IsHr() {
    try {
      /* wave169: and HR's own access profile, as in wave46 */
      return typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && (WA40_MINE.role === 'admin' || WA40_MINE.role === 'leader' || (WA40_MINE.profile === 'hr' && WA40_MINE.role !== 'blocked' && WA40_MINE.role !== 'viewer'));
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  function wa47Days(a, b) {
    try { return Math.max(0, Math.round((new Date(b || Date.now()) - new Date(a)) / 86400000)); } catch (e) { return 0; }
  }
  function wa47Bars(title, rows) {
    if (!rows.length) return '';
    var max = 0;
    rows.forEach(function (r) { if (r.value > max) max = r.value; });
    if (!max) max = 1;
    return '<div class="w47chart"><h4>' + esc(title) + '</h4>' + rows.map(function (r) {
      return '<div class="w47bar' + (r.warn ? ' warn' : '') + '" title="' + esc(r.label + ': ' + r.text) + '">'
        + '<span class="lb">' + esc(r.label) + '</span>'
        + '<span class="tr"><i style="width:' + Math.max(2, Math.round(100 * r.value / max)) + '%"></i></span>'
        + '<span class="vl">' + esc(r.text) + '</span></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  try {
    if (typeof WA37_WEEK_ORDER !== 'undefined') {
      if (!WA37_WEEK_ORDER['90 DAYS']) WA37_WEEK_ORDER['90 DAYS'] = 6;
      if (!WA37_WEEK_ORDER['6 MONTHS']) WA37_WEEK_ORDER['6 MONTHS'] = 7;
    }
  } catch (e) { /* absent */ }

  /* ================= HR centre: three toggled dashboards ================= */
  var WA47_VIEW = 'onboarding';
  var WA47 = { reqs: [], log: [], types: {}, loaded: 0 };
  var CAND_STAGES = ['applied', 'phone screen', 'interview', 'second round', 'offer', 'hired', 'declined'];

  async function wa47Load(force) {
    if (WA47.loaded && !force && (Date.now() - WA47.loaded) < 30000) return;
    try {
      var r1 = await db.from('dept_requests').select('*').in('type_key', ['hiring-request', 'onboarding-request']).order('created_at');
      WA47.reqs = (r1 && r1.data) || [];
      var r2 = await db.from('change_log').select('entity_id,old_value,new_value,changed_at').eq('entity_type', 'request').eq('field', 'status').order('changed_at');
      WA47.log = (r2 && r2.data) || [];
      var r3 = await db.from('request_types').select('key,label,statuses').in('key', ['hiring-request', 'onboarding-request']);
      ((r3 && r3.data) || []).forEach(function (t) { WA47.types[t.key] = t; });
      WA47.loaded = Date.now();
    } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
  }
  function wa47Req(id) { return WA47.reqs.filter(function (r) { return String(r.id) === String(id); })[0]; }
  function wa47Det(r) {
    var d = (r && r.details && typeof r.details === 'object') ? r.details : {};
    return { jd_url: d.jd_url || '', candidates: Array.isArray(d.candidates) ? d.candidates : [], task_refs: Array.isArray(d.task_refs) ? d.task_refs : [], rest: d };
  }

  function wa47ViewOf(sec) {
    var h3 = sec.querySelector('h3');
    var t = h3 ? h3.textContent : '';
    if (t.indexOf('Trainings') === 0) return 'training';
    if (t.indexOf('Recruitment') === 0) return 'recruitment';
    return 'onboarding';
  }
  function wa47Apply() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body');
    if (!body) return;
    body.querySelectorAll('.w46sec').forEach(function (sec) {
      sec.style.display = wa47ViewOf(sec) === WA47_VIEW ? '' : 'none';
    });
    body.querySelectorAll('.w47tab').forEach(function (c) {
      c.classList.toggle('on', c.getAttribute('data-w47v') === WA47_VIEW);
    });
  }
  function wa47Decorate() {
    try {
      var body = document.getElementById('w46body');
      if (!body || !body.querySelector('.w46sec')) return;
      if (!body.querySelector('.w47tabs')) {
        body.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<div class="w47tabs">'
          + '<button class="w47tab" data-w47v="onboarding">Onboarding</button>'
          + '<button class="w47tab" data-w47v="training">Training</button>'
          + '<button class="w47tab" data-w47v="recruitment">Recruitment</button>'
          + '</div>');
      }
      /* searchable assign modal takes over from the plain one */
      body.querySelectorAll('[data-w44as]').forEach(function (a) {
        a.setAttribute('data-w47as', a.getAttribute('data-w44as'));
        a.removeAttribute('data-w44as');
      });
      /* add-a-section pill on the master */
      var master = document.getElementById('w46master');
      if (master && !master.parentElement.querySelector('[data-w47sec]')) {
        master.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', '<div style="margin:0 0 6px"><a data-w47sec="1">+ Add a section (90 DAYS, 6 MONTHS...)</a></div>');
      }
      wa47Apply();
      wa47Charts();
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  var WA47_CHARTING = false;
  async function wa47Charts() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body');
    if (!body || body.querySelector('.w47chart') || WA47_CHARTING) return;
    WA47_CHARTING = true;
    try {
      await wa47Load(false);
      var secs = Array.prototype.slice.call(body.querySelectorAll('.w46sec'));
      var onbSec = null, trSec = null, recSec = null;
      secs.forEach(function (s) {
        var v = wa47ViewOf(s);
        var h3 = (s.querySelector('h3') || {}).textContent || '';
        if (v === 'training' && !trSec) trSec = s;
        if (v === 'recruitment' && !recSec) recSec = s;
        if (v === 'onboarding' && h3.indexOf('Onboarding') === 0) onbSec = s;
      });
      /* onboarding: average completion by module across active onboardings */
      if (onbSec && typeof WA37 !== 'undefined' && typeof wa37Weeks === 'function') {
        var act = WA37.onbs.filter(function (o) { return o.status === 'active'; });
        if (act.length) {
          var stepsBy = {};
          WA37.steps.forEach(function (s) {
            if (act.some(function (o) { return o.id === s.onboarding_id; })) {
              (stepsBy[s.template_id] = stepsBy[s.template_id] || []).push(s);
            }
          });
          var rows = [];
          wa37Weeks().forEach(function (wk) {
            var tot = 0, done = 0;
            wk.steps.forEach(function (t) {
              (stepsBy[t.id] || []).forEach(function (s) { tot++; if (s.done) done++; });
            });
            if (tot) rows.push({ label: wk.name, value: Math.round(100 * done / tot), text: Math.round(100 * done / tot) + '%' });
          });
          onbSec.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', wa47Bars('Average completion by module (active onboardings)', rows));
        }
      }
      /* trainings: completion by category */
      if (trSec && typeof WA36_TRAININGS !== 'undefined') {
        var catOf = {};
        WA36_TRAININGS.forEach(function (t) { catOf[String(t.id)] = t.category || 'General'; });
        var agg = {};
        (typeof WA36_PROG !== 'undefined' ? WA36_PROG : []).forEach(function (p) {
          var c = catOf[String(p.training_id)] || 'General';
          agg[c] = agg[c] || { t: 0, d: 0 };
          agg[c].t++;
          if (p.status === 'done') agg[c].d++;
        });
        var rows2 = Object.keys(agg).map(function (c) {
          return { label: c, value: Math.round(100 * agg[c].d / agg[c].t), text: agg[c].d + ' of ' + agg[c].t };
        });
        rows2.sort(function (a, b) { return b.value - a.value; });
        trSec.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', wa47Bars('Completion by category (all assignments)', rows2));
      }
      /* recruitment: avg days per stage + days open per position + detail pills */
      if (recSec) {
        var hires = WA47.reqs.filter(function (r) { return r.type_key === 'hiring-request'; });
        var stageAgg = {}, stageOrder = (WA47.types['hiring-request'] || {}).statuses || [];
        hires.forEach(function (r) {
          var tr = WA47.log.filter(function (l) { return String(l.entity_id) === String(r.id); });
          var prevAt = r.created_at, prevStage = tr.length ? tr[0].old_value : r.status;
          tr.forEach(function (x) {
            var k = prevStage || '';
            stageAgg[k] = stageAgg[k] || { n: 0, d: 0 };
            stageAgg[k].n++;
            stageAgg[k].d += wa47Days(prevAt, x.changed_at);
            prevAt = x.changed_at;
            prevStage = x.new_value;
          });
        });
        var rows3 = [];
        stageOrder.forEach(function (s) {
          if (stageAgg[s] && stageAgg[s].n) {
            var avg = Math.round(stageAgg[s].d / stageAgg[s].n);
            rows3.push({ label: s, value: avg, text: avg + 'd avg' });
          }
        });
        var openRows = hires.filter(function (r) { return !r.done; }).map(function (r) {
          var d = wa47Days(r.created_at);
          return { label: r.title, value: d, text: d + 'd', warn: d > 30 };
        });
        var charts = wa47Bars('Average days per stage (all hiring requests)', rows3)
          + wa47Bars('Days open per position', openRows);
        var anchor = recSec.querySelector('h3');
        var tiles = recSec.querySelector('.w44tiles');
        if (tiles) tiles.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', charts);
        else if (anchor) anchor.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', charts);
        /* candidates + details pill on each hiring row */
        recSec.querySelectorAll('select[data-w46st]').forEach(function (sel) {
          var id = sel.getAttribute('data-w46st');
          var r = wa47Req(id);
          if (!r || r.type_key !== 'hiring-request') return;
          if (sel.parentElement.querySelector('[data-w47open="' + id + '"]')) return;
          var det = wa47Det(r);
          sel.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend',
            (det.candidates.length ? '<span class="w47cnum">' + det.candidates.length + ' candidate' + (det.candidates.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span>' : '')
            + '<a data-w47open="' + id + '">candidates and details</a>');
        });
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    WA47_CHARTING = false;
  }

  /* watch the panel so decoration survives every internal re-render */
  function wa47Watch() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!panel || panel.dataset.w47mo) return;
    panel.dataset.w47mo = '1';
    var mo = new MutationObserver(function () { wa47Decorate(); });
    mo.observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    wa47Decorate();
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa47 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa47();
      try {
        if (window.WA_NAV && WA_NAV.pages) {
          WA_NAV.pages.forEach(function (pg) {
            if (pg.id !== 'hrcentre') return;
            var orig = pg.onShow;
            pg.onShow = function (h) {
              var sub = String(h || '').split('/')[1];
              if (sub === 'training' || sub === 'recruitment' || sub === 'onboarding') WA47_VIEW = sub;
              if (orig) orig(h);
              wa47Watch();
            };
          });
          if ((location.hash || '').indexOf('#hrcentre') === 0) { try { applyHash(); } catch (e2) { /* boot */ } }
        }
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  /* ---- recruitment detail modal ---- */
  var WA47_CAND = [], WA47_TASKS = [];
  function wa47CandList() {
    var host = document.getElementById('w47cl');
    if (!host) return;
    host.innerHTML = WA47_CAND.length ? WA47_CAND.map(function (c, i) {
      return '<div class="w47cand"><b style="min-width:110px">' + esc(c.name) + '</b>'
        + '<select class="w36sel" data-w47cs="' + i + '">' + CAND_STAGES.map(function (s) {
          return '<option value="' + s + '"' + (s === c.stage ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + s + '</option>';
        }).join('') + '</select>'
        + '<input type="text" data-w47cno="' + i + '" placeholder="notes" value="' + esc(c.notes || '') + '" style="flex:1;min-width:120px" />'
        + '<a class="w37edit" style="color:#b3402f !important" data-w47cx="' + i + '">remove</a></div>';
    }).join('') : '<p class="m-sub">No candidates yet.</p>';
  }
  function wa47TaskList() {
    var host = document.getElementById('w47tl');
    if (!host) return;
    var tasks = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []);
    host.innerHTML = WA47_TASKS.length ? WA47_TASKS.map(function (ref, i) {
      var t = tasks.filter(function (x) { return String(x.ref) === String(ref); })[0];
      return '<div class="w47cand"><a href="#task/' + esc(String(ref)) + '" style="flex:1;color:#103F39;text-decoration:none;font-weight:600" onclick="closeModal()">'
        + esc(t ? t.title : 'Task ' + ref) + '</a>'
        + (t && t.done ? '<span class="w46stage">done</span>' : '')
        + '<a class="w37edit" style="color:#b3402f !important" data-w47tx="' + i + '">unlink</a></div>';
    }).join('') : '<p class="m-sub">No linked tasks yet.</p>';
  }
  function wa47Detail(id) {
    var r = wa47Req(id);
    if (!r) return;
    var det = wa47Det(r);
    WA47_CAND = det.candidates.map(function (c) { return { name: c.name, stage: c.stage || 'applied', notes: c.notes || '' }; });
    WA47_TASKS = det.task_refs.slice();
    var hrTasks = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (t) { return t.department === 'hr' && !t.done; });
    openModal('<h3>' + esc(r.title) + '</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">Stage: ' + esc(r.status) + ' &middot; opened ' + wa47Days(r.created_at) + ' days ago</p>'
      + '<label>Job description link</label><input type="text" id="w47jd" value="' + esc(det.jd_url) + '" placeholder="https://docs.google.com/..." />'
      + (det.jd_url ? '<p class="m-sub" style="margin:2px 0"><a href="' + esc(det.jd_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:#103F39">Open the job description</a></p>' : '')
      + '<h4 style="margin:12px 0 2px;font-size:12.5px">Candidates</h4><div id="w47cl"></div>'
      + '<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">'
      + '<input type="text" id="w47cn" placeholder="Candidate name" style="flex:1;min-width:130px" />'
      + '<select class="w36sel" id="w47cstg">' + CAND_STAGES.map(function (s) { return '<option>' + s + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'
      + '<button class="btn ghost mini" data-w47cadd="1">Add</button></div>'
      + '<h4 style="margin:12px 0 2px;font-size:12.5px">Linked recruitment tasks</h4><div id="w47tl"></div>'
      + '<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">'
      + '<select class="w36sel" id="w47ts" style="max-width:280px"><option value="">Link an open HR task...</option>'
      + hrTasks.map(function (t) { return '<option value="' + esc(String(t.ref)) + '">' + esc(t.title) + '</option>'; }).join('')
      + '</select><button class="btn ghost mini" data-w47tadd="1">Link</button></div>'
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w47dsv" data-w47req="' + r.id + '">Save</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w47dcx">Cancel</button></div>');
    wa47CandList();
    wa47TaskList();
    document.getElementById('w47dcx').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w47dsv').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      /* pull edited stages and notes */
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-w47cs]').forEach(function (s) { WA47_CAND[Number(s.getAttribute('data-w47cs'))].stage = s.value; });
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-w47cno]').forEach(function (s) { WA47_CAND[Number(s.getAttribute('data-w47cno'))].notes = s.value; });
      var merged = {};
      Object.keys(det.rest).forEach(function (k) { merged[k] = det.rest[k]; });
      merged.jd_url = (document.getElementById('w47jd').value || '').trim() || null;
      merged.candidates = WA47_CAND;
      merged.task_refs = WA47_TASKS;
      var res = await db.from('dept_requests').update({ details: merged, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'HR' }).eq('id', r.id);
      if (res.error) { toast('Could not save it'); return; }
      closeModal();
      toast('Saved');
      await wa47Load(true);
      var body = document.getElementById('w46body');
      if (body) { body.querySelectorAll('.w47chart').forEach(function (c) { c.remove(); }); wa47Charts(); }
    });
  }

  /* ---- add a section to the master onboarding ---- */
  function wa47SecModal() {
    openModal('<h3>Add a section to the master onboarding</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">A section is a module like WEEK 1 or 60 DAYS. It appears once it has a first step.</p>'
      + '<label>Section name</label><input type="text" id="w47sn" list="w47sl" placeholder="90 DAYS" style="text-transform:uppercase" />'
      + '<datalist id="w47sl"><option value="90 DAYS"></option><option value="6 MONTHS"></option><option value="WEEK 5"></option></datalist>'
      + '<label>First step in this section</label><input type="text" id="w47fs" placeholder="What they need to do" />'
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w47ss">Add it</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w47sc">Cancel</button></div>');
    document.getElementById('w47sc').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w47ss').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var sec = (document.getElementById('w47sn').value || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      var fs = (document.getElementById('w47fs').value || '').trim();
      if (!sec || !fs) { toast('Name the section and its first step'); return; }
      var r = await db.from('onboarding_template').insert({
        id: 'wa' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10),
        section: sec, name: fs, sort: 10, active: true, content_pending: false
      });
      if (r.error) { toast('Could not add it'); return; }
      try {
        if (typeof WA37_WEEK_ORDER !== 'undefined' && !WA37_WEEK_ORDER[sec]) {
          var mx = 0;
          Object.keys(WA37_WEEK_ORDER).forEach(function (k) { if (WA37_WEEK_ORDER[k] < 9 && WA37_WEEK_ORDER[k] > mx) mx = WA37_WEEK_ORDER[k]; });
          WA37_WEEK_ORDER[sec] = mx + 1;
        }
      } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      await wa37Load(true);
      toast('Section added');
      wa37Master();
    });
  }

  /* ---- searchable training assignment ---- */
  function wa47AssignModal(name) {
    var rows = (typeof WA36_PROG !== 'undefined' ? WA36_PROG : []).filter(function (p) {
      return String(p.person_name || '').toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase();
    });
    var have = {};
    rows.forEach(function (p) { have[String(p.training_id)] = p.status; });
    var list = (typeof WA36_TRAININGS !== 'undefined' ? WA36_TRAININGS : []).slice();
    list.sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.category).localeCompare(String(b.category)) || String(a.title).localeCompare(String(b.title)); });
    var html = '<h3>Trainings for ' + esc(name) + '</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">Tick to assign, untick to remove. Completed trainings stay on their record.</p>'
      + '<input type="search" id="w47q" placeholder="Search trainings..." style="width:100%;margin:2px 0 8px" />'
      + '<div style="max-height:300px;overflow:auto">';
    list.forEach(function (t) {
      var stt = have[String(t.id)];
      html += '<label data-w47tr="1" data-search="' + esc((t.title + ' ' + (t.category || '')).toLowerCase()) + '" style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;padding:3px 0;font-size:13px">'
        + '<input type="checkbox" class="w47cb" value="' + t.id + '"' + (stt ? ' checked' : '') + (stt === 'done' ? ' disabled' : '') + ' style="width:15px;height:15px" /> '
        + esc(t.title) + ' <span style="color:#797773;font-size:11px">' + esc(t.category || '') + '</span>'
        + (stt === 'done' ? ' <span class="w36pill done">done</span>' : '') + '</label>';
    });
    html += '</div><div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w47asv">Save</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w47acx">Cancel</button></div>';
    openModal(html);
    document.getElementById('w47q').addEventListener('input', function () {
      var q = this.value.toLowerCase();
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-w47tr]').forEach(function (l) {
        l.style.display = !q || l.getAttribute('data-search').indexOf(q) >= 0 ? 'flex' : 'none';
      });
    });
    document.getElementById('w47acx').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w47asv').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var picked = {};
      document.querySelectorAll('.w47cb:checked').forEach(function (c) { picked[c.value] = 1; });
      var adds = [], removals = [];
      Object.keys(picked).forEach(function (tid) { if (!have[tid]) adds.push(Number(tid)); });
      Object.keys(have).forEach(function (tid) { if (!picked[tid] && have[tid] !== 'done') removals.push(Number(tid)); });
      var by = (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'HR';
      var ok = true;
      if (adds.length) {
        var r1 = await db.from('training_progress').upsert(adds.map(function (tid) {
          return { training_id: tid, person_name: name, status: 'assigned', assigned_by: by, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
        }), { onConflict: 'training_id,person_name' });
        if (r1.error) ok = false;
      }
      for (var i = 0; i < removals.length; i++) {
        var r2 = await db.from('training_progress').delete().eq('training_id', removals[i]).eq('person_name', name).neq('status', 'done');
        if (r2.error) ok = false;
      }
      if (!ok) { toast('Some changes did not save'); return; }
      closeModal();
      toast('Saved: ' + adds.length + ' assigned, ' + removals.length + ' removed');
      try { await wa36Load(true); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      var body = document.getElementById('w46body');
      if (body) { location.hash = '#hrcentre/training'; applyHash(); }
    });
  }

  /* ================= volunteering: master program SOP + roles ================= */
  var WA47_ROLES = [], WA47_RLOADED = false;
  async function wa47RolesLoad(force) {
    if (WA47_RLOADED && !force) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('sop_roles').select('*').order('name');
      WA47_ROLES = (r && r.data) || [];
      WA47_RLOADED = true;
    } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
  }
  if (typeof waVolPaint === 'function') {
    var __vp47 = waVolPaint;
    waVolPaint = function () {
      __vp47.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        var chip = document.querySelector('#volunteering [data-vl="sop"]');
        if (chip) chip.textContent = 'Master program SOP';
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  function wa47RolesHtml() {
    var people = (typeof WA28_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA28_PEOPLE : []);
    var html = '<div class="w47roles" id="w47roles"><h4>Roles: bulk assignment</h4>'
      + '<p class="m-sub" style="margin:0 0 6px">A role bundles master steps (for example Coordinating and confirming volunteers). Pick a person on a role and every linked step gets that owner, including upcoming events.</p>';
    if (!WA47_ROLES.length) html += '<p class="m-sub">No roles yet.</p>';
    WA47_ROLES.forEach(function (ro) {
      var ids = Array.isArray(ro.step_ids) ? ro.step_ids : [];
      var owners = {};
      (typeof WA_VOL_TMPL !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_TMPL : []).forEach(function (t) {
        if (ids.indexOf(t.id) >= 0 && t.default_owner) owners[t.default_owner] = (owners[t.default_owner] || 0) + 1;
      });
      var cur = Object.keys(owners).sort(function (a, b) { return owners[b] - owners[a]; })[0] || '';
      html += '<div class="w46row"><b style="min-width:150px">' + esc(ro.name) + '</b>'
        + '<span class="w46days">' + ids.length + ' steps</span>'
        + '<select class="w36sel" data-w47rb="' + ro.id + '"><option value="">Assign to...</option>'
        + people.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + esc(p.name) + '"' + (p.name === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(p.name) + '</option>'; }).join('')
        + '</select>'
        + '<a data-w47re="' + ro.id + '">edit</a><a data-w47rd="' + ro.id + '">delete</a></div>';
    });
    html += '<div style="margin-top:6px"><a data-w47ra="1">+ Create a role</a></div></div>';
    return html;
  }
  if (typeof waVolRenderSop === 'function') {
    var __sop47 = waVolRenderSop;
    waVolRenderSop = function () {
      __sop47.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
        var intro = document.querySelector('#volunteering .vl-empty');
        if (intro && !document.getElementById('w47roles')) {
          intro.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', wa47RolesHtml());
          wa47RolesLoad(false).then(function () {
            var host = document.getElementById('w47roles');
            if (host) host.outerHTML = wa47RolesHtml();
          });
        }
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  function wa47RoleModal(rid) {
    var ro = WA47_ROLES.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(rid); })[0];
    var ids = ro && Array.isArray(ro.step_ids) ? ro.step_ids : [];
    var steps = (typeof WA_VOL_TMPL !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_TMPL : []);
    var html = '<h3>' + (ro ? 'Edit role' : 'Create a role') + '</h3>'
