      + '<label>Role name</label><input type="text" id="w47rn" value="' + esc(ro ? ro.name : '') + '" placeholder="Coordinating and confirming volunteers" />'
      + '<label style="margin-top:8px;display:block">Steps in this role</label>'
      + '<input type="search" id="w47rq" placeholder="Search the master SOP..." style="width:100%;margin:2px 0 6px" />'
      + '<div style="max-height:280px;overflow:auto">';
    steps.forEach(function (t) {
      html += '<label data-w47sr="1" data-search="' + esc(((t.section || '') + ' ' + t.name).toLowerCase()) + '" style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;padding:2px 0;font-size:12.5px">'
        + '<input type="checkbox" class="w47rcb" value="' + esc(t.id) + '"' + (ids.indexOf(t.id) >= 0 ? ' checked' : '') + ' style="width:14px;height:14px;flex:none" /> '
        + '<span style="color:#797773;font-size:10.5px;flex:none">' + esc(t.section || '') + '</span> ' + esc(t.name) + '</label>';
    });
    html += '</div><div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w47rsv">Save</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w47rcx">Cancel</button></div>';
    openModal(html);
    document.getElementById('w47rq').addEventListener('input', function () {
      var q = this.value.toLowerCase();
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-w47sr]').forEach(function (l) {
        l.style.display = !q || l.getAttribute('data-search').indexOf(q) >= 0 ? 'flex' : 'none';
      });
    });
    document.getElementById('w47rcx').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w47rsv').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var nm = (document.getElementById('w47rn').value || '').trim();
      if (!nm) { toast('Name the role'); return; }
      var picked = [];
      document.querySelectorAll('.w47rcb:checked').forEach(function (c) { picked.push(c.value); });
      var patch = { name: nm, step_ids: picked, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff' };
      var r;
      if (ro) r = await db.from('sop_roles').update(patch).eq('id', ro.id);
      else r = await db.from('sop_roles').insert(patch);
      if (r.error) { toast('Could not save the role'); return; }
      closeModal();
      toast('Role saved (' + picked.length + ' steps)');
      await wa47RolesLoad(true);
      var host = document.getElementById('w47roles');
      if (host) host.outerHTML = wa47RolesHtml();
    });
  }

  /* ================= agendas grouped by department ================= */
  if (typeof waRenderAgendasPage === 'function') {
    var __ag47 = waRenderAgendasPage;
    waRenderAgendasPage = async function () {
      await __ag47.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        var fd = (document.getElementById('agFilterDept') || {}).value || 'all';
        if (fd !== 'all') return;
        var body = document.getElementById('agPageBody');
        if (!body) return;
        var rows = Array.prototype.slice.call(body.querySelectorAll('.ag-row'));
        if (rows.length < 2) return;
        var byId = {};
        (typeof AGENDAS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? AGENDAS_CACHE : []).forEach(function (a) { byId[String(a.id)] = a; });
        var groups = {};
        rows.forEach(function (row) {
          var a = byId[String(row.getAttribute('data-ag'))];
          var g = (!a || (a.scope || 'department') !== 'department') ? '__exec' : (a.department || '__other');
          (groups[g] = groups[g] || []).push({ row: row, a: a });
        });
        var order = ['__exec'].concat(typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' ? DEPT_ORDER : []).concat(['__other']);
        var stOrder = { draft: 0, final: 1, done: 2 };
        var frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
        order.forEach(function (g) {
          if (!groups[g] || !groups[g].length) return;
          var label = g === '__exec' ? 'Executive and leaders meetings' : g === '__other' ? 'Other' : ((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[g]) || g);
          var color = g === '__exec' ? '#6F64AB' : ((typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[g]) || '#797773');
          var h = document.createElement('div');
          h.className = 'w47agh';
          h.style.background = color;
          h.textContent = label + ' (' + groups[g].length + ')';
          frag.appendChild(h);
          groups[g].sort(function (x, y) {
            var sx = x.a ? (stOrder[x.a.status] !== undefined ? stOrder[x.a.status] : 1) : 1;
            var sy = y.a ? (stOrder[y.a.status] !== undefined ? stOrder[y.a.status] : 1) : 1;
            return sx - sy || ((y.a ? y.a.id : 0) - (x.a ? x.a.id : 0));
          });
          groups[g].forEach(function (x) { frag.appendChild(x.row); });
        });
        body.insertBefore(frag, body.firstChild);
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  /* ================= wiring ================= */
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa47Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa47Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var v = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w47tab');
      if (v) {
        WA47_VIEW = v.getAttribute('data-w47v');
        try { history.replaceState(null, '', '#hrcentre/' + WA47_VIEW); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ }
        wa47Apply();
        return;
      }
      var op = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w47open]');
      if (op) { if (wa47IsHr()) wa47Detail(op.getAttribute('data-w47open')); return; }
      var as = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w47as]');
      if (as) { if (wa47IsHr()) wa47AssignModal(as.getAttribute('data-w47as')); return; }
      var sc = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w47sec]');
      if (sc) { if (wa47IsHr()) wa47SecModal(); return; }
      var ca = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w47cadd]');
      if (ca) {
        var nm = (document.getElementById('w47cn').value || '').trim();
        if (!nm) { toast('Name the candidate'); return; }
        WA47_CAND.push({ name: nm, stage: document.getElementById('w47cstg').value, notes: '' });
        document.getElementById('w47cn').value = '';
        wa47CandList();
        return;
      }
      var cx = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w47cx]');
      if (cx) {
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-w47cs]').forEach(function (s) { WA47_CAND[Number(s.getAttribute('data-w47cs'))].stage = s.value; });
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-w47cno]').forEach(function (s) { WA47_CAND[Number(s.getAttribute('data-w47cno'))].notes = s.value; });
        WA47_CAND.splice(Number(cx.getAttribute('data-w47cx')), 1);
        wa47CandList();
        return;
      }
      var ta = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w47tadd]');
      if (ta) {
        var sel = document.getElementById('w47ts');
        if (sel && sel.value && WA47_TASKS.indexOf(sel.value) < 0) { WA47_TASKS.push(sel.value); wa47TaskList(); }
        return;
      }
      var tx = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w47tx]');
      if (tx) { WA47_TASKS.splice(Number(tx.getAttribute('data-w47tx')), 1); wa47TaskList(); return; }
      var ra = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w47ra]');
      if (ra) { if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff()) wa47RoleModal(null); return; }
      var re = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w47re]');
      if (re) { if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff()) wa47RoleModal(re.getAttribute('data-w47re')); return; }
      var rd = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w47rd]');
      if (rd) {
        if (!(typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff())) return;
        if (!confirm('Delete this role? Step owners stay as they are.')) return;
        db.from('sop_roles').delete().eq('id', Number(rd.getAttribute('data-w47rd'))).then(async function (r) {
          if (r.error) { toast('Could not delete it'); return; }
          await wa47RolesLoad(true);
          var host = document.getElementById('w47roles');
          if (host) host.outerHTML = wa47RolesHtml();
          toast('Role deleted');
        });
        return;
      }
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('change', async function (e) {
      var rb = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w47rb]');
      if (rb && rb.value) {
        if (!(typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff())) return;
        var ro = WA47_ROLES.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(rb.getAttribute('data-w47rb')); })[0];
        if (!ro) return;
        var ids = Array.isArray(ro.step_ids) ? ro.step_ids : [];
        if (!ids.length) { toast('This role has no steps yet'); return; }
        var r = await db.from('sop_template').update({ default_owner: rb.value, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).in('id', ids);
        if (r.error) { toast('Could not assign the role'); return; }
        try {
          (typeof WA_VOL_TMPL !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_TMPL : []).forEach(function (t) {
            if (ids.indexOf(t.id) >= 0) t.default_owner = rb.value;
          });
        } catch (e2) { /* fine */ }
        toast(rb.value + ' now owns ' + ids.length + ' steps (upcoming events updated)');
        try { waVolRenderSop(); } catch (e3) { /* fine */ }
        return;
      }
    });
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v48 visible dropdown buttons
   (02/08/26)
   The expand and collapse controls on tasks stop hiding.
   - Subtask toggles in the volunteering SOP and event checklists
     become real pill buttons: a bigger caret button plus the
     "N subtasks" count badge, both clickable, with hover states.
   - Section dropdowns get a clear caret and a hover highlight:
     volunteering phases, onboarding weeks (which had no caret at
     all), the master onboarding weeks, decisions dropdowns, goals
     quarters and departments, meeting updates, and the Decided list.
   Pure styling plus one click-forwarder; no behavior changes.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (document.getElementById('wa48css')) return;
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa48css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    /* subtask toggle: caret pill + clickable count pill */
    '.w26c{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-width:28px;height:24px;' +
    'border:1px solid #cfe0da;background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:999px;font-size:13px;color:#103F39;' +
    'margin:0 8px 0 0;padding:0;cursor:pointer;flex:none;}' +
    '.w26c:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.12);border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.45);}' +
    '.w26n{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;margin-left:8px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;' +
    'color:#103F39;border:1px solid #cfe0da;background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:999px;padding:2px 11px;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w26n:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.12);border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.45);}' +
    /* volunteering phase sections: bigger caret, hover */
    '.vl-phase>summary:before{font-size:15px;color:#103F39;font-weight:700;}' +
    '.vl-phase>summary:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.05);}' +
    '.vl-phase>summary{border-radius:11px;}' +
    '.vl-phase[open]>summary{border-radius:11px 11px 0 0;}' +
    /* onboarding weeks had no caret at all */
    '.w37week>summary:before{content:"\\25B8";color:#103F39;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;flex:none;}' +
    '.w37week[open]>summary:before{content:"\\25BE";}' +
    '.w37week>summary:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.05);border-radius:16px;}' +
    '.w37week[open]>summary:hover{border-radius:16px 16px 0 0;}' +
    /* master onboarding weeks, decisions dropdowns: hover + louder chev */
    '.w46wk>summary:hover,.w39dd>summary:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.05);border-radius:12px;}' +
    '.w46wk>summary .chev,.w39dd>summary .chev{font-size:15px;color:#103F39;font-weight:700;}' +
    /* goals quarters and departments, meeting updates, decided list: hover */
    '.w29q>summary:hover,.w29d>summary:hover,.wa21-upd>summary:hover,details.completed summary:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.06);border-radius:9px;}' +
    '.w29q>summary,.w29d>summary,.wa21-upd>summary{padding-left:4px;padding-right:4px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* the count badge toggles too */
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa48Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa48Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var n = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w26n');
      if (!n) return;
      var name = n.closest('.vl-name');
      var btn = name && name.querySelector('.w26c');
      if (btn && typeof wa26Toggle === 'function') wa26Toggle(btn);
    });
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v49 the recruitment playbook
   (02/08/26)
   The hiring process from the CMP x WA strategy deck lives in the HR
   centre, right under the recruitment pipeline: the five recruitment
   philosophies, who to recruit for each level of the ladder, what
   happens after "offer accepted", quick links to the interview
   question templates, the offer letter template, the HR SOP and the
   strategy deck, plus the three Hub-styled diagrams (chapter
   structure, five year plan, event scale model) embedded as
   dropdowns.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa49) return;
  window.__wa49 = 1;

  var DOCS = 'https://zwnmulmnvopclzcvytce.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/web/docs/';
  var LINKS = [
    ['Interview question templates', 'https://docs.google.com/document/d/1S8MasO6YF7Upa8DW8z2dfpfP41UQ_aaDgQmsNiS4hh8/edit'],
    ['Offer letter template', 'https://docs.google.com/document/d/1GB4ujl3d3CarREes3Yh5mbW5VKnseUJh/edit'],
    ['HR onboarding SOP (doc)', 'https://docs.google.com/document/d/1OWeVlCHCSLPMCDD7DbfpO_OidcuBvPRvRNE5VueqWpg/edit'],
    ['Strategy deck (CMP x WA, PDF)', DOCS + 'cmp-x-wa-final-deliverable.pdf']
  ];
  var DIAGRAMS = [
    ['National team and chapter structure', 'diag1_chapter_structure.svg'],
    ['The five year plan at a glance', 'diag2_five_year_plan.svg'],
    ['The event scale model', 'diag3_event_scale.svg']
  ];

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa49css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w49pb{background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:12px;padding:12px 16px;margin-top:12px;}' +
    '.w49pb h4{margin:0 0 6px;font-size:11.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.6px;color:#797773;}' +
    '.w49phil{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 0 10px;}' +
    '.w49phil span{background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);color:#103F39;border-radius:999px;padding:4px 13px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w49tbl{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:12.5px;margin:0 0 10px;}' +
    '.w49tbl th{text-align:left;font-size:10.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px;color:#797773;padding:3px 8px;}' +
    '.w49tbl td{padding:5px 8px;border-top:1px solid #f0f2f1;vertical-align:top;}' +
    '.w49links a{display:inline-block;background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:999px;padding:3px 11px;' +
    'font-size:11.5px;color:#103F39 !important;text-decoration:none !important;cursor:pointer;font-weight:600;line-height:1.5;margin:1px 2px;}' +
    '.w49links a:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.4);}' +
    '.w49dg{background:#F4F2EA;border-radius:12px;margin:6px 0;padding:2px 12px;}' +
    '.w49dg > summary{list-style:none;cursor:pointer;display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;padding:9px 2px;font-weight:700;font-size:13px;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w49dg > summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none;}' +
    '.w49dg > summary:before{content:"\\25B8";color:#103F39;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w49dg[open] > summary:before{content:"\\25BE";}' +
    '.w49dg img{width:100%;border-radius:10px;margin:4px 0 10px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  function wa49Html() {
    return '<div class="w49pb">'
      + '<h4>Recruitment playbook (from the CMP x WA strategy deck)</h4>'
      + '<div class="w49phil">'
      + '<span>Recruitment is an active process</span>'
      + '<span>Lean teams work better</span>'
      + '<span>Integrity, intelligence, energy</span>'
      + '<span>Referrals over experience</span>'
      + '<span>Second in commands can be taught</span>'
      + '</div>'
      + '<p style="font-size:12.5px;color:#1F201E;margin:0 0 10px">Recruit critical thinkers for top level roles, let high energy people be second in command, and keep every team lean. Target structure: 3 levels, 5 divisions, 26 positions.</p>'
      + '<table class="w49tbl"><thead><tr><th>Level</th><th>What they do</th><th>Screen hardest for</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
      + '<tr><td><b>Board of Directors</b></td><td>Supporting cast to the executive team; help implement the vision</td><td>Vision, integrity, referrals</td></tr>'
      + '<tr><td><b>Executive (C-suite)</b></td><td>Report to the ED; cultivate the next generation of leaders</td><td>Quick learning, self starting, problem solving</td></tr>'
      + '<tr><td><b>Chapter President</b></td><td>Starts a new club; regular contact with the executive team</td><td>Quick learning, referrals, vision, energy</td></tr>'
      + '<tr><td><b>Chapter VP / Director</b></td><td>Supports the president; develops next year&#39;s team</td><td>Self starting, energy, integrity</td></tr>'
      + '<tr><td><b>Brand Ambassador</b></td><td>Networks on campus and spreads the mission</td><td>Energy, integrity</td></tr>'
      + '</tbody></table>'
      + '<p style="font-size:12.5px;color:#1F201E;margin:0 0 10px">After a candidate reaches <b>offer accepted</b> in the pipeline above: send the offer letter, and once they accept, the onboarding email and account setup (the Welcome kit on the Onboarding dashboard has both prefilled).</p>'
      + '<h4>Templates and references</h4>'
      + '<div class="w49links">' + LINKS.map(function (l) {
        return '<a href="' + l[1] + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + l[0] + '</a>';
      }).join('') + '</div>'
      + '<h4 style="margin-top:12px">Diagrams</h4>'
      + DIAGRAMS.map(function (d) {
        return '<details class="w49dg"><summary>' + d[0] + '</summary>'
          + '<img src="' + DOCS + d[1] + '" alt="' + d[0] + '" loading="lazy" /></details>';
      }).join('')
      + '</div>';
  }

  function wa49Place() {
    try {
      var body = document.getElementById('w46body');
      if (!body || body.querySelector('.w49pb')) return;
      var secs = body.querySelectorAll('.w46sec');
      for (var i = 0; i < secs.length; i++) {
        var h3 = secs[i].querySelector('h3');
        if (h3 && h3.textContent.indexOf('Recruitment') === 0) {
          secs[i].insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', wa49Html());
          return;
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  function wa49Watch() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!panel || panel.dataset.w49mo) return;
    panel.dataset.w49mo = '1';
    var mo = new MutationObserver(function () { wa49Place(); });
    mo.observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    wa49Place();
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp49 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      __sp49(id);
      try { if (id === 'hrcentre') wa49Watch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa49 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa49();
      try { wa49Watch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v50 requests suggested from meetings
   (02/08/26)
   Transcripts now feed the requests portal, confirm-first. A scanner
   (edge function transcript-requests, cron every 2 hours) reads each
   newly processed meeting and captures explicit cross-team asks as
   pending suggestions with a verbatim quote and a confidence score.
   This wave adds the inbox: a "Suggested from meetings" block at the
   top of the Requests page where staff Accept (files the real request
   through the normal plumbing, attributed to the accepter) or Dismiss.
   Nothing is ever filed without a human click.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa50) return;
  window.__wa50 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa50css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w50box{background:#F4F2EA;border-radius:14px;padding:12px 16px;margin:0 0 14px;}' +
    '.w50box h3{margin:0 0 4px;font-size:12px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.7px;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w50row{background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:12px;padding:10px 14px;margin:8px 0;}' +
    '.w50t{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:13.5px;font-weight:700;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w50dep{font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;color:#FFFAF2;border-radius:999px;padding:2px 10px;}' +
    '.w50conf{font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;color:#8a6414;background:rgba(220,165,39,.16);border-radius:999px;padding:2px 9px;}' +
    '.w50q{font-size:12px;color:#797773;font-style:italic;margin:4px 0 2px;}' +
    '.w50d{font-size:12.5px;color:#1F201E;margin:2px 0 6px;}' +
    '[data-w50a],[data-w50x],[data-w50tx]{display:inline-block;background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:999px;padding:3px 12px;' +
    'font-size:11.5px;color:#103F39 !important;text-decoration:none !important;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;line-height:1.5;margin:1px 4px 1px 0;}' +
    '[data-w50a]{background:#103F39;color:#FFFAF2 !important;border-color:#103F39;}' +
    '[data-w50a]:hover{background:#103F39;}' +
    '[data-w50x]:hover,[data-w50tx]:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.4);}' +
    '[data-w50x]{color:#b3402f !important;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  var WA50 = { rows: [], loaded: false };
  async function wa50Load() {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('request_suggestions').select('*').eq('status', 'pending').order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(20);
      WA50.rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      WA50.loaded = true;
    } catch (e) { WA50.rows = []; }
  }

  function wa50Html() {
    if (!WA50.rows.length) return '';
    var dl = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined') ? DEPT_LABEL : {};
    var dc = (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined') ? DEPT_COLOR : {};
    var html = '<div class="w50box" id="w50box"><h3>Suggested from meetings (' + WA50.rows.length + ')</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub" style="margin:0 0 4px">The Hub spotted these asks in recent transcripts. Nothing is filed until you accept it.</p>';
    WA50.rows.forEach(function (s) {
      html += '<div class="w50row">'
        + '<div class="w50t"><span class="w50dep" style="background:' + (dc[s.department] || '#797773') + '">' + esc(dl[s.department] || s.department) + '</span>'
        + esc(s.title)
        + '<span class="w50conf">confidence ' + (s.confidence || '?') + '/10</span></div>'
        + (s.quote ? '<div class="w50q">&quot;' + esc(s.quote) + '&quot;</div>' : '')
        + (s.details ? '<div class="w50d">' + esc(s.details) + '</div>' : '')
        + '<div><a data-w50a="' + s.id + '">Accept and file it</a>'
        + '<a data-w50x="' + s.id + '">Dismiss</a>'
        + '<a data-w50tx="' + s.transcript_id + '">Open the meeting</a>'
        + (s.assignee ? '<span style="font-size:11.5px;color:#797773">suggested assignee: ' + esc(s.assignee) + '</span>' : '')
        + '</div></div>';
    });
    html += '</div>';
    return html;
  }

  async function wa50Inject() {
    try {
      if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
      var body = document.getElementById('reqBody');
      if (!body) return;
      if (!WA50.loaded) await wa50Load();
      var old = document.getElementById('w50box');
      if (old) old.remove();
      if (WA50.rows.length) body.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', wa50Html());
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  if (typeof renderRequestsModule === 'function') {
    var __rr50 = renderRequestsModule;
    renderRequestsModule = function () {
      var out = __rr50.apply(this, arguments);
      WA50.loaded = false;
      wa50Inject();
      return out;
    };
  }

  async function wa50Accept(id, el) {
    var s = WA50.rows.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0];
    if (!s) return;
    el.textContent = 'Filing...';
    var by = (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff';
    var status = 'requested';
    try {
      var rt = await db.from('request_types').select('default_status').eq('key', s.type_key).maybeSingle();
      if (rt && rt.data && rt.data.default_status) status = rt.data.default_status;
    } catch (e) { /* default */ }
    var details = (s.details || '') + (s.quote ? '\n\nFrom the meeting: "' + s.quote + '"' : '');
    var ins = await db.from('dept_requests').insert({
      type_key: s.type_key || 'other', department: s.department, title: s.title,
      details: details || null, status: status, done: false,
      requested_by: by + ' (from a meeting)',
      assignee: s.assignee || null
    }).select('id').single();
    if (ins.error) { toast('Could not file it'); el.textContent = 'Accept and file it'; return; }
    await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'request', entity_id: String(ins.data.id), field: 'created', old_value: null, new_value: s.title, changed_by: by + ' (from a meeting)' });
    await db.from('request_suggestions').update({ status: 'accepted', request_id: ins.data.id, decided_by: by, decided_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', s.id);
    toast('Filed to ' + ((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[s.department]) || s.department));
    WA50.loaded = false;
    try { renderRequestsModule(); } catch (e) { wa50Inject(); }
  }
  async function wa50Dismiss(id) {
    var by = (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff';
    var r = await db.from('request_suggestions').update({ status: 'dismissed', decided_by: by, decided_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', Number(id));
    if (r.error) { toast('Could not dismiss it'); return; }
    WA50.rows = WA50.rows.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) !== String(id); });
    var old = document.getElementById('w50box');
    if (old) old.remove();
    var body = document.getElementById('reqBody');
    if (body && WA50.rows.length) body.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', wa50Html());
    toast('Dismissed');
  }

  if (!document.body.dataset.wa50Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa50Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w50a]');
      if (a) { if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff()) wa50Accept(a.getAttribute('data-w50a'), a); return; }
      var x = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w50x]');
      if (x) { if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff()) wa50Dismiss(x.getAttribute('data-w50x')); return; }
      var t = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w50tx]');
      if (t) { location.hash = '#tx/' + t.getAttribute('data-w50tx'); return; }
    });
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v51 comments on every task
   (02/08/26)
   Every task can carry a conversation.
   - The latest comment shows right on the task row (author, when, and
     a source chip for meeting or Slack notes), with an "N comments"
     pill that opens the full thread.
   - The thread modal shows every comment newest first with its source
     (you, a meeting transcript, or a Slack digest, each linking back
     to the source) and a box to add your own.
   - Automatic comments arrive from the transcript scanner (v2 of the
     transcript-requests function): when a meeting or Slack pull
     clearly discusses an open task, a progress note is attached with
     a verbatim quote. Informational only; nothing changes state.
   Works on department boards and project pages.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa51) return;
  window.__wa51 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa51css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w51last{display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:baseline;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:11.8px;color:#5b6660;margin-top:3px;}' +
    '.w51last .who{font-weight:700;color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w51src{font-size:9.5px;font-weight:800;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.4px;border-radius:999px;padding:1px 7px;}' +
    '.w51src.transcript{background:rgba(111,100,171,.14);color:#6F64AB;}' +
    '.w51src.slack{background:rgba(58,110,165,.14);color:#3a6ea5;}' +
    '.w51src.manual{background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);color:#103F39;}' +
    '[data-w51c]{display:inline-block;background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:999px;padding:1px 9px;' +
    'font-size:10.5px;color:#103F39 !important;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;line-height:1.6;margin-left:6px;}' +
    '[data-w51c]:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.4);}' +
    '.w51cm{border-top:1px solid #f0f2f1;padding:8px 0;}' +
    '.w51cm .hd{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;font-size:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;}' +
    '.w51cm .hd b{color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w51cm .hd .dt{color:#797773;font-size:11px;}' +
    '.w51cm .bd{font-size:13px;color:#1F201E;margin-top:3px;}' +
    '.w51cm .qt{font-size:11.5px;color:#797773;font-style:italic;margin-top:2px;}' +
    '.w51cm .hd a{color:#103F39;font-size:11px;text-decoration:none;font-weight:600;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  /* ---- data: one load, latest + counts per task ---- */
  var WA51 = { latest: {}, count: {}, loaded: false };
  async function wa51Load(force) {
    if (WA51.loaded && !force) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('task_comments').select('id,task_id,body,author,source,created_at').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(600);
      WA51.latest = {};
      WA51.count = {};
      ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (c) {
        var k = String(c.task_id);
        WA51.count[k] = (WA51.count[k] || 0) + 1;
        if (!WA51.latest[k]) WA51.latest[k] = c;
      });
      WA51.loaded = true;
    } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
  }
  function wa51Day(iso) {
    try { return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return ''; }
  }

  /* ---- row decoration ---- */
  function wa51RowHtml(tid) {
    var k = String(tid);
    var n = WA51.count[k] || 0;
    var pill = '<a data-w51c="' + k + '">' + (n ? n + ' comment' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's') : '+ comment') + '</a>';
    var c = WA51.latest[k];
    if (!c) return '<div class="w51last">' + pill + '</div>';
    return '<div class="w51last"><span class="w51src ' + esc(c.source || 'manual') + '">' + (c.source === 'transcript' ? 'meeting' : esc(c.source || 'note')) + '</span>'
      + '<span class="who">' + esc(c.author || '') + ':</span>'
      + '<span>' + esc(String(c.body || '').slice(0, 140)) + (String(c.body || '').length > 140 ? '...' : '') + '</span>'
      + '<span style="color:#797773">' + wa51Day(c.created_at) + '</span>' + pill + '</div>';
  }
  function wa51Decorate() {
    try {
      if (typeof isStaff === 'function' && !isStaff()) return;
      document.querySelectorAll('li[data-task]').forEach(function (li) {
        var tid = li.getAttribute('data-task');
        var body = li.querySelector('.ci-body');
        if (!body) return;
        var old = body.querySelector('.w51last');
        var html = wa51RowHtml(tid);
        if (old) { old.outerHTML = html; } else { body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html); }
      });
      document.querySelectorAll('.w27-task').forEach(function (row) {
        var cb = row.querySelector('input[data-w27t]');
        if (!cb) return;
        var tid = cb.getAttribute('data-w27t');
        var old = row.querySelector('.w51last');
        var html = wa51RowHtml(tid);
        if (old) { old.outerHTML = html; } else { row.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html); }
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  async function wa51Refresh(force) {
    await wa51Load(force);
    wa51Decorate();
  }

  /* ---- thread modal ---- */
  async function wa51Modal(tid) {
    var task = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (t) { return String(t.ref) === String(tid) || String(t.id) === String(tid); })[0];
    var title = task ? (task.title || '') : ('Task #' + tid);
    var r = null;
    try { r = await db.from('task_comments').select('*').eq('task_id', Number(tid)).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(50); } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
    var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
    var list = rows.length ? rows.map(function (c) {
      return '<div class="w51cm"><div class="hd">'
        + '<span class="w51src ' + esc(c.source || 'manual') + '">' + (c.source === 'transcript' ? 'meeting' : esc(c.source || 'note')) + '</span>'
        + '<b>' + esc(c.author || 'someone') + '</b>'
        + '<span class="dt">' + wa51Day(c.created_at) + '</span>'
        + (c.source_ref && c.source !== 'manual' ? '<a href="#tx/' + esc(String(c.source_ref)) + '" onclick="closeModal()">' + esc(c.source_label || 'open the meeting') + '</a>' : '')
        + '</div><div class="bd">' + (window.w138Mark ? window.w138Mark(esc(c.body)) : esc(c.body)) + '</div>'
        + (c.quote ? '<div class="qt">&quot;' + esc(c.quote) + '&quot;</div>' : '')
        + '</div>';
    }).join('') : '<p class="m-sub">No comments yet. Start the thread below.</p>';
    openModal('<h3 style="font-size:15px">' + esc(title) + '</h3>'
      + '<div style="max-height:320px;overflow:auto;margin:6px 0">' + list + '</div>'
      + '<textarea id="w51new" placeholder="Add a comment..." style="width:100%;min-height:64px"></textarea>'
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w51add" data-w51t="' + esc(String(tid)) + '">Add the comment</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w51cl">Close</button></div>');
    document.getElementById('w51cl').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w51add').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var txt = (document.getElementById('w51new').value || '').trim();
      if (!txt) { toast('Write the comment first'); return; }
      var ins = await db.from('task_comments').insert({
        task_id: Number(tid), body: txt.slice(0, 800),
        author: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff', source: 'manual'
      });
      if (ins.error) { toast('Could not save the comment'); return; }
      toast('Comment added');
      await wa51Refresh(true);
      wa51Modal(tid);
    });
  }

  /* ---- wiring ---- */
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa51Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa51Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w51c]');
      if (a && typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff()) wa51Modal(a.getAttribute('data-w51c'));
    });
  }
  if (typeof renderSection === 'function') {
    var __rs51 = renderSection;
    renderSection = function (id) {
      __rs51(id);
      wa51Decorate();
    };
  }
  if (typeof renderAllSections === 'function') {
    var __ras51 = renderAllSections;
    renderAllSections = function () {
      __ras51.apply(this, arguments);
      wa51Decorate();
    };
  }
  if (typeof wa27RenderProject === 'function') {
    var __pr51 = wa27RenderProject;
    wa27RenderProject = function () {
      var out = __pr51.apply(this, arguments);
      setTimeout(wa51Decorate, 80);
      return out;
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa51 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa51();
      try { await wa51Refresh(false); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v52 projects, due dates, and hours move to HR
   (03/08/26)
   Three things in one wave.
   1. Any task can be linked to a project by hand: every task row grows
      a "project" pill (current project or + project) opening a picker
      of active projects across departments, plus a due date pill.
   2. Due dates everywhere: set or clear a date from the row (with
      quick picks), overdue tasks get a light yellow tint, and the
      transcript scanner (v3) now suggests due dates it hears in
      meetings; suggestions show on the row as confirm-first chips.
   3. Hours leave the department pages and the dashboard. The HR centre
      gets an Hours tab: average weekly hours per team, who is below
      their minimum (managers and up 6h, everyone else 4h), and who is
      not checking in at all. The HR centre itself is now limited to
      the HR access list (Steel, Suhaib, Ian, Natasha, Valex, Tuna).
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa52) return;
  window.__wa52 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa52css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w52late{background:rgba(220,165,39,.14) !important;border-radius:10px;}' +
    '.w52row{display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:3px;font-size:11.5px;}' +
    'span.w52row{display:inline-flex;margin:0 0 0 8px;}' +
    '[data-w52p],[data-w52d]{display:inline-block;background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:999px;padding:1px 9px;' +
    'font-size:10.5px;color:#103F39 !important;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;line-height:1.6;text-decoration:none !important;}' +
    '[data-w52p]:hover,[data-w52d]:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.4);}' +
    '.w52sug{display:inline-flex;gap:7px;align-items:center;background:rgba(220,165,39,.16);color:#8a6414;border-radius:999px;padding:1px 10px;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w52sug a{cursor:pointer;color:#103F39;font-weight:800;}' +
    '.w52sug a:hover{text-decoration:underline;}' +
    '.w52sug [data-w52no]{color:#b3402f;}' +
