    '.w52quick a{display:inline-block;background:#FFFAF2;border:1px solid #DEE0D8;border-radius:999px;padding:2px 11px;font-size:11px;color:#103F39;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w52quick a:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.1);border-color:rgba(16,63,57,.4);}' +
    '.w52flags{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;}' +
    '.w52flag{background:rgba(179,64,47,.1);color:#b3402f;border-radius:999px;padding:4px 13px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w52flag small{font-weight:600;opacity:.75;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  function wa52Today() {
    var d = new Date();
    return d.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' + d.getDate()).slice(-2);
  }
  function wa52Day(iso) {
    try { return new Date(iso + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return iso; }
  }
  function wa52T(tid) {
    var a = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []);
    for (var i = 0; i < a.length; i++) { if (String(a[i].id) === String(tid)) return a[i]; }
    return null;
  }
  function wa52ProjName(pid) {
    if (!pid) return null;
    var p = (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_PROJECTS : []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(pid); })[0];
    if (!p) return 'project ' + pid;
    return (typeof wa27Name === 'function') ? wa27Name(p) : p.name;
  }

  /* ---- pending due date suggestions ---- */
  var WA52 = { sug: {}, loaded: false };
  async function wa52Load(force) {
    if (WA52.loaded && !force) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('task_due_suggestions').select('*').eq('status', 'pending').order('id', { ascending: false }).limit(200);
      WA52.sug = {};
      ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (s) {
        var k = String(s.task_id);
        if (!WA52.sug[k]) WA52.sug[k] = s;
      });
      WA52.loaded = true;
    } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
  }

  /* ---- row decoration ---- */
  function wa52Bits(t, withProject) {
    var tid = String(t.id);
    var h = '';
    if (withProject) {
      var pn = wa52ProjName(t.project_id);
      h += '<a data-w52p="' + tid + '">' + (pn ? 'project: ' + esc(pn) : '+ project') + '</a>';
    }
    h += '<a data-w52d="' + tid + '">' + (t.due_date ? 'due ' + esc(wa52Day(t.due_date)) : '+ due date') + '</a>';
    var s = WA52.sug[tid];
    if (s && !t.due_date && !t.done) {
      h += '<span class="w52sug" title="' + esc(s.quote || '') + '">The Hub suggests ' + esc(wa52Day(s.due_date))
        + ' <a data-w52ok="' + s.id + '" data-w52task="' + tid + '">accept</a>'
        + '<a data-w52no="' + s.id + '" data-w52task="' + tid + '">dismiss</a></span>';
    }
    return h;
  }
  function wa52Decorate() {
    try {
      var today = wa52Today();
      var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();
      document.querySelectorAll('li[data-task]').forEach(function (li) {
        var t = wa52T(li.getAttribute('data-task'));
        if (!t) return;
        li.classList.toggle('w52late', !t.done && !!t.due_date && t.due_date < today);
        if (!staff) return;
        var body = li.querySelector('.ci-body');
        if (!body) return;
        var html = '<div class="w52row">' + wa52Bits(t, true) + '</div>';
        var old = body.querySelector('.w52row');
        if (old) { old.outerHTML = html; return; }
        var anchor = body.querySelector('.w51last');
        if (anchor) anchor.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', html);
        else body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
      });
      document.querySelectorAll('.w27-task').forEach(function (row) {
        var cb = row.querySelector('input[data-w27t]');
        if (!cb) return;
        var t = wa52T(cb.getAttribute('data-w27t'));
        if (!t) return;
        row.classList.toggle('w52late', !t.done && !!t.due_date && t.due_date < today);
        if (!staff) return;
        var meta = row.querySelector('.meta');
        if (!meta) return;
        var html = '<span class="w52row">' + wa52Bits(t, false) + '</span>';
        var old2 = row.querySelector('.w52row');
        if (old2) { old2.outerHTML = html; } else { meta.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html); }
      });
      /* strip any hours leftovers from department pages and the dashboard */
      document.querySelectorAll('h3.block').forEach(function (h3) {
        if (h3.textContent.indexOf('Hours in (') === 0) {
          var nx = h3.nextElementSibling;
          if (nx && nx.classList && nx.classList.contains('team-tags')) nx.remove();
          h3.remove();
        }
      });
      document.querySelectorAll('.card h2').forEach(function (h2) {
        if (h2.textContent === 'Team hours') { try { h2.parentElement.remove(); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ } }
      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  /* ---- writes and refresh ---- */
  async function wa52AfterWrite() {
    try { if (typeof wa27Load === 'function') await wa27Load(true); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try {
      var pp = document.getElementById('projectpage');
      if (typeof WA27_VIEW !== 'undefined' && WA27_VIEW && pp && pp.classList.contains('on')) {
        wa27RenderProject(WA27_VIEW.ref, WA27_VIEW.dept);
      } else if (typeof renderAllSections === 'function') {
        renderAllSections();
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try {
      if (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' && typeof wa27DeptCard === 'function') {
        DEPT_ORDER.forEach(function (d) { try { wa27DeptCard(d); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ } });
      }
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    setTimeout(wa52Decorate, 60);
  }

  function wa52ProjModal(tid) {
    var t = wa52T(tid);
    if (!t) return;
    var byDept = {};
    (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_PROJECTS : []).forEach(function (p) {
      (byDept[p.department] = byDept[p.department] || []).push(p);
    });
    var opts = '<option value="">General tasks (no project)</option>';
    Object.keys(byDept).sort().forEach(function (d) {
      var label = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d;
      opts += '<optgroup label="' + esc(label) + '">' + byDept[d].map(function (p) {
        return '<option value="' + p.id + '"' + (String(t.project_id || '') === String(p.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(wa52ProjName(p.id)) + '</option>';
      }).join('') + '</optgroup>';
    });
    openModal('<h3>Link to a project</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">' + esc(t.title || '') + '</p>'
      + '<label>Project</label><select id="w52pp">' + opts + '</select>'
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w52pgo">Save</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w52pc">Cancel</button></div>');
    document.getElementById('w52pc').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w52pgo').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var el = this;
      el.disabled = true;
      var v = document.getElementById('w52pp').value;
      var nv = v ? Number(v) : null;
      var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').update({
        project_id: nv,
        updated_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff',
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      }).eq('id', Number(tid));
      if (r.error) { el.disabled = false; toast('Could not link it'); return; }
      try { logChange('dept_task', Number(tid), 'project', wa52ProjName(t.project_id) || 'General', wa52ProjName(nv) || 'General'); } catch (e) { /* log only */ }
      t.project_id = nv;
      closeModal();
      toast(nv ? 'Linked to ' + (wa52ProjName(nv) || 'the project') : 'Moved to General tasks');
      wa52AfterWrite();
    });
  }

  function wa52DueModal(tid) {
    var t = wa52T(tid);
    if (!t) return;
    var s = WA52.sug[String(tid)];
    openModal('<h3>Due date</h3>'
      + '<p class="m-sub">' + esc(t.title || '') + '</p>'
      + '<label>Due on</label><input type="date" id="w52dd" value="' + esc(t.due_date || '') + '" />'
      + '<div class="w52quick">'
      + '<a data-w52q="1">Tomorrow</a><a data-w52q="7">In a week</a><a data-w52q="14">In two weeks</a><a data-w52q="eom">End of month</a>'
      + '</div>'
      + (s && !t.due_date ? '<p class="m-sub" style="margin-top:8px">The Hub heard <b>' + esc(wa52Day(s.due_date)) + '</b> in a meeting'
        + (s.quote ? ': &quot;' + esc(String(s.quote).slice(0, 140)) + '&quot;' : '') + '</p>' : '')
      + '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w52dgo">Save</button> '
      + (t.due_date ? '<button class="btn ghost" id="w52dclr">Clear the date</button> ' : '')
      + '<button class="btn ghost" id="w52dc">Cancel</button></div>');
    document.getElementById('w52dc').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-w52q]').forEach(function (a) {
      a.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var q = a.getAttribute('data-w52q');
        var d = new Date();
        if (q === 'eom') d = new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth() + 1, 0);
        else d.setDate(d.getDate() + Number(q));
        document.getElementById('w52dd').value = d.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' + d.getDate()).slice(-2);
      });
    });
    async function save(v) {
      var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').update({
        due_date: v,
        updated_by: (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff',
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      }).eq('id', Number(tid));
      if (r.error) { toast('Could not save the date'); return; }
      try { logChange('dept_task', Number(tid), 'due_date', t.due_date, v); } catch (e) { /* log only */ }
      t.due_date = v;
      closeModal();
      toast(v ? 'Due ' + wa52Day(v) : 'Date cleared');
      wa52AfterWrite();
    }
    document.getElementById('w52dgo').addEventListener('click', function () { save(document.getElementById('w52dd').value || null); });
    var clr = document.getElementById('w52dclr');
    if (clr) clr.addEventListener('click', function () { save(null); });
  }

  async function wa52Accept(sid, tid) {
    var s = WA52.sug[String(tid)];
    if (!s || String(s.id) !== String(sid)) return;
    var by = (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff';
    var t = wa52T(tid);
    var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').update({ due_date: s.due_date, updated_by: by, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', Number(tid));
    if (r.error) { toast('Could not set the date'); return; }
    await db.from('task_due_suggestions').update({ status: 'accepted', decided_by: by, decided_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', Number(sid));
    try { logChange('dept_task', Number(tid), 'due_date', t ? t.due_date : null, s.due_date); } catch (e) { /* log only */ }
    if (t) t.due_date = s.due_date;
    delete WA52.sug[String(tid)];
    toast('Due ' + wa52Day(s.due_date));
    wa52AfterWrite();
  }
  async function wa52Dismiss(sid, tid) {
    var by = (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff';
    var r = await db.from('task_due_suggestions').update({ status: 'dismissed', decided_by: by, decided_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', Number(sid));
    if (r.error) { toast('Could not dismiss it'); return; }
    delete WA52.sug[String(tid)];
    toast('Dismissed');
    wa52Decorate();
  }

  /* ---- hours leave the department pages ---- */
  try { hoursBlock = function () { return ''; }; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  try { hoursCardHtml = function () { return ''; }; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  /* ---- HR access list ---- */
  var WA52_HR = [
    'steel.macdonald@welfareavenue.com',
    'suhaib.abdillahi@welfareavenue.com',
    'ian.odhiambo@welfareavenue.com',
    'natasha.sharp@welfareavenue.com',
    'valex.sanon@welfareavenue.com',
    'tuna.kaynak@welfareavenue.com'
  ];
  function wa52HrOk() {
    /* wave127: with an access profile, the HR centre follows the profile; HR records stay with the HR access list in the database */
    try { if (window.w127 && typeof window.w127.hrOk === 'function') { var v127 = window.w127.hrOk(); if (v127 === true || v127 === false) return v127; } } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try {
      return WA52_HR.indexOf(String((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase()) >= 0;
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }

  /* ---- the Hours dashboard in the HR centre ---- */
  var WA52_LEAD = /\b(lead|leader|director|manager|chair|co-)/i;
  function wa52Min(m) { return (m.department === 'board' || WA52_LEAD.test(m.role || '')) ? 6 : 4; }
  function wa52HoursData() {
    var members = (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m.active !== false; });
    var hours = (typeof DB_HOURS !== 'undefined' ? DB_HOURS : []);
    var thisWeek = (typeof weekStartOf === 'function') ? weekStartOf(new Date()) : '';
    var wk = [];
    for (var i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
      var d = new Date();
      d.setDate(d.getDate() - 7 * i);
      wk.push(weekStartOf(d));
    }
    var byKey = {};
    hours.forEach(function (h) {
      var n = String(h.member_name || h.slack_name || '').toLowerCase();
      if (!n) return;
      var r = byKey[n] || (byKey[n] = { cur: 0, w3: 0, matched: false });
      var hh = Number(h.hours || 0);
      if (h.week_start === thisWeek) r.cur += hh;
      else if (wk.indexOf(h.week_start) >= 0) r.w3 += hh;
    });
    function lookup(name) {
      var full = String(name || '').toLowerCase();
      if (byKey[full]) return byKey[full];
      var ft = full.split(/\s+/)[0];
      for (var k in byKey) { if (k.split(/\s+/)[0] === ft) return byKey[k]; }
      return null;
    }
    var rows = [], silent = [];
    members.forEach(function (m) {
      var h = lookup(m.name);
      var min = wa52Min(m);
      if (!h) { silent.push({ m: m, min: min }); return; }
      h.matched = true;
      rows.push({ m: m, min: min, cur: h.cur, avg: h.w3 / 3 });
    });
    var unmatched = [];
    for (var k2 in byKey) { if (!byKey[k2].matched) unmatched.push(k2); }
    return { rows: rows, silent: silent, unmatched: unmatched };
  }
  function wa52HoursHtml() {
    var d = wa52HoursData();
    var dl = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined') ? DEPT_LABEL : {};
    var below = d.rows.filter(function (r) { return r.avg < r.min; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.avg - a.min) - (b.avg - b.min); });
    var byDept = {};
    d.rows.forEach(function (r) {
      var k = r.m.department || 'other';
      (byDept[k] = byDept[k] || []).push(r.avg);
    });
    var maxAvg = 0.1;
    var teamRows = Object.keys(byDept).map(function (k) {
      var a = byDept[k];
      var avg = a.reduce(function (s2, x) { return s2 + x; }, 0) / a.length;
      if (avg > maxAvg) maxAvg = avg;
      return { k: k, avg: avg, n: a.length };
    }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.avg - a.avg; });
    var html = '<div class="w44tiles">'
      + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + d.rows.length + '</b><span>people checking in</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w44tile' + (below.length ? ' warn' : '') + '"><b>' + below.length + '</b><span>below their minimum</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w44tile' + (d.silent.length ? ' warn' : '') + '"><b>' + d.silent.length + '</b><span>not checking in</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + (d.rows.length - below.length) + '</b><span>meeting their minimum</span></div>'
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w47chart"><h4>Average weekly hours per team (last 3 full weeks, people with check-ins)</h4>'
      + (teamRows.length ? teamRows.map(function (r) {
        var w = Math.max(2, Math.round(r.avg / maxAvg * 100));
        return '<div class="w47bar"><span class="lb">' + esc(dl[r.k] || r.k) + ' (' + r.n + ')</span>'
          + '<span class="tr"><i style="width:' + w + '%"></i></span><span class="vl">' + r.avg.toFixed(1) + 'h</span></div>';
      }).join('') : '<p class="m-sub">No tracked check-ins yet.</p>')
      + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="w47chart"><h4>Below their weekly minimum (managers and up 6h, everyone else 4h)</h4>'
      + (below.length ? '<table class="w49tbl"><thead><tr><th>Person</th><th>Team</th><th>Role</th><th>Avg / min</th><th>This week</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
        + below.map(function (r) {
          return '<tr><td><b>' + esc(r.m.name) + '</b></td><td>' + esc(dl[r.m.department] || r.m.department || '') + '</td>'
            + '<td>' + esc(r.m.role || '') + '</td>'
            + '<td><span style="color:#b3402f;font-weight:700">' + r.avg.toFixed(1) + 'h</span> / ' + r.min + 'h</td>'
            + '<td>' + r.cur.toFixed(1) + 'h</td></tr>';
        }).join('') + '</tbody></table>'
        : '<p class="m-sub">Everyone with check-ins is at or above their minimum.</p>')
      + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="w47chart"><h4>Not checking in at all (no tracked check-ins in the last 4 weeks)</h4>'
      + (d.silent.length ? '<div class="w52flags">' + d.silent.map(function (s) {
        return '<span class="w52flag">' + esc(s.m.name) + ' <small>' + esc(dl[s.m.department] || s.m.department || '') + ' &middot; min ' + s.min + 'h/wk</small></span>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="m-sub">Everyone on the roster has at least one tracked check-in.</p>')
      + (d.unmatched.length ? '<p class="m-sub" style="margin-top:8px">Check-in names with no roster match: ' + d.unmatched.map(esc).join(', ') + '</p>' : '')
      + '<p class="m-sub" style="margin-top:8px">Hours come from the Slack check-in-out channels: a check in or out counts at its message time, and sessions missing a check out are not counted. Minimums: 6h per week for managers and up, 4h for everyone else.</p>'
      + '</div>';
    return html;
  }

  function wa52HrDecorate() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!panel) return;
    if (!wa52HrOk()) {
      if (!panel.querySelector('#w52denied') && panel.innerHTML !== '') {
        panel.innerHTML = '<div class="card" id="w52denied"><h2>HR centre</h2>'
          + '<p class="read">This area is limited to the HR access list: Steel, Suhaib, Ian, Natasha, Valex and Tuna. Ask Steel if you need access.</p></div>';
      }
      return;
    }
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body');
    if (!body) return;
    var tabs = body.querySelector('.w47tabs');
    if (tabs && !tabs.querySelector('[data-w47v="hours"]')) {
      tabs.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<button class="w47tab" data-w47v="hours">Hours</button>');
    }
    var sec = body.querySelector('#w52hours');
    if (!sec) {
      body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<div id="w52hours" style="display:none"></div>');
      sec = body.querySelector('#w52hours');
      try { sec.innerHTML = wa52HoursHtml(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    }
    if (String(location.hash || '').indexOf('#hrcentre/hours') === 0 && sec.style.display === 'none' && !sec.dataset.w52shown) {
      sec.dataset.w52shown = '1';
      sec.style.display = '';
      body.querySelectorAll('.w46sec').forEach(function (x) { x.style.display = 'none'; });
      body.querySelectorAll('.w47tab').forEach(function (c) { c.classList.toggle('on', c.getAttribute('data-w47v') === 'hours'); });
    }
  }
  function wa52HrWatch() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!panel) return;
    if (!panel.dataset.w52mo) {
      panel.dataset.w52mo = '1';
      var mo = new MutationObserver(function () { wa52HrDecorate(); });
      mo.observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }
    wa52HrDecorate();
  }

  /* ---- wiring ---- */
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa52Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa52Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var staff = typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff();
      var p = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w52p]');
      if (p) { if (staff) wa52ProjModal(p.getAttribute('data-w52p')); return; }
      var d = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w52d]');
      if (d) { if (staff) wa52DueModal(d.getAttribute('data-w52d')); return; }
      var ok = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w52ok]');
      if (ok) { if (staff) wa52Accept(ok.getAttribute('data-w52ok'), ok.getAttribute('data-w52task')); return; }
      var no = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w52no]');
      if (no) { if (staff) wa52Dismiss(no.getAttribute('data-w52no'), no.getAttribute('data-w52task')); return; }
      var tb = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w47tab');
      if (tb) {
        var sec = document.getElementById('w52hours');
        if (sec) {
          if (tb.getAttribute('data-w47v') === 'hours') {
            try { sec.innerHTML = wa52HoursHtml(); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ }
            sec.style.display = '';
          } else {
            sec.style.display = 'none';
          }
        }
      }
    });
  }

  if (typeof renderSection === 'function') {
    var __rs52 = renderSection;
    renderSection = function (id) {
      __rs52(id);
      wa52Decorate();
    };
  }
  if (typeof renderAllSections === 'function') {
    var __ras52 = renderAllSections;
    renderAllSections = function () {
      __ras52.apply(this, arguments);
      wa52Decorate();
    };
  }
  if (typeof wa27RenderProject === 'function') {
    var __pr52 = wa27RenderProject;
    wa27RenderProject = function () {
      var out = __pr52.apply(this, arguments);
      setTimeout(wa52Decorate, 140);
      return out;
    };
  }
  if (typeof wa40Enforce === 'function') {
    var __enf52 = wa40Enforce;
    wa40Enforce = function () {
      __enf52.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        if (!wa52HrOk()) {
          var tb = document.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="hrcentre"]');
          if (tb) tb.style.display = 'none';
        }
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp52 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      __sp52(id);
      try { if (id === 'hrcentre') wa52HrWatch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa52 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa52();
      try { await wa52Load(false); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      try { wa52Decorate(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      try { wa52HrWatch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      try { wa40Enforce(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v53 agendas: only yours, and purpose-driven drafting
   (03/08/26)
   1. Visibility: non-admins now only see agendas they are part of:
      named attendee (or with_person), their own department's meetings,
      leaders meetings if they lead (or sit on the board), or agendas
      they generated themselves. Admins (hub_roles) still see all.
      Filter applied at the cache level so every agenda surface follows.
   2. Purpose-driven drafting: a "Purpose and structure" box on the
      agenda builder. Whatever you type rides along to the build-agenda
      function (v5), which uses it as the blueprint: the agenda mirrors
      the stated structure and pulls only tasks and topics that serve
      it. The purpose is stored and shown on the drafted agenda.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa53) return;
  window.__wa53 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa53css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '#w53purpose{width:100%;min-height:52px;margin-top:8px;padding:8px 10px;border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.35);' +
    'border-radius:10px;font-size:12.5px;font-family:inherit;resize:vertical;box-sizing:border-box;}' +
    '.w53pl{font-size:12px;color:#797773;background:rgba(111,100,171,.07);border-radius:8px;padding:6px 10px;margin:2px 0 8px;}' +
    '.w53pl b{color:#6F64AB;}' +
    '.w53note{font-size:11.5px;color:#797773;margin:4px 0 0;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  var WA53_LEAD = /\b(lead|leader|director|manager|chair|co-)/i;

  function wa53IsAdmin() {
    try { return typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin'; } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  function wa53First(s) { return String(s || '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0].toLowerCase(); }
  function wa53Mine(a) {
    try {
      if (wa53IsAdmin()) return true;
      var me = (typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined') ? WA_ME : null;
      var email = String((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase();
      var pfx = email.split('@')[0];
      /* you always see what you generated */
      if (pfx && String(a.created_by || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(pfx) >= 0) return true;
      var myFirst = me ? wa53First(me.name) : (pfx ? pfx.split('.')[0] : '');
      if (myFirst) {
        var att = Array.isArray(a.attendees) ? a.attendees : [];
        for (var i = 0; i < att.length; i++) { if (wa53First(att[i]) === myFirst) return true; }
        if (a.with_person && String(a.with_person).toLowerCase().indexOf(myFirst) >= 0) return true;
      }
      var scope = a.scope || 'department';
      if (scope === 'department' && me && a.department && a.department === me.department) return true;
      if (scope === 'leaders' && me && (me.department === 'board' || WA53_LEAD.test(me.role || ''))) return true;
      return false;
    } catch (e) { return true; }
  }

  /* filter the agenda cache after every load */
  if (typeof waLoadAgendas === 'function') {
    var __la53 = waLoadAgendas;
    waLoadAgendas = async function () {
      await __la53.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        if (wa53IsAdmin()) return;
        if (typeof waResolveMe === 'function') { try { await waResolveMe(); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ } }
        if (typeof AGENDAS_CACHE !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(AGENDAS_CACHE)) {
          AGENDAS_CACHE = AGENDAS_CACHE.filter(wa53Mine);
          if (typeof AG_ITEMS_CACHE !== 'undefined' && Array.isArray(AG_ITEMS_CACHE)) {
            var keep = {};
            AGENDAS_CACHE.forEach(function (a) { keep[String(a.id)] = 1; });
            AG_ITEMS_CACHE = AG_ITEMS_CACHE.filter(function (i) { return keep[String(i.agenda_id)]; });
          }
        }
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  /* the purpose box on the builder */
  function wa53EnsureBox() {
    try {
      var gen = document.getElementById('agNewGen');
      if (!gen || document.getElementById('w53purpose')) return;
      var host = gen.closest('.ag-new') || gen.parentElement;
      if (!host) return;
      host.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
        '<textarea id="w53purpose" placeholder="Purpose and structure (optional): what this meeting is for and how it should run. Example: monthly marketing planning, 15 min on last month, then the content calendar, then blockers."></textarea>'
        + '<p class="w53note">The Hub treats this as the blueprint: the agenda follows your structure and only pulls in the tasks and topics that fit it.</p>');
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }

  /* show the purpose on drafted agendas */
  if (typeof waAgendaRowHtml === 'function') {
    var __row53 = waAgendaRowHtml;
    waAgendaRowHtml = function (a) {
      var html = __row53.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        if (a && a.purpose) {
          html = html.replace('<div class="ag-body">',
            '<div class="ag-body"><div class="w53pl"><b>Purpose:</b> ' + esc(String(a.purpose).slice(0, 400)) + '</div>');
        }
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return html;
    };
  }

  if (typeof waRenderAgendasPage === 'function') {
    var __rap53 = waRenderAgendasPage;
    waRenderAgendasPage = async function () {
      var out = await __rap53.apply(this, arguments);
      wa53EnsureBox();
      return out;
    };
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp53 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      __sp53(id);
      try { if (id === 'agendas') wa53EnsureBox(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa53 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa53();
      /* ride the purpose along on every build-agenda call (wrapped here so
         it captures the real client, after auth and offline stubs settle) */
      try {
        if (db && db.functions && db.functions.invoke && !db.functions.__w53) {
          var __inv53 = db.functions.invoke.bind(db.functions);
          db.functions.invoke = function (name, opts) {
            try {
              if (name === 'build-agenda') {
                var ta = document.getElementById('w53purpose');
                var v = ta && (ta.value || '').trim();
                if (v) {
                  opts = opts || {};
                  opts.body = Object.assign({}, opts.body || {}, { purpose: v.slice(0, 1200) });
                }
              }
            } catch (e) { /* pass through */ }
            return __inv53(name, opts);
          };
          db.functions.__w53 = 1;
        }
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      try { wa53EnsureBox(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v54 hours: editable minimums, board exempt, tasks per week
   (03/08/26)
   Rebuilds the HR centre Hours dashboard (on top of v52's section):
   - The weekly minimums (was hardcoded 6h managers and up, 4h everyone
     else) now live in public.hub_settings 'hours_minimums' and are
     editable right in the tab (two inputs + Save, change-logged).
   - The board is exempt: no minimum applies to board members and they
     are never flagged for not checking in; if they do check in their
     hours still show.
   - Tasks completed per week: every person now shows how many tasks
     they closed per week (average of the last 3 full weeks, plus this
     week), matched by task owner. The below-minimum-only table becomes
     a full people table with below-minimum rows highlighted first.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa54) return;
  window.__wa54 = 1;

  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'wa54css';
  st.textContent = '' +
    '.w54edit{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;background:#FFFAF2;border-radius:12px;padding:10px 14px;margin:0 0 10px;font-size:12.5px;color:#1F201E;}' +
    '.w54edit b{color:#103F39;}' +
    '.w54edit input{width:58px;padding:4px 8px;border:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.35);border-radius:8px;font-size:12.5px;}' +
    '.w54low td{background:rgba(179,64,47,.06);}' +
    '.w54ex{font-size:10px;font-weight:800;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.4px;color:#797773;background:#ECEBE3;border-radius:999px;padding:1px 8px;}';
  document.head.appendChild(st);

  var WA54 = { min: { manager: 6, member: 4 }, loaded: false };
  async function wa54LoadMin(force) {
    if (WA54.loaded && !force) return;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('hub_settings').select('value').eq('key', 'hours_minimums').maybeSingle();
      var v = r && r.data && r.data.value;
      if (v) {
        var mg = Number(v.manager), mm = Number(v.member);
        if (mg > 0) WA54.min.manager = mg;
        if (mm > 0) WA54.min.member = mm;
      }
      WA54.loaded = true;
    } catch (e) { /* defaults hold */ }
  }

  var WA54_LEAD = /\b(lead|leader|director|manager|chair|co-)/i;
  /* board members are exempt: min 0, never flagged */
  function wa54Min(m) {
    if (m.department === 'board') return 0;
    return WA54_LEAD.test(m.role || '') ? WA54.min.manager : WA54.min.member;
  }

  function wa54Data() {
    var members = (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m.active !== false; });
    var hours = (typeof DB_HOURS !== 'undefined' ? DB_HOURS : []);
    var tasks = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []);
    var thisWeek = (typeof weekStartOf === 'function') ? weekStartOf(new Date()) : '';
    var wk = [];
    for (var i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
      var d = new Date();
      d.setDate(d.getDate() - 7 * i);
      wk.push(weekStartOf(d));
    }
    var byKey = {};
    hours.forEach(function (h) {
      var n = String(h.member_name || h.slack_name || '').toLowerCase();
      if (!n) return;
      var r = byKey[n] || (byKey[n] = { cur: 0, w3: 0, matched: false });
      var hh = Number(h.hours || 0);
      if (h.week_start === thisWeek) r.cur += hh;
      else if (wk.indexOf(h.week_start) >= 0) r.w3 += hh;
    });
    function lookup(name) {
      var full = String(name || '').toLowerCase();
      if (byKey[full]) return byKey[full];
      var ft = full.split(/\s+/)[0];
      for (var k in byKey) { if (k.split(/\s+/)[0] === ft) return byKey[k]; }
      return null;
    }
    /* tasks closed per week, by owner text */
    var doneRows = [];
    tasks.forEach(function (t) {
      if (!t.done || !t.closed_at) return;
      var ws;
      try { ws = weekStartOf(new Date(t.closed_at)); } catch (e) { return; }
      if (ws === thisWeek) doneRows.push({ owner: String(t.owner || '').toLowerCase(), cur: 1, w3: 0 });
      else if (wk.indexOf(ws) >= 0) doneRows.push({ owner: String(t.owner || '').toLowerCase(), cur: 0, w3: 1 });
    });
    function taskStats(name) {
      var ft = String(name || '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0].toLowerCase();
      var cur = 0, w3 = 0;
      if (ft) {
        doneRows.forEach(function (r) {
          if (r.owner.indexOf(ft) >= 0) { cur += r.cur; w3 += r.w3; }
        });
      }
      return { cur: cur, avg: w3 / 3 };
    }
    var rows = [], silent = [];
    members.forEach(function (m) {
      var h = lookup(m.name);
      var min = wa54Min(m);
      var ts = taskStats(m.name);
      if (!h) {
        if (m.department !== 'board') silent.push({ m: m, min: min, tasks: ts });
        return;
      }
      h.matched = true;
      rows.push({ m: m, min: min, cur: h.cur, avg: h.w3 / 3, tasks: ts });
    });
    var unmatched = [];
    for (var k2 in byKey) { if (!byKey[k2].matched) unmatched.push(k2); }
    return { rows: rows, silent: silent, unmatched: unmatched };
  }

  function wa54Html() {
    var d = wa54Data();
    var dl = (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined') ? DEPT_LABEL : {};
    var below = d.rows.filter(function (r) { return r.min > 0 && r.avg < r.min; });
    var byDept = {};
    d.rows.forEach(function (r) {
      var k = r.m.department || 'other';
      (byDept[k] = byDept[k] || []).push(r.avg);
    });
    var maxAvg = 0.1;
    var teamRows = Object.keys(byDept).map(function (k) {
      var a = byDept[k];
      var avg = a.reduce(function (s2, x) { return s2 + x; }, 0) / a.length;
      if (avg > maxAvg) maxAvg = avg;
      return { k: k, avg: avg, n: a.length };
    }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.avg - a.avg; });
    var minTxt = 'managers and up ' + WA54.min.manager + 'h, everyone else ' + WA54.min.member + 'h, board exempt';
    var people = d.rows.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
      var ba = a.min > 0 && a.avg < a.min ? (a.avg - a.min) : 999;
      var bb = b.min > 0 && b.avg < b.min ? (b.avg - b.min) : 999;
      return ba - bb || b.avg - a.avg;
    });
    var html = '<div class="w54edit"><b>Weekly minimums:</b>'
      + ' managers and up <input type="number" id="w54mgr" min="1" max="40" value="' + WA54.min.manager + '" />h,'
      + ' everyone else <input type="number" id="w54mem" min="1" max="40" value="' + WA54.min.member + '" />h'
      + ' <button class="btn mini" data-w54save="1">Save</button>'
      + '<span style="color:#797773;font-size:11.5px">the board is exempt from check-ins</span></div>';
    html += '<div class="w44tiles">'
      + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + d.rows.length + '</b><span>people checking in</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w44tile' + (below.length ? ' warn' : '') + '"><b>' + below.length + '</b><span>below their minimum</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w44tile' + (d.silent.length ? ' warn' : '') + '"><b>' + d.silent.length + '</b><span>not checking in</span></div>'
      + '<div class="w44tile"><b>' + (d.rows.length - below.length) + '</b><span>meeting their minimum</span></div>'
      + '</div>'
      + '<div class="w47chart"><h4>Average weekly hours per team (last 3 full weeks, people with check-ins)</h4>'
      + (teamRows.length ? teamRows.map(function (r) {
        var w = Math.max(2, Math.round(r.avg / maxAvg * 100));
        return '<div class="w47bar"><span class="lb">' + esc(dl[r.k] || r.k) + ' (' + r.n + ')</span>'
          + '<span class="tr"><i style="width:' + w + '%"></i></span><span class="vl">' + r.avg.toFixed(1) + 'h</span></div>';
      }).join('') : '<p class="m-sub">No tracked check-ins yet.</p>')
      + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="w47chart"><h4>People (' + esc(minTxt) + '; below-minimum rows first)</h4>'
      + (people.length ? '<table class="w49tbl"><thead><tr><th>Person</th><th>Team</th><th>Avg h/wk / min</th><th>This week</th><th>Tasks done/wk</th><th>Tasks this week</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
        + people.map(function (r) {
          var low = r.min > 0 && r.avg < r.min;
          var minCell = r.min > 0
            ? (low ? '<span style="color:#b3402f;font-weight:700">' + r.avg.toFixed(1) + 'h</span> / ' + r.min + 'h'
                   : r.avg.toFixed(1) + 'h / ' + r.min + 'h')
            : r.avg.toFixed(1) + 'h <span class="w54ex">exempt</span>';
          return '<tr' + (low ? ' class="w54low"' : '') + '><td><b>' + esc(r.m.name) + '</b></td>'
            + '<td>' + esc(dl[r.m.department] || r.m.department || '') + '</td>'
            + '<td>' + minCell + '</td>'
            + '<td>' + r.cur.toFixed(1) + 'h</td>'
            + '<td>' + r.tasks.avg.toFixed(1) + '</td>'
            + '<td>' + r.tasks.cur + '</td></tr>';
        }).join('') + '</tbody></table>'
        : '<p class="m-sub">No tracked check-ins yet.</p>')
      + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="w47chart"><h4>Not checking in at all (no tracked check-ins in the last 4 weeks; board exempt)</h4>'
      + (d.silent.length ? '<div class="w52flags">' + d.silent.map(function (s) {
        return '<span class="w52flag">' + esc(s.m.name) + ' <small>' + esc(dl[s.m.department] || s.m.department || '') + ' &middot; min ' + s.min + 'h/wk'
          + (s.tasks.avg ? ' &middot; ' + s.tasks.avg.toFixed(1) + ' tasks/wk' : '') + '</small></span>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="m-sub">Everyone outside the board has at least one tracked check-in.</p>')
      + (d.unmatched.length ? '<p class="m-sub" style="margin-top:8px">Check-in names with no roster match: ' + d.unmatched.map(esc).join(', ') + '</p>' : '')
      + '<p class="m-sub" style="margin-top:8px">Hours come from the Slack check-in-out channels: a check in or out counts at its message time, and sessions missing a check out are not counted. Tasks done/wk counts completed board tasks by owner, averaged over the last 3 full weeks. Minimums are editable above; the board is exempt.</p>'
      + '</div>';
    return html;
  }

  function wa54Rewrite() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('w52hours');
    if (!sec) return;
    try { sec.innerHTML = wa54Html(); sec.dataset.w54done = '1'; } catch (e) { /* keep v52's version */ }
  }

  async function wa54Save(btn) {
    var mg = Math.max(1, Math.min(40, Number((document.getElementById('w54mgr') || {}).value) || 0));
    var mm = Math.max(1, Math.min(40, Number((document.getElementById('w54mem') || {}).value) || 0));
    if (!mg || !mm) { toast('Both minimums need a number of hours'); return; }
    btn.disabled = true;
    var by = (typeof who === 'function') ? who() : 'staff';
    var old = WA54.min.manager + '/' + WA54.min.member;
    var r = await db.from('hub_settings').upsert({
      key: 'hours_minimums',
      value: { manager: mg, member: mm },
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      updated_by: by
    });
    btn.disabled = false;
    if (r.error) { toast('Could not save the minimums'); return; }
    WA54.min = { manager: mg, member: mm };
    try {
      await db.from('change_log').insert({
        entity_type: 'settings', entity_id: 'hours_minimums', field: 'minimums',
        old_value: old, new_value: mg + '/' + mm, changed_by: by
      });
    } catch (e) { /* log only */ }
    toast('Minimums saved: ' + mg + 'h managers and up, ' + mm + 'h everyone else');
    wa54Rewrite();
  }

  /* rewrite v52's hours section whenever it appears or the tab opens */
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa54Wired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa54Wired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var sv = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w54save]');
      if (sv) { wa54Save(sv); return; }
      var tb = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w47tab');
      /* runs after v52's listener (registered later), so v52 has already
         rebuilt the section; overwrite it with the settings-driven version */
      if (tb && tb.getAttribute('data-w47v') === 'hours') wa54Rewrite();
    });
  }
  function wa54Watch() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre');
    if (!panel) return;
    if (!panel.dataset.w54mo) {
      panel.dataset.w54mo = '1';
      var mo = new MutationObserver(function () {
        var sec = document.getElementById('w52hours');
        if (sec && !sec.dataset.w54done) wa54Rewrite();
      });
      mo.observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }
    var sec = document.getElementById('w52hours');
    if (sec && !sec.dataset.w54done) wa54Rewrite();
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp54 = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      __sp54(id);
      try { if (id === 'hrcentre') wa54Watch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa54 = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      await __sa54();
      try { await wa54LoadMin(false); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      try { wa54Watch(); } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v55 the Hours tab becomes Commitment
   (03/08/26)
   Cosmetic rename: the HR centre tab (and the dashboard it opens) is
   now called Commitment. Internals keep the 'hours' key so v52/v54
   wiring and the #hrcentre/hours deep link continue to work.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa55) return;
  window.__wa55 = 1;

  function wa55Rename() {
    try {
      var btn = document.querySelector('.w47tab[data-w47v="hours"]');
